śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
niyatakālakāṇḍam
maṅgalācaraṇam
yena pratyaham abhrasindhupayasi snānādibhiḥ karmabhir
yajñaiḥ karmabhir apy apāstakalibhir nītaḥ parām unnatim |
dharmo vismṛtavān kṛtasya virahakleśaṃ sa lakṣmīdharaḥ
kāṇḍaṃ naiyatakālikaṃ kalayati prītyai tṛtīyaṃ satām ||
pratijñā
dantānāṃ dhāvanaṃ prātaḥsnānaṃ snānavidhir japaḥ |
[2]        tarpaṇaṃ vaiśvadevādipañcayajñavishis tataḥ ||
bhojanaṃ pallavopetaṃ kṛtyaṃ yad bhojanottaram |
sapallavaṃ rātrikṛtyaṃ parvakṛtyam anantaram ||
tithikṛtyaṃ ca kāntāradīpadānavidhis tathā |
gośuśrūṣā vṛṣotsargaḥ purāṇaśravaṇe vidhiḥ ||
iti lakṣmīdharākhyena varṇyate śuddhabuddhinā |
caturdaśa ca parvāṇi kāṇḍe naiyatakālike ||
[3]
atha niyatakālakṛtyāni
tatra dakṣaḥ |
uktaṃ karma kramo nokto na kālas tv ata eva hi |
dvijānāṃ tu hitārthāya dakṣas tu svayam abravīt ||
prātar utthāya kartavyaṃ yad dvijena dine dine |
tat sarvaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi dvijānām upakārakam ||
udayāstamanaṃ yāvan na vipraḥ kṣaṇiko bhavet |
nityanaimittakair yuktaḥ kāmyaiś cānyair agarhitaiḥ ||
svakaṃ karma parityajya yad anyat kurute dvijaḥ |
ajñānād atha vā lobhāt tyaktena patito bhavet ||
divasasyādyabhāge tu kṛtyaṃ tasyopadiśyate |
dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca caturthe pañcame tathā ||
ṣaṣṭhe ca saptame caiva tv aṣṭame ca pṛthak pṛthak |
vibhāgeṣv eṣu yat karma tat pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
[4] “uktaṃ karma” manvādibhiḥ | kramakālau tu noktau | “ata eva” tasmāt kāraṇāt, “dvijānāṃ hitārthāya” “dakṣas” tad ubhayam uktavān | “kṣaṇiko” ‘tra śāstrānumatavyāpārarahitaḥ | “anyair agarhitaiḥ” aniṣiddhaiḥ dṛṣṭārthaiḥ | “svakaṃ karma” svavarṇāśramavihitam | “parityajya” cirakālaṃ tyaktvety arthaḥ | divasaśabdo ‘tra uṣaḥkālādipradoṣaparyantakālaparaḥ, ajahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā | dakṣeṇaiva “divasasya” ity abhidhāya uṣaḥkālopakramaṃ pradoṣaparyantaṃ kṛtyābhidhānāt |
manuḥ [4.93] |
utthāyāvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet svakāle cāparāṃ ciram ||
[5] “āvaśyakaṃ” mūtrapurīṣotsargaḥ | “samāhitaḥ” niyatacittaḥ | “aparāṃ” paścimāṃ saṃdhyām | “ciraṃ” nakṣatrodayād ūrdhvam api | etac ca niragniviṣayam | sāgner nakṣatrodayād arvāg eva homavidhināt saṃdhyākarmasamāpteḥ |
[6]
1
dantadhāvanam
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.98] |
śarīracintāṃ nirvartya kṛtaśaucavidhir dvijaḥ |
prātaḥsaṃdhyām upāsīta dantadhāvanapūrvikām ||
dakṣaḥ |
uṣaḥkāle tu saṃprāpte śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathārthavat |
tataḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta dantadhāvanapūrvakam ||
“uṣaḥkālaś” ca lohitadigupalakṣitakālāt prāg īṣaddikprakāśavān kālaḥ | “yathārthavat” yathavihitapādaśaucakaṃ mṛjjalādisaṃkhyāvad ity arthaḥ |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
mukhe paryuṣite nityaṃ bhavaty aprayato naraḥ |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
[7] “aprayataḥ” aśuciḥ | “bhakaṣayed” iti dantasaṃbandhād gauṇam abhidhānaṃ pūrvottarakālācamanarūpabhakṣaṇadharmaprāptyartham |
atra yamaḥ |
āmrapaunā[lā]śabilvānām apāmārgaśirīṣayoḥ |
bhakṣayet prātar utthāya vāgyato dantadhāvanam ||
“paunā[lā]śaḥ” āmrātakavṛkṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
kāle palāśakovidāraśleṣmātakatilvakaśākavṛkṣanirguṇḍīśikhaṇḍīveṇuvarjam | plakṣamāṣakabadarīkarañjaśamīśākā nityety eke | dadhitthaharītakyaś ca karṇaśālāmalakān ity apare | bilvakhādirāmrapaunā[lā]śaśirīṣāpāmārgāṇām ekatamam anārdraṃ nātiśuṣkaṃ nātisthūlam āpothitāgram anauṣṭhagranthy udaṅmukho vāgyata āsīno dantadhāvanaṃ bhakṣayet |
[8] “kāle” uṣaḥkāle | “kovidāraḥ” śvetapuṣpaḥ kāñcanāla[ra]sadṛśaḥ | “śleṣmātakaḥ” bahuvāraḥ | “tilvakaḥ” cillakaḥ | “śākavṛkṣaḥ” śākavaṇa iti loke prasiddhaḥ | “nirguṇḍī” mayūraśikhā sinduvāraḥ | “śikhaṇḍī” śikhaṇḍisaṃjñaḥ | kaṇṭakigulma ity apare | “dadhitthaḥ” kapitthaḥ | “śālaḥ” śaṅkuvṛkṣaḥ | “eke” “apare” ity ubhayatra varjayitveti śeṣaḥ | ārdram atra sadyaśchinnam, tato ‘nyad anārdram | “nātiśuṣkam” ity abhidhānāt prakṣālanasya vihitatvāc ca ārdratvamātrasyāvaśaṃbhāvāt | “āpothitāgram” īṣaccūrṇitāgram | “anauṣṭhagranthi” oṣṭhasamīpe granthir yasmin nāsti tad anauṣṭhagranthi |
viṣṇuḥ [61.1–16] |
na pālāśaṃ dantadhāvanaṃ syāt | na śleṣmāntakāriṣṭakavibhītakadhava-dhanvanajam | na ca kovidāraśamīpīlupippaleṅguda[9]guggulujam | na ca bandhūkanirguṇḍīśigrutilvakatindukajam | na pāribhadrāmlikāmocakyāśālmalī-śaṇajam | na madhuram | nāmlam | nordhvaśuṣkam | na susuṣiram | na pūtigandhi | na picchilam | na dakṣiṇāparāśābhimukhaḥ | adyād udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā | vaṭāsanārkakhadirakarañjakaravīrasarjārimedāpāmārgamālatīkakumbhabilvānām anyatamam | kāṣāyaṃ kaṭukaṃ tiktaṃ vā |
kanīnyagrasamasthaulyaṃ sakūrcaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam |
prātar bhuktvā ca yatavāg bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
“ariṣṭako” ‘tra rīṭhīvṛkṣaḥ | “dhanvanaḥ” dhāmiṇa iti prasiddhaḥ | “pīluḥ” guḍaphalaḥ, yaś ca pīlur iti paścima[10]deśe prasiddhaḥ | “iṅgudaḥ” iṅguvākaḥ kaṇṭakivṛkṣaḥ | “pāribhadraḥ” phalahaṭṭa iti prasiddhaḥ | “amlīkā” tintilī | “ūrdhvaśuṣkam” śuṣkam eva vṛkṣe | “sasuṣiram” chidrayuktam | “asanaḥ” āsana ity prasiddhaḥ | “arimedaḥ” viṭkhādiraḥ | “mālatī” jātī | “kāṣāyaṃ” tiktaṃ kaṭukaṃ vā | avihitāpratiṣiddham anyad api boddhavyam | “sakūrcaṃ” cūrṇatāgram | “prātar bhuktvā ca” ity anenoktasya dantadhāvanasya kāladvayae karaṇam ity uktam |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.7.138–140] |
nāradādyuktavārkṣeyam aṣṭāṅgulam apāṭitam |
satvacaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ syāt tad agreṇa pradhāvayet ||
utthāya netre prakṣālya śucir bhūtvā samāhitaḥ |
parijapya ca mantreṇa bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
[11]        āyur balaṃ yaśo varcaḥ prajāḥ paśu vasūni ca |
brahma prajñāṃ ca medhāṃ ca tv anno dhehi vanaspate ||
“nāradādyuktavārkṣeyam” nāradādiśikṣābhihitavṛkṣasaṃbhavam | tathā ca nāradaśikṣā |
āmrapaunāśabilvānām apāmārgaśirīṣayoḥ |
vāgyataḥ prātar utthāya bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
khadiraś ca karañjaś ca karavīrakadambayoḥ |
sarve kaṇṭakinaḥ puṇyāḥ kṣīriṇaś ca yaśasvinaḥ || iti |
“parijapya mantreṇa” iti, mantreṇābhimantrya dantadhāvanaṃ bhakṣayed ity arthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ pāduke dantadhāvanam |
varjayed bhūtikāmas tu pālāśaṃ nityam ātmavān ||
na pālāśe “pāduke: pādapīṭhe, āsnṃ, śayanaṃ, yānaṃ dantadhāvanaṃ, vā kuryāt | “yānaṃ” śakaṭādi |
[12] uśanā |
nāṅgulibhir dantān prakṣālayet |
viṣṇuḥ [61.17] |
prakṣālya bhaṅktvā taj jahyāc chucau deśe prayatnataḥ |
amāvāsyāṃ ca nāśnīyād dantakāṣṭhaṃ kathaṃcana ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.46–51] |
mukhe paryuṣite nityaṃ bhavaty aprayato naraḥ |
tasmāc chuṣkam athārdraṃ vā bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
khadiraś ca kadambaś ca karañjaś ca vaṭas tathā |
tintilī veṇupṛṣṭhaṃ ca āmranibau tathaiva ca ||
apāmārgaś ca bilvaś ca arkaś codumbaras tathā |
ete praśastāḥ kathitā dantadhāvanakarmaṇi ||
dantakāṣṭhasya vakṣyāmi samāsena praśastatām |
sarve kaṇṭakinaḥ puṇyāḥ kṣīriṇaś ca yaśasvinaḥ ||
aṣṭāṅgulena mānena tat pramāṇam ihocyate |
prādeśamātram atha vā tena dantān viśodhayet ||
[13]         prātipaddarśaṣaṣṭīṣu navamyāṃ caiva sattamāḥ |
dantānāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃyogo dahaty āsaptamaṃ kulam ||
alābhe dantakāṣṭhānāṃ pratiṣiddhe tathā dine |
apāṃ dvādaśagaṇḍūṣair mukhaśuddhir vidhīyate ||
“veṇupṛṣṭhaṃ” vaṃśasya tvagbhāgaḥ | etena “veṇuvarjam” iti hārītoktam itaravaṃśāvayavaviṣayaṃ boddhavyam | “tasmāc chuṣkam athārdraṃ vā” ity abhidhānāt śuṣkapratiṣedhaḥ “nordhvaśuṣkam” iti viṣṇuvacanād ūrdhvaśuṣkaviṣayo boddhavyaḥ |
mahābhārate |
varjayed dantakāṣṭhāni varjanīyāni nityaśaḥ |
bhakṣayec chāstradṛṣṭāni parvasv api ca varjayet ||
“parvasu” iti | parvāṇy atra —
caturdaśy aṣṭamī caiva tv amāvāsyātha pūrṇimā |
parvāṇy etāni rājendra ravisaṃkrāntir eva ca ||
iti viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.118] proktāni |
[14] tathā |
prakṣālya hastau pādau ca mukhaṃ ca susamāhitaḥ |
dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddhṛtya kṛtvā jānvantarā tataḥ ||
tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaṭukaṃ sugandhi kaṇṭakānvitam |
kṣīriṇo vṛkṣagulmād vā bhakṣayed dantadhāvanam ||
tyājyaṃ sapatram ajñātam ūrdhvaśuṣkaṃ ca pāṭitam |
tvagvihīnaṃ granthimukhaṃ tathā pālāśaśāṃśapam ||
ṛjuṃ vitastimātraṃ tu kīṭāgnibhir adūṣitam |
prāṅmukhaś copaviṣṭas tu bhakṣayed vāgyato naraḥ ||
prakṣālya ca śucau deśe dantadhāvanam utsṛjet |
patite ‘bhimukhe samyak bhojyam āpnoty abhīpsitam ||
“dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddhṛtya” iti, upavītaṃ krtvety arthaḥ | “gulmāḥ” askandhā mallikādayaḥ | “śāṃśapam” śiṃśapā[15]vṛkṣodbhavam | atra ca śucitvāpādake dantadhāvane dantakāṣṭhaviśeṣaniyamavidhinārthān niraste ‘pi kāṣṭhāntre, punar niṣedho mukhyālābhe pratinidhitvenāpi tadupādānanirāsārtham | yat tu vihitapratiṣiddhaṃ tasya niṣedhasaṃbandhena kevalavihitāpekṣayā kiṃcinnyūnatvāt, kevalavihitālābha upādānam | etasyāpy asaṃbhave ‘vihitāpratiṣiddham upādeyam | kevalaniṣiddhaṃ tu sarvathā nopādeyam | dvādaśagaṇḍūṣair eva tatra mukhaśuddhir āpādanīyeti |
hārītaḥ |
dantadhāvanaṃ bhakṣayed aviraktaṃ sodakam ekāntam utsṛjya snāto vāgyatḥ śucir ahataśuklavāsā agnihotrādidevatārthān kuryāt |
[16] “aviraktam” sarasam | “sodakam” prakṣālitam | “agnihotrādidevatārtham” agnihotrādīn, devatārthāṃś ca devapūjādīn |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajabhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite
kṛtyakalpatarau dantadhāvanaparva |
[17]
2
atha prātaḥsnānam
tatra chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.7.137] |
yathāhani tathā prātar nityaṃ snāyād anāturaḥ |
dantān prakṣālya nadyādau gehe cet tad amantravat ||
tathā |
alpatvād dhomakālasya bahutvāt snānakarmaṇaḥ |
prātar na tanuyāt snānaṃ homalopo vigarhitaḥ ||
“yathāhani” iti, yatheti kartavyatayā mṛdālambhādikayā madhyāhne snānaṃ kuryāt tathaiva prātar apīty arthaḥ | “anāturaḥ” ity abhidhānāc cāturaḥ snānanimittāprāyatyarahitaḥ, saṃmārjanādinā śaucam āpādya saṃdhyāṃ kuryāt | dantadhāvanam adhikaṃ [18] prātaḥ | “gehe ced” iti, tat prātaḥsnānaṃ yadi gṛhe kenacin nimittena karoti, tadā snānāṅgamantrarahitaṃ tanuyāt, saṃkṣepeṇa kuryād ity arthaḥ | saṃkṣepaś ca yogiyājñavalkyoktaḥ “tīrthaparikalpanajalābhimantraṇācamanamārjanāntarjalasnānādyaghamarṣaṇasūktena trir āvṛttena” ity evaṃrūpaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
atyantamalinaḥ kāyo navachidrasamanvitaḥ |
sravaty eṣa divā rātrau prātaḥsnānaṃ viśodhanam ||
klidyanti ca suṣuptasya indriyāṇi sravanti ca |
aṅgāni samatāṃ yānti uttamāny adhamāni ca ||
[19] tathā |
asnātvā nācaret karma japahomādi kiṃcana |
lālāsvedasamākīrṇaḥ śayanād utthitaḥ pumān ||
prātaḥsnānaṃ praśaṃsanti dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakaraṃ śubham |
sarvam arhati śuddhātmā prātaḥsnāyī japādikam ||
“samatāṃ yānti” iti, uttamāny apy aṅgāni cakṣurādīni kledasaṃparkād adhamāṅgatulyāni bhavanti | “asnātvā nācared” ity atra hetuḥ — “lālāsvedasamākīrṇaḥ” iti | yata iti śeṣaḥ | “dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakaram” dṛṣṭaṃ malāpanayanād aṅgaśuddhiḥ | adṛṣṭaṃ nityatvena pāpakṣayaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [64.40–42] |
snāto ‘dhikārī bhavati daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
[20]        pavitrāṇāṃ tathā japye dāne ca vidhicodite ||
alakṣmīḥ kālakarṇī ca duḥsvapnaṃ durvicintitam |
ammātreṇābhiṣiktasya naśyanta iti dhāraṇā ||
yāmyaṃ hi yātanāduḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī na paśyati |
nityasnānena pūyante ye ‘pi pāpakṛto janāḥ ||
“kālakarṇī” duḥsahasya rakṣaso duhitā | “durvicintitam” aśubhacintitam | “ammātreṇa” uddhṛtenānuddhṛtena vety arthaḥ | “dhāraṇā” niścayaḥ | “yāmyaṃ” yamalokabhavam |
dakṣaḥ |
saṃdhyāsnānam ṛgantena madhyāhne ca tataḥ punaḥ |
upāste yas tu no saṃdhyāṃ brāhmaṇo hi viśeṣataḥ |
sa jīvann eva śūdraḥ syān mṛtaḥ śvā caiva jāyate ||
[21] viṣṇuḥ [90.29] |
ya icched vipulān bhogāṃś candrasūryagrahopamān |
prātaḥsnāyī bhaven nityaṃ māsau dvau māghaphālgunau ||
yamaḥ |
prātaḥsnāyī ca satataṃ māsaudvau māghaphālgunau |
devān pitṝn samabhyarcya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
devapitṛsamabhyarcanaṃ tarpaṇādinā
viṣṇuḥ [89.1–4] |
māsaḥ kārtiko ‘gnidaivatyaḥ | agniś ca sarvadevānāṃ mukham | tasmāt kārtike bahiḥsnāyī gāyatrījapanirataḥ sakṛd eva haviṣyāśī vatsarakṛtāt pāpāt pūto bhavati |
kārtikaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ nityasnāyī jitendriyaḥ |
japan haviṣyabhuk śāntaḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
“japan” āvaśayakakṛtyāntaravarjite kāle | “haviṣyaṃ” — “haviṣyeṣu yavā mukhyā” ityādivacanabodhitam | [22] viṣṇusmṛtau snānaprakaraṇāt prakaraṇāntarādhikaraṇanyāyena kāmyasnānāntaram idam | prātaḥśabdaś cātrodayapūrvakālavacanaḥ, “prātaḥsnāyy aruṇakaragrastāṃ prācīm avalokya snāyāt” iti viṣṇuvacanena [64.9] sarvatra prātaḥsnāne tasyaiva kālasya prāpitatvāt | yat tu “prātaḥkālo muhūrtāṃs trīn” iti matsyapurāṇādyuktam, tac chrāddhaviṣayam | evaṃ kārtike bahiḥsnānam api kāmyam | etena strīśūdrāṇām apy adhikāro ‘stīti | evaṃ kārtike bahiḥsnānaṃ prātaḥkāletarakāle ‘pi tenaiva nyāyena kāmyaṃ snānāntaram |
āpastambaḥ [1.30.6–9] |
atha snātakavratāni | pūrveṇa grāmān niṣkramaṇapraveśanāni śīlayed uttareṇa vā | saṃdhyayor bahirgrāmād āsanaṃ vāgyataś ca | vipratiṣedhe śrutilakṣaṇaṃ balīyaḥ |
“śīlayet” yathā tacchīlatā niṣpadyate tathā [23] kuryāt | “vipratiṣedhe” śrautena homādinā saha virodhe | śrutilakṣaṇaṃ śrautam evānuṣṭheyatvena balīyaḥ, na smārtam |
dakṣaḥ |
saṃdhyākarmāvasāne tu svayaṃhomo vidhīyate |
svayaṃhome phalaṃ yat tu tad anyena na jāyate ||
ṛtvik putro gurur bhrātā bhāgineyo ‘tha viṭpatiḥ |
etair eva hutaṃ yat tu tad dhutaṃ svayam eva tu ||
“guruḥ” pitrādiḥ | “viṭpatiḥ” jāmātā |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.99] |
hutvāgnīn sūryadaivatyān japen mantrān samāhitaḥ |
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.58–59] |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ sanakṣatrām upakramya yathāvidhi |
gāyatrīm abhyaset tāvad yāvad ādityadarśanam ||
[24]        tataś cāvasathaṃ prāpya homaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
devakāryaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gurumaṅgalavīkṣaṇam ||
devakāryasya sarvasya pūrvāhṇas tu vidhīyate |
devakāryāṇi pūrvāhṇe manuṣyāṇāṃ tu madhyame ||
pitṝṇām aparāhṇe tu kāryāṇy etāni yatnataḥ |
divasasyādyabhāge tu sarvam etat samācaret ||
“maṅgalam” brāhmaṇādi vakṣyamāṇaṃ nāradoktam | yad etad devakāryādyuktaṃ tat sarvaṃ divasasyādyabhāgeṣu pūrvāhṇasya prathamabhāge samācared ity arthaḥ |
varāhapurāṇe [211.14–15] |
udayān niḥsṛtaṃ sūryaṃ yas tu bhaktyā naro dvija |
[25]        dadhyakṣatāñjalibhis tu tisṛbhiḥ pūjayec chuciḥ ||
tasya bhāvaprapannasya cāśubhaṃ yat samarjitam |
tatkṣaṇād eva nirdagdhaṃ bhasmībhavati kāṣṭhavat ||
“bhāvaprapannasya” bhaktyā śaraṇaṃ gatasya |
manuḥ [4.152] |
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanam añjanam |
pūrvāhṇa eva kurvīta devatānāṃ ca pūjanam ||
“maitram” purīṣotsargam, mitradevatākatvāt pāyos tatsaṃbandhāt so’pi tathoktaḥ | “prasādhanam” keśaracanānulepanādi |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.22] |
svācāntaś ca tataḥ kuryāt pumān keśaprasādhanam |
ādarśāñjanamāṅgalyadūrvādyālambhanāni ca ||
“añjanam” sauvīrāñjanādi | ādiśabdena dadhyādīnām upādānam |
[26] brahmapurāṇe |
svam ātmānaṃ ghṛte paśyed yadīcchec cirajīvitam |
atha dakṣaḥ |
dvitīye ca tathā bhāge devābhyāso vidhīyate |
vedasvīkaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ vicāro ‘byasanaṃ japaḥ ||
taddānaṃ caiva śiṣyebhyo vedābhyāso hi pañcadhā |
samitpuṣpakuśādīnāṃ sa kālaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ||
upādanasyeti śeṣaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.13.19–22; 1.14.1] |
niveśe nirvṛtte saṃvatsare saṃvatsare dvau dvau māsau samāhita ācāryakule vased bhūyaḥ śrutam icchann iti śvetaketuḥ | etena hy ahaṃ yogena bhūyaḥ pūrvasmāt kālāc chrutam akurvīti | tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham | niveśe nirvṛtte naiyamikāni śrūyante | agnihotram atithayo yac cānyad evaṃyuktam |
[27] “niveśo” vivāhaḥ | “samāhito” ‘ananyamanāḥ | “etena” anantaroktena “dvau dvau māsau” ityādinā | “yogena” māsadvayaparyantopādhyāyasaṃbandhena | “pūrvasmāt kālāt” vivāhaprāgbhāvinaḥ kālāt | “bhūyo” bahukālam | “śrutam” mīmāṃsābhyasanam | “akurvi” kṛtavān asmi | “vipratiṣiddhaṃ śāstraiḥ” agnihotrādīnāṃ yāvajjīvam āvaśyakatvapratipādakair viruddham |
āpastambaḥ [2.5.14] |
yayā vidyayā na viroceta punar ācāryam upetya sādhayet |
“na viroceta” nojjvalakajñāno bhavet |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.99] |
vedārthān adhigacchec ca śāstrāṇi vividhāni ca |
manuḥ [4.19–20] |
buddhivṛddhikarāṇy āśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca |
[28]        nityaṃ śāstrāṇy avekṣeta nigamāṃś caiva vaidikān ||
yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samadhigacchati |
tathā tathā vijānāti vijñānaṃ cāsya rocate ||
“buddhivṛddhikarāṇi” tarkamīmāṃsādīni | “dhanyāni” dhanāya hitāny arthaśāstrāṇi| “hitāni” āyurvedādīni | “niyamāḥ” padārthanirṇayahetavo nighaṇṭuprabhṛtayaḥ | “rocate” ujjvalaṃ bhavati |
yamaḥ |
śāstrārthamārgam āśritya ye gatā ye ca saṃsthitāḥ |
ye budhyanti mahātmānas te yānti paramāṃ gatim ||
dānena tapasā yajñair upavāsavratais tathā |
na tāṃ gatim avāpnoti vidyayā yām avāpnuyāt ||
“gatāḥ” śāstrārthamārgeṇa pravṛttāḥ | “saṃsthitāḥ” saṃprāptāḥ, lokāntaraṃ gatā iti yāvat | “budhyanti” anutiṣṭhanta āsate |
[29] dakṣaḥ |
tṛtīya ca tathā bhāge poṣyavargārthasādhanam |
poṣyavargaś ca –
mātā pitā gurur bhāryā prajā dīnaḥ samāśritaḥ |
abhyāgato ‘tithiś cāgniḥ poṣyavarga udāhṛtaḥ ||
gautamaḥ [9.63–64] |
yogakṣemārtham īśvaram adhigacchet | nānyam anyatra devagurudhārmikebhyaḥ |
alabdhalābho “yogaḥ” | labdharakṣaṇaṃ “kṣemaḥ” | “anyam” anīśvaram |
manuḥ [4.153–154] |
daivatāny abhigacchet tu dhārmikāṃś ca dvijottamān |
īśvaraṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ gurūn eva ca parvasu ||
[30]        abhivādayet tato vṛddhān dadyāc caivāsanaṃ svakam |
kṛtāñjalir upāsīta gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato ‘nviyāt ||
“abhigacchet” ābhimukhyena pūjādyarthaṃ gacchat | “gurūn eva” ity evakāro bhinnakramo “gacched” ityanantaraṃ boddhavyaḥ |
chāgaleyo yamaś ca dvitīya |
yatīnāṃ darśanaṃ caiva sparśanaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ tathā |
kurvāṇaḥ pūyate nityaṃ tasmāt paśyet tu nityaśaḥ ||
agnicit kapilā satrī rājā bhukṣur mahodadhiḥ |
dṛṣṭamātrāḥ punanty ete tasmāt paśyet tu nityaśaḥ ||
“paśyed” ity anena sparśanabhāṣaṇe apy upādīyete ‘jahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā | “agnicit” kṛtāgnicayanaḥ | “kapilā” kapilavarṇā gauḥ | “satrī” kṛtasatrayāgaḥ | “bhikṣuḥ” yatiḥ |
[31] varāhapurāṇe [290.64–65] |
vāmanaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā varāhaṃ ca jalotthitam |
namasyec caiva yo bhaktyā sa pāpebhyaḥ pramucyate ||
yajvā miṣṭānnadaḥ satrī śatāyur dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ |
jñānaniṣṭhāāṃs tapaḥsiddhān dṛṣṭvā pāpāt pramucyate ||
“śucis” tīrthādipūtaḥ | “jñānaniṣṭo” ‘dhyātmaratiḥ |
nāradaḥ [17.54–55] |
loke ‘smin maṅgalāny aṣṭau brāhmaṇo gaur hutāśanaḥ |
hiraṇyaṃ sarpir āditya āpo rājā tathāṣṭamaḥ ||
etāni satataṃ paśyen namasyed arcayec ca yaḥ |
pradakṣiṇaṃ ca kurvīta tatāsyāyur na hīyate ||
etāni ca brāhmaṇādīnām aṣṭānāṃ maṅgalānāṃ darśananamaskārārcanapradakṣiṇīkaraṇāni samuditāny āyuḥkāmasya tṛtīyabhāga eva kartavyatvena vihitāni | divasasyādyabhāge tu maṅgalādyālambhanaṃ kevalam |
[32] vāmapurāṇe |
kṛtvā śiraḥsnānam athāhnikaṃ ca
saṃpūjya toyena pitṝn sadevān |
homaṃ ca kṛtvālabhanaṃ śubhānāṃ
tato bahir nirgamanaṃ praśastam ||
dūrvāṃ dadhi sarpir athodakumbhaṃ
dhenuṃ savatsāṃ vṛṣabhaṃ suvarṇam |
mṛd gomayaṃ svastikam akṣatāni
lājā madhu brāhmaṇakanyakāś ca ||
śvetāni puṣpāṇi tathā śamīṃ ca
hutāsanaṃ candanam arkabimbam |
aśvatthavṛkṣaṃ ca samālabheta
tataś ca kuryān nijajātidharmam ||
deśānuśiṣṭaṃ kuladharmam agryaṃ
sagotradharmaṃ na hi saṃtyajeta |
“saṃpūjya toyena pitṛn” iti, anena prātaḥsnāne [33] pitṛtarpaṇaṃ kartavyam ity uktam | atra ca śiraḥsnānādi sarvaṃ yathākālaprāptam anūdya “tatas tu kuryān nijajātidharmam” ityādi tṛtīyabhāge vidhīyate | nijajātidharmaś ca svasvajātivihito ‘rthārjanādirūpaḥ | tatrāpi yasmin deśe yasyāṃ jātau yasmin kule yo ‘rjanopāyā vihito na ninditaḥ, sa evānuṣṭheya ity arthaḥ |
atha dakṣaḥ |
caturthe tu tathā bhāge snānārthaṃ mṛdam āharet |
tilapuṣpakuśādīni snānaṃ cākṛtrime jale ||
ādiśabdena gomayadūrvādyupādānam | “snānaṃ” kuryād iti śeṣaḥ | “akṛtrime” nadyādau |
śātātapaḥ |
valmīkākhūtkarāl lepāj jalāc ca pativṛkṣayoḥ |
kṛtaśaucāvaśiṣṭāc ca na grāhyāḥ saptamṛttikāḥ ||
“ākhūtkaro” mūṣikotkaraḥ | “lepāt” kuḍyādīnām iti [34] śeṣaḥ | “jalāt” antarjalāt | “pathivṛkṣayoḥ” saṃbandhinīti seṣaḥ |
śucau deśe tu saṃgrāhyā śarkarāśmādivarjitā |
raktā gaurī tathā śvetā mṛttikā trividhā smṛtā ||
“śucau deśe” sthiteti śeṣaḥ | ādiśabdena keśakīṭādyupādānam | raktatvādiniyamāt kṛṣṇavarṇā mṛttikā snānānaṅgam ity uktam |
na mṛdaṃ nodakaṃ vāpi na niśāyāṃ tu gomayam |
na gomūtraṃ pradoṣe tu gṛhṇīyād buddhimān naraḥ ||
“niśāyām” iti mṛdudakagomayaiḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | etac ca mṛdādyadṛṣṭakarmārthatvena niśāyāṃ na grāhyam |
manuḥ [4.129] |
na snānam ācared bhuktvā nāturo na mahāniśi |
[35]        na vāsobhir na cājasraṃ nāvijñāne jalāśaye ||
“na snānam ācared bhuktvā” iti, kṛtabhojanasya yadṛcchāsnānaniṣedho nityasya snānasya bhojanottaraprāptyabhāvāt | cāṇḍālādisparśanimittasya tu “nāśuciḥ kṣaṇam ap tiṣṭhed” iti virodhena pratiṣeddhum aśakyatvād iti medhātithiḥ | “āturasya” vyādhitasya tu sarvathā vāruṇasnānaniṣedhaḥ | “mahāniśā” rātrer madhyamaṃ praharadvayam | “ajasraṃ” anavarataṃ śraddhājāḍyādinā | “avijñāte” gādhāgādhatayāninditakartṛkatvādinā vā |
jābāliḥ |
na pārakye sadā snāyān na bhuktvā na mahāniśi |
[36]        nārdram ekaṃ ca vasanaṃ paridadhyāt kadācana ||
“pārakye” parakīye kūpādau sadā na snāyāt | akṛtrimāsaṃbhave tu tatrāpi snāyāt, “parakīye ‘pi kadācana” iti vacanāt | sarvadā puruṣasyaikam ārdraṃ ca vāso niṣiddham | evaṃ ca puruṣasya sarvadā vastradvayadhāraṇaniyamāt snānakāle ‘pi tatparityāgo pramāṇaṃ nāstīti |
tathā |
trayodaśyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ daśamyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
śūdraviṭkṣatriyāḥ snānaṃ nācareyuḥ kathaṃcana ||
“ambho’vagāhanaṃ snānaṃ vihitaṃ sārvavarṇikam” iti yat sāmānyena prasaktaṃ snānaṃ tad anena trayodaśyādiṣu śūdrādīnāṃ yathākramaṃ niṣidhyate |
[37] baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.24–25] |
na nagnaḥ snāyāt | na naktaṃ snāyāt |
āpastambaḥ [1.32.7–8] |
saśiro ‘vamajjanam apsu varjayet | astamite ca snānam |
udakaṃ praviśya saśiraskaṃ rāgato nimajjanaṃ na kuryāt | “astamite ca snānam” varjayed ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | etad api rāgaprāptasnānamātraviṣayam |
parāśaraḥ [12.22] |
divākarakaraiḥ pūtaṃ divā snānaṃ praśasyate |
apraśastaṃ niśi snānaṃ rahor anyatra darśanāt ||
yathā snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca sūryasya grahaṇe divā |
somasyāpi tathā rātrau snānaṃ dānaṃ vidhīyate ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
ubhe saṃdhye tu snātavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiś ca gṛhāśritaiḥ |
[38]        tisṛṣv api ca saṃdhyāsu snātavyaṃ tu tapasvibhiḥ ||
“tapasvī” vānaprasthādiḥ |
śaṅkhalikitau |
anaśnann anagnaḥ snāyāt | nāpsu meheta | nodgharṣaṇaṃ kuryāt | na pādena pāṇinā vā jalam abhihanyāt | yasmād āpo vai sarvā devatāḥ | na sravantīṃ vṛthātikrāmed anavasicya | amedhyodakaṃ pariharet | nālpodake snāyāt | na samudrodakam avagāhet |
“anaśnan” tāmbūlādibhakṣaṇam akurvāṇaḥ | “mehanaṃ” mūtrotsargaḥ | “udgharṣaṇaṃ” akgamalāpakarṣaṇam | “apsu na kuryād” ity anvayaḥ | “atikrāmet” laṅghayet | “vṛtha” niṣprayojanam | “anavasicya” tarpaṇam akṛtvā | “amedhyaṃ” aśuci | “pariharet” na kiṃcit tatra kuryāt | “alpodake na snāyād” iti, prabhūtasumanoharodakasaṃbhave | “prabhūte vidyamāne” [39] ityādi vacanāt | “nāvagāhet” antaḥ praviśya na snāyāt |
devalaḥ |
na nadīṣu nadīṃ brūyāt parvateṣu ca parvatam |
nānyat praśaṃset tatrasthas tīrtheṣv āyataneṣu ca ||
hārītaḥ |
na catvaropadvārayoḥ snāyāt |
“catvaraṃ” yakṣādibalidānasthānam | “upadvāraṃ” dvārasamīpam |
punar hārītaḥ |
na snānavarṇakayor agraṃ prayacchet | anyatra devagurubrāhmaṇebhyaḥ |
snāty aneneti “snānaṃ” kuśādi | “varṇakaṃ” sugandhidravyam, udvartanādivarṇakaratvāt |
[40] viṣṇuḥ [64.1–9] |
paranipāneṣu na snānam ācaret | ācared vā pañcapiṇḍān uddhṛtyāpadi | nājīrṇī | na cāturaḥ | na nagnaḥ | na rāhudarśanavarjaṃ rātrau | na saṃdhyāyām | prātaḥsnāyy aruṇakaragrastāṃ prācīm avalokya snāyat |
“āpadi” akṛtrime jale snānāsaṃbhave | “saṃdhyāyāṃ” sāyaṃsaṃdhyāyām |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
atha snānavidhiḥ | parakṛtān setūn kūpāṃś ca varjayet | aṃśabhāk tatra setukṛt trīn piṇḍān uddhṛtya snāyāt |
manuḥ [4.209] |
parakīyanipāneṣu snāyān naiva kadācana |
[41]        nipānakartuḥ snātā hi duṣkṛtāṃśena lipayate ||
“parakīyaṃ” parakṛtamātram | tat tu pratiṣṭhitam apratiṣṭhitaṃ vā, aviśeṣeṇa “parakṛtān” iti paiṭhīnasi-baudhāyanavacanānusārāt | svakārite tu na virodhaḥ | “nipānaṃ” jalādhāraḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.5.1–5] |
tapasyam apām avagāhanam | devatās tarpayitvā pitṛtarpaṇam | anutīrtham apa utsiñced “ūrjaṃ vahantīr” iti | athāpy udāharanti |
sravantīṣv aniruddhāsu trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
prātar utthāya kurvīran devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam ||
niruddhāsu na kurvīrann aṃśabhāk tatra setukṛt |
tasmāt parakṛtān setūn kūpāṃś ca parivarjayet ||
uddhṛtya vāpi trīn piṇḍān kuryād āpatsu no sadā |
[42]                niruddhāsu tu mṛtpiṇḍān kūpāt trīn trīn ghaṭāṃs tathā ||
tapase hitaṃ “tapasyam” avagāhanam | tapaś cātra nityanaimittikādikarmāṇuṣṭhānam abhimatam | snātasya tatrādhikārāt | “anutīrthaṃ” tīrthaṃ lakṣmīkṛtya, devapitrāditīrthenety arthaḥ | “ūrjaṃ vahantīr” iti tarpaṇāntimapadārthopādānam etāvān ekaḥ prayoga iti jñāpanārtham | “niruddhāsu” niruddhapravāhāsu |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
neṣṭakācite pitṝṃs tarpayet | vāpītaḍagodapāneṣu sapta pañca trīn vā piṇḍān uddhṛtya devapitṝṃs tarpayet |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.159] |
pañca piṇḍān anuddhṛtya na snāyāt paravāriṣu |
snāyān nadīdevakhātahradeṣu ca saraḥsu ca ||
paiṭhīnasi-baudhāyanavacanāt snānakartur mṛtpiṇḍāmbughaṭa[43]trayoddharaṇaṃ setukūpaviṣayam | pañcapiṇḍoddharaṇaṃ tu viṣṇūktam etadatiriktakṛtrimajalaviṣayam | yat tu śaṅkhalikhitoktaṃ saptapañcatrayamṛtpiṇḍoddharaṇaṃ tat tarpaṇārthaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vāpīkūpataḍāgaviṣayam |
manuḥ [4.203] |
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca |
snānaṃ samācaren nityaṃ gartaprasravaṇeṣu ca ||
“devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu” devasaṃbandhitvena prasiddheṣu saraḥsu | “gartaḥ” –
“dhanuḥsahasrāṇy aṣṭau tu gatir yāsāṃ na divyate: |
iti vakṣyamāṇaḥ | “prasravaṇaṃ” nirjharaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.26] |
kūpeṣūddhṛtatoyena snānaṃ kurvīta vā bhuvi |
snāyītoddhṛtatoyena yadi vā bhuvy asaṃbhave ||
[44] “kūpeṣu” tat toyaṃ tenoddhṛtena bhuvi sthitaḥ snāyāt | “asaṃbhave” anyatrāpi taḍāgādāv avagāhya snānasaṃbhava ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [64.16–17] |
snāyāt prasravaṇadevakhātasarovareṣu | uddhṛtād bhūmiṣṭham udakaṃ puṇyaṃ sthāvarāt prasravaṇaṃ tasmān nādeyaṃ tasmād api sādhuparigṛhītaṃ sarvata eva gārhyam |
‘sādhuparigṛhītaṃ” yathā rāmaparigṛhītaṃ citrakūṭādau mandākinyādi |
śaṅkhaḥ |
sarvatīrthāni puṇyāni pāpaghnāni sadā nṛṇām |
parasparānapekṣāṇi kathitāni manīṣibhiḥ ||
sarve prasravaṇāḥ puṇyāḥ sarve puṇyāḥ śiloccayāḥ |
nadyaḥ puṇyāḥ sadā sarvā jāhnavī ca viśeṣataḥ ||
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaś caiva susaṃyataḥ |
[45]        vidyā tapaś ca kīrtiś ca sa tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
nṛṇāṃ pāpakṛtāṃ tīrthe pāpasya śamanaṃ bhavet |
yathoktaphaladaṃ tīrthaṃ bhavec chraddhātmanāṃ nṛṇām ||
“hastasaṃyamaḥ” ninditapratigrahādinivṛttiḥ | “pādasaṃyamaḥ” agamyadeśagamananivṛttiḥ | “manaḥsaṃyamaḥ” kāmakrodhādinivṛttiḥ | “vidyā” sacchāstravedarūpā | “tapaḥ” cāndrāyaṇādiḥ | “kīrtiḥ” dhārmikatvādinā prasiddhiḥ | na narasya pāpakṛtaḥ sarvathā tīrthaṃ niṣphalam ity uktam — “nṛṇām” iti |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
prabhūte vidyamāne tu udake sumanohare |
nālpodake dvijaḥ snāyān nadīṃ cotsṛjya kṛtrime ||
trirātraphaladā nadyo yāḥ kāścid asamudragāḥ |
samudragās tu pakṣasya māsasya saritāṃ patiḥ ||
vṛthā tūṣṇodakasnānaṃ vṛthā japyam avaidikam |
[46]        vṛthā tv aśrotriye dānaṃ vṛthā bhuktam asākṣikam ||
“trirātraphaladāḥ” nadīvyatirikte jale yat trirātrasnāne phalaṃ tatphaladāḥ | evaṃ “pakṣasya māsasya” ity api boddhavyam | “vṛthā” śarīraśuddhivyatiriktasnānaphalaśūnyam |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
śivaliṅgasamīpasthaṃ yat toyaṃ purataḥ sthitam |
śivaliṅgeti taj jñeyaṃ tatra snātvā divaṃ vrajet ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.7.140–151] |
yavyadvayaṃ śrāvaṇādi sarvā nadyo rajasvalāḥ |
tāsu snānaṃ na kurvīta varjayitvā samudragāḥ ||
nabhonabhasyayor madhye sarvā nadyo rajasvalāḥ |
tāsu snānaṃ na kurvīta devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam ||
dhanuḥsahasrāṇy aṣṭau tu gatir yāsāṃ na vidyate |
na tā nadīśabdavahā gartās te parikīrtitāḥ ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge pretasnāne tathaiva ca |
[47]        candrasūryagrahe caiva rajodoṣo na vidyate ||
vedāś chandāṃśi sarvāṇi brahmādyāś ca divaukasaḥ |
jalārthino hi pitaro marīcyādyās tatharṣayaḥ ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge snānārthaṃ brahmavādinaḥ |
yiyāsūn anugacchanti saṃhṛṣṭā hy aśarīriṇaḥ ||
samāgamaś ca yatraiṣāṃ tatrānye bhavo malāḥ |
nūnaṃ sarve kṣayaṃ yānti kum utaikaṃ nadīrajaḥ ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ sicyamānānām antarālaṃ samāśritaḥ |
saṃpibed yaḥ śarīreṇa parṣanmuktajalacchaṭāḥ ||
vidyādīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmān putrādīn nāry api dhruvān |
āmuṣmikāny api sukhāny āpnuyān nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
aśucy aśucinā dattam āmamṛcchakalādinā |
anirgatadaśāhās tu pretā rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
svardhuny ambhaḥsamāni syuḥ sarvāṇy ambhāṃsi bhūtale |
kūpasthāny api somārkagrahaṇe nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[48] “yavyo” māsaḥ, “yavyā māsāḥ sumekaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ” iti śataphathaśruteḥ | “rajasvalāḥ” aviśuddhāḥ | “samudragāḥ” sākṣātpratyabhijñāyamānasamudragamanāḥ | “dhanuḥ” hastacatuṣṭayaparimāṇam | ṛṣīṇāṃ tarpaṇe kriyamāṇe ‘ntarālasāṃbandhī yo jalabindusamuhas taṃ “pibet” śarīreṇa saṃpratīcched ity arthaḥ | “aśuci” dravyam | “aśucinā” puruṣeṇa | “āmamṛcchakalādinā” apakvamṛtkapālādinā | “anirgatadaśāhāḥ” janmaprabhṛti daśāhābhyantare ye mṛtās te pretāḥ | “rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate” tad dattaṃ tadgāmi bhavati | “samāgamaś ca yatraiṣām” ityādinā yo rajaḥprabhṛti sarvaprakārodakadoṣābhāva uktas tadanuvādo ‘yaṃ “aśucy aśucinā” ityādi | “svardhunī” gaṅgā |
[49] brahmapurāṇe |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ snānam ācaret |
tarpaṇaṃ tu bhavet tasya cāṅgatvena prakīrtitam ||
dakṣaḥ [2.48–49] |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ snānam ucyate |
teṣāṃ madhye tu yan nityaṃ tat punar bhidyate tridhā ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ pārśve mantravat tu jale smṛtam |
saṃdhyāsnānam ubhābhyāṃ tu snānabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
“pārśve” na jalamadhya iti tātparyam | “saṃdhyāsnānaṃ” prātaḥsnānam | “ubhābhyām” iti, jale sthale ca |
śaṅkhaḥ [8.1–11] |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ kriyāṅgaṃ malakarṣaṇam |
kriyāsnānaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣoḍhā snānaṃ prakīrtitam ||
asnātas tu pumān nārho japyāgnihavanādiṣu |
prātaḥsnānaṃ tadarthaṃ tu nityasnānaṃ prakīrtitam |
[50]        cāṇḍālaśavapūyādi spṛṣṭvāsnātāṃ rajasvalāḥ |
snānārhas tu yataḥ snāti snānaṃ naimittikaṃ hi tat ||
puṣyasnānādikaṃ yat tu daivajñavidhicoditam |
tad dhi kāmyaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ nākāmas tat prayojayet ||
japtukāmaḥ pavitrāṇi cārciṣyan devatāḥ pitṝn |
snānaṃ samācared yas tu kriyāṅgaṃ tat prakīrtitam ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ nāma snānam abhyaṅgapūrvakam |
malāpakarṣaṇārthaṃ tu pravṛttis tasya nānyathā ||
saraḥsu devakhāteṣu tīrtheṣu ca nadīṣu ca |
kriyāsnānaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ snānaṃ tatra matā kriyā ||
tatra kāmyaṃ ca kartavyaṃ yathāvad vidhicoditam |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ caiva kriyāṅgaṃ malakarṣaṇam ||
tīrthābhāve tu kartavyam uṣṇodakaparodakaiḥ |
snātasya vahnitaptena tathaiva paravāriṇā ||
śarīraśuddhir vijñeyā na tu snānaphalaṃ labet |
adbhir gātrāṇi śudhyanti tīrthasnānād bhavet phalam ||
[51]        saraḥsu devakhāteṣu tīrtheṣu ca nadīṣu ca |
snānam eva kriyā yasmāt snānāt puṇyaphalaṃ matam ||
“abhyaṅgapūrvakam” iti malāpakarṣaṇasnānopalakṣaṇam | tīrthaśabdo ‘tra tīrthabhūtavāpīkūpataḍāgādiparaḥ | “matā” abhimatā | “yathāvat” puṣyasnānādyuktetikartavyatā | “nityaṃ naimittikam” ity etenārthād idam uktam – kāmyasnānakriyāsnāne uṣṇodakaparodakābhyāṃ na kartavye iti | “paravāri” parakṛtrimajalam, akṛtrimaṃ ca pareṇopārjitam | “snānaphalaṃ” svargādi |
hārītaḥ |
pañca snānāni viprāṇāṃ kīrtitāni manīṣibhiḥ |
āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ brāhmam vāyavyaṃ divyam eva ca ||
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānam adbhir vāruṇam ucyate |
āpo hi ṣṭheti ca brāhmaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam ||
abhir ātapavarṣābhir divyaṃ snānam ihocyate |
etais tu mantrataḥ snātvā tīrthānāṃ phalam āpnuyāt ||
[52] “āgneyam” agniśabdo ‘trāgnikārye bhasmani vartate lakṣaṇayā | “sarvatrāgnikalibhyāṃ ḍhagvaktavya” iti ḍhak | “āpo hi ṣṭhā” iti mantracatuṣṭayopalakṣaṇam | “etaiḥ” āgneyādibhiḥ | “mantrato” mṛdālmbhādisādhanamantroccāraṇena |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
kāladoṣād asāmarthān na śaknoti yadā hy asau |
tadā jñātvā ṛṣibhyas tu mantrair dṛṣṭaṃ tu mārjanam ||
śan na āpas tu drupadā āpo hi ṣṭāghamarṣaṇam |
etaiś caturbhir ṛṅmantrair mantrasnānam udāhṛtam ||
snānam abdaivatair mantrair mārjanaṃ prāṇasaṃyamaḥ |
aghamarṣaṇasūktena aśvamedhāvabhṛtsamam ||
aprāyatye samutpanne snānam eva tu kārayet |
pūrvoddiṣṭais tathā mantrair anyathā mārjanaṃ bhavet ||
“kāladoṣaḥ” ativṛṣṭyādiḥ “asāmarthyaṃ” śarīrāpāṭavahetuḥ | kālasyālpatvena saṃpūrṇavāruṇasnānavidhikaraṇāyogyatvaṃ [53] vā | “snānam abdaivatair mantair” iti, abdaivatair mārjanam, “aghamarṣaṇsūktena prāṇasaṃyama” ity anvayaḥ | īdṛśamantrasnānaṃ pūrvoktamantrasnānād utkṛṣṭam | “aprāyatyam” aśuddhiḥ | “snānam” atrāplavanam | “anyathā” aśuddhyabhāve |
punar yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
asāmarthāc charīrasya kālaśaktyādyapekṣayā |
mantrasnānāditaḥ sapta kecid icchanti sūrayaḥ ||
māntraṃ bhaumaṃ tathāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyam eva ca |
vāruṇaṃ mānasaṃ caiva saptasnānaṃ prakīrtitam ||
apo hi ṣṭheti vai māntraṃ mṛdālambhas tu pārthivam |
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam ||
yat tu sātapavarṣeṇa snānaṃ tad divyam ucyate |
vāruṇaṃ cāvagāhāt tu mānasaṃ viṣṇucintanam ||
śastaṃ snānaṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ mantrasnānakrameṇa tu |
[54]        kālād deśād asāmarthāt sarvaṃ tulyaphalaṃ smṛtam ||
mānasaṃ pravaraṃ snānaṃ kecid icchanti sūrayaḥ |
ātmatīrthapraśaṃsāyāṃ vyāsena paṭhitaṃ yataḥ ||
mantrasnānakrameṇa yathoddiṣṭaṃ sarvam eva snānaṃ praśastam | atraiva hetuḥ – “kālād deśād” iti | yata iti śeṣaḥ | vāruṇaṃ mukhyaṃ snānam | kāladoṣādau tv anyad api māntrādi tattulyaphalam |
skandapurāṇe |
puṇyaṃ kanakhale yac ca prayāge yac ca sundari |
tat phalaṃ sakalaṃ devi bhūtisnāne dine dine ||
tathā |
ucchiṣṭaṃ vā pramattaṃ vā naravāhananandite |
bhūtispṛṣṭaṃ hi na naraṃ dharṣayanti vināyakāḥ ||
“pramattaḥ” anavahitaḥ | “dharṣayanti” abhibhavanti |
[55] viṣṇupurāṇe [2.9.12–13, 15–16] |
ākāśagaṅgāsalilaṃ samādāya gabhāstimān |
anabhragatam evorvyāṃ sadyaḥ kṣipati raśmibhiḥ ||
tasya saṃsparśanirdhūtapāpapaṅko dvijottamaḥ |
na yāti narakaṃ martyo divyaṃ snānaṃ hi tat smṛtam ||
kṛttikādiṣu ṛkṣeṣu viṣameṣv ambu yad divaḥ |
dṛṣṭārkaṃ patati jñeyaṃ tad gāṅgaṃ diggajojjhitam ||
yugmarkṣeṣu ca yat toyaṃ pataty arkojjhitaṃ divaḥ |
tat sūryaraśmibhiḥ sadyaḥ samādāya nirasyate ||
“viṣameṣu” prathamatṛtīyapañcamasaptamanavamādiṣu kṛttikāXXgaśiraḥpunarvasuprabhṛtiṣu | “yugmarkṣeṣu” sameṣu rohiṇyārdrāprabhṛtiṣu |
tathā [2.9.17] |
ubhayaṃ puṇyam atyarthaṃ nṛṇāṃ pāpaharaṃ dvija |
ākāśagaṅgāsalilaṃ divyaṃ snānaṃ mahāmune ||
[56] vārāhapurāṇe |
dakṣiṇāvartaśaṅkhena tilamiśrodakena tu |
udake nābhimātre tu yaḥ kuryād abhiṣecanam ||
prāksrotasi tu vai nadyāṃ naras v ekāgramānasaḥ |
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati ||
“prāksrotasi” pūrvadikpravāhāyām |
acchinnapadmapatreṇa sarvaratnodakena ca |
srotaso vai naraḥ snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
dakṣiṇāvartaśaṅkhena pātra audumbare sthitam |
udakaṃ yaḥ pratīccheta śirasā hṛṣṭamānasaḥ ||
tasya janmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati |
[57] “audumbare” tāmre | “śirasā” iti saptamyarthe tṛtīyā |
jābāliḥ |
aśiraskaṃ bhavet snānaṃ snānāśaktau tu karmiṇām |
ārdreṇa vāsasā vāpi mārjanaṃ daihikaṃ viduḥ ||
śira ity anuvṛttau manuḥ [4.82] |
na ca snāyād vinā tataḥ ||
anena snānaśaktau satyāṃ vihitaṃ snānaṃ saśiraskam eva kartavyam ity uktam |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.35] |
śiraḥsnātas tu kurvīta daivaṃ pitryam athāpi vā |
atha snānavidhiḥ |
tatra śaṅkhaḥ [9.1–15] |
kriyāsnānaṃ tu vakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ |
mṛdbhir adbhiś ca kartavyaṃ śaucam ādau yathāvidhi |
[58]        jale ‘bhimagnas tūnmajya upaspṛśya yathāvidhi ||
tīrthasyāvāhanaṃ kuryāt tat pravakṣyāmy ataḥ param |
“prapadye varuṇaṃ devam ambhasāṃ patim ūrjitam ||
yācitaṃ dehi me tīrtha sarvapāpāpanuttaye |
tīrtham āvāhayiṣyāmi sarvāghaviniṣūdanam ||
sāṃnidhyam asmin sve toye bhajatāṃ madanugrahāt |
rudrān prapadye varadān sarvān apsuṣadḥ svayam ||
sarvān apsuṣadaś caiva prapadye praṇatah sthitaḥ |
devam apsuṣadaṃ vahniṃ prapadye ‘ghaniṣūdanam ||
āpaḥ puṇyāḥ pavitrāś ca prapadye śaraṇaṃ tathā |
rudrāś cāgniś ca sarpāś ca varuṇas tv āpa eva ca ||
śamayantv āśu me pāpaṃ punantu ca sadā mama” |
ity evam uktvā kartavyaṃ tatra saṃmārjanaṃ jale ||
āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ |
hiraṇyavarṇeti ca vai ṛgbhiś catasṛbhis tathā ||
śan no devīr iti tathā śan na āpas tathaiva ca |
[59]        idam āpaḥ pravahata tathā mantram udīrayet ||
evaṃ saṃmārjanaṃ kṛtvā chanda ārṣaṃ ca daivatam |
aghamarṣaṇasūktasya saṃsmaret prayataḥ sadā ||
chanda ānuṣṭubhaṃ tasya ṛṣiś caivāghamarṣaṇaḥ |
devatā bhāvavṛttaś ca pāpaghnasya prakīrtitaḥ ||
tato ‘mbhasi nimajjet tu triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam |
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ ||
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanam |
anena vidhinā snātvā abmadhye snānavāsasā ||
parivartitavāsāś cet tīrthatīra upaspṛśet |
udakasyāpradānād dhi snānaśāṭīṃ na pīḍayet ||
anena vidhinā snātaḥ puṇyaṃ phalam upāśnute |
“bhajatām” ity asya “varuṇa” iti vipariṇatenānvayaḥ | “hiraṇyavareti”, “hiraṇyavarṇā śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” ityādyāś catasras taittirīya-maitrāyaṇīyayoḥ | “śan na āpa” iti, “śan na āpo dhanvanyā” iti kaṭhaśākhāyām | “pāpaghnasya” aghamarṣaṇasūktasyety anvayaḥ |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
etac chrutvātha vacanaṃ yājñavalkyasya vai tadā |
[60]        ṛṣayaḥ saṃśitātmānaḥ pṛcchanti snānaniścayam ||
snānam abdaivatair mantrair yat tvayoktaṃ purānagha |
tadācakṣva viśeṣeṇa snānasya tu vidhiṃ prabho ||
tān pratyuvāca prītātmā yājñavalkyo ‘mitadyutiḥ |
śṛṇudhvaṃ vakṣyate snānaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
mṛttilān gomayaṃ darbhān puṣpāṇi surabhīṇi ca |
āharet snānakāle tu snānārthaṃ prayataḥ śuciḥ ||
gatvodakāntaṃ viviktam āsthāpyaitat pṛthak kṣitau |
tridhā kṛtvā mṛdaṃ tāṃ tu gomayaṃ tu vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
adhamottamamadhyānām aṅgānāṃ kṣālanaṃ tu vai |
bhāgaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak kuryāt kṣālane mṛdasaṃkaraḥ ||
tathā |
adbhir mṛdbhiś ca caraṇau prakṣālyācamya vai śuciḥ |
uruṃ hīti ṛcā toyam upasthāya pradakṣiṇam ||
āvartayet tad udakaṃ ye te śatam iti ṛcā |
[61]        sumitriyā ity añjalim uddhared daivataṃ smaran ||
durmitriyā iti dveṣyaṃ dhyāyāṃś cāpaḥ prasecayet ||
adbhir mṛdbhiś ca gātrāṇi kramaśas tv avanejayet |
ekayā tu śiraḥ kṣālya dvābhyāṃ nābhes tathopari ||
kaṭivastyūru jaṅghe ca caraṇau ca tribhis tribhiḥ |
prakṣālya hastau cācamya namaskṛtya jalaṃ tu tat ||
yat kiṃ ceti ca mantreṇa namasyet prayatāñjaliḥ |
yatra sthāne tu yat tīrthaṃ nadī puṇyatamā ca yā ||
tāṃ dhyāyen manasāvāhya cānyatreṣṭaṃ vicintayet |
gaṅgādipuṇyatīrthāni kṛtrimādiṣu saṃsmaret ||
ud uttamam iti viśet taj jalaṃ prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ |
yena devāḥ pavitreti kuryād ālambhanaṃ tribhiḥ ||
savyāhṛtibhiḥ paścād ācāmet prayato ‘pi san |
ālabheta mṛdāṅgāni idaṃ viṣṇur iti ṛcā ||
bhāskarābhimukho majjed āpo asmān iti ṛcā |
[62]        tato ‘vamṛṣya gātrāṇi nimajyonmajya vai punaḥ ||
ācamya gomayenāpi mānastokyā samālabhet |
tato ‘bhiṣicya mantrais tu vāruṇaiś ca yathākramam ||
imaṃ me varuṇety ṛgbhyāṃ tvan naḥ sa tvan na ity api |
māpa ud uttamam iti muñcatv avabhṛtheti ca ||
abhiṣicya tad ātmānaṃ nimajyācamya vai punaḥ |
darbhais tu pāvayen mantrair abliṅgaiḥ pāvanaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir idam āpo haviṣmatīḥ |
devīr āpa iti dvābhyām apo devā iti ṛcā ||
drupadād iva ity ṛcā śanno devīr apāṃ rasam |
[63]        apo devīḥ pāvamānyaḥ punantv ādyā ṛco nava ||
citpatir meti ca śanaiḥ pāvyātmānaṃ samāhitaḥ |
hiraṇyavarṇā iti ca pāvamānyas tathāparāḥ ||
taratsamāḥ śuddhavatīḥ pavitrāṇy api śaktitaḥ |
vāruṇīś ca ṛcaḥ sūktaṃ śaktitaḥ samprayojayet ||
jalamadhye sthito vipraḥ śuddhabhāvo hariṃ smaret |
oṃkāreṇa vyāhṛtibhir gāyatryā tu samāhitaḥ ||
ādāv ante ca kurvīta hy abhiṣekaṃ yathākramam |
apāṃ madhye sthitasyaivaṃ mārjanaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
antarjale japen mantraṃ triḥkṛtvas tv aghamarṣaṇam |
drupadāṃ vā trir abhyasyed āyaṃ gaur iti vā tryṛcam ||
haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ṛcaṃ trir āvartya japed atha |
anyāni caiva sūktāni smārtadṛṣṭāny anusmaret ||
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ trir japed atha |
[64]        āvartayed vā praṇavaṃ smared vā viṣṇum avyayam ||
viṣṇor āyatanaṃ hy āpaḥ sa hy apāṃ patir ucyate |
tasyaiva sūnavas tv etās tasmāt taṃ hy apsu saṃsmaret ||
narād āpaḥ prasūtā vai tena nārā iti smṛtāḥ |
tā evāsyāyanaṃ hy āpas tasmān nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yaṃ hi vratānāṃ vedānāṃ yamasya niyamasya ca |
bhoktāraṃ yajñatapasāṃ dhyāyināṃ dhyeyam eva ca ||
dhyāyan nārāyaṇaṃ nityaṃ snānādiṣu ca karmasu |
prāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu duṣkṛtān mucyate pumān ||
pramādāt kurvatāṃ karma pracyavaty adhvareṣu yat |
smaraṇād eva tad viṣṇoḥ saṃpūrṇaṃ syādi iti smṛtiḥ ||
tad viṣṇor iti mantreṇa majjed apsu punaḥ punaḥ |
gāyatrī vaiṣṇavī hy eṣā viṣṇoḥ saṃsmaraṇaya vai ||
pādena pāṇinā vāpi yaṣṭavyā vastreṇa codakam |
na hanyān naiva vādec ca na ca pakṣobhayed budhaḥ ||
na kuryāt kasyacit pīḍāṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
ācarann abhiṣekaṃ tu karmāṇy anyāni nācaret ||
yo ‘sau vistaraśaḥ proktaḥ snānasya vidhir uttamaḥ |
asāmarthyān na kuryāc cet tatrāyaṃ vidhir ucyate ||
snānam antarjalaṃ caivaṃ mārjanācamane tathā |
[65]        jalābhimantraṇaṃ caiva tīrthasya parikalpanam ||
aghamarṣaṇasūktena trirāvṛttena nityaśaḥ |
snānācaraṇam ity etat samuddiṣṭaṃ mahātmabhiḥ ||
anyān vā vāruṇān mantrān kāmataḥ saṃprayojayet |
yathākālaṃ yathādeśaṃ jñātvā kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
“etat” mṛttikādi | “gomayaṃ ca” “tridhā kṛtvā” ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “adhamam aṅgam” nābher adhaḥ | “uttamam aṅgam” śiraḥ | “madhyamam aṅgam” nābher upari kaṇṭhaparyantam | “mṛdasaṃkaraḥ” pṛthakkṛtamṛdbhāgāmelanam | “uruṃ hīti” “uruṃ hi rājā varuṇa” ityādikā | “ye te śatam iti” “ye te śataṃ varuṇam” ityādikā | “sumitriyā” iti, “sumitriyā na āpa” iti yajuḥ | “durmitriyā” iti, “durmitriyās tasmai santu” iti yajuḥ | “yat kiṃ ca” iti, “yat kiṃ cedaṃ varuṇa” ityādikā ṛk | “anyatra” uddhṛtajale | “iṣṭaṃ tīrtham” prayāgādi | “kṛtrimaṃ” puṣkariṇyādi | kṛtrimādiṣv [66] ityādiśabdenākṛtrimatīrthabhūtahradādigrahaṇam | “ud uttamam” iti, “ud uttamaṃ varuṇa pāśam” ityādikā ṛk | “prāṅmukhaḥ kuryād” iti, “ālambhanam” ity anvayaḥ | “ālambhanam” hastena nadyādijalasparśanam | “idaṃ viṣṇur” iti, “idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrame” iti ṛk | mṛdālambhanaṃ cārthād aṅgaprakṣālanārthaṃ tridhākṛtamṛdvyatiriktamṛda, tridhākṛtāyā aṅgaprakṣālane viniyuktatvāt | “āpo asmān” iti “āpo asmān mātara” iti ṛk | “bhākarābhimukho majjed” ity etat taḍāgādau boddhavyam | tad uktaṃ nṛsiṃhapurāṇe |
nadyām sravatsu ca snāyāt pratisrotaḥ sthito dvijaḥ |
taḍāgādiṣu toyeṣu pratyarkaṃ snānam ācaret || iti |
“mānastokyā”, “mānastoke tanaye māna” ity anayā ṛcā | “imaṃ ma” iti, “imaṃ me varuṇa śrudhī havam” iti, “tat tvā yāmi” iti ṛcau | “tvan na” iti, “tvan no agne varuṇasya” ityādikā ṛk | “sa tvan na” iti, sa tvan no [67] agne ‘vamo bhavoti” ityādikā ṛk | “māpa” iti, “māpo mauṣadhīḥ” ityādikā ṛk | “ud uttamam” iti, “ud uttamaṃ varuṇa pāśam” ityādikā| “muñcatu” iti, “muñcantu mā śapathād” ityādikā | “avabhṛtha” iti, “avabhṛthanicumpuṇa” ityādi yajuḥ | “idam āpa” iti, “idam āpaḥ pravahata” iti | “haviṣmatīr” iti, “haviṣmatīr imā āpa” iti | “devīr āpa iti dvābhyām” iti, “devīr āpo apānnapād” iti | “kārṣir asi” “samudrasya tvā” iti dve | “apo devā” iti, “apo devā madhumatīḥ” iti | “drupadād iva” iti, “drupadād iva mumucāna” iti | “śan no devīr” iti, “śan no devīr abhiṣṭaye” iti | “apāṃ rasam” iti, “apāṃ rasam udvayasam” iti | “āpo devīr” iti, “āpo devīr upasṛja” iti | “pāvamānyaḥ” “punantu mā pitara” ityādyā nava [68] ṛcaḥ | “citpatir mā” iti, “citpatir mā punātu” iti, “vākpatir mā punātu” anye ca “acchidreṇa pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhiḥ” ity anuṣaṅgeṇa mantradvayam | “devo mā savitā punātv acchidreṇa pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhiḥ” iti ca tṛtīyo mantraḥ | etair mantraiḥ “pāvya” pāvayitvety arthaḥ | “hiraṇyavarṇā” uktāḥ | aparāḥ “pāvamānyaḥ” “pāvamānīḥ svastyayanīḥ” ity ādikāḥ | “taratsamāḥ” “taratsamandī dhāvati” ityādikāḥ | “śuddhavatīḥ” “etonvindras tvāma” ityādikā | “pavitrāṇi” puruṣasūktādīni | “vāruṇa ṛcaḥ” aparā api | “sūktaṃ” ca vāruṇam eva | “ādāv ante ca” iti, ādau pāvanasya pāvanasyānte cety arthaḥ | “abhiṣekaḥ” śirasi hastena jalakṣepaḥ | evam abhiṣekavat praṇavavyāhṛtigāyatrībhir mārjanam api jalasthena kartavyam ity arthaḥ | “āyaṃ gaur” iti, “āyaṃ gauḥ pṛśni akramīt” iti | “haṃsaḥ śuciṣad” iti, “haṃsaḥ śuciṣad vasu” ityādikā | smārta[69]dṛṣṭān” smārtair manvādibhir dṛṣṭāni | yathā “yuñjate mana” ity anuvākaḥ | “viṣṇum avyayam” vyāpinaṃ kāraṇapuruṣam ity arthaḥ | “prāyaścitteṣu” pāpakṣayahetuṣu sarveṣu karmasu nārāyaṇaṃ dhyāyan duṣkṛtān mucyate | yadi tu pramādād aṅgavaikalyam api syāt tadāpi saṃpūrṇaphalatvam evety arthaḥ | “tad viṣṇoḥ” iti, “tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti” iti | “anyāni” iti, ācarann abhiṣekaṃ pādābhighātaparapīḍāparyantāni nācaret | etena snānam evāṅgahīnaṃ bhavatīti darśitam | “yo ‘sau” iti, yadi tu kāladeśāsāmarthyādivaśād vistareṇa snānavidhiṃ kartum asamarthas tadāghamarṣaṇasūktena trir āvṛttena tīrthaparikalpana-jalābhimantraṇācamanamārjanāntarjalajpasnāni kuryād ity arthaḥ | pūrvapratipannakramavat tīrthaparikalpanādipadārthānuvādena mantramātraviparītakramābhidhāne ‘pi sa eva kramo boddhavyaḥ | yadi tu kāladeśavaśāt śaknoti, tadāparān api vāruṇān mantrān prayojayed ity arthaḥ|
[70] baudhāyanaḥ [2.8.1–12] |
atha hastau prakṣālya kamaṇḍaluṃ mṛtpiṇḍaṃ ca parigṛhya tīrthaṃ gatvā triḥ pādau prakṣālayate trir ātmānam | atha haike bruvate – śmaśānam āpo devagṛhaṃ goṣṭhaṃ yatra ca brāhmaṇā aprakṣālya tu pādau nāntaḥ praveṣṭavyam | athāpo ‘bhiprapadyate –
hiraṇyaśṛṅgaṃ varuṇaṃ papadye tīrthaṃ me dahi yācitaḥ |
yan mayā bhuktam asādhūnāṃ pāpebhyaś ca pratigrahaḥ ||
yan me vācā manasā karmaṇā vā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |
tan ma indro varuṇo bṛhaspatiḥ savitā ca punantu punaḥ punaḥ || iti |
athāñjalināpa upahanti “sumitriyā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santv” iti | tāṃ diśaṃ nirukṣati yasyām asya diśi dveṣyo bhavati “durmitriyās tasmai bhūyāsur yo ‘smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣma” iti | athāpa upaspṛśya triḥ pradakṣiṇam udakam āvartayati “yad apāṃ krūraṃ yad amedhyaṃ yad aśāṇtaṃ tad apagacchatād” iti | [71] apsu nimajyonmajya | nāpsu sataḥ prayamaṇaṃ vidyate na vāsaḥpalpūlanaṃ nopasparśanam | yady aparuddhāḥ syur etenopatiṣṭhate “namo ‘gnaye ‘psumate nama indrāya namo varuṇāya namo vāruṇyai namo ‘dbhyaḥ” iti | uttīryācamyācāntaḥ punar ācāmet |
āpo vā idaṃ sarvaṃ viśvā bhūtāny āpaḥ prāṇā vā āpaḥ ||
āpaḥ punantu pṛthivīṃ pṛthivī pūtā punātu mām |
punantu brahmaṇaspatir brahmapūtā punātu mām ||
yad ucchiṣṭam abhojyaṃ ca yadvā duścaritaṃ mama |
sarvaṃ punantu mām āpo ‘satāṃ ca pratigrahaṃ svāhā || iti |
pavitre kṛtvādbhir mārjayati “āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvaḥ” iti tisṛbhiḥ, “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā” iti catasṛbhiḥ | “pavamānaḥ suvarjana” ity etenānuvākena mārjayitvāntarjalagato ‘ghamarṣaṇena trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvottīrya vāsaḥ pīṣayitvā prakṣālitopavātāny akliṣṭāni vāsāṃsi paridhāyāpa ācamya darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayet |
[72] “tīrthaṃ gatvā” tīrtham atra jalāśayamātraṃ vivakṣitam | “ātmānaṃ” svaśarīram | “triḥ prakṣālayata” ity anvayaḥ | “apo ‘bhiprapadyate” “hiraṇyaśṛṅga” ityādi mantraiḥ | “apa upahanti” iti, upahantir atra grahaṇe vartate | “yasyāṃ diśi” “asya” snānakartuḥ “dveṣyo bhavati” ity anvayaḥ | “nirukṣati” siñcati | “prayamaṇaṃ” itas tato gamanaṃ prataraṇaṃ vā | “palpūlanaṃ” malanirṇejanam | ayaṃ ca “plpūla lavanapavanayor” ity asya caurādikasya lyuṭi prayogaḥ | “upasaprāsnam” atra gātramalodgharṣaṇam | etat sarvaṃ “apsu sato na vidyata” ity anvayaḥ | “yady uparuddhāḥ syuḥ” āpa iti śeṣaḥ | uparodho ‘tra prayaṇādir eva | sa yadi pramādataḥ kṛtas tadā “namo ‘gnaye” ity ādinā mantreṇāpām upasthānaṃ kuryāt | “pavitre” avicchinnāgre dvidalamātre prādeśasaṃmite | “upavātāni” śuṣkāṇi | “akliṣṭāni” ajīrṇāni |
kātyāyanaḥ [snānasūtram, 1–20] |
athāto nityasnānam | nadyādau | mṛdgomayakuśatilasumanasa āhṛtya | udakāntaṃ gatvā śucau deśe snāpya | prakṣālya pāṇipādam | kuśopagraho baddhaśikhī yajñopavītī [73] cācamya | uruṃ hīti yogam āmantryāvartayed ye te śatam iti | “sumitriyā na” ity apoñjalinādāya “durmitriyā” iti dveṣyaṃ prati niṣiñcet | kaṭiṃ vastyūru jaṅghe caraṇau mṛdā tris triḥ prakṣālya hastau ca | ācamya namasyodakam ālabhed aṅgāni mṛdedaṃ viṣṇur iti | sūryābhimukho nimajjet | āpo asmān iti snātvodidābhya ity unmajya nimajyonmajyācamya | gomayena vilimpen mānastoka iti | tato ‘bhiṣiñced imaṃ me varuṇeti catasṛbhir māpa uduttamaṃ muñcantv avabhṛtheti | ante caitan nimajyācamya darbhaiḥ pāvayed āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir idam āpo haviṣmatīr devīr āpa iti dvābhyām āpo devā drupadād iva śan no devīr apāṃ rasam apo [74] devīḥ punantu meti navabhiś citpatir meti | oṃkāreṇa vyāhṛtibhir gāyatryā cādāv ante ca | antarjale cāghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayet | drupadād ivāyaṃ gaur iti vā tṛcaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ vā saśirasam om iti vā | viṣṇor vā smaraṇam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
atha snānavidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ pravakṣyāmy anupūrvaśaḥ |
yena snātvā divaṃ yānti śraddadhānā dvijottamāḥ ||
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca |
snānaṃ samācaren nityaṃ gartaprasravaṇeṣu ca ||
pārakyeṣu nipāneṣu na snāyād dhi kadācana |
nipānakartuḥ snātvā tu duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate ||
alābhe devakhātānāṃ sarasāṃ saritāṃ tathā |
uddhṛtya caturaḥ piṇḍān parakye snānam ācaret ||
arugdivācaret snānaṃ madhyāhnāt prāg viśeṣataḥ |
prayato mṛdaṃ ādāya pūrvām ārdraṃ ca gomayam ||
sthāpayitvā tathācamya tataḥ snānaṃ samācaret |
prakṣālya hastau pādau ca śikhābandhaṃ samācaret ||
mṛdaikayā śiraḥ kṣālyaṃ dvābhyāṃ nābhes tathopari |
adhaś catasṛbhiḥ kāryaṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ pādau tathaiva ca ||
[75]        prakṣālya sarvakāyaṃ ca dvir ācamya yathāvidhi |
tataḥ saṃmārjanaṃ kuryān mṛdā pūrvaṃ tu mantravat ||
“aśvakrānte rathakrānte viṣṇukrānte vasundhare |
uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa kṛṣṇena śatabāhunā ||
mṛttike tvāṃ ca gṛhṇāmi prajayā ca dhanena ca |
mṛttike brahmadattāsi kāśyapenābhimantritā ||
mṛttike jahi me sarvaṃ yan mayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |
mṛttike dehi me puṣṭiṃ tvayi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||”
punaś ca gomayenaivam agram agram iti bruvan |
“agram agraṃ carantīnām oṣadhīnāṃ rasaṃ vane ||
tāsām ṛṣabhapatnīnāṃ pavitraṃ kāyaśodhanam |
tvaṃ me rogāṃś ca śokāṃś ca pāpaṃ ca nuda gomaya ||”
kāṇḍāt kāṇḍād iti dvābhyām aṅgam aṅgam upaspṛśet |
“kāṇḍāt kāṇḍāt prarohantī paruṣaḥ paruṣaspari ||
evā no dūrve pratanu sahasreṇa śatena ca |
yā śatena pratanoṣi sahasreṇa virohasi ||
tasyāste devīṣṭake vidhema haviṣā vayam |”
kṛtvaivaṃ mārjanaṃ mantrair aśvakrāntādibhis tataḥ ||
[76]        īheta devīr amṛtaṃ pārāvatasya rātiṣu |
ye te śatam iti dvābhyāṃ tīrthāny āvāhayet tataḥ ||
kurukṣetraṃ gayāṃ gaṅgāṃ prabhāsaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca |
tato mahāvyāhṛtibhir gāyatryā cābhimantrayet ||
āpo hi ṣṭhedam āpaś ca drupadādiva ity api |
tathā hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ pāvamānībhir antataḥ ||
tato ‘rkam ikṣya soṃkāraṃ nimajyāntarjale budhaḥ |
prāṇāyāmāṃś ca kurvīta gāyatrīṃ cāghamarṣaṇam ||
yathoktaiḥ kṣobhitas tais tu majjet trir daṇḍāvat tataḥ |
padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭikhaṇḍam, 20.145–157] |
nairmalyaṃ bhāvaśuddhiś ca vinā snānaṃ na vidyate |
tasmān manoviśuddhyarthaṃ snānam ādau vidhīyate ||
anuddhṛtair uddhṛtair vā jalaiḥ snānaṃ samācaret |
tīrthaṃ prakalpayed vidvān mūlamantreṇa mantravit ||
[77]        om namo nārāyaṇāyeti mūlamantra udāhṛtaḥ |
darbhapāṇis tu vidhinā hy ācāntaḥ prayataḥ śuciḥ ||
caturhastasamāyuktaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ |
prakalpyāvāhayed gaṅgām ebhir mantrair vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
“oṃ viṣṇoḥ pādaprasūtāsi vaiṣṇavī viṣṇupūjitā |
pāhi nas tv enasas tv asmād ājanmamaraṇāntikāt ||
tisraḥ koṭyo ‘rdhakoṭī ca tīrthānāṃ vāyur abravīt |
divi bhuvy antarikṣe ca tāni te santi jāhnavi ||
nandinīty eva te nāma deveṣu nalinīti ca |
nandā pṛthvī ca subhagā viśvakāyā śivā sitā ||
vidyādharī suprasannā tathā lokaprasādinī |
kṣemā ca jāhnavī caiva śāntā śāntipradāyinī||”
etāni puṇyanāmāni snānakāle prakīrtayet |
bhavet saṃnihitā tatra gaṅgā tripathagāminī ||
saptavārābhijaptena karasaṃpuṭayojitam |
mūrdhni kuryāj jalaṃ bhūyas triś catuḥ pañca sapta vā ||
snānaṃ kuryān mṛdā tadvad āmantrya tu vidhānataḥ |
“aśvakrānte rathakrānte viṣṇukrānte vasundhare ||
mṛttike hara me pāpaṃ yan mayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |
uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa kṛṣṇena śatabāhunā ||
[78]        namas te sarvalokānāṃ bhava vāriṇi suvrate |
āruhya mama gātrāṇi sarvapāpaṃ pramocaya ||”
narasiṃhapurāṇe [57.65–70] |
nadyāṃ sravatsu ca snāyāt pratisrotaḥ sthito dvijaḥ |
taḍāgādiṣu toyeṣu pratyarkaṃ snānam ācaret ||
śucau deśe samabhyukṣya sthāpayet kuśamṛttilān |
mṛttoyena svakaṃ dehaṃ bahiḥ saṃśodhya yatnataḥ ||
snānaśāṭīṃ ca saṃśodhya kuryād ācamanaṃ budhaḥ |
śanair jalaṃ praviśyātha named varuṇam appatim ||
hariṃ hy evaṃ smaran budhyā nimajjec charavaj jale |
tatas tīraṃ samāsādya apa ācamya mantrataḥ ||
prokṣayed varuṇaṃ devaṃ mantrair vā pāvamānibhiḥ |
kuśāgrasthena toyena prokṣyātmānaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
ālabhen mṛttikāṃ gātre idaṃ viṣṇur iti dvijaḥ |
tato nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ saṃsmaran praviśej jalam ||
[79]        nimajyāntarjale samyak prapaṭhed aghamarṣaṇam |
“pratyarkam” ādityābhimukho bhūtvā |
viṣṇuḥ [64.18–23] |
mṛttoyaiḥ kṛtamalāpakarṣo ‘psu nimajyāpa upaspṛśyāpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir hiraṇyavarṇeti catasṛbhir idam āpaḥ pravahata iti ca tīrtham abhimantrayet | tato ‘psu nimagnas trir aghamarṣaṇam japet | tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam iti vā | drupadāṃ sāvitrīṃ vā | yuñjate mana ity anuvākaṃ vā | puruṣasūktaṃ vā |
“idam āpaḥ pravahata” iti, “idam āpaḥ pravahata yat kiṃcid duritaṃ mayi” ityādikā |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
hiraṇyavarṇā iti sūktena snātvā śaucaṃ kṛtvāpāṃ madhye trīn prāṇāyāmān kuryāt |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra, 22.312] |
drupadādiveti yo mantro vede vājasaneyake |
[80]        antarjale trir āvartya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
dakṣaḥ |
mārjanaṃ jalamadhye tu prāṇāyāmo yatas tataḥ |
upasthānaṃ tataḥ paścāt sāvitrījapa ucyate ||
“yatas tataḥ” jale sthale ca |
āpastambaḥ [2.22.13] |
śanair apo ‘bhyupeyād abhighnann abhimukha ādityam udakam upaspṛśed iti sarvatrodakasparśanavidhiḥ |
“abhighnan” pāṇinodakaṃ tāḍayan, jalacaraprāṇyapasāraṇāya iti seṣaḥ | “sarvatra” sravatsv asravatsu ca | etac ca āpastambasmṛtau vānaprasthaprakaraṇe samāmnātatvād vānaprasthasyaivedṛśaṃ snānam |
śāṅkhāyanagṛhyam |
savastro ‘har ahar āplutyādhyanudako ‘nyad vastram ācchādayet |
“savastraḥ” iti dvitīyavastraprāptyartham, ekavastrasya nagnatvapratiṣedhena prāptatvād iti brahmadattabhāṣayam | “adhyanudakaḥ” śirovyatiriktagātreṣv anuddhṛtodakaḥ |
aṅgirāḥ |
vinā darbheṇa yat snānaṃ yac ca dānaṃ vinodakam |
[81]        asaṃkhyātaṃ ca yaj japtaṃ tat sarvaṃ niṣprayojanam ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
tūṣṇīm evāvagāheta yadā syād aśucir naraḥ |
ācamya prayataḥ paścāt snānaṃ vidhivad ācaret ||
sparśenādbhir dūṣitābhir uddhṛtābhis tu mānavaḥ |
snānaṃ samācared yas tu na sa śudhyati karhicit ||
agrāhyās tv āgatā hy āpo nādyāḥ prathamavegikāḥ |
prakṣobhitās tu kenāpi yāś ca tīrthād viniḥsṛtāḥ ||
upasthānādir yas tāsāṃ mantravān kīrtito vidhiḥ |
nivedanāntas tat snānam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ ||
niṣekāt prabhṛtir yāvac chmaśānaṃ śrutidarśanāt |
nāmantravad dvijātīnāṃ karma kiṃcit pravartate ||
tathā |
matsyakacchapamaṇḍūkās toye magnā divāniśam |
vasanto ‘pi ca te snātā phalaṃ nārhanti karhicit ||
śraddhāvidhisamāyuktaṃ karma yat kriyate nṛbhiḥ |
suviśuddhena bhāvena tad ānantyāya kalpate ||
vidhihīnaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaṃ kṛtam aśraddhayā ca yat |
tad dharanty asurās tasya mūḍhasya tv akṛtātmanaḥ ||
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ caiva mantravat snānam iṣyate |
[82]        tūṣṇīm eva hi śūdrasya sanamaskārakaṃ smṛtam ||
agamyāgamanāt streyāt pāpebhyaś ca pratigrahāt |
rahasy ācaritāt pāpān mucyate snānam ācaran ||
prakartum asamarthaś cej juhotiyajatikriyāḥ |
snānadhyānajapair dānair ātmānaṃ śodhayed budhaḥ ||
yad eva snānaṃ kurute viśuddhenāntarātmanā |
tenaiva sarvam āpnoti vidhiyajñakriyāphalam ||
gṛhītvā yamadhanur vai niyamajyātataṃ dṛḍham |
samdadhīta damaśaraṃ svargalakṣye prayatnataḥ ||
yasyedam āyudhaṃ nāsti svargasādhanam uttamam |
api janmasahasreṣu na sādhayati tat param||
“aśuciḥ” snānanimittena cāṇḍālasparśādinā | “nadyām” iti prāk śuṣkanadīviṣayam | “prakṣobhitāḥ” mahiṣādinātyantakaluṣīkṛtāḥ | “tīrthād viniḥsṛtāḥ” tīrthaṃ parityajya gatāstatattīrthatvenāpratyabhijñāyamānāḥ (?) | “upasthānam” “uruṃ hi rājā” ityādimantreṇa | “tāsām” apām | “nivedanāntaḥ” “devā gātuvida” iti japyanivedanāntaḥ | “niṣekaḥ” garbhādhānam | “vidhihīnam” yathokavidhihīnam | “śūdrasya sanamaskārakam” iti, śūdra āplavanamātram udakanamaskāramāteṇa [83] kuryād ity arthaḥ | “tat param” tat svargādi paraṃ śreṣṭham |
punar yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
eṣa vo ‘bhihitaḥ kṛtsnaḥ snānasya paramo vidhiḥ |
guṇāś ca tasyācaraṇe doṣāś caivāniṣevaṇe ||
tathā |
snātvaivaṃ vāsasī dhaute tv akliṣṭe paridhāya ca |
prakṣālyorū mṛdaṅghrī ca hastau prakṣālya vai tathā ||
abhāve dhautavastrasya śāṇakṣaumājināni ca |
kutapaṃ yogapaṭṭaṃ vā dvivāsā yena vai bhavet ||
yāvad devān ṛṣīṃś caiva pitṝṃś caiva na tarpayet |
tāvan na pīḍayed vastraṃ yena snāto na codake ||
niṣpīḍayti yaḥ pūrvaṃ snānavastraṃ tu tarpaṇāt |
nirāśāḥ pitaras tasya yānti devā samaharṣibhiḥ ||
“kutapo” nepālakambalaḥ | “dvivāsā yena” iti, anantaroktaiḥ kutapādibhir api dvivastratvaṃ vidhātavyam | “yena snātaḥ” yena vastreṇa snātaḥ | “na codake” “vastraṃ pīḍayed” iti saṃbandhaḥ |
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.72–73] |
na raktam ulbaṇaṃ vāso na jīrṇaṃ tu praśasyate |
[84]        malāktaṃ ca daśāhīnaṃ varjayed ambaraṃ budhaḥ ||
tataḥ prakṣālayet pādau mṛttoyena vicakṣaṇaḥ |
parāśaraḥ [12.12–13] |
brāhmaṇaṃ snātum āyāntam anugacchanti devatāḥ |
pitaraś ca mahābhāgā vāyubhūtā jalārthinaḥ ||
snātvā nirasya vāso ‘nyaj jaṅghe prakṣālya cāmbhasi |
apavitrīkṛte te tu kaupīnaś cyotavāriṇā ||
nrāśāḥ pitaro yānti vastraniṣpīḍane kṛte |
tasmān niṣpīḍayed vastraṃ nākṛtvā pitṛtarpaṇam ||
jalamadhye tu yaḥ kaścid dvijātir jñānadurbalaḥ |
niṣpīḍayati tad vastraṃ snānaṃ tasya vṛthā bhavet ||
“kaupīnaś cyotavāriṇā” kaupīnaṃ jaghanapradeśaḥ, tataś cyutena jalena |
snānavidhyanantaraṃ śaṅkhaḥ |
udakasyāpradānād dhi snānaśāṭīṃ na pīḍayet |
anena vidhinā snātaḥ puṇyaṃ phalam upāśnute ||
hārītaḥ |
snātvā na gātram avamṛjyān na śiro vidhunuyān nottarīyaviparyāsaṃ kuryāt |
[85] viṣṇuḥ [64.10–15] |
snātaḥ śiro nāvadhunet | nāṅgebhyas toyam uddharet | na tailavase saṃspṛśet | nāprakṣālitaṃ pūrvadhṛtaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt | snāta eva soṣṇīṣo dhaute vāsasī bibhṛyāt | na mlecchāntyajapatitaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt |
tailaṃ ca vasā ca “tailavase” | “pūrvadhṛtam” aṅgeṣv iti śeṣaḥ | “uṣṇīṣaṃ” ca keśaśmaśrujalāpakarṣaṇārthaṃ śiroveṣṭanaṃ tāvanmātrakālam eva yāvatā jalāpakarṣaṇaṃ bhavati |
tathā ca mahābhārate |
āplutaḥ sādhivāsena jalena ca sugandhinā |
rājahaṃsanibhaṃ prāpya coṣṇīṣaṃ śithilārpitam ||
jalakṣayanimittaṃ vai veṣṭayāmāsa mūrdhje ||
“śithilārpitam” agāḍhabaddhaḥ |
manuḥ [4.83] |
śiraḥsnātas tu tailena nāṅge kiṃcid api spṛśet |
yadā śiraḥsnānaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati, tadā kiṃcid apy aṅgaṃ tailasaṃbandhi na kāryam | tailaśabdaś ca yogarūḍhyā tilataile vartate | [86] sārṣapatailādiṣu “snehe tailac” [vārtikam 3118] iti tailacpratyatāt tatra prayogaḥ |
iti snānavidhiḥ |
madhyāhne snātvā tataḥ saṃdhyopāsanaṃ pūrvoktavidhinā kartavyam,
saṃdhyātrayaṃ tu kartavyaṃ dvijenātmavidā sadā |
iti yogiyājñavalkyavacanāt, śaṅkhena ca madhyāhnasaṃdhyām abhidhāya,
eṣa eva vidhiḥ proktaḥ saṃdhyayoś ca dvijātiṣu |
ity abhidhānāt |
tad uktaṃ śaṅkhena [9.16–20] |
ācamyaivaṃ purā proktās tīrthasaṃmārjane tu ye |
mantrās tair mantritaṃ toyaṃ mūrdhni bhūmau tathā kṣipet ||
kṣiptena mūrdhni toyena pāpam asya praṇaśyati |
bhūmau kṣiptena hanyante asurā devaśatravaḥ ||
[87]        vyāhṛtīḥ kīrtayec caiva tathaivāvyayam akṣaram |
upaspṛśet tataḥ paścān mantreṇānena dharmataḥ ||
“antaś carasi bhūteṣu guhāyāṃ viśvatomukhaḥ |
tvaṃ yajñas tvaṃ vaṣaṭkāra āpo jyotī raso ‘mṛtam ||”
ācamya tu tataḥ paścād ādityābhimukho jalam |
ud u tyaṃ jātavedasam iti mantreṇa nikṣipet ||
eṣa eva vidhiḥ proktaḥ saṃdhyayoś ca dvijātiṣu |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhed āsīnaḥ paścimāṃ tathā ||
tato japet pavitrāṇi pavitraṃ vā svaśaktitaḥ |
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvā dīrgham āyur avāpnuyuḥ ||
“purā proktāḥ” “āpo hi ṣṭhā” ityādayaḥ “idam āpaḥ pravahata” ityantāḥ śaṅkhoktā eva | “pavitrāṇi” śaṅkhoktāny evāghamarṣaṇādīni |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
śrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakalpakāṇḍe snānaparva ||
[88]
3
atha japavidhiḥ
tatra baudhāyanaḥ [2.8.12–14] |
apa ācamya darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayec chatakṛtvo ‘parimitakṛtvo vā daśāvaram | athādityam upatiṣṭhate ud vayaṃ tamasas pari, ud u tyam, citram, tac cakṣur devahitam, ya udagād iti | athāpy udāharanti praṇavo vyāhṛtayaḥ sāvitrī cety ete pañca brahmayajñā ahar ahar brāḥmaṇaṃ kilbiṣāt pāvayanti |
“aparimitakṛtvaḥ” iti, daśabhya ūrdhvaṃ śatād arvāg uktaśatādisaṃkhyāvyatirekeṇapi boddhayvam, “daśavarām” ity abhidhānāt | “ud vayaṃ tamasas pari” iti, “ud vayaṃ tamasas pari paśyanto jyotir uttaram” ityādikā ṛk | “ud u tyam” iti, “ud u tyaṃ jātavedasam” ityādikā | “citram” [89] iti, “citraṃ devānām” ityādikā | “ya udagāt”, “ya udagān mahatorṇavād” ityādikā |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
ācāntaḥ punar ācāmen mantravat snānabhojane |
drupadāṃ ca trir āvartya tathā caivāghamarṣaṇam ||
gāyatrīṃ vā trir āvartya mahāvyāhṛtibhis tathā |
sopāṃśupraṇavenāpi āpaḥ pītā aghāpahāḥ ||
ācamya pāvya cātmānaṃ trir ācamya śanair asūn |
athopatiṣṭhed ādityam ūrdhvaṃ puṣpānvitaṃ jalam ||
prakṣipyod vayam ud u tyaṃ citraṃ tac cakṣur ity āpi |
haṃsaḥ śuciṣad etāni śubhāni pāvanāni ca ||
etaj japed ūrdhvabāhuḥ sūryaṃ vīkṣya samāhitaḥ |
gāyatryā tu yathāśakti copasthāya divākaram ||
vibhrāḍ ity anuvākena sūktena puruṣasya ca |
śivasaṃkalpena tathā maṇḍalabrāhmaṇena ca ||
[90]        divākīrtyaiś ca sauraiś ca mantrair anyaiś ca śaktitaḥ |
japayajño hi kartavyaḥ sarvadevapraṇītakaiḥ ||
pavitrair vividhaiś canyair guhyopaniṣadā tathā |
adhyātmavidyā vividhā japtavyā japasiddhaye ||
padakṣiṇaṃ samāvṛtya namaskṛtyopaviśya ca |
darbheṣu darbhapāṇiḥ syāt prāṅmukhas tu kṛtāñjaliḥ ||
svādhyāyaṃ tu yathāśakti brahmayajñārtham ācaret |
ākeśād ānakhāgrāc ca paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ ||
yaḥ sragvy api dvijo ‘dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito ‘nvaham |
ādāv ārabhya vedaṃ tu snātvopary upari kramāt ||
yad adhīte ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā saḥ svādhyāya iti smṛtaḥ |
“mantravat” “drupadāv iva” ityādimantrābhimantritam | ayaṃ cācamane mantravikalpaḥ phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā vyavasthitaḥ | “pāvya” mārjanaṃ kṛtvā | “vibhrāḍ” ity anuvākaḥ “vibhāḍ bṛhat pibatu somyaṃ madhu” ityādiḥ | “śivasaṃkalpaḥ” “yaj jāgrato dūram” ityādiḥ | “divākīrtyaiḥ" [91] adhyetṛsaṃpradāyena divā paṭhanīyaiḥ śatarudriyādibhiḥ | “sarvavedapraṇītakaiḥ” sarvavedapaṭhitaiḥ | “pavitraiḥ” pāvanaiḥ | “guhyopaniṣadā” sākṣāt paramātmapratipādakopaniṣadbhāgena | “adhyātmavidyā” upaniṣadbhyo ‘nyāpi | “sragvī” iti samāvṛttopalakṣaṇam |
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.77, 85, 87, 88] |
darbheṣu darbhapāṇiḥ san prāṅmukhaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
prāṇāyāmāṃs tu kurvīta yathāśāstram atandritaḥ ||
japed ahar ahaḥ snātvā sāvitrīṃ japavid dvijaḥ |
atha puṣpāñjaliṃ gatvā bhānave cordhvabāhukaḥ ||
ud u tyaṃ ca japen mantraṃ citraṃ tac cakṣur ity api |
pradakṣiṇam upāvṛtya namaskṛtya tataḥ prabhum ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau ca |
sarvavedapavitrāṇi vakṣyāmy aham ataḥ param |
yeṣāṃ japaiś ca homaiś ca pūyante nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[92]        aghamarṣaṇaṃ devakṛtaṃ śuddhavatyas taratsamāḥ |
kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ pāvamānyo ‘tha durgā sāvitry athaiva ca ||
abhīṣaṅgāḥ padastobhāḥ sāmāni vyāhṛtīs tathā |
bhāruṇḍāni ca sāmāni gāyatraṃ raivataṃ tathā ||
puruṣavrataṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca tathā devavratāni ca |
abliṅgā bārhaspatyaṃ ca vāksūktaṃ madhvṛcas tathā ||
śatarudriyam atharvaśiras trisuparṇaṃ mahāvratam |
gosūktaṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca indraśuddhe ca sāmanī ||
trīṇy ājyadohāni rathantaraṃ ca
agnivrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhac ca |
etāni gītāni punanti jantūn
jātismaratvaṃ labhate ya icchet ||
“devakṛtam” “devakṛtasyainasa” ityādayo mantrāḥ | “durgā” “jātavedasa” ityādi durgāprakāśikā ṛk | [93] “sāvitrī” “tat savituḥ” ityādikā | “abhīṣaṅgāḥ” “purojitī vo andhasa” ityādisāmatrayam | “padastobhāḥ” aṣṭeḍādisāmacatuṣṭayam | “sāmāni vyāhṛtīs tathā” iti vyāhṛtisāmānīty arthaḥ | “bhāruṇḍāni” “yat te kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna ā tutoda pipīlaḥ sarpa” ityādīny ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyākāni sāmāni rāṇāyanīyānāṃ prasiddhāni | “abliṅgāḥ” “āpo hi ṣṭhā” ityādayaḥ | “bārhapatyaṃ” “gaṇānāṃ tvā gaṇapatim” ityādikā ṛk | “vāksūktaṃ” “bṛhaspate prathamaṃ vāco agram” ityādi | “madhvṛcaḥ” “madhu vātā ṛtāpate” ityādikās tisraḥ | “śatarudriyam” “namas te rudra manyave” ityādyekādaśānuvākāḥ | “atharvaśiraḥ” “brahmā devānāṃ prathamaḥ” ityādi | “trisuparṇaṃ” “brahma metu [94] mām” ityādyanuvākatrayaṃ taittirīyāṇāṃ prasiddham | “mahāvratam” “somaṃ rājānāṃ” sāma | “gosuktāśvasūkte” sāmanī | “yad indrāhaṃ yathā tvam” ity asyām ṛci prasiddhe | “indraśuddhe sāmanī” “etonvindraṃ stavāma” ity asyām ṛci prasiddhe | atrismṛtau caitāny adhikāni | atriḥ |
vimalaṃ śivasaṃkalpaṃ vivarṇaṃ rohitaṃ tataḥ |
“vimalaṃ” “haṃsaḥ śuciṣad” ityādi | viṣṇusmṛtau nārāyaṇīyam adhikam | “nārāyaṇīyaṃ” taittirīyāṇām upaniṣat |
manuḥ |
vedam eva japen nityaṃ yathākālam atandritaḥ |
taṃ hy āhuḥ paramaṃ dharmam upadharmo ‘nya ucyate ||
vedābhyāsena satataṃ śaucena tapasaiva ca |
adroheṇa ca bhūtānāṃ jātiṃ smarati paurvikīm ||
saṃsmṛtya paurvikīṃ jātiṃ brahmaivābhyasate dvijaḥ |
brahmābhyāsena vājasram anantaṃ sukham aśnute ||
[95] “upadharmaḥ” alpaphalo dharmaḥ | “tapaḥ” kṛcchrādi | “brhma” vedaḥ | “anantaṃ” cirakālopabhogyam |
hārītaḥ |
praṇavo vyāhṛtiḥ gāyatrī ceti sāvitraṃ yena pāpebhyo mucyate | śataṃ japtvā mānasāt pūto bhavati | sahasraṃ japtvā vākkṛtāt pūto bhavati | daśasahasrāṇi japtvā sarvataḥ pūtātmā bhavati | āyuḥ prāṇaṃ mā nirmājīr iti mārjanaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | devīr āpaḥ śuddhā vayaṃ hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucaya āpo asmān mātara iti śuddhavatīr apsu japed apāmārgaṃ vā juhuyāt pūto bhavatīti | imāṃ dhiyaṃ śataṃ te rājan taratsamandyas taran japet pūto bhavati | tvaṃ soma tanūkṛdbhya iti tanukṛtaṃ pavitraṃ yena tanukṛtād anyakṛtāc ca mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | agna āyūṃṣi pavasa iti sapta śucimat pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ [96] copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | agneḥ samidasyabhiśastyā mā pāhīti pañcadaśakenābhiśaṃsanān mucyate | samudrād ūrmir iti tisra ṛṣabhaṃ pavitraṃ yenendriyakṛtān mucyate | mātāpitarāv atikramya yad antarikṣam ity ādityam upatiṣṭet pūto bhavati | ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā iti pañca namas te rājan varuṇeti catvāro dhāmno dhāmna ud uttamam ava te heḍa iti nirvaruṇaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | devīr āp ity anuvākam apsu japet pūto bhavati | imaṃ stomam arhata iti rocanaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | suparṇo ‘si garutmān iti sauparṇaṃ pavitraṃ prāg viṣṇukramebhyo viṣagaradaṣṭopaghāteṣūpadiśyate | kayā niścitra [97] iti tisro vāmadevyaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | rucaṃ no dhehīti surucaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | na tam ahna iti sūktaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | tat tvāyāmīti vāruṇaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo mucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād ādityaṃ copatiṣṭhet pūto bhavati | punantu mā pitara iti ṣoḍaśa pāvamānīr apsu japet pūto bhavati | yad devā devaheḍanam iti kūṣmāṇḍyas tābhir ājyaṃ juhuyāt pūto bhavati | pavitram asi drupadād iveti cāghamarṣaṇam antarjale trir āvartayitvā mucyate brahmahatyāyāḥ | sarvārtiṣv āpatsu vāsamāptikaṃ japed āpado mucyate | ad iti cet tayeti ca sāvitraṃ yat kiṃ cedaṃ varuṇam antarjale trir āvartya japet sarvapāpebhyaḥ pramucyate | ā kṛṣṇenety ādityam upatiṣṭhet sarvapūtātmā bhavati | [98] na ṣaḍarcajāpī yoniṃ vindati jātismaraś ca bhavati | evaṃ pratidiśam aindrayāmyasaumyavāruṇavāyavyāgneyasaurair upatiṣṭhet sarvapūtātmā bhavatrīti |
“mānasāt” mānasāt pāpāt | “vākkṛtāt” vākkṛtapāpāt. | “sarvato” vāṅmanaḥkāyakṛtebhyaḥ pāpebhyaḥ | “mārjanam” iti samākhyānād etanmantramārjanād eva phalam | “apāmārgam” apāmārgasamidhaḥ, hārītenaiva samidrūpatvena homābhidhānāt | “taran” naukādinā, “sutarmāṇam adhināvaṃ ruhema” iti mantravarṇāt | “tanukṛtāt” kāyikāt | “anyakṛtāt” mānasād vācikāc ca putraśiṣyādikṛtād vā pāpād iti śeṣaḥ | “śucimat” śucitvapratipādakaṃ “pavasa” ityādiṣūktam | “abhiśaṃsanam” abhiśāpaḥ | abhiśāpakartur etad iti jayasvāmī  | “nirvaruṇam” ity asya nāma | “viṣṇukramebhyaḥ” “viṣṇoḥ kramo ‘si” ityādimantrebhyaḥ | “garaḥ” mantrauṣadhyādinā pratibaddhavīryaṃ viṣam | “daṣṭaḥ” śvādinā | “vāmadevyaṃ” vāmadevaṛṣidṛṣṭam | “ārtiḥ” vyādhyādikṛtā | “samāptikaṃ” “agner manve prathama” ityādir anuvākaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.101] |
vedātharvapurāṇāni setihāsāni śaktitaḥ |
japayajñārthasidhyarthaṃ vidyāṃ cādhyātmikīṃ japet ||
[99] viṣṇuḥ [64.36–39] |
snātaś ca pavitrāṇi yathāśakti japet | viśeṣataḥ sāvitrīm | puruṣasūktaṃ vā | naitābhyām adhikam asti |
manur yamaś ca prathame [Manu 2.81–84] |
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo ‘vyayāḥ |
tripadā caiva gāyatrī vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇo mukham ||
yo ‘dhīte ‘hany ahany etāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇy atandritaḥ |
sa brahma param abhyeti vāyubhūtaḥ khamūrtimān ||
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
sāvitryās tu paraṃ nāsti maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate ||
kṣaranti sarvā vaidikyo juhotiyajatikriyāḥ |
akṣaraṃ tv akṣaraṃ jñeyaṃ brahma caiva prajāpatiḥ ||
“etāṃ” gāyatrīm | “vāyubhūtaḥ” vāyuvac chīghragatiḥ, liṅgaśarīraniṣṭho vā | “khamūrtimān” ākāśavad vyāypī san paramātmā bhavati | “kṣaranti” vināśinyo bhavanti | “akṣaraṃ” oṃkāraḥ, tasya mokṣarūpaṃ phalam | na kṣaratīty akṣaram | “prajāpatitvaṃ” ca tatpratipādakatvenoṃkārasya mantavyam | “akṣaratvaṃ” brahmatvam |
yamaṃ ṛṣaya ūcuḥ |
kiṃ vai paramakaṃ brahma kiṃ vai paramakaṃ tapaḥ |
[100]        upavāsāt paraṃ kiṃ vai kiṃ ca maunād viśiṣyate ||
yama uvāca |
oṃkāraḥ paramaṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
sāvitryāḥ paramaṃ nāsti maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate ||
kṣarantīha kriyāḥ sarvāḥ prayuktā vaidikīr bhuvi |
akṣaraṃ tv akṣaraṃ vidyād brahma caiva prajāpatiḥ ||
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma pāvanaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam |
gāyatryās tu paraṃ nāsti maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate ||
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
gāyatrīṃ tu japen nityaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm ||
gāyatrīṃ caiva vedāś ca tulayātolayat prabhuḥ |
ekataś caturo vedān sāṅgān sa sapadakramān ||
ekataś caiva gāyatrīṃ tulyarūpā tu sā smṛtā |
“saḥ” prajāpatiḥ | “sapadakramān” padakramasahitān |
aṅgirāḥ |
praṇavādyās tathā vedāḥ praṇave paryupasthitāḥ |
vāṅmayaṃ praṇavaḥ sarvam abhyaset praṇavaṃ tataḥ ||
praṇave nityayuktasya vyāhṛtīṣu ca saptasu |
tripadāyāṃ ca gāyatryāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit ||
[101] dakṣaḥ |
savitā devatā yasyā mukham agnis tripāt sthitā |
viśvāmitra ṛṣiś chando gāyatrī sā viśiṣyate ||
“tripāt” tripadā gāyatrī yā sthiteti yojyam |
yamaḥ |
na tathā vedajāpena pāpaṃ nirdahati dvijaḥ |
yathā sāvitrijāpena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
śaṅkhaḥ [12.1–31] |
iti vedapavitrāṇy abhihitāni | etebhyaḥ sāvitrī viśiṣyate | nāghamarṣaṇāt param antarjale | na vyāhṛtibhyaḥ paraṃ home | na sāvitryāḥ paraṃ japye | kuśavṛṣyām āsīnaḥ kuśottarāyāṃ vā kuśapavitrapāṇir udaṅmukhaḥ sūryābhimukho vākṣamālām ādāya devatāṃ dhyāyan japaṃ kuryāt | suvarṇamaṇimuktāphala-sphaṭikapadmākṣendrākṣaputrajīvakānām anyatamād akṣamālāṃ kuryāt | kuśagranthikṛtāṃ vā hastopayāmair vā gaṇayet | ādau devatām ārṣaṃ chandaś ca saṃsmaret | tataḥ sapraṇavā vyāhṛtīr ādāv āvartya gāyatrīm āvartayet | athāsyāḥ savitā devatā ṛṣir viśvāmitro gāyatrī chandaḥ | oṃkārasya praṇavākhyā | oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ svaḥ oṃ mahaḥ oṃ janaḥ oṃ tapaḥ oṃ satyaṃ iti mahāvyāhṛtayaḥ | āpo jyotī [102] rato ‘mṛtaṃ brahma bhūr bhuvaḥ svar om iti śīrṣam | atra ca bhavanti ślokāḥ |
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
ye japanti sadā teṣāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit ||
śatajaptā tu sā devī dinapāpapraṇāśanī |
sahasrajaptā tu tathā pātakebhyaḥ pramocinī ||
daśasāhasrajāpena sarvapāpapraṇāśinī |
lakṣajaptā tu sā devī mahāpātakanāśinī ||
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
surāpaś ca viśudhyanti lakṣajāpān na saṃśayaḥ ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā kalyaṃ kalyaṃ samāhitaḥ |
ahorātrakṛtāt pāpāt tatkṣaṇād eva mucyate ||
savyāhṛtīḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ ||
gāyatrī vedajananī gāyatrī pāpanāśinī |
gāyatryās tu paraṃ nāsti divi ceha ca pāvanam ||
hastatrāṇapadā devī patatāṃ narakārṇave |
tasmāt tām abhyasen nityaṃ brāhmaṇo hṛdaye śuciḥ ||
gāyatrījāpanirataṃ havyakavyeṣu yojayet |
tasmin na tiṣṭhate pāpam abbindur iva puṣkare ||
[103]                japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyed brahmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kuryād anyaṃ na vā kuryān maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate ||
upāṃśu syāc chataguṇaḥ sāhasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
noccair japyaṃ budhaḥ kuryāt sāvitryās tu viśeṣataḥ ||
sāvitrījapyanirataḥ svargam āpnoti mānavaḥ |
sāvitrījapyanirato mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena snātaḥ prayatamānasaḥ |
gāyatrīṃ tu japed bhaktyā sarvakalmaṣanāśinīm ||
“vṛṣī” svalpamāsanam | “indrākṣaḥ” ārukam | “hastopayāmaḥ” aṅgulīnamanam | “ādau” japārambhe | “hṛdaye śuciḥ” śuddhamanāḥ | “mokṣopāyaṃ” tattvajñānam |
yogiyājñavalkyo manu-yama-vasiṣṭha-hārītāś cādyaślokadvaye |
ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
japyenaiva tu saṃsiddho brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kuryād anyaṃ na vā kuryān maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate ||
na caṃkraman na ca hasan na pārśvam avalokayan |
nāpāśrito na jalpaṃś ca cāvṛttaśirās tathā ||
na padā padam ākramya na caiva hi tathā karau |
[104]        na cāsamāhitamanā na ca saṃśrāvayan japet ||
pracchannāni ca dānāni jñānaṃ ca nirahaṃkṛtam |
japyāni ca suguptāni teṣāṃ phalam anantakam ||
upāṃśujapayuktasya śaṃsyāc chataguṇo bhavet |
sāhasro mānasaḥ prokto yasmād dhyānasamo hi saḥ ||
oṣṭhaspandanamātreṇa yatopāṃśu tad adhvani |
kṛtvā jihvāṃ nirvikalpāṃ cintayet tad dhi mānasam ||
tiṣṭhaṃś ced vīkṣamāṇo ‘rkaṃ japaṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ |
anyathā prāṅmukhaḥ kuryād vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa tu ||
prākkūleṣu kuśeṣv evam āsīnaś cāsane śubhe |
nātyucchrite nātinīce darbhapāṇiḥ susaṃyutaḥ ||
sphaṭikendrākṣarudrākṣaiḥ putrajīvasamudbhavaiḥ |
akṣamālā tu kartavyā uttamā hy uttarottarā ||
koṭyādikā bhaved vṛddhir akṣamālā viśeṣataḥ |
japasya kriyamāṇasya tasmāc chreṣṭā parā parā ||
abhāve tv akṣamālāyāḥ kuśagranthyāgrapāṇinā |
japa eva hi kartavya ekāgramanasaiva tu ||
dyāyeta manasā mantraṃ jihvauṣṭhau na tu cālayet |
na kampayec chiro grīvāṃ dantān naiva prakāśayet ||
[105]        yakṣarākṣasabhūtāni siddhavidyādharoragāḥ |
haranti prasabhaṃ yasmāt tasmād guptaṃ samācaret ||
jalānte cāgnyagāre vā jale devālaye ‘pi vā |
gavāṃ goṣṭhe puṇyatīrthe siddhakṣetre ‘tha vā gṛhe ||
gṛhe hy ekaguṇaṃ japyaṃ nadyāṃ tu dviguṇaṃ smṛtam |
gavāṃ goṣṭhe daśaguṇam agnyāgāre daśādhikam ||
siddhakṣetreṣu tīrtheṣu devatāyāś ca saṃnidhau |
sahasraṃ śatakoṭīnām anantaṃ viṣṇusaṃnidhau ||
śākayāvakabhaikṣyāṇi payo mūlaphalāni ca |
dadhi sarpis tathā hy āpaḥ praśastā hy uttarottaram ||
caravo hy upavāsaś ca bhaikṣyaṃ naktam ayācitam |
bisaśṛṅgāṭaśālūkahaviṣyānnāni yāni tu ||
etāny anuvratāny āhuḥ śastāni japakarmaṇi |
japakāle na bhāṣeta vratahomādikeṣu ca ||
eteṣv evāvasaktaṃ tu yady āgacched dvijottamaḥ |
abhivādya tato vipraṃ yogakṣemaṃ tu kīrtayet ||
strīśūdrapatitāṃś caiva rāsabhaṃ ca rajasvalām |
japakāle na bhāṣeta vratahomādikeṣu ca ||
tūṣṇīm āsīta tu japaṃś caṇḍālapatirādikān |
dṛṣṭvā tān vāry upaspṛśyābhāṣya snātvā punar japet ||
[106]        ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
saurān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ ||
raudramaitrāsurān mantrān rākṣasān ābhicārikān |
vyāhṛtyālabhya cātmānam apaḥ spṛṣṭvānyad ācaret ||
etān vyāhṛtya raudrādīn aspṛṣṭvātha jalaṃ dvijaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ yat kurute karma tad bhavaty ayathāyatham ||
yadi vāgyamalopaḥ syāj japādiṣu kathaṃcana |
vyāhared vaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ smared vā viṣṇum avyayam ||
“pākayajñāḥ” brahmayajñavyatiriktā devayajñādayaḥ | “vidhiyajñāḥ” jyotiṣṭomādayaḥ | mitram eva “mitraḥ”, sarveṣāṃ bhūtānām api śeṣaḥ | sarvabhūtamitratvaṃ ca hiṃsāṅgakratu-vyatiriktāhiṃsāṅgakajapayajñānuṣṭhānena | “caṃkraman” itas tatas calan | “apāśritaḥ” kuḍyādāv iti śeṣaḥ | “karau” padākramyeti śeṣaḥ | “śaṃsyāt” uccaiḥ paṭhanīyād “upāṃśujapayuktasya” yo japaḥ saḥ “śataguṇo bhaved” ity arthaḥ | koṭyādiśabdenārbudādisaṃkhyāparigrahaḥ | “prasabhaṃ” haṭhāt | “caravo” annashālīpākaviśeṣāḥ | “bisaḥ” mṛṇālaḥ | “haviṣyānnāni” yavaṣāṣṭikādīni | “anuvratāni” japayajñavratasyāṅgabhūtāni | “eteṣu” japahomādiṣu | “avasaktaṃ” [107] vyāpṛtam | “tān ābhāṣya” iti saṃbandhaḥ | “ayathāyatham” niṣphalam |
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.78–82] |
trividho japayajñaḥ syāt tasya bhedaṃ nibodhata |
vācikaś ca upāṃśuś ca mānasaś ca tridhā smṛtaḥ ||
trayāṇāṃ japayajñānāṃ śreyān syād uttarottaraḥ |
yad uccanīcasvaritaiḥ spaṣṭaśabdavad akṣaraiḥ ||
mantram uccārayed vyaktaṃ japayajñaḥ sa vācikaḥ |
śanair uccārayen mantram īṣad oṣṭhau pracālayet ||
kiṃcic chabdaṃ svayaṃ vidyād upāṃśu sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |
dhiyā yad akṣaraśreṇyo varṇād varṇaṃ padāt padam ||
śabdārthacintanābhyāsaḥ sa ukto mānaso japaḥ |
“kiṃcic chabdaṃ svayaṃ vidyād” iti, yathā taṃ dhvanim anyo na jānīyād ity arthaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sāvitryā mānasena ca svādhyāyena japo vyākhyāto brahmayajñaś ca |
“japaḥ” itaramantrajapaḥ | yathā gāyatryāḥ svādhyāye mānase itikartavyatāphalatvam, tathā jape brahmayajñe cety arthaḥ |
[108] brahmayajñādhikāre āpastambaḥ [1.11.18–20] |
tasya vidhiḥ | akṛtaprātarāśa udakāntaṃ gatvā prayataḥ śucau deśe ‘dhīyīta yathāsvādhyāyam utsṛjan vācā | manasā cānadhyāye |
“yathāsvādhyāyam utsṛjann” iti, yad adhītaṃ tat tyajan vācādhīyīta | anadhyāye punar manasādhīyīta |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [26.14] |
yathāgnir vāyunā dhūto haviṣā caiva dīpyate |
evaṃ japyaparo nityaṃ mantrayuktaḥ sadā dvijaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
yamayitvendriyagrāmaṃ kāmakrodhāv ubhāv api |
brahmacārī ca jāpī ca brahmalokam avāpnuyāt ||
laghuhārītaḥ |
jape home tathā dāne svādhyāye pitṛtarpaṇe |
aśūnyau tu karau kuryāt suvarṇarajataiḥ kuśaiḥ ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
na klinnavāsāḥ sthalago japādīn ācared budhaḥ |
[109]        vastraniścyotanaṃ pretāḥ parivārya pibanti hi ||
vratād ṛte nārdravāsā naikavāsāḥ samācaret |
na jīrṇena na nīlena parikliṣṭena vā japet |
“parivārya” veṣṭayitvā | “parikliṣṭena” malinena |
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.77, 85, 87–90] |
darbheṣu darbhapāṇiḥ san prāṅmukhaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
prāṇāyāmāṃs tu kurvīta yathāśāstram atandritaḥ ||
japed ahar ahaḥ snātvā sāvitrīṃ japavid dvijaḥ |
atha puṣpāñjaliṃ datvā bhānave cordhvabāhukaḥ ||
ud u tyaṃ ca japen mantraṃ citraṃ tac cakṣur ity api |
pradakṣiṇam upāvṛtya namaskṛtya tataḥ prabhum ||
pitrarṣigaṇadevādīn adbhiḥ saṃtarpayet tataḥ |
devān devagaṇāṃś caiva munīn munigaṇān api ||
pitṝn pitṛgaṇāṃś caiva nityaṃ saṃtarpayet tataḥ |
snātavastraṃ tataḥ pīḍya punar ācamanaṃ varet ||
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
śrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakalpakāṇḍe japavidhiḥ ||
[110]
4
atha tarpaṇavidhiḥ
tatra viṣṇuḥ [64.24–25] |
snātaś cārdravāsā devapitṛtarpaṇam ambhaḥstha eva kuryāt | parivartitavāsāś cet tīrthatīram uttīrya |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.100] |
snātvā devān pitṝṃś caiva tarpayed arcayet tathā |
“devān” brahmādīn | “arcayet” talliṅgair mantraiḥ puṣpākṣatādibhiḥ | “pitṝṃś ca” śrāddhena |
śātātapaḥ |
tarpaṇaṃ ca śuciḥ kuryāt pratyahaṃ snātako dvijaḥ |
devebhyaś ca ṛṣibhyaś ca pitṛbhyaś ca yathākramam ||
“śuciḥ” mantrasnānādināpi | anena ca pradhānatayā nityatarpaṇaṃ vidhīyate, pratyaham ity abhidhānāt, tarpaṇam upakramya
tasmāt sadaiva kartavyam akurvan sahasainasā |
[111]        yujyate brāhmaṇaḥ kurvan viśvam etad bibharti hi ||
ityādichandogapariśiṣṭavacanāc ca [2.23] |
viṣṇuḥ [64.27–35] |
snātvā ca vidhivad upaspṛśet | puruṣasūktena pratyṛcaṃ puruṣāya puṣpāñjaliṃ dadyāt | udakāñjaliṃ cādāv eva | divyena tīrthena devānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt | pitryeṇa pitṛtarpaṇam | ādau svavaṃśyānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ saṃbandhibāndhavānām | tataḥ suhṛdām | evaṃ nityasnāyī syāt |
“svavaṃśyāḥ: mātāpitṛvaṃśajāḥ pitrādayo mātāmahāś ca |
japāntaraṃ chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.18–23] |
yavādbhis tarpayed devān satilādbhiḥ pitṝn api |
nāmānte tarpayāmīti ādāv om iti hi bruvan ||
brahmāṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ prajāpatiṃ devāṃś chandāṃsi vedān ṛṣīn purāṇān ācāryān gandharvān itarācāryān | saṃvatsaraṃ sāvayavaṃ devīr apsaraso devānugān nāgān sāgarān parvatān sarito divyān manuṣyān yakṣān rakṣāṃsi suparṇān piśācān pṛthivīm oṣadhīḥ paśūn vanaspatīn bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham ity upavītī | atha prācīnāvītī yamaṃ yamapuruṣaṃ kavyavāhamanalaṃ somam aryamaṇam agniṣvāttān somapān barhiṣado ‘tha svān [112] pitṝn mātāmahān iti pratipuruṣam abhyaset | jyeṣṭhabhrātṛśvaśurapitṛvyamātulāṃś ca mātāpitṛvaṃśyā ye cānye matta udakam arhanti tāṃs tarpayāmīty ayam avasānāñjaliḥ | atha ślokaḥ:
chāyāṃ yethecchec charadātapārtaḥ
payaḥ pipāsuḥ kṣudhito ‘ttum annam |
bālo janitrīṃ jananī ca bālaṃ
yoṣitpumāṃsaṃ puruṣaś ca yoṣām ||
tathā bhūtāni sarvāṇi sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca |
viprād udakam icchanti sarve hy udakakāṅkṣiṇaḥ ||
tasmāt sadaiva kartavyam akurvan mahatainasā |
yujyate brāhmaṇaḥ kurvan viśvam etad bibharti hi ||
“purāṇān” ity ācāryaviśeṣaṇam | “pratipuruṣam abhyased” ity anena pitrādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ mātāmahānāṃ ca trayāṇām udakāñjalir āvartata ity uktam |
japānantaraṃ yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
tataḥ saṃtarpayed devān ṛṣīn devagaṇāṃs tathā |
brahmāṇaṃ tarpayet pūrvaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ prajāpatim ||
devāṃś chandāṃsi vedāṃś ca ṛṣīṃś caiva tapodhanān |
ācāryāṃś caiva gandharvān ācāryān itarāṃs tathā ||
saṃvatsaraṃ sāvayavaṃ devīś cāpsarasas tathā |
[113]        tathā devānugān nāgān sāgarān parvatāṃs tathā ||
sarito ‘tha manuṣyāṃś ca yakṣarakṣāṃsi caiva hi |
piśācāṃś ca suparṇāṃś ca bhūtāny atha paśūṃs tathā ||
vanaspatīṃś cauṣadhīś ca bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham |
anvārabdhena savyena pāṇinā dakṣiṇena tu ||
tṛpyatām iti sektavyaṃ nāmnā tu praṇavādinā |
āvāhya pūrvavan mantrair āstīrya ca kuśān śubhān ||
prāgagreṣu surān samyag dakṣiṇāgreṣu vai pitṝn |
savyaṃ jānuṃ tato ‘nvācya pāṇibhyāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
talliṅgais tarpayen mantraiḥ sarvān pitṛgaṇāṃs tathā |
mātāmahāṃś ca satataṃ śraddhayā tarpayed budhaḥ ||
prācīnāvīty udakaṃ tu prasiñced vai tilānvitam |
