yady uddhṛtaṃ prasiñcet tu tilān saṃmiśrayej jale ||
ato ‘nyathā tu savyena tilā grāhyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
dakṣiṇe pitṛtīrthena jalaṃ siñced yathāvidhi ||
dakṣiṇenaiva gṛhṇīyāt pitṛtīrthasamīpataḥ |
tilānām apy alābhe tu suvarṇarajatānvitam ||
tadabhāve niṣiñcet tu darbhair mantreṇa vāpy atha |
kavyavāḍ analaṃ somaṃ yamam aryamaṇaṃ tathā ||
agniṣvāttān somapāṃś ca tathā barhiṣadaḥ pitṝn |
[114]        yadi syāj jīvapitṛka etān divyān pitṝṃs tathā ||
yebhyo vāpi pitā dadyāt tebhyo vāpi pradāpayet |
etāṃś ca vakṣyamāṇāṃś ca pramītapiṛko dvijaḥ ||
vasūn rudrāṃs tathādityān namaskārasvadhānvitān |
ete sarvasya pitara eṣv āyattāś ca mānavāḥ ||
ācāryāṃś ca pitṝṇ svāṃśca pitṛprabhṛti nāmataḥ |
mantraiś ca deyam udakaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītivardhanaiḥ ||
udīratām aṅgirasa āyantv ity ūrjam ity api |
pitṛbhya iti ye ceha madhuvātā iti tryṛcam ||
pitṝn dhyāyan prasiñcan vai japen mantrān yathākramam |
tṛpyadhvam iti ca trir vai tataḥ pāñjalir ānataḥ ||
namo va iti japtvā vai tato mātāmahān sakhīn |
tarpayed ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ dharmaṃ paramam āsthitaḥ ||
mātā mātṛṣvasā caiva mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
duhitā ca svasā caiva śiṣyartvigjñātibāndhavāḥ ||
nāmatas tu svadhākārais tarpyāḥ syur anupūrvaśaḥ |
[115]        savarṇebhyo jalaṃ dadyān nānyavarṇe kadācana ||
pūrvaṃ svapitaras tarpyāḥ paścād anyāṃś ca tarpayet |
nāstikyabhāvād yaś cāpi na tarpayati vai sutaḥ ||
pibanti dehaniḥsrāvaṃ pitaro ‘sya jalārthinaḥ |
“pūrvavan mantraiḥ” pūrvaṃ yājñavalkyasaṃhitāyāṃ śrāddhaprakaraṇe uktaiḥ “viśvedevāsa āgata” “uśantas tvā” ityādimantraiḥ | “kavyavād analaḥ” ekā devatā | “yebhyo vāpi” iti, pituḥ pitrādibhyaḥ | “etān divyapitṛṇ” kavyavād analādīn vakṣyamāṇān vasvādīn pitṝṃś ca | “namaskārasvadhānvitān” tarpayed iti śeṣaḥ | atra ca
devān brahmaṛṣīn sarvān tarpayetākṣatodakaiḥ |
iti padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭtikhaṇḍam 20.165] abhidānād devarṣimanuṣyatarpaṇe yavā niyatāḥ | pitṛtarpaṇe tu yogiyājñavalkyena snānaprakaraṇe “mṛttikān gomayam” iti tilānāṃ nityavad aṅgatvābhidhānāt “tilodakam” iti ca tatrābhidhānāt tilā niyatāḥ,
tilānām apy abhāve tu suvarṇarajatānvitam |
[116] ity abhidhānāt | tilābhāve ‘pi pitṛtarpaṇaṃ bhavaty eva | pitrādidevatoddeśena jalatyāgarūpaṃ cedaṃ tarpaṇākhyaṃ karma na prasekamātram, dadyād ity abhidhānāt, pitrādīnāṃ tatra tatra tṛptiśravaṇāt | atra ca devatarpaṇe īdrśaṃ vākyam — “oṃ brahmā tṛpyatām | oṃ viṣṇus tṛpyatām” ity evaṃprakāraṃ pratidaivataṃ kartavyam | manuṣyatarpaṇe tu — “oM vasavas tṛpyatām idaṃ jalaṃ tebhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ” ity evaṃrūpaṃ vākyam | pitṛtarpaṇe tu ā– “oṃ amukagotro ‘smatpitāmukaśarmā tṛpyatām idaṃ jalaṃ tasmai svadhā namaḥ” ity evaṃrūpaṃ vakyaṃ kartavyam |
padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭikhaṇḍam, 20.151–170] |
brahmāṇaṃ tarpayet pūrvaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ prajāpatim |
devā yakṣās tathā nāgā gandharvāpsaraso ‘surāḥ ||
krūrāḥ sarpāḥ suparṇāś ca taravo jambhakāḥ khagāḥ |
vidyādharā jaladharās tathaivākāśagāminaḥ ||
nirādhārāś ca ye jīvāḥ pāpe dharme ratāś ca ye |
teṣām āpyāyanāyaitad dīyate salilaṃ mayā ||
kṛtopavītī devebhyo nivītī ca bhavet tataḥ |
[117]        manuṣyāṃs tarpayed bhaktyā ṛṣiputrān ṛṣīṃs tathā ||
sanakaś ca sanandaś ca tṛtīyaś ca sanātanaḥ |
kapilaś cāsuriś caiva voḍhuḥ pañcaśikhas tathā ||
sarve te tṛptim āyāntu maddattenāmbunā sadā |
marīcim atryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum ||
pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradam eva ca |
devān brahmaṛṣīn sarvāṃs tarpayed akṣatodakaiḥ ||
apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā savyaṃ jānv ācya bhūtale |
agniṣvāttāṃs tathā saumyān haviṣmantas tathoṣmapān ||
sukālino barhiṣadas tathā caivājyapān punaḥ |
tarpayec ca pitṝn bhaktyā satilodakacandanaiḥ ||
darbhapāṇis tu vidhinā pretāṃs tāṃs tarpayet tataḥ |
pitrādīn nāmagotreṇa tathā mātāmahān api ||
saṃtarpya bhaktyā vidhivad imaṃ mantram udīrayet |
“ye ‘bāndhavā bāndhavā vā ye ‘nyajanmani bāndhavāḥ ||
te tṛptim atulāṃ yāntu yaś cāsmatto ‘bhivāñchati” |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.27–36] |
śucivastradharaḥ snāto devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
[118]        teṣām eva hi tīrthena kurvīta susamāhitaḥ ||
trir apaḥ prīṇanārthāya devānām apavarjayet |
ṛṣīṇāṃ ca yathānyāyaṃsakṛccāpi prajāpateḥ ||
pitṝṇāṃ prīṇanārthāya trir apaḥ pṛthivīpate |
pitāmahebhyaś ca tathā prīṇayet prapitāmahān ||
mātāmahāya tatpitre tatpitre ca samāhitaḥ |
dadyāt pitryeṇa tīrthena kāmyaṃ cānyac chṛṇuṣva me ||
mātre pramātre tatmātre gurupatnyai tathaiva ca |
gurave mātulādīnāṃ snigdhamitrāya bhūbhuje ||
idaṃ cāpi japed ambu dadyād ātmecchayā nṛpa |
upakārāya bhūtānāṃ kṛtadevāditarpaṇaḥ ||
devāsurās tathā yakṣā nāgā gandharvarākṣasāḥ |
piśācā guhyakāḥ siddhāḥ kūṣmāṇḍās taravaḥ khagāḥ ||
jalecarā bhūnilayā vāyvādārāś ca jantavaḥ |
prītim ete prayāntv āśu maddattenāmbunākhilāḥ ||
narakeṣu samasteṣu yātanāsu ca ye sthitāḥ |
teṣām āpyāyanāyaitad dīyate salilaṃ mayā ||
ye ‘bāndhavā bāndhavā vā ya ‘nyajanmani bāndhavāḥ |
te tṛptim akhilā yāntu yaś cāsmatto ‘bhivāñchati ||
“trir apaḥ prīṇanārthāya” iti, etasmād vacanād devānām ṛṣīṇāṃ pratyekam añjalitrayadānam | prajāpateś ca tanmadhya eka evāñjaliḥ | pitrādīnāṃ tu vṛddhapramātāmahāntānām añjalitrayaṃ [119] pratyekam | mātrāditarpaṇe kāmyatvābhidhānaṃ phalaviśeṣakathanārtham, nityatā tu smṛtyantarānusārāt |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.8.15–2.10.4] |
pūtaḥ pañcabhir brahmayajñair adbhir evāpsu yathottaraṃ devatās tarpayati | agniḥ prajāpatiḥ somo rudro ‘ditir bṛhaspatiḥ sarpā ity etāni prāgdvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇi sāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | vasūṃś ca tarpayāmi | pitaro ‘ryamā bhagaḥ savitā tvaṣṭā vāyur indrāgnī ity etāni dakṣiṇadvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇi sāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | rudrāṃś ca tarpayāmi | mitra indro mahāpitara āpo viśvedevā brahmā viṣṇur ityetāni pratyagdvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇi sāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | ādityāṃś ca tarpayāmi | varuṇo vasavo ‘ja ekapād ahibudhnyaḥ pūṣāśvinau yama ity etāny udagdvārāṇi daivatāni sanakṣatrāṇi sagrahāṇi sāhorātrāṇi samuhūrtāni tarpayāmi | viṣvān devāṃs tarpayāmi | sādhyāṃś ca tarpayāmi | brahmāṇaṃ tarpayāmi | prajāpatiṃ tarpayāmi | [caturmukhaṃ tarpayāmi | parameṣṭhinaṃ tarpayāmi |] hiraṇyagarbhaṃ tarpayāmi | svayaṃbhuvaṃ tarpayāmi | brahmapārṣadāṃs tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūḥ puruṣaṃ [120] tarpayāmi | oṃ bhuvaḥ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmi | oṃ suvaḥ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūs tarpayāmi | oṃ bhuvas tarpayāmi | oṃ suvas tarpayāmi | oṃ mahas tarpayāmi | oṃ janas tarpayāmi | oṃ namas tarpayāmi | sakhīn svadhā namas tarpayāmi | sakhipatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | jñātīn svadhā namas tarpayāmi | jñātipatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | amātyān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | amātyapatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | sarvān svadhā namas tarpayāmi | sarvapatnīḥ svadhā namas tarpayāmi | anutīrtham apa utsiñcati “ūrjaṃ vahantīr amṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ kīlālaṃ parisrutaṃ svadhā stha tarpayat me pitṝn | tṛpyata tṛpyata tṛpyata” iti | nārdravāsā naikavastro daivāni karmāṇy anusaṃcaret | pitṛkarmāṇi cety ekeṣām |
“pañcabhir brahmayajñaiḥ” praṇavavyāhṛtitrayagāyatrījapātmakaiḥ, baudhāyanenaiva “praṇavo vyāhṛtayaḥ sāvitrī ca” [2.7.14] iti pūrvam abhidhānāt | “yathottaraṃ” yā yā devatā brahmādikā tāṃ tāṃ tenaiva krameṇety arthaḥ | “anutīrthaṃ” pitrāditīrthaṃ lakṣyīkṛtya | “ekeṣām” iti vacanāj jalasthatarpaṇe ārdravastratāpy abhimatā | tarpaṇajalaprakṣepaś ca jalasthena sthalasthena ca puruṣeṇa tarpaṇe kriyamāṇe jalamadhye kartavyaḥ | na tu sthalasthena [124] sthala eveti niyamaḥ | sthalasthatarpaṇa eva baudhāyanena “pūtaḥ pañcabhir brahmayajñair adbhir evāpsu yathottaraṃ devatās tarpayati” [2.8.15] ity apām eva tarpaṇajalaprakṣepādhikaraṇatvenābhi-dhānāt | praṇavavyāhṛtitrayasāvitrīgāyatrīrūpāṇāṃ pañcabrahmayajñānāṃ vastraparidhānottara-kālam abhidhānāt | yat tu “vāsaḥ pīḍayitvā” [2.8.12] ity uktvā tadabhidhānaṃ tad baudhāyanīyānāṃ tarpaṇāt prāvyaṃ snānāṅgam iti na tato ‘psv ity etasya jalasthatarpaṇaviṣayatā | yat tu “nārdravāsaḥ” [2.10.4] ityādi tarpaṇānte baudhāyanīyaṃ vacanaṃ tena sthalatarpaṇam eva draḍhīkṛtam |
śaṅkhaḥ [13.1–15] |
snātaḥ kṛtajapyo ‘ntarjānur udaṅmukhaś ca divyena tīrthena devān udakena tarpayet | atha tarpaṇavidhiḥ | oṃ bhagavantaṃ śeṣaṃ tarpayāmi | kālāgnirudraṃ santānavarāham ajinaṃ rukmabhaumaṃ śailabhaumaṃ lalāṭabhaumaṃ raktabhaumaṃ śvetabhaumaṃ kṛṣṇabhaumam iti pātālasaptakam | jambūdvīpaṃ śākadvīpaṃ kuśadvīpaṃ krauñcadvīpaṃ śālmalidvīpaṃ plakṣadvīpaṃ duṣkaradvīpam iti dvīpasaptakam | lokālokākhyaṃ parvataṃ sudhānāmānaṃ śaṅkhapadaṃ [125] ketumantaṃ hiraṇyaromāṇām iti kalpasthāyino lokapālān | lavaṇodakaṃ kṣīrodakaṃ ghṛtodakaṃ dadhyudakaṃ surodakam ikṣurasodakaṃ svādūdakam iti samudrasaptakaṃ sāgaracatuṣkam | śṛṅgavantaṃ śvetaṃ nīlaṃ meruṃ mālyavantaṃ gandhamādanaṃ niṣadhaṃ hemakūṭaṃ himavantam iti mahāparvatān | mahendraṃ malayaṃ sahyaṃ śuktimantam ṛkṣavantaṃ vindhyaṃ pāriyātram iti sapta kulavarvatān | kailāsamainākapramukhāṃś ca saptaparvatān | bindusaraḥpramukhāni sarāṃsi | saptapravāhām gaṅgām | trilokavāhinīṃ gaṅgām | sindhum | sarasvatīm | yamunām | prathamapuṣkaram | dvitīyapuṣkaram | tṛtīyapuṣkaram | prayāgam | naimiṣam | gayāśīrṣam | sarvatīrthāni | sarvaprasravaṇāni | sarvāḥ saritaḥ | indratīrtham | aśvatthapramukhān vanaspatīn | yavapramukhā oṣadhīḥ | mānasottarākhyaṃ parvatam | lokapālān | indram | śacīm | vajram | airāvatam | mālatim | citrasenapramukhān gandharvāpsarasaḥ | mudo nāmāpsarasaḥ | paśuno nāmāpsarasaḥ | āyuṣo nāmāpsarasaḥ | sudhā nāmāpsarasaḥ | pṛṣṭamo nāmāpsarasaḥ | ūrjā nāmāpsarasaḥ | agnisantām | svāhām | agnīdham | yamam | dharmam | śriyam | satyam | tapaḥ | saṃyajñam | dakṣiṇām | dīkṣām | brahmacaryam | vyavasāyam | [126] | viśākham | skandagrahān | bālagrahān | skandapārṣadān | rudrapārṣadān | bhūtāni | bhaumān | rudrān | antarikṣān | rudrān | vidyārudrān | sarvagatān rudrān | mātṝḥ | yogeśvarīm | devapatnīḥ | devamātṛgaṇān | dhanādhipān | vināyakam | mitam | saṃmitam | śālakaṭaṅkaṭau | kūṣmāṇḍam | rājaputrān | dharmam | artham | kāmam | gatim | [127] nidrām | kṣudham | aditiḥ | āyatim | niyatim | kīrtim | | bhāratam | manum | viṣṇum | [128] yamam | aṅgirasam | vasiṣṭham | nāradam | dakṣam | saṃvartam | śātātapam | parāśaram | āpastambam | auśanasam | kṛṣṇadvaipāyanam | kātyāyanam | bṛhaspatim | devānucarān | devānugān | oṃ ekajyotiṣam | dvijyotiṣam | trijyotiṣam | caturjyotiṣam | ekaśakram | dviśakram | triśakram | indram | āpodṛśam | nabhaḥ | praviśakyam | sitam | saṃmitam | ṛtujitam | pratisadṛkṣaṃ | amṛtāśinam | prītinam | yugam | vṛkṣam | sarasamatim | dhanvantarim | ugram | dhunim | bhīmam | atiyuktam | ṛkṣapādam | saham | dyutim | vāyum | adhṛṣyam | vāsam | kāsam | jayam | vijayam | ity ekonapañcāśanmarutaḥ | bhuvanam | bhāvanam | parjanyam | svajanyam | kratum | vasum | mūrdhānam | vājam | vājastavam | prasavam | āpyāyanam | ṛkṣaṃ | iti dvādaśa bhṛgūn | manum | manvantaram | prāṇam | apānam | citim | chayam | mayam | haṃsam | naram | nārāyaṇam | vibhum | prabhum | iti dvādaśa sādhyān || savitāram | dhātāram | mitram | aryamaṇam | pūṣaṇam | aṃśum | tvaṣṭāram | vivasvantam | indram | viṣṇum | varuṇam | bhagam | iti dvādaśāsityān || pāvakam | [pinākinam | mṛgavyādham | sarpam |] sūryam | nirṛtim | haram | ajaikapādam | ahirbudhnyam | dhūmaketum | kapālinam | ity ekādaśa rudrān || ātmānam | ajamanam | ṛkṣaṃ | damam | [130] sadam | prāṇam | haviṣmantam | gariṣṭham | ṛtam | satyam | iti daśāṅgirsaḥ || kratum | dakṣam | vasum | satyam | kālam | kāmam | dhurim | rocanam | mārdravam | purūravasam | iti daśa viśvān devān || dharam | dhruvam | somam | āpam | analam | anilam | pratyūṣam | prabhāsam | ity aṣṭau vasūn || nāsatyaṃ dasram ity aśvinau | etān devagaṇān sānucarān | svāyaṃbhuvam | sāvitrīm | sarvān devān | satīm | devīm | lakṣmīm | dharām | aniruddham | pradumnam | saṃkarṣaṇam | vāsudevam | bhūlokam | bhuvarlokam | svarlokam | maharlokam | janalokam | tapolokam | satyalokam | brahmāṇḍam | pṛthivīm | apaḥ | vahnim | vāyum | ākāśam | manaḥ | śuddhim | āsanam | avyaktaṃ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmīti | idaṃ cānte pratyṛcaṃ puruṣasūktenāñjalīn dadyāt puṣpāñjaliṃ ca bhaktyā | atha kṛtāpasavyo dakṣiṇābhimukho ‘ntarjānuḥ pitryeṇa tīrthena pitṝṇāṃ yathāśraddhaṃ yathāprakāśam udakaṃ dadyāt | sauvarṇena pātreṇa rājatenaudumbareṇa khaḍgapātreṇa vā | śaṅkunāpy udakaṃ pitṛtīrthaṃ spṛśan dadyāt | pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya mātre pitāmahyai prapitāmahyai vā [mātāmahāya pramātāmahāya vṛddha[131]pramātāmahāya mātāmahyai pramātāmahyai vṛddhapramātāmahyai] ca | ā saptamāt puruṣāt pitṛpakṣe yāvatāṃ nāma jānīyāt | pitṛpakṣāṇām udakatarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā gurūṇāṃ kuryāt | gurūṇāṃ kṛtvā mātṛpakṣāṇāṃ kuryāt | mātṛpakṣāṇāṃ kṛtvā saṃbandhibāndhavānāṃ kuryāt | teṣāṃ kṛtvā suhṛdāṃ kuryāt | bhavati cātra vacanam |
vinā rūpyasuvarṇena vinā tāmratilena ca |
vinā darbhaiś ca mantraiś ca pitṝṇāṃ nopatiṣṭhate ||
sauvarṇarājatābhyāṃ tu khaḍgenaudumbareṇa vā |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti pitṝṇāṃ tu tilodakam ||
himena saha yad dattaṃ kṣīreṇa madhunātha vā |
tad apy akṣayatāṃ yāti pitṝṇāṃ ca tilodakam ||
“antarjānuḥ” jānunor antaḥkṛtahastaḥ | atra ca yady api “oṃ bhagavantaṃ śeṣaṃ tarpayāmi” iti sarvānte ca “avyaktaṃ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmi” ity uktam, tathāpi kālāgnirudrādiṣv api pratyekaṃ tarpayāmīti saṃbandhanīyam | karmatvena kriyāpekṣatvāt | kriyāyāś ca tarpayāmīty etasyātraiva vidyamānatvāt | idaṃ puruṣasūktena pratyṛcam añjalidānaṃ puṣpadānaṃ ca “avyaktaṃ puruṣaṃ tarpayāmi” iti tarpaṇānte tarpaṇaprayogamadhya eva | “yathāśraddham” jñāyamāneṣv [132] api yeṣu śraddhā bhavati | “yathāprakāśam” yathānāmajñānam | “śaṅkunā” kīlakena prastutadravyāṇām eva | “guravaḥ” atrācāryādayaḥ | suvarṇarajatatāmrakhaḍgānāṃ pratyekaśaḥ sāmastyena vā yathāsaṃbhavam aṅgatvam | kṣīramadhuhimacandanānāṃ guṇaphalatvam |
marīciḥ |
muktahastaṃ tu dātavyaṃ na mudrāṃ tatra darśayet |
vāmahaste tilā grāhyā muktvā hastaṃ tu dakṣiṇam ||
devān pitṝṃs tarpayet tu tṛptais tair mudito vrajet |
anabhyarcya yadā yāti tadā bhavati niṣphalaḥ ||
rājate manasā yāyāt suvarṇe hastanirgatam |
tileṣu ca kṣaṇaṃ gacchet tāmre tu dvimuhūrtataḥ ||
darbhe saptamuhūrtena mantrayuktaṃ tad akṣayam |
yatra yatra hi yo yasya tatra tasyopatiṣṭhate ||
bahugoṣu yathā naṣṭāṃ mātaraṃ labhate sutaḥ |
manasā yasya yad dattaṃ tad dhi tasyopatiṣṭhate ||
“mudrā” tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃyogaviśeṣaḥ | “bhuktvā hastaṃ tu dakṣiṇam” iti, dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ tilarahitaṃ kuryāt | “yatra [133] tatra” iti, “yatra” deśādau, “yaḥ” pitrādiḥ, “yasya” putrādeḥ | “tasyopatiṣṭhate” tadgāmī bhavati | “manasā dattam” manasā kḷptam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pavitrair abhyukṣya vasubhyo brahmaṇe vaiśravaṇāyety udakaṃ ninayet | udīratām iti tribhir mantrais trīn udakāñjalīn ninayet | pitṛbandhubhyo nāma gṛhṇan | ācāryāya mātre mātāmahāya mātulebhyaś ca |
yamaḥ |
sapiṇḍānāṃ ca bandhūnāṃ kṛtvādāv udakakriyām |
suhṛtsaṃbandhivargāṇāṃ tato dadyāj jalāñjalim ||
tathā |
datvā jalāñjalīn sapta kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm |
dharmarājaṃ samuddiśya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
anarkābhydite kāle kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm |
snātaḥ saṃtarpya tu yamaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
yatra kvacana nadyāṃ hi snātvā kṛṣṇacaturdaśīm |
saṃtarpya dharmarājānaṃ mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ ||
[134] manu-śātātapa-yogiyājñavalkyāḥ |
ya evaṃ tarpayaty adbhiḥ pitṝn snātvā dvijottamaḥ |
tenaiva sarvam āpnoti pitṛyajñakriyāphalam ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
snātaḥ saṃtarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā pitṝṇāṃ tu tilāmbhasā |
pitṛyajñam avāpnoti prīṇāti ca tathā pitṝn ||
hārītaḥ |
na sravantīṃ vṛthātikrāmet | evaṃ hy āha |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva kāṅkṣanti saritaṃ prati |
adatte tu nirāśāste pratiyānti yathāgatam ||
atra pitṛgāthāsu yamaḥ
api naḥ sa kule bhūyād yo no dadyāj jalāñjalim |
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ ||
tathaiva viṣṇuḥ |
kule ‘smākaṃ sa jantuḥ syād yo no dadyāj jalāñjalim |
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ ||
uśanā |
na veṣṭitaśirāḥ kṛṣṇakāṣāyavāsasā devapitṛkāryaṃ kuryāt |
[135] yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
vastraniṣpīḍitaṃ toyaṃ snātasyocchiṣṭabhāginaḥ |
bhāgadheyaṃ śrutiṃ prāha tasmān niṣpīḍayet sthale ||
pūrve niṣpīḍanaṃ kecit prāg devapitṛtarpaṇāt |
snānavastrasya necchanti tasmād ūrdhvaṃ nipīḍayet ||
annaprakaravat tasya cāpasavyena pīḍanam |
pīḍayitvā tataḥ paścāj japaṃ kuryāt suvistaram ||
“ucchiṣṭabhāginaḥ” –
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād darbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigatam ajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca |
dāsavargasya tat pitre bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate ||
iti manūktāḥ | tenaitān buddhisthīkṛtya vastraniṣpīḍanodakaṃ dātavyam | “pūrve” ācāryāḥ | “annaprakaraḥ” śrāddhe vikiradānam | “suvistarajapaś” ca, “ācāntaḥ punar ācāmed” ityādiyogiyājñavalkyādyuktapavitravargasya japeti kartavyatāshitasya boddhavyaḥ |
tathā |
nipīḍya snānavastraṃ tu ācamya prayataḥ śuciḥ |
[136]        devānām arcanaṃ kuryād brahmādīnām amatsaraḥ ||
brāhmavaiṣṇavaraudrais tu sāvitrair maitravāruṇaiḥ |
talliṅgair arcayen mantraiḥ sarvān devān samāhitaḥ ||
dhyātvā praṇavapūrvaṃ tu daivataṃ tu samāhitaḥ |
namaskāreṇa puṣpāṇi vinyaset tu pṛthak pṛthak ||
āvāhanādikaṃ karma yat tu noktaṃ mayā tv iha |
tat sarvaṃ praṇavenaiva kartavyaṃ cakrapāṇinaḥ ||
dadyāt puruṣasūktena yaḥ puṣpāṇy apa eva vā |
arcitaṃ syāj jagad idaṃ tena sarvaṃ carācaram ||
viṣṇur brahmā ca rudraś ca viṣṇur devo divākaraḥ |
tasmāt pūjyatamaṃ nānyam ahaṃ manye janārdanāt ||
sāvitryā dadyād dhūpādi yathāśakti samāhitaḥ |
evaṃ saṃpūjya deveśaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhyātvā nirañjanam ||
tato ‘valokayed arkaṃ haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ṛcā |
sa yāti brahmaṇaḥ sadma snātvekṣetānayā tu yaḥ ||
adṛśramasyeti mantrair upāsyātha divākaram |
saṃvarcaseti pāṇibhyāṃ toyena vimṛjen mukham ||
svayaṃbhūr ity upsthāya sūryasyeti pradakṣiṇam |
āvṛtya tu namaskuryād diśo digdevatā api ||
brahmaṇe ‘gnaye pṛthivyai cauṣadhibhyas tathaiva ca |
[137]        vāce ca vācaspate viṣṇave mahate tathā ||
etābhyo devatābhyaś ca namaskārādi vai jalam |
datvā namasyet kramaśas tv antai vai sarvakarmaṇām ||
namo ‘dbhyo namo ‘ppataye varuṇāya namo namaḥ |
ity uktvāpo namaskṛtyāvanau devāṃś ca nāmavat ||
idam āpaḥ pravahatā ghāmno dhāmnas tathaiva ca |
vimocanaṃ tu tīrthasya āpyāyasveti vai japet ||
devā gātu vida iti kṛtvā japyanivedanam |
prakṣālya tīrthadeśaṃ tu gatvā svaṃ dharmam ācaret ||
brahmaviṣṇurudrā ekaikena talliṅgena mantreṇārcanīyāḥ | savitā tu tribhiḥ, “sāvitrair” iti copādeyagatabahutvasaṃkhyānirdeśāt | tasya ca triṣv eva caritārthatvāt | mitrāvaruṇasya tribhis tribhir eva, atrāpi bahuvacananirdeśāt | “talliṅgaiḥ” tadbrahmādidevatāprakāśanasamarthaiḥ | “dhyātvā praṇavam” ityādinā tu  teṣām eva pūjāprakāro ‘bhihitaḥ | viṣṇupūjāyām āvāhanādiḥ oṃkāreṇa, puruṣasūktena ca puṣpadānam udakadānaṃ vā | “dhūpādi” ity ādiśabdena dīpanaivedyavādyānām abhidhānam | "nirañjanam" niṣprapañcaṃ paraṃ brahma | "diśaḥ" prācyādikāḥ | "digdevatāḥ" indrādyāḥ | “avanau” bhūmau | “devān” [138] namaskṛtya | “japyanivedanam” anena japyena paramātmanā prīyatām ity evaṃrūpam | “svaṃ dharmaṃ” svavarṇāśramavihitam |
tathā |
sravantyādiṣv athācamya sopānatko hy asaṃspṛśan |
āgataḥ sodapātras tu yatnena śucir eva saḥ ||
tenodakena dravyāṇi prokṣyācamya punar gṛham |
tataḥ karmāṇi kurvīta nityaṃ vai yāni kāni ca ||
pātrādirahitaṃ toyam uddhṛtaṃ savyapāṇinā |
na tena prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād vastraniṣpīḍanena ca ||
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ mukhyaṃ pātraṃ prakīrtitam |
tadalābhe smṛtaṃ pātraṃ sravate yan na dhāritam ||
“asaṃspṛśan” aśucyādīn iti śeṣaḥ | “prokṣaṇam” gṛhasthitapuṣpanaivedyādidravyāṇām | “yan na dhāritam” iti nārikelādipātram upāttam |
śātātapaḥ |
bahir nadyādiṣv ācāntaḥ sodakaḥ kiṃcid aspṛśan |
rathyāgato ‘pi yatnena śucir eveha mānavaḥ ||
yat pātrarahitaṃ toyaṃ dhṛtaṃ savyena pāṇinā |
na tena prokṣayed dravyaṃ vastraniṣpīḍanena vā ||
[139]        nādhovastraikadeśena śudhyartham apa āharet |
yady ānītaṃ tu savyena prokṣayed dakṣiṇena tu ||
“aspṛśan yatnena” iti saṃbandhaḥ | “dravyaṃ” gṛhasthitaṃ puṣpopahārādi | “mādhovastraikadeśena" itarajalapātrasyāsaṃbhave ‘pi nādhovastrānītena jalena prokṣayed ity arthaḥ |
tarpaṇānantaraṃ viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.39–41] |
ācamya ca tato dadyāt sūryāya salilāñjalim |
namo vivasvate brahman bhāsvate viṣṇutejase ||
jagatsavitre śucaye savitre karmadāyine |
tato gṛhārcanaṃ kuryād abhīṣṭasurapūjanam ||
jalābhiṣekapuṣpāṇāṃ dhūpādīnāṃ nivedanaiḥ |
padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭikhaṇḍam, 20.170–177] |
ācamya vidhivat samyag ālikhet padmam agrataḥ |
akṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhiḥ satilāruṇacandanaiḥ ||
arghyapādye prayatnena sūryanāmānukīrtanaiḥ |
namas te viṣṇurūpāya namas te brahmarūpiṇe ||
sahasraraśmaye nityaṃ namas te sarvatejase |
namas te rudravapuṣe namas te bhaktavatsala ||
padmanābha namas te ‘stu kuṇḍalāṅgadabhūṣita |
namas te sarvalokeśa suptānām api budhyase ||
[140]        sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ caiva sarvaṃ paśyasi sarvadā |
satyadeva namas te ‘stu prasīda mama bhāskara ||
divākara namas te ‘stu prabhākara namo ‘stu te |
evaṃ sūryaṃ namaskṛtya triḥ kṛtvā ca pradakṣiṇam ||
dvijaṃ gāṃ kāñcanaṃ spṛṣṭvā tato viṣṇugṛhaṃ vrajet |
āśrayasthaṃ tataḥ pūjya pratimāṃ cāpi pūjayet ||
brahmayajñānantaram narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.91–93] |
tato ‘rghyaṃ bhānave dadyāt tilapuṣpajalānvitam |
utthāya mūrdhaparyantaṃ haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ṛcā ||
jaladevaṃ namaskṛtya tato gṛhagataḥ punaḥ |
vidhinā puruṣasūktasya tatra viṣṇuṃ samarcayet ||
vaiśvadevaṃ tataḥ kuryād balikarma yathāvidhi |
“puruṣasūktavidhiś” ca purāṇantarād avagantavyaḥ pūjāprakaraṇe |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
śrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakāṇḍe tarpaṇaparva ||
[141]
5
vaiśvadevādipañcayajñavidhiḥ
atha dakṣaḥ [2.56] |
pañcame ca tathā bhāge saṃvibhāgo yathārhataḥ |
devapitṛmanuṣyāṇāṃ kīṭānāṃ copadiśyate ||
“yathārhataḥ” yathāyogyam |
vyāsaḥ |
vaivāhike ‘gnau kurvīta pākayajñān aśeṣataḥ |
āpady api hi kaṣṭāyāṃ pañcayajñān na hāpayet ||
svargāpavargayoḥ prāptiṃ mahāyajñaiḥ pracakṣate |
“vaivāhiko ‘gniḥ” āvasathyaḥ |
gautamaḥ [5.3–6] |
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ | nityasvādhyāyaḥ | pitṛbhyaś codakadānam | yathotsāham anyat |
[142] manuṣyapūjā atithipūjā | “ṛṣipūjā” svādhyāyādhyayanam | ṛṣiyajñapitṛyajñayoḥ pūrvam abhihitayor api “nityasvādhyāyaḥ” ityādinā punar abhidhānam itarayajñatrayānuṣṭhānāsaṃpattāv api kiṃcid aṅgavaikalyenodakadravyeṇāpy ādareṇāvaśyānuṣṭheyatvārtham | etad evoktaṃ “yathotsāham anyad” ity anena |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
śeṣabhuk mahāpañcayajñān ahar ahar nirvaped āpannaśākodakebhyaḥ |
udvāhānantaraṃ saṃvartaḥ |
tato pañcamāhāyajñān kuryād ahar ahar dvijaḥ |
na hāpayet tu tān prājñaḥ śrūyante hi śrutāv api ||
atha tathaiva devalaḥ |
tadanantaram agnīn ādadhīta | gṛhītāgnihotro devapitṛṛṣyatithyabhyāgata-bhṛtyātmapūnanaṃ sucaritrānuṣṭhānaṃ ca kurvīta | atra kavyahavyasvādhyāyair devapitṛṛṣīn pṛthak pṛthak pūjayet | svāhety agnihotrapravṛtti havyaṃ tad devān prasādayati | svadheti paitṛkapravṛtti kavyaṃ tatpitṝn prīṇayati | om ity adhyayanapravṛttiḥ svādhyāyaḥ sa munīṃs toṣayati |
[143] “abhyāgato” atithir anyo ‘pi prāghuṇakaḥ | “sucaritram” sadācāraḥ | “agnihotrapravṛtti” agnihotre pravṛttir yasya | agnohotraśabdo ‘trāgnimātrahomaparaḥ |
jābāliḥ |
ahany ahani kartavyaṃ pitṛdaivatapūjanam |
hanteti hantakāraṃ ca manuṣyebhyo hi pāvanam ||
manuḥ [3.68–69] |
pañcasūnā gṛhasthasya cullī peṣṇam upaskaraḥ |
kaṇḍanī codakumbhaś ca vadhyante yās tu vāhayan ||
āsāṃ krameṇa sarvāsāṃ niṣkṛtyarthaṃ maharṣibhiḥ |
pañca kḷptā mahāyajñāḥ pratyahaṃ gṛhamedhinām ||
“sūnā” prāṇivadhasthānam | “upaskaraḥ” gṛhopakaraṇabhāṇḍakuṇḍādiḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
atha sūnā vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | jaṅgamasthāvarādīn prāṇinaḥ sūdayantīti sūnāḥ | etāḥ pañcavidhā bhavanti | drutāvataraṇāvagāhanavikṣobhaṇavikṣepaṇāpūtagrahaṇa-yānādibhir ādyāṃ kurvanti | avelāvispaṣṭadrutagamanākramaṇādibhir dvitīyām | [144] āhananagrahaṇabandhanacchedanabhedanakuṭṭanotpāṭanādibhis tṛtīyām | ākramaṇagharṣaṇapeṣaṇādibhir caturthīm | ādīpanatāpanasvedanabharjana-pacanādibhiḥ pañcamīm | tad etāḥ pañcabhūtā nirayayonīr ahar ahaḥ prajāḥ kurvanti | agniguruśuśrūṣāsvādhyāyair āditaḥ sūnātrayaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ pāvayanti | pañca pañcabhiḥ pākayajñair gṛhasthavānaprasthāḥ pāvayanti | pavitrajñānadhyānair bhikṣavaḥ sūnādvayaṃ pāvayanti | aniṣkriyā dantasūnāyām |
“sūdayanti” prāṇair viyojayanti | “dturāvataraṇam” śīghraṃ jalapraveśanam | “vikṣobhaṇam” āloḍanam | “vikṣepaṇam” itas tato jalaprakṣepaḥ | apūtagrahaṇaṃ vastrādināṃ kṛmikīṭādinivāraṇam akṛtvā jalagrahaṇam | “ādyām” prathamām | “avelāgamanam” andhakārādau gamanam | “avispaṣṭaḥ” unmārgādiḥ | “drutaṃ” śīghram | “ākramaṇaṃ” pādādinādhiṣṭhānam | “ākramaṇaṃ” sasyādīnāṃ balivardādinā | “ādīpanaṃ” kāṣṭhādīnām | “tāpanaṃ” toyādeḥ | “svedanam” utsedanaṃ dhānyādeḥ | “bharjanaṃ” yavādīnām | “pacanaṃ” taṇḍulādīnām | bhikṣūṇāṃ sūnādvayam ādyam, vṛkṣādicchedanādīnām asaṃbhavāt | “aniṣkriyā” iti, anantaroktapañcayajñānuṣṭhāne dantasūnājanitaduritasya na niṣkriyā ity uktam | “dantasūnā” dantaiś carvaṇe kriyamāṇe yā bhavati |
[145] chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.25–29] |
pañcānām atha satrāṇāṃ mahatām ucyate vidhiḥ |
yair iṣṭvā satataṃ vipraḥ prāpnuyāt sadma śāśvatam ||
devabhūtapitṛbrahmamanuṣyāṇām anukramāt |
mahāsatrāṇi jānīyāt ta eveha mahāmakhāḥ ||
adhyāpanaṃ brahmayajñaḥ pitṛyajñas tu tarpaṇam |
homo daivo balir bhauto nṛyajño ‘tithipūjanam ||
śrāddhaṃ vā pitṛyajñaḥ syāt pitryo balir athāpi vā |
yaś ca śrutijapaḥ prokto brahmayajñaḥ sa ucyate ||
sa cārvāk tarpaṇāt kāryaḥ paścād vā prātar āhuteḥ |
vaiśvadevāvasāne vā nānyatra tu nimittakāt ||
tarpaṇaśrāddhapitṛyajñabalidānānāṃ pitṛyajñatvaṃ yathāsaṃbhavam adhikāribhedena vyavasthitaṃ na punas tulyavikalpaḥ | nimittam eva nimittakam iti “nimittakād” antaroktād brahmayajñakālatrayād anyatra brahmayajñaṃ na kuryād ity arthaḥ |
manur yama-viṣṇu-devalāś ca tṛtīye |
adhyāpanaṃ brahmayajñaḥ pitṛyajñas tu tarpaṇam |
homo daivo balir bhauto nṛyajño ‘tithipūjanam ||
pañcaitān yo mahāyajñān na hāpayati śaktitaḥ |
sa gṛhe ‘pi vasan nityaṃ sūnādoṣair na lipyate ||
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca yaḥ |
[146]        na nirvapati pañcānām ucchvasan na sa jīvati ||
ahutaṃ ca hutaṃ caiva tatha prahutam eva ca |
brāhmaṃ hutaṃ prāśitaṃ ca pañcayajñān pracakṣate ||
japo ‘huto huto homaḥ prahuto bhautiko baliḥ |
brāhmaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgrārcā prāśitaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
hārītaḥ |
huto nirvāpo ‘hutaḥ prahutaḥ prāśita iti pañca pākayajñāḥ | hutaṃ devānām | piṇḍanirvāpaḥ pitṝṇām | vāstupālabhūtebhyo baliharaṇam ahutam | yad uddhṛtyānnaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadāti saḥ prahutaḥ | yac chiṣṭaṃ prāśnāti sa yajamānabhāgas tasyaitad āhuḥ prāśitam iti | tad etad ṛcoktam |
sāyaṃprātar upakḷptaṃ prajānāṃ
yo niryāty āmṛtam anṛṇo ‘tti vidvān |
bhāgaṃ devebhyo ‘pradāyopakḷptam
andhaḥ pāpīyān aśnute apracetāḥ || iti |
tasmād ahar ahar juhuyāt | annābhāve kenacid ā kāṣṭhāt | devebhyaś cāpo nisargāt | pitṛbhyaś cāpo nivāpāt | bhūtebhyaś cāpo nisargāt | manuṣyebhyaś codapātrāṇi | pratyahaṃ devatāḥ | tithiveśeṣād vā |
nirvāpo ‘sminn astīti “nirvāpaḥ” | “tad etad” iti, tad etad āvaśyakatvam ṛcā pratipāditaṃ “sāyaṃprātar” ityādikayā | tasyāś [147] cāpy arthaḥ — “sāyaṃprātaḥ” pākāgreṇa pākayajñaṃ pañcalakṣaṇaṃ yat karma “prajānāṃ niryātva” devādiṛṇāpākaraṇārthaṃ pradāya yo bhuṅkte sa devādīnām anṛṇaḥ san amṛtam ivātti | “vidvān” jñānī | yas tu “upakḷptaṃ bhāgaṃ devebhyo apradāya bhuṅkte” “apracetāḥ” ajñaḥ saḥ “andhaḥ” pāpa eva | “nivāpaḥ” pitur uddeśena brāhmaṇāya śuṣkānnaphalamūlānām udakāntānāṃ dānam | “pratyahaṃ devatāḥ” pūjanīyā iti śeṣaḥ | punarvacanād viśvedevadevatābhya anyā etā devatāḥ | yathā vakṣyati hārītaḥ “agnyādayo devā diviṣṭhā” ityādi | “tithiviśeṣād vā” pārāśarādyuktapratipadāditithiṣu brahmādidevapūjā kartavyā |
jābāliḥ |
snātvā mahāmakhānāṃ tu prathamaṃ tarpaṇaṃ smṛtam |
dviyo homas tu sāvitryā bhautas tu balikarmaṇā ||
brāhmo vedajapenaiva nṛyajño ‘tithipūjanam |
ayācitāhṛtair dravyair ācānte pūrṇadakṣiṇām ||
“mahāmakhānām” iti nirdhāraṇe ṣaṣṭhī | “sāvitryā” yo “homa” sa “divyo” yajñaḥ | ayaṃ ca sāvitryā homena divyayajñaḥ śākhāviśeṣe vyavasthitaḥ | “ayācitāhṛtaiḥ” iti vihitavṛttyupārjtopalakṣaṇam | “pūrṇadakṣiṇam” pūrṇā dakṣiṇā yatra pūjane tat tathā |
manuḥ [3.80–81] |
ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtāny atithayas tathā |
[148]        āśāsate kuṭumbibhyas tebhyaḥ kāryaṃ vijānatā ||
svādhyāyenārcayetarṣīn homair devān yathāvidhi |
pitṝn śrāddhena nṝn annair bhūtāni balikarmaṇā ||
hārītaḥ |
yad devebhyo juhoti devalokaṃ tena jayati | yat pitṛbhyaḥ pitṛlokaṃ tena | yat svādhyāyam adhīte ṛṣilokaṃ tena | yad bhūtebhyo balpuniṃ harati bhūtalokaṃ tena | tasmād devapitṛṛṣibhūtebhyo hutvodīkṣetāgodohanāt |
punar hārītaḥ |
devārthe ca pitṝṇāṃ ca brāhmaṇārthe ca nityaśaḥ |
svaṃ cinvatāṃ svavṛtteṣu yo naḥ kleśaḥ sa nas tapaḥ ||
devān pitṝn ṛṣīṃś caiva bhūtāni brāhmaṇāṃs tathā |
tarpayan vidhinā vipro brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||
so ‘yaṃ brahmapathaḥ śrīmān purā proktaḥ svayaṃbhuvā |
vartayan vidhinā vipro brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||
“svaṃ” dhanam | “cinvatāṃ” saṃcayanaṃ kurvatām | “svavṛtteṣu” pratigrahādiṣu |
manuḥ [3.75–76] |
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
daiva karmaṇi yukto hi bibhartīdaṃ carācaraiḥ ||
[149]        agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
laukike vaidike vāpi hutotsṛṣṭe jale kṣitau |
vaiśvadevas tu kartavyaḥ pañcabhūtāpanuttaye ||
vaiśvadevena ye hīnā ātithyena vivarjitāḥ |
sarve te vṛṣalāḥ jñeyāḥ prāptavedā api dvijāḥ ||
“laukike” pākasādhane | “vaidike” āvasathye | “hutotsṛṣṭe” anyena homaṃ kṛtvā tyakte | “hṛtotsṛṣṭe” iti pāṭhe tv āhṛtyonmṛjyety arthaḥ | anantaroktāgnitrayāsaṃbhave jalam | tadasaṃbhave bhūḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.34] |
sāyaṃprātar vaiśvadevaḥ kārtavyo balikarma ca |
anaśnatāpi satatam anyathā kilbiṣī bhavet ||
tathā [2.47–48]
agnyādir gautamenokto homaḥ śākala eva vā |
anāhitāgner apy eṣa yujyate balibhiḥ saha ||
“agnyādiḥ” “agnir dhanvantarir viśvedevāḥ prajāpatiḥ [150] sviṣṭakṛd” iti gautamoktaḥ | “śākalaś” ca “devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi” ityādiḥ | ayaṃ ca jyotiṣṭome | āhavanīye “ṣaṭ ṣaṭ śakalāny abhyādadhīta” ityādinā vihitakāṣṭhaśakalasādhyātvāc chākalahoma ity ucyate | “anāhitāgneḥ” śrautasmārtāgnirahitasya |
agnirāḥ |
śālāgnau vipaced annaṃ laukike vāpi nityaśaḥ |
yasminn eva paced annaṃ tasmin homo vidhīyate ||
“śālāgniḥ” āvasathyaḥ |
manuḥ [4.21] |
ṛṣiyajñaṃ devayajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ ca sarvadā |
nṛyajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ ca yathāśakti na hāpayet ||
“yathāśakti” pakvānnāsaṃbhave phalamūlādināpi |
pañca kḷptā mahāyajñāḥ pratyahaṃ gṛhamedhinaḥ |
ity anenāvaśakartavyatvena pūrvaṃ pratipannānāṃ punarvacanaṃ snātakavratatvajñāpanārthaṃ vakṣyamāṇopayogārthaṃ ca |
tathā [4.22–24] |
etān eke mahāyajñān yajñaśāstravido janāḥ |
anīhamānāḥ satatam indriyeṣv eva juhvati ||
vācy eke juhvati prāṇaṃ prāṇe vācaṃ ca sarvadā |
[151]        vāci prāṇe ca paśyanto yajñanirvṛttim akṣayām ||
jñānenaivāpare viprā yajanty etair makhaiḥ sadā |
jñānamūlāṃ kriyāṃ teṣāṃ paśyanto jñānacakṣuṣā ||
“yajñaśāstravidaḥ” sopaniṣatkavedārthavidhaḥ | “anīhamānāḥ” bāhyayajñopakaraṇārtham aceṣṭamānāḥ | “satataṃ juhvati” ity anvayaḥ | indriyāṇāṃ yajñānāṃ ca homyatvānupapatteḥ sāmarthyād buddhīndriyāṇāṃ rūpādyabhimukhāḥ pañca vṛttayaḥ pratyāhāradvāreṇa homyās tannirodhadvāreṇa pañcanirvṛttiṃ sādhayantīty arthaḥ | “eke” svādhyāyaparāḥ santaḥ vāgvṛttyanuguṇāṃ prāṇavṛttiṃ kurvanti | “prāṇe vācaṃ” prāṇāyāmaparāḥ prāṇavṛttyanuguṇāṃ vācaṃ kurvanti | atha vā sarvadā śabdoccāraṇe prāṇakriyāyāṃ ca yajñanirvṛttiṃ kurvanti | “vāci prāṇe ca yajñanirvṛttiṃ” śāstrataḥ paśyantaḥ | “akṣayāṃ” phaladvāreṇa | tathā ca śrutiḥ:
yāvad vai puruṣo bhāṣate na tāvat prāṇituṃ śaknoti prāṇaṃ tadā vāci juhoti | ete anante amṛte āhutī jāgrac ca svapaṃś ca satataṃ juhoti |
iti | “jñānena” paramātmajñānena vaiśvānarādyālambhanena vā “yajante” | “etaiḥ” uktalakṣaṇaiḥ | “eṣāṃ” yajñānām | “jñānamūlāṃ” jñānapradhānām | “kriyām” anuṣṭhānam | “paśyantaḥ” upaniṣadjñānacakṣuṣā | etad uktaṃ bhavati — adhikṛtair bāhyasādhanapañcayajñānuṣṭhānāsaṃ-bhava etaiḥ prāṇāyāmādibhir api snātakavratalopanimittadoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ pañcayajñasaṃpattiḥ kartavyā |
[152] śaṅkhalikhitau |
ata ūrdhvaṃ devayajñaḥ sarveṣām upadiśyante |
āśramadharmāvirodhena pratiniyatānām oṣadhīnāṃ kodravacaṇaka-māṣamasūroddālakakulatthavarjyaṃ nirvapaṇīyam | taṇḍulān vā prātaḥ patnyai dadyāt svayaṃ vādhiśrayet | susaṃmṛṣṭagṛhadvāropalepanadhūpajapyaprayato niyataḥ sāyaṃprātar annād āvaśyakāni kuryāt | agnaye jātavedase svāhety agnau | tūṣṇīṃ dvitīyām | ud u tyaṃ jātavedasam ity ādityam upatiṣṭheta |
“āśramadharmāvirodhena” iti vānaprasthārtham | tena vānaprasthena svāśramavihitāphālakṛṣṭa-nīvārādyannena devayajñādi “nirvapaṇīyam” nirvartanīyam | “patnyai dadyād” adhiśrayaṇārtham iti śeṣaḥ | “svayaṃ vā” iti patnīkartṛkapākāsaṃbhavaviṣayam | “susaṃsṛṣṭeti”, susaṃsṛṣṭayor gṛhadvārayoḥ “upalepane”, devatādyarthaṃ “dhūpe”, pavitrāṇāṃ “jape”, ‘prayataḥ” śuciḥ
“niyato” niyamavān | “annād” adanīyāt | “āvaśyakāni” devayajñādīni | “agnau” juhuyād iti śeṣaḥ | “dvitīyām” āhutim iti śeṣaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.3.1–17] |
āryāḥ prayatā vaiśvadeve ‘nnasaṃskartāraḥ | bhāṣāṃ kāsaṃ kṣavathum ity abhimukho ‘nnaṃ varjayet | keśān aṅgaṃ vāsaś cālabhyāpa [153] upaspṛśet | āryādhiṣṭhitā vā śūdrā annasaṃskartāraḥ syuḥ | teṣāṃ sa evācamanakalpaḥ | adhikam ahar ahaḥ keśaśmaśrunakhalomavāpanam | udakopasparśanaṃ ca saha vāsasā | api cāṣṭamīṣv eva parvasu vā vaperan | parokṣaṃ annaṃ saṃskṛtam agnāv adhiśrityādbhiḥ prokṣet | tad devapavitram ity ācakṣate | siddhe ‘nne tiṣṭhan bhūtam iti svāmine prabrūyāt | tat subhūtaṃ virāḍ annaṃ tan mā kṣāyīti prativacanaḥ | gṛhamedhino yad aśanīyaṃ tasya homā balayaś ca svargapuṣṭisaṃyuktāḥ | teṣāṃ mantrāṇām upayoge dvādaśāham adhaśayyā brahmacaryaṃ kṣāralavaṇavarjanaṃ ca | uttamasyaikarātram upavāsaḥ | balīnāṃ tasya deśasya saṃskāro hastena parimṛjyābhyukṣya nyupya paścāt pariṣecanam | aupāsane pacane vā ṣaḍbhir adyaiḥ pratimantraṃ hastena juhuyāt | ubhayataḥ pariṣecanaṃ yathā purastāt |
“āryāḥ” sādhavo ‘tra dvijātayaḥ | “prayatāḥ” śucayaḥ | “vaiśvadeve” vaiśvadevakarmaṇi | “kṣavathuḥ” hikkā | “keśān” iti, keśānāṃ prayatānāṃ sparśe apa upaspṛśed iti vidhiḥ | “adhiṣṭhitāḥ” avekṣitāḥ | “sa eva” āryāṇām “ācamanakalpaḥ” | “aṣṭamīṣu” tithiṣu | “parokṣam” āryānadhiṣṭhitam | “tad devapavitram” tad annaṃ devasampradāne ‘pi pavitram, kiṃ punaḥ svayaṃ bhojane | “gṛhamedhinaḥ” iti gṛhasthasya | yadāpy agnipakvenāpi prāṇavṛttis tadā tenaiva homā balayaś cāvaśyaṃ [154] kartavyāḥ | “upayoge” ādyaprayogakāle | adhaḥśayyādi kṣāralavaṇavarjanāntaṃ vratam | “uttamasya” tu balimantrasyādye prayogakāle dvādaśarātrād anantaram “eka upavāso” adhikaḥ | “tasya” balideśasya sarvasya saṃskāra āvṛtyā na tu tantreṇa | “nyupya” nirvāpaṃ kṛtvā | “ubhayataḥ” ādāv ante ca | “pariṣecanaṃ” balīnām udakena pariveṣṭanam |
candogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.153–154] |
bhūtapravācane patnī yady asannihitā bhavet |
rajorogādinā tatra kathaṃ kurvanti yājñikāḥ ||
mahānase ‘nnaṃ yā kuryāt savarṇāṃ tāṃ pravācayet |
praṇavādy api vā kuryāt kātyāyanavaco yathā ||
“bhūtapravācanam” siddhe ‘nne svāminā bhūtam annam iti patnyā vācanam | tatra patnyā asannidhāne anyā api yā pākakartrī savarṇā tāṃ vācayed ity arthaḥ | yadi tu bhūtamiti vaktuṃ sā na śaknoti tadānyad api oṃkārādi saṃmatipratipādakaṃ brūyāḍ ity abhiprāyaḥ |
manuḥ [3.84–86]
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya gṛhye ‘gnau vidhipūrvakam |
ābhyaḥ kuryād devatābhyo brāhmaṇo homam anvaham ||
agneḥ somasya caivādau tayoś caiva samastayoḥ |
[155]        viśveṣāṃ caiva devānāṃ dhanvantaraya eva ca ||
kuhvai caivānumatyai ca prajāpataya eva ca |
saha dyāvāpṛthivyoś ca tathā sviṣṭakṛte ‘ntataḥ ||
“samastayoḥ” agnīṣomābhyām | “saha dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ” dyāvāpṛthivībhyām ity evaṃ kartavayam |
viṣṇuḥ [67.1–3] |
athāgniṃ parisamūhya paryukṣya paristīrya parisamādhāya sarvataḥ pākād agram uddhṛtya juhuyāt | vāsudevāya saṃkarṣaṇāya pradyumnāyāniruddhāya puruṣāya satyāyācyutāya vāsudevāya | agnaye somāya mitrāya varuṇāyendrāyendrāgnibhyāṃ viśvebhyo devebhyo prajāpataye ‘numatyai dhanvantaraye vastoṣpataye ‘gnaye sviṣṭakṛte iti |
gautamaḥ [5.11] |
agnāv agnir dhanvantarit viśvedevāḥ prajāpatiḥ sviṣṭakṛd iti homāḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.131] |
haviṣyeṣu yavā mukhyās tad anu vrīhayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
māṣakodravagaurādīn sarvālābhe’pi varjayet ||
“gauro” ‘sya mitasarṣapaḥ |
[156] āpastambaḥ [2.15.14–18] |
na kṣāralavaṇahomo vidyate | tathāvarānnasaṃsṛṣṭasya vā | ahaviṣyasya homa udīcīnam uṣṇaṁ bhasmāpohya tasmiñ juhuyāt tad dhutam ahutaṃ cāgnau bhavati | na strī juhuyāt | nānupetaḥ |
“kṣāralavaṇam” ūṣaralavaṇaḥ | “aparānnasaṃsṛṣṭam” annāntarasaṃbaddham | “”ahaviṣyam” māṣādi | yadi tu kṣāralavaṇādyaiva homyaṃ bhavati tadā “udīcīnam” uttaradigbhāgabhavam “uṣṇaṃ bhasmāpohya” “tasmin” agnau “juhuyād” ity arthaḥ|
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.3–6] |
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya sāyaṃprātar gṛhye ‘gnau juhuyāt | gṛhadevatābhyo baliṃ haret | śrotriyāya datvā brahmacāriṇe cānantaraṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt | tato ‘tithīn bhojayet |
“vaiśvadevasya” viśvedevadevatākasyānnasya |
śātātapaḥ |
bhūtayajñaṃ tathā śrāddhaṃ nityaṃ tv atithitarpaṇam |
kramenānena kartavyaṃ svādhyāyādhyayanaṃ sadā ||
ayaṃ ca śākhāviśeṣavyavasthito bhūtayajñādikramaḥ |
[157] yājñavalkyaḥ [1.103–104] |
devebhyas tu hutād annāc cheṣād bhūtabaliṃ haret |
annaṃ bhūmau śvacāṇḍālavāyasebhyo vinikṣipet ||
atha manuḥ [3.87–93] |
evaṃ samyag ghavir hutvā sarvadikṣu pradakṣiṇam |
indrāntakāppatīndubhyaḥ sānugebhyo baliṃ haret ||
marudbhya iti tu dvāri musalolūkhale haret |
ucchīrṣake śriyai kuryād bhadrakālyai tu pādataḥ ||
brahmavāstoṣpatibhyāṃ tu vāstumadhye baliṃ haret |
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balim ākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcarebhya eva ca ||
pṛṣṭhavāstuni kurvīta baliṃ sarvānubhūtaye |
pitṛbhyo baliśeṣaṃ tu sarvaṃ dakṣiṇato haret ||
śunāṃ ca patitānāṃ ca śvapacāṃ pāparogiṇām |
vāyasānāṃ kṛmīṇāṃ ca śanakair nikṣiped bhuvi ||
[158]        evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhutāni brāhmaṇo nityam arcati |
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ tejomūrti patharjunā||
tathā [3.121] |
sāyaṃ tv annasya siddhasya patny amantraṃ baliṃ haret |
vaiśvadevaṃ hi nāmaitat sāyaṃ prātar vidhīyate ||
“evam” iti, devayajñaprakāreṇa | “ucchīrṣake” gṛhasthaśayyāśiraḥpradeśe, vāstupuruṣaśiraḥpradeśe vā | “pādataḥ” gṛhasthasyaiva śayyāpādapradeśe | “brahmavāstoṣpatibhyām” iti, evam eva balidāne prayogaḥ | “pṛṣṭavāstuni” paścād gṛhe mūtroccārakaraṇasthāna ity arthaḥ | “sarvānubhūtaye” sarvānubhūtidevatāyai | “śanakaiḥ” yathānnopaghātaḥ katham api na bhavati | vāyasādīnāṃ copakārāya baliḥ kriyamāṇas tatra deśe dātavyo yatra teṣām upayogasaṃbhavaḥ | “arcati” ity anena śvādīnām api balidāne ‘nādaro na kartavya ity uktam | “patharjunā” ṛjunā pathā | “sāyaṃ tu” iti, idaṃ ca sāyaṃ siddhenānnena patnyā amantrakaṃ yajamānatatputrādīnām asannidhāne | “haret” dadyāt |
śvādibhyo bhūtebhyo balidānam uktvā, – yamaḥ |
sarvam eva yathāśakti nirvaped vidhipūrvakam |
evaṃ yukto vrajet svargaṃ gṛhastho nātra sāṃśayaḥ ||
[159] hastena ity anuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [2.3.18–23; 2.4.1–5] |
evaṃ balīnāṃ deśe deśe samavetānāṃ sakṛt sakṛd ante pariṣecanam | sati sūpasaṃsṛṣṭena kāryāḥ | apareṇāgniṃ saptamāṣṭamābhyām udagapavargam | udadhānasannidhau navamena | madhye ‘gārasya daśamaikādaśābhyāṃ prāgapavargam | uttarapūrvadeśe ‘gārasyottaraiś caturbhiḥ | śayyādeśe kāmaliṅgena | dehalyām antarikṣaliṅgena | uttareṇāpidhānyām | uttarair brahmasadane | dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛliṅgena prācīnāvīty avācīnapāṇiḥ kuryāt | raudra uttaro yathā devatābhyaḥ | tayor nānā pariṣecanaṃ dharmabhedāt | naktam evottamena vaihāyasam | ya etān avyagro yathopadeśaṃ kurute nityaḥ svargaḥ puṣṭiś ca |
“evaṃ balīnām” iti, yathā “agnaye svāhā” ityādiṣūktavaiśvadevāhutīnām ādāv ante ca “adite anumanyasva” ityādibhir mantraiḥ “pariṣecanam”, evaṃ “balīnāṃ” “samavetānāṃ” militānām | “sati” iti, saty avakāśe “sūpasaṃsṛṣṭe” parasparasaṃkīrṇadeśe balayo na kāryā ity arthaḥ | “spatamāṣṭamābhyāṃ” “dharmāya svāhā” “adharmāya svāhā” ity ābhyām | “udagapavargam” udaksamāptir bhavati | “udadhānasannidhau navamena” “adbhyaḥ svāhā” iti | “udadhānaṃ” maṇikādi | “madhy ‘gārasya” gṛhasya | “daśamaikādaśābhyām” “oṣadhivanaspatibhyaḥ [160] svāhā” “rakṣodevajanebhyaḥ svāhā” iti | “prāgapavargaṃ” prāksaṃstham | “uttarapūrvadeśe” aparājitāyāṃ diśi | “agārasyottaraiś caturbhiḥ” “gṛhebhyaḥ svāhā” “avasānebhyaḥ svāhā” “avasānapatibhyaḥ svāhā” “sarvabhūtebhyaḥ svāhā” iti | “kāmaliṅgena” kāmāya svāhā” iti | “apidhānyāṃ” kapāṭe | “uttaraiḥ” mantrāḥ “pṛthivyai svāhā” ityādayaḥ “brahmaṇe” ity antāḥ | “pitṛliṅgena” “svadhā pitṛbhyaḥ” iti | “raudro mantro” “namo rudrāya paśupataye svāhā” iti | “dharmabhedāt” yathoktam — “pitaro vākyam icchanti bhāvam icchanti devatāḥ” iti, tasmāt pitṛkeṣu samantrakam eveti | “uttamena” “ye bhūtāḥ pracaranti” ity anena “vaihāyasam” eva baliṃ kuryān nānyam |
gautamaḥ [5.18] |
digdevatābhyaś ca yathāsvam | dvārṣu marudbhyaḥ | gṛhadevatābhyaḥ praviśya | brahmaṇe madhye | adbhya udakumbhe | ākāśāyāntarikṣe | naktaṃcarebhyaś ca sāyam |
“yathāsvaṃ” yasyā diśo yā devatā indrādikā tasyāḥ tasyāṃ diśi balir deyā ity arthaḥ | “digdevatābhyaś” ceti, cakārāt tatpuruṣebhyaś ca | “praviśya” gṛhamiti śeṣaḥ | “sāyaṃ ca” “naktaṃcarebhya” ity adhikam |
viṣṇuḥ [67.4–26] |
tato ‘nnaśeṣeṇa balim upaharet | takṣopatakṣābhyām | abhitaḥ pūrveṇāgnim | ambā nāmāsīti dulānāmāsīti [161] nitatnī nāmāsīti cupuṇīkā nāmāsīti amrayantī nāmāsīti meghayantī nāmāsīti varṣantī nāmāsīti sarvāsām | nandini subhage sumaṅgali bhadrakālīti saktiṣv abhipradakṣiṇam | sthūṇāyāṃ dhruvāyāṃ śriyai hiraṇyakeśyai vanaspatibhyaś ca | dharmādharmayor dvāre mṛtyave ca | udadhāne varuṇāya | viṣṇava ity ulūkhale | marudbhya iti dṛṣadi | upari śaraṇe vaiśravaṇāya rājñe bhūtebhyaś ca | indrāyendrapuruṣebhya iti pūrvārdhe | yamāya yamapuruṣebhya iti dakṣiṇārdhe | varuṇāya varuṇapuruṣebhya iti paścimārdhe | somāya somapuruṣebhya ity uttarārdhe | brahmaṇe brahmapuruṣebhya iti madhye | ūrdhvam ākāśāya | divācarebhyo bhūtebhya iti sthaṇḍile | naktaṃcarebhyo bhūtebhya iti naktam | tato dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya mātre pitāmahyai prapitāmahyai svanāmagotrābhyāṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuryāt | piṇḍānāṃ cānulepanapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyāni dadyāt | udakakalaśam upanidhāya svastyayanaṃ vācayet | śvakākaśvapacāṃ bhuvi nirvapet |
“abhitaḥ” dakṣiṇottarayoḥ | “saktiṣu” koṇeṣu | “upari śaraṇe” uparitane gṛhe | idaṃ ca kaṭhānāṃ vyavasthitam |
[162] śaṅkhalikhitau |
brahmaṇe nama iti brahmasthale baliṃ haret | somāyety udakumbhe | vāyava iti vāstugṛhe | gṛhapataya iti gṛhadvāre | prajāpataya iti garbhagṛhe | śan no devīr ity agnikārye | dikpatibhyas tatpuruṣebhya iti paścimārdhe | somāya somapuruṣebhya ity uttarārdhe | brahmaṇe brahmapuruṣebhyaḥ pratidiśam | nakṣatragrahadevatābhyo ‘ntarikṣe | sarvataḥ paśūnāṃ pataye | namo devebhya iti prāgudīcyām | brahmasthale svadhā pitṛbhya iti dakṣiṇena nivāpya godohamātraṃ kālam anvākāṅkṣed atithim |
“brahmasthale” gṛhamadhye | “anvākāṅkṣet” pratīkṣeta |
balidānavidhau chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.44] |
piṇḍavac ca paścimā pratipattiḥ |
tathā [2.35–38] |
amuṣmai nama ity evaṃ balidānaṃ vidhīyate |
balidānapradānārthaṃ namaskāraḥ kṛto yataḥ ||
svāhākāravaṣaṭkāranamaskārā divaukasām |
[163]        svadhākāraḥ pitṝṇāṃ ca hantakāro nṛṇāṃ yataḥ ||
svadhākāreṇa nirvapet pitryaṃ balim ataḥ sadā |
tam apy eke namaskāraiḥ kurvate neti gautamaḥ |
na cāvarārdhyā balayo bhavanti mahāmārjāraśravaṇapramāṇāt | ekatra cet kṛtsnā bhavantītaretaram asaṃsaktāś ca |
“piṇḍavad” iti, yathā piṇḍānāṃ pratipattir agnikṣepādikā tathā balīnām api | “na cāvarārdhyā” iti, biḍālakarṇapramāṇād ūnā balayo na bhavanti |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.42–49] |
apūrvam agnihotraṃ ca kuryāt prāg brahmaṇe tataḥ |
prajāpatiṃ samuddiśya dadyād āhutim ādarāt ||
gṛhyābhyaḥ kāśyapāyātha tato ‘numataye kramāt |
taccheṣaṃ maṇike pṛthvīparjanyādbhyaḥ kṣipet tataḥ ||
dvāre dhātur vidhātuś ca madhye ca brahmaṇe kṣipet |
gṛhasya puruṣavyāghra digdevānāṃ ca me śṛṇu ||
indrāya dharmarājāya varuṇāya tathendave |
prācyādiṣu budho dadyād dhutaśeṣātmakaṃ balim ||
[164]        prāguttare ca digbhāge dhanvantaribaliṃ budhaḥ |
nirvaped vaiśvadevaṃ ca karma kuryād ataḥ param ||
vāyavye vāyave dikṣu samantāsu tato diśām |
brahmaṇe cāntarikṣāya mānave ca kṣīped balim ||
viśvedevān viśvabhūtān tato viśvapatīn pitṝn |
yakṣmāṇaṃ ca samuddiśya baliṃ dadyān nareśvara ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.94–101] |
saṃpūjayet tato vahniṃ deyāś cāhutayaḥ kramāt |
prathamāṃ brahmaṇe kuryāt prajānāṃ pataye tataḥ ||
tṛtīyāṃ caiva gṛhyābhyaḥ kāśyapāya tathāparām |
tato ‘numataye dadyād dadyād gṛhabaliṃ tathā ||
pūrvākhyātaṃ mayā yad yan nityakarmakriyāvidhau |
vaiśvadevaṃ tataḥ kuryād balayas tatra me śṛṇu ||
yathāsthānavibhāgaṃ tu devān uddiśya vai pṛthak |
parjanyādbhyo dharitrīṇāṃ dadyāt tu maṇike trayam ||
dhātre vidhātre ca dvābhyāṃ dadyāc ca dvārayoḥ kramāt |
vāyave pratidigbhyaś ca digbhyaḥ prācyāditaḥ kramāt ||
brahmaṇe cāntarikṣāya sūryāya ca yathākramam |
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo viśvabhūtebhya eva ca ||
[165]        uṣase bhūtapataye dadyāc cottaratas tataḥ |
svadhā nama ity uktvāpi pitṛbhyaś cāpi dakṣiṇe ||
kṛtvāpasavyaṃ vāyavyāṃ yakṣmaitat te ‘vanejanam |
annāvaśeṣamiśraṃ vai toyaṃ dadyād yathāvidhi ||
tathā [29.21] |
tatas toyam upādāya teṣām ācamanāya vai |
sthāneṣu nikṣipet prājño nāmnā tūddiśya devatāḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.50–57] |
tato ‘nyad annam ādāya bhūmibhāge śucau punaḥ |
dadyād aśeṣabhūtebhyaḥ svecchayā tat samāhitaḥ ||
devā manuṣyāḥ paśavo vayāṃsi
siddhāḥ sayakṣoragadaityasaṅghāḥ |
pretāḥ piśācās taravaḥ samastā
ye cānnam icchanti mayā pradattam ||
pipīlikāḥ kīṭapataṅgakādyā
bubhukṣitāḥ karmanibandhabaddhāḥ |
prayāntu te tṛptim idaṃ mayānnaṃ
tebhyo visṛṣṭaṃ sukhino bhavantu ||
yeṣāṃ na mātā na pitā na bandhur
naivānnasiddhir na tathānnam asti |
tattṛptaye ‘nnaṃ bhuvi dattam etat
te yāntu tṛptiṃ muditā bhavantu ||
[166]        bhūtāni sarvāṇi tathānnam etad
ahaṃ ca viṣṇur na yato ‘nyad asti |
tasmād ahaṃ bhūtanikāyabhūtam
annaṃ prayacchāmi bhavāya teṣām ||
caturdaśo bhūtagaṇo ya eṣa
tatra sthitā ye ‘khilabhūtasaṅghāḥ |
tṛptyartham annaṃ hi mayā visṛṣṭaṃ
teṣām idaṃ tu muditā bhavantu ||
ity uccārya naro dadyād annaṃ śraddāsamanvitam |
bhuvi bhūtopakārāya gṛhī sarvāśrayo yataḥ ||
śvacaṇḍālavihaṅgānāṃ bhuvi dadyāt tato naraḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.4.10–11] |
agraṃ ca deyam | atithīn evāgre bhojayet |
“agram” grāsacatuṣṭayaṃ viṣṇupurāṇoktam |
baudhyanaḥ [2.5.15] |
śrotriyāyāgraṃ dadyāt |
manuḥ [3.94–98] |
kṛtvaitad balikarmaivam atithiṃ pūrvam āśayet |
bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyād vidhivad brahmacāriṇe ||
[167]        yat puṇyaphalam āpnoti gāṃ datvā vidhivad guroḥ |
tat puṇyaphalam āpnoti bhikṣāṃ datvā tu bhikṣave ||
bhikṣām apy udapātraṃ vā satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam |
vedatattvārthaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet ||
naśyanti havyakavyāni narāṇām avijānatām |
bhasmībhūteṣu vipreṣu mohād dattāni dātṛbhiḥ ||
vidyātapaḥsamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu |
nistārayati durgāc ca mahataś cāpi kilbiṣāt ||
viṣṇuḥ [67.26–28] |
bhikṣāṃ ca dadyāt | atithipūjane ca paraṃ yatnam ātiṣṭhet |
yamaḥ |
satkṛtya bhikṣave bhikṣāṃ yaḥ prayacchati mānavaḥ |
gopradānasamaṃ puṇyaṃ tasyāha bhagavān yamaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.108] |
satkṛtya bhikṣave bhikṣā dātavyā savratāya ca |
“savrato” brahmacārī |
śātātapaḥ |
grāsamātrā bhaved bhikṣā puṣkalaṃ tu caturguṇam |
puṣkalāṇi ca catvāri hantakāraṃ vidur budhāḥ ||
[168] āpastambaḥ [1.3.26] |
strīṇāṃ ca pratyācakṣāṇānāṃ samāhito brahmacāṛīṣṭaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ prajāṃ paśūn brahmavarcasam annādyaṃ vṛṅkte | tasmād u ha vai brahmacārisaṅghaṃ carantaṃ na pratyācakṣīta | “api haiṣv evaṃvidha evaṃvrataḥ syād” iti hi brāhmaṇam |
“pratyācakṣāṇāṃ” bhikṣārtham āgatānāṃ pratyākhyānaṃ kurvantīnām | “eṣu” brahmacāriṣu | “evaṃvrataḥ” bhikṣādānavrataḥ |
yamaḥ |
hastadattā tu yā bhikṣā salilaṃ vyañjanāni ca |
bhuktvā tv aśucitāṃ yāti datvā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.5] |
śrotiyāyāgraṃ datvā brahmacāriṇe cānantaraṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt |
“śrāddham” ity anuvṛttau, śātātapaḥ |
yathāśakti abhirūpāṇāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam |
apavarge tu sarvatra nityam eva prakīrtitam ||
“abhirūpāṇāṃ” viduṣām | “apavargaḥ” pañcayajñasamāptiḥ |
[169] manu-śātātapau śaṅkhaś ca prathame |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena ca |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āharan ||
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
kuryād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
tṛptyarthaṃ vai pitṝṇāṃ tu svātmanaḥ śreya icchatā ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.30–32] |
apy ekam āśayed vipraṃ pitṛyajñārthasiddhaye |
adaivaṃ nāśti ced anyo bhoktā bhojyam athāpi vā ||
apy uddhṛtya yathāśakti kiṃcid annaṃ yathāvidhi |
pitṛbhyo ‘tha manuṣyebhyo dadyād ahar ahar dvijaḥ ||
pitṛbhya idam ity uktvā svadhākāraṃ ca kārayet |
“adaivam” viśvedevavarjitam |
[170] āpastambaḥ [2.18.6–19] |
nityaśrāddham | bahir grāmāc chucayaḥ śucau deśe saṃskurvanti | tatra navāni dravyāṇi | yair annaṃ saṃskriyate yeṣu ca bhujyate | tāni ca bhuktavadbhyo dadyāt | samudetāṃś ca bhojayet | na cātadguṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet | evaṃ saṃvatsaram | teṣām uttamaṃ lohenājena kārayam | mānaṃ ca kārayet praticchannam | tasyottarārdhe brāhmaṇān bhojayet | ubhayān paśyati brāhmaṇāṃś ca bhuñjānānān māne ca pitṝn ity upadiśanti | kṛtākṛtam ata ūrdhvam | śrāddhena tṛptiṃ vedayante pitaraḥ |
“nityaśrāddham” iti, nityam ahar ahaḥ kriyamāṇaḥ | “śucayaḥ” iti punarvacanam ādareṇa sarvaprakāreṇa śaucasyāṅgatvārtham | “samudetāḥ” samuddhṛtāḥ, jātividyādibhir utkṛṣṭā ity arthaḥ | “na cātadguṇāya” iti, atadguṇāya anantaroktasamudetatvaguṇaśūnyāya | “ucchiṣṭaṃ” cātra śrāddhapākaśeṣaḥ | “teṣām” ityādi, teṣām ahar ahaḥ kriyamāṇānāṃ śrāddhānāṃ yad “uttamaṃ” śrāddhaṃ saṃvatsarasyānte, tal lohitachāgamāṃsamiśreṇānnena kartavyam | “mānaṃ ca” iti, mīyante prakṣipyante śayanāsanādisādhanāni dravyāṇi yatra tan mānaṃ gṛham | “praticchannaṃ” sarvato veṣṭitam | “tasya” ca gṛhasya “uttarārdhe” uttarabhāge “brāḥmaṇān bhojayet” | tasmiṃś ca māne “brāhmaṇān bhuñjānān” “pitṝn” pitṛrūpeṇa “paśyati” paśyed ity arthaḥ | “kṛtākṛtam” iti, evaṃ saṃvatsare kṛte ata ūrdhvaṃ nityaśrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavaty akṛtaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | akaraṇe doṣo nāsti karaṇe ca [171] phalam astīty uktaṃ bhavati | “śrāddhena” iti, anenaivaṃ saṃvatsaraśrāddhena svakīyāṃ yāvajjīvatṛptiṃ “vedayante” kathayanti |
matsyapurāṇe [16.5–6] |
nityaṃ tāvat pravakṣyāmi cārghyāvāhanavarjitam |
adaivaṃ tad vijānīyāt pārvaṇaṃ parvasu smṛtam ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.64] |
pitrarthaṃ cāparaṃ vipram ekam apy āśayen nṛpa |
tad deśyaṃ viditācārasaṃbhūtiṃ pāñcayajñikam ||
padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭikhaṇḍam, 15.139–140] |
ekaṃ vedāntagaṃ vipraṃ bhojayec chraddhayānvitaḥ |
tasya bhuktā bhavet koṭir viprāṇāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.66] |
dadyāc ca bhikṣātritayaṃ parivrāḍbrahmacāriṇām |
svecchayā ca budho dadyād vibhave saty avāritam ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe [58.98–101] |
bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyāt parivrāḍbrahmacāriṇe |
kalpitānnāt sumuddhṛtya sarvavyañjanasaṃyutām ||
akṛte vaiśvadeve tu bhikṣārthaṃ gṛham āgate |
uddhṛtya vaiśvadevānnaṃ bhikṣāṃ datvā visarjayet ||
[172]        vaiśvadevakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ sadyo bhikṣur vyapohati |
na tu bhikṣukṛtaṃ doṣaṃ vaiśvadevo vyapohati ||
padmapurāṇe [sṛṣṭikhaṇdam, 15.140–141] |
yaḥ pātrapūraṇīṃ bhikṣāṃ yatibhyaḥ saṃprayacchati |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo nāsau durgatim āpnuyāt ||
brahmapurāṇe |
pūrvaṃ davair jitā daityāḥ saṃgrāmāc ca parāṅmukhāḥ |
kapālapāṇayo jagmuḥ kecin nagnavratās tathā ||
kecin muṇḍā lohitākṣāḥ kāṣāyavasanās tathā |
sātvatāś ca durācārāḥ śaucācāravivarjitāḥ ||
narāsthikeśasaṃchannāḥ kecid vyājena dānavāḥ |
yajñeṣu rakṣasāṃ bhāgo deva ity eva saṃsmaran ||
viprebhyo mūrtim adbhyaś ca devebhyaś ca gṛhād bahiḥ |
nityaṃ satpuruṣaḥ kuryād eteṣv apy adhikāṃ dayām ||
tathā |
pākhaṇḍināṃ cāpi na yatra bhikṣāṃ
kurvanti nindāṃ ca bahir gatānām |
kṣamāvatāṃ saṃsthitānāṃ svaśāstre
śūnyāṭavyāṃ bhikṣayā vartatāṃ ca ||
[173]         vedān paṭhan tv agnihotrāṇi samyag
vratopavāsāṃś ca carantu kiṃ tat |
spardhāṃ kṛtvā sādhujaneṣu nityaṃ
ghorāṇi pāpāni samācaranti ||
āstāṃ kim ebhir bahubhiḥ pralāpaiḥ
pākhaṇḍināṃ rogiṇāṃ cātha madhye |
vikarmiṇo duṣṭatarā bhavanti
duṣṭāsu nārīṣu yathā patighnī ||
duḥkhākulaṃ jagad etad vicārya
namo ‘stu dharmāya iti bruvaṃś ca |
dadyād annaṃ sarvagataṃ ca viṣṇuṃ
praṇamya vai dvādaśaparvamātram ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [29.35–37] |
grāsapramāṇā bhikṣā syād agraṃ grāsacatuṣṭayam |
agraṃ caturguṇaṃ prāhur hantakāraṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
bhojanaṃ hantakāraṃ vā agraṃ bhikṣām athāpi vā |
adatvā na ca bhoktavyaṃ yathavibhavam ātmanaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
ghāsamuṣṭiṃ paragave sānnaṃ dadyāt tu yaḥ sadā |
akṛtvā svayam āhāraṃ svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
brahmapurāṇe |
saurabheyyaḥ sarvahitāḥ pavitrāḥ puṇyarāśayaḥ |
[174]        pratigṛhṇantu me grāsaṃ gāvas trailokyamātaraḥ ||
dadyād anena mantreṇa gavāṃ grāsaṃ sadaiva hi |
gavāṃ kaṇḍūyanaṃ ghāsaṃ grāsam āhnikam eva vā |
datvā bhaven mahāpuṇyaṃ gopradānasamaṃ nṛṇām ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
tṛṇodakādisaṃyuktaṃ yaḥ pradadyād gavāhnikam |
kapilāśatadānasya phalaṃ vidyān na saṃśayaḥ ||
pañcabhūte śive puṇye pavitre sūryasaṃbhave |
pratīcchemaṃ mayā dattaṃ saurabheyi namo ‘stu te ||
balidānānantaraṃ viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.58, 59, 63] |
tato godohamātraṃ vai kālaṃ tiṣṭhed gṛhāṅgaṇe |
atithigrahaṇārthāya tadūrdhvaṃ vā yathecchayā ||
atithiṃ tatra saṃprāptaṃ pūjayet svāgatādinā |
hiraṇyagarbhabudhyā taṃ manyetābhyāgataṃ gṛhī ||
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakalpakāṇḍe vaiśvadevādiparva ||
[175]
6
athātithipūjā
tatra pitṛdānānantaraṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.6] |
tato ‘tithīn bhojayec chreyāṃsam ānupūrvyeṇa |
“śreyāṃsam” jātyādibhir utkṛṣṭam | “ānupūrvyeṇa” krameṇa | yo yo jātyādibhir utkṛṣṭas taṃ taṃ prathamaṃ krameṇa bhojayed ity arthaḥ |
manur viṣṇuś ca prathame |
saṃprāptāya tv atithaye pradadyād āsanodake |
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam ||
agnīn hutvā vidhānena yat puṇyaphalam āpyate |
tena tulyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ vā brāhmaṇe tarpite phalam ||
mantrakarmaviparyāsād duritād durgatād api |
tatphalaṃ naśyate kartur idam aśraddhayā kṛtam ||
śilān apy uñchato nityaṃ pañcāgnīn api juhvataḥ |
[176]        sarvaṃ sukṛtam ādatte brāhmaṇo ‘narcito vasan ||
tṛṇāni bhūmir udakaṃ vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā |
etāny api satāṃ gehe nocchidyante kathaṃcana ||
“saṃprāptāya” nimantraṇaṃ vinā svayam āgatāya | “udakam” pādadhāvanādyupayogi | “vidhipūrvakam” atithipūjoktaprakāreṇa | “tatphalam” agnihotraphalam | “idam” āsanādidānam | “śilān” lūnasasyaśeṣān | “uñchataḥ” uccinvataḥ | anena ca daridreṇāpy atithipūjanaṃ kartavyam ity uktam | “sūnṛtā” priyā satyā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
godohanamātraṃ kālam anvākāṅkṣet | atithiḥ śrotriyo vedavratī yatidharmā naiṣṭhikaḥ samānavṛttir yo ‘nyo vābhyāgacchet tasmin kāle tam arcayitvāśnīyāt | kṛśavṛtter api brāhmaṇo hy anaśnan sukṛtam ādatte | paryaśnataḥ prajāḥ kṛntanti |
“śrotriyaḥ” ekaśākhāyā apy adhyetā | “vedavratī” upakurvāṇakaḥ | “yatidharmā” yatiḥ | “naiṣṭhiko” brahmacārī | [177] “samānavṛttiḥ” tulyajīvanopāyaḥ, yathā gṛhasthasya gṛhasthaḥ | “paryaśnataḥ” atithiṃ parityajyāśnataḥ |
tathā |
prārthayante yathā sarve nipānaṃ mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
evaṃ gṛhasthaṃ saṃpannaṃ prārthayantīha sādhavaḥ ||
nāvamanyeta vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇam | brāhmaṇo hy agnir ivāprameyaḥ sarva eva | yathā praṇītaś cāpraṇītaś cāgnir hi daivatam evaṃ vidvāṃś cāvidvāṃś ca brāhmaṇaḥ pūjya eva sarveṣām | yatra hi brāhmaṇo na bhuṅkte tad dhutam apy agnāv ahutam evāsya tad dhaviḥ | tatra devā api na gṛhṇanti svaṃ bhāgam | atidevā hi brāhmaṇāḥ | brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādād devā api svargam ajayan |
“praṇītaḥ” vaidikasaṃskārasaṃskṛtāgniḥ | “atidevāḥ” devebhyo ‘tiśayitāḥ |
punaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
vayovarṇavidyātapaḥsaṃpannāya pādyam arghyam ācamanīyaṃ annaviśeṣān tasmai śaktito dadyāt | [priyapraśnottareṇa] sahāsīta | [yānavantam āyānārohaṇād ayānavantam anujñāparyantam ananujñāna āsīmāntam anuvrajya visarjayet | yadi tatraiva vastum icchat tadā śayyādinā śayanopakaraṇena pūjayet |] pradoṣe ‘nujñāpya śayīta | pūrvaṃ pratibudhyeta | prasthitam anuvrajet | [178] sametya nyāyato nivarteta | vedyudyānārāmasabhāprapātaḍāgadevagṛhamahādrumasthāna-nadīnām anyatarasmiṃs taṃ pradakṣiṇṃ kuryād vācam utsṛjya punar darśanāyeti |
vedyādīnām anyatarasmin sthāne “punar darśanāya” ity uktvā taṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt |
hārītaḥ |
atithiś ca yadāgacched yatir vaikhānasaḥ samānavṛttiḥ snātako vā tasya svāgatam arghyapādyācamanīyam āsanaṃ ca pradāya yāś cauṣadhayaḥ saṃnihitās tāś copaharet | taṃ prayāntam anuvrajan viṣṇukramān anukrāmati | modante ‘sya pitaraḥ pitāmahāḥ prapitāmahāḥ | tenānujñāto nivarteta | vasec ced vidhivat paricaraṇam |
“snātakaḥ” atrārthād akṛtavivāhaḥ, samānavṛttipadena gṛhasthasyopāttatvāt | “oṣadhayo” vrīhyādayaḥ | “viṣṇukramāḥ” iṣṭyaṅgabhūtāni mantravan nigamāni |
punar hārītaḥ |
viśvarūpaṃ brahma dvividham āhuḥ | paraṃ śabdabrahma ca | brahma sarvā devatāḥ | brahmamayatvād brahmasaṃbhavād brahmaṇy adhikārāc ca brāhmaṇāḥ sarvadevatyā bhavanti | yasyai yasyai devatāyai brāḥmaṇas [179] tarpayati tāṃ tāṃ prīṇāti | brāhmaṇasya vai tṛptiṃ devapitaro ‘nutṛpyante | na hi tat skandate na cyavate, na yātayāmi bhavati | brāhmaṇāh kāraṇam | na hi brāhmaṇābhibhāvinām agnir havyaṃ vahati | na cāsya devāḥ pitaraḥ pratigṛhṇanti | brāhmaṇakāraṇābhi-bhāvināṃ nāyaṃ loko na para ity ācāryāḥ | yajñopavītino devāḥ | prācīnāvītinaḥ pitaraḥ | vidyāsnātā āgneyāḥ | vratasnātā aindrāḥ | ubhayasnātā vaiśvānarāḥ | sarvā asya devatā gṛham āgacchanti | yasyaivaṃ vidvān brāhmaṇo gṛham abhyeti tam anabhyuttiṣṭhataḥ prāṇadevatā apakrāmanti | tataḥ prāṇaiḥ pāpīyān yātayāmatvam upaiti | yadainam abhyuttiṣṭhati nāsya brāhmaṇadevatā apakrāmati | ato na prāṇaiḥ pāpīyān yātayāmatvam upaiti | tasyaiṣa yad āha svāgatam iti gṛhadevatās tena prīṇāti | yad āsanādibhir arcayaty agniṃ yajñaṃ ca tena prīṇāti | yat pādābhiṣecanaṃ kurute pitḍṃs tena prīṇāti | yad annenābhipūjayati prajāpatiṃ tena prīṇāti | yad enaṃ yāntam anuyāti śreyasyo brahmavarcasyas tena sarvān kāmān avāpnoti |
“viśvarūpaṃ” jagatpravṛttitvā | “brahmayatvād” vedamayatvāt | “brahmasaṃbhavād” upanayane brahmarūpāyā gāyatryā [180] mātuḥ saṃbhavatvā | “brahmaṇi” vede sati sarvadevatātṛptiḥ parāmṛśyate | “brāhmaṇakāraṇābhibhāvināṃ” brāhmaṇaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ ye ‘bhibhavanti teṣāṃ tathā |
punar hārītaḥ |
`        sarvātmanā tu devā vai brāhmaṇeṣu vyavasthitāḥ |
agnivac ca dvijaṃ prāhus tasmād vipro ‘dhidaivatam ||
yathāgnau saṃsthitā devās tadvad vipreṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.6.3—2.8.4] |
agnir iva jvalann atithir abhyāgacchati | dharmeṇa vedānām ekaikāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati | svadharmayuktaṃ kuṭumbinam abhyāgacchati dharmapuraskāro nānyaprayojanaḥ so ‘tithir bhavati | tasya pūjāyāṃ śāntiḥ svargaḥ puṣṭiś ca | tam abhimukho ‘bhyāgamya yathāvayaḥ sametya tasyāsanam āhārayet | śaktiviṣaye nābahupādam āsanaṃ bhavatīty eke | tasya pādau prakṣālayec chūdramithunāv ity eke | anyataro ‘bhiṣecane syāt | [181] tasyodakam āhārayen mṛnmayenety eke | nodakam āhārayed asamāvṛttaḥ | adhyayanasāṃvṛttiś cātrādhikā | sāntvayitvā tarpayed rasair bhakṣyair adbhir avarārdhyeneva | āvasathaṃ dadyād upariśayyām upastaraṇam upadhānaṃ sāvastaraṇam abhyañjanaṃ ceti | annasaṃskāram āhūya vrīhīn yavān vā tadarthān nirvapet | uddhṛtāny annāny avekṣetedaṃ bhūyo nedam iti | bhūya uddharety eva brūyāt | dviṣan dviṣato vā nānnam aśnīyād doṣeṇa vā mīmāṃsamānasya mīmāṃsitasya vā | pāpmānaṃ hi sa tasya bhakṣayatīti vijñāyate | sa eṣa prājāpatyaḥ kuṭumbino yajño nityapratataḥ | yo ‘tithīnām agniḥ sa āhavanīyo yaḥ kuṭumbe sa gārhapatyo yasmin pacyate so ‘nvāhāryapacanaḥ | ūrjaṃ puṣtiṃ prajāṃ paśūn iṣṭāpūrtam iti gṛhāṇām aśnāti yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti | paya upasecanam annam agniṣṭomasaṃmitaṃ sarpiṣokthyasaṃmitaṃ [182] madhunātirātrasaṃmitam, māṃsena dvādaśāhasaṃmitam udakena prajāvṛddhir āyuṣaś ca | priyā apriyāś cātithayaḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayantīti vijñāyate | sa yat prātar madhyandine sāyam iti dadāti savanāny eva tāni bhavanti | yad anutiṣṭhaty udavasyaty eva tat | yat sāntvayati sā dakṣiṇā praśaṃsā | yat saṃsādhayati te viṣṇukramāḥ | yad upāvartate so ‘vabhṛthaḥ | iti brāhmaṇam | rājānaṃ ced atithir abhyāgaccec chreyasīm asmai pūjām ātmanaḥ kārayet | āhitāgniṃ ced atithir abhyāgacchet svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt | vrātya kvāvātsīr iti | vrātyodakam [183] iti | vrātya tarpayaṃs tv iti | purāgnihotrasya homād upāṃśu japet | vrātya yathā te manas tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti | yasyoddhṛteṣv ahuteṣv agniṣv atithir abhyāgacchet svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt | vrātyātisṛja hoṣyāmīty atisṛṣṭena hotavyam anatisṛṣṭaś cej juhuyād doṣaṃ brāhmaṇam āha | ekarātraṃ ced atithīn vāsayet pārthivāṃl lokān abhijayati dvitīyayāntarikṣāṃs tṛtīyayā divyāṃś caturthyāṃ parāvato lokān aparimitābhir aparimitāṃl lokān abhijayatīti vijñāyate | asamudetaś ced atithir bruvāṇa āgacched āsanam udakam annaṃ śrotriyāya dadāmīty eva dadyād evam asya samṛddhaṃ bhavati | yena kṛtāvasathaḥ syād atithir na taṃ pratyuttiṣṭhet pratyavarohed vā purastāc ced abhivāditaḥ | śeṣabhojy atithīnāṃ syāt | na rasān gṛhe bhuñjītānavaśeṣam atithibhyaḥ | nātmārtham abhirūpam annaṃ pācayet |
“svadharmayuktaḥ” svavarṇāśramācāravān | “dharmapuraaskāraḥ” tīrthayātrādidharma-kṛtyaparaḥ, prakārāntareṇa prāṇadhāraṇasamartho vā, na punar annalolupaḥ | “yathāvayaḥ sametya” anyūnasamādhi[184]kavayo ‘nusāreṇātithiṃ yathāyogyam upasaṃgrahaṇādinā militvā | “abahupādam” dvipādapīṭhādiḥ | “śūdramithunau” śūdradvayam, tayor madhye | eko “abhiṣecane” pādaprakṣālaṇārtham udakadāne vyāpriyeta | “tasya” atitheḥ “udakam” arghyārtham āharet | “mṛnmayena” pātreṇa | “nodakam āhārayet” mṛnmayenety anuṣaṅgaḥ | “asamāvṛtto” brahmacārī ced atithir abhyāgacchati tadā tasyārghyādidānānantaram “adhyayanasaṃvṛttiḥ” kartavyā, militvā tena saha paṭhanīyaṃ kiṃcit | “sāntvayitvā” pāṭhād anantaram abhijanādibhiḥ stutiṃ kṛtvā | “rasair bhakṣyair adbhir avarādhyenety eva”, “avarārdhyena” jaghanyakalpena | rasādyasaṃbhave ‘dbhir api tarpayed ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “upariśayyā” khaṭvādikā | “upastaraṇam” tūlikādi | “upadhānam” gendukādi | “avastaraṇam” tūlikoparipaṭaḥ | “abhyañjanam” tailādi | “vrīhīn yavān vā” iti tṛptisādhanārthopalakṣaṇam idam | “uddhṛtāni” bhojanapātre kṛtāni | “brūyāt” patnīm iti śeṣaḥ | “doṣeṇa mīmāṃsamānasya” doṣatvena vicāryamāṇasya | “mīmāṃsitasya” doṣatvenaiva | “ātmanā sreyasīm” ātmanaḥ utkṛṣṭām “pūjām” “vrātya [185] kvātsīr” iti praśnamantraḥ | “vrātyodakam” ity udakadānamantraḥ | agnihotrahomāt prāg āgate ‘tithāv ete “vrātya” ityādimantrāḥ “upāṃśu” uccāyaṇīyāḥ | “uddhṛteṣu” kṛtavihāreṣu | “atisṛṣṭaḥ” ādiṣṭaḥ | “brāhmaṇaṃ” vidhāyako vedabhāgaḥ | “asamudetaḥ” apakṛṣṭaḥ | “bruvāṇaḥ” atithir asmīti vadan | “śrotriyāya dadāmi” iti buddhiṃ kṛtveti śeṣaḥ | “yena” gṛheṇa | “kṛtāvasathaḥ” dattavāsaḥ | “tam” gṛhiṇaṃ dṛṣṭvāsau nāsanāt “pratyuttiṣṭhet”, khaṭvādibhyaś ca “na pratyavarohet” | “atithibhyaḥ” āgatebhyaḥ lavaṇakṣīrādīn rasān anavasthāpya na sākalyena bhuñjīta |
punar āpastambaḥ [2.8.14; 2.9.1–4] |
atithiṃ nirākṛtya yatra gate bhojane smaret tato viramyopoṣya | śvobhūte yathāmanasaṃ tarpayitvā saṃsādhayet | yānavantam ā yānāt | yāvan nānujānīyād itaram | apratibhāyāṃ sīmno nivarteta |
“atithiṃ nirākṛtya” atithidharmeṇa dhṛtaṃ kenacid vismaraṇādinā bhojanādy akārayitvā | “yatra gate bhojane” yāvatparyantaṃ gate bhojane, kiṃcit kṛte ‘rdhakṛte vā “smaret”, “tato” bhojanād “viramya” sāyaṃbhojanam akṛtvā saṃsthātavyam | [186] “yathāmanasam” yatra yatrāsya rucit bhavati tena “tarpayitvā” | “saṃsādhanam” anuvrajanam | “yānavantam” aśvādiyānayuktam atithim anuvrajet | “itaram” ayānavantaṃ yāvad anujñām anuvrajet | yadi tv atither anujñātuṃ “pratibhā” na bhavati tadā grāmasīmāntaṃ gatvānanujñāto ‘pi nivarteta |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.13–15] |
brāhmaṇāyābhyāgatāyāpa ānayanti | vijñāyate hi | vaiśvānaraḥ praviśaty atithir brāhmaṇo gṛhān | tasmād apa ānayanti | anyatra varṣābhyaḥ | tāṃ ca śāntiṃ janā vidur iti | taṃ bhojayitvopāsīta | tato gacchantam āsīmāntam anuvrajet | anujñānāḍ vā |
“brāhmaṇāya” atithirūpāyābhyāgatāyānnadānāt prathamataḥ pānādyartham udakam ānayanti | atraiva kāraṇābhidhānaṃ “vaiśvānara” ityādinā | na caitad artham udakānayanaṃ sarvadā | kiṃ tu “anyatra varṣābhyaḥ” varṣāsu nety arthaḥ | yataḥ “tāṃ hi śāntiṃ janā vidur” iti, “tām” udakāharaṇalakṣaṇaṃ “śāntiṃ” śamanaṃ prārthanājanitaṃ te “janāḥ” sarve “vidur” iti | anena prasiddhiḥ sūciteti bhartṛyajñaḥ |
gautamaḥ [5.32–38] |
śrotriyasya tu pādyam arghyam annaviśeṣāṃś ca prakārayet | [187] nityaṃ vā saṃskāraviśiṣṭam | madhyato ‘nnadānam avaidye sādhuvṛtte | viparīte tu tṛṇodakabhūmi | svāgatam antataḥ | pūjānabhyāsaś ca |
“annaviśeṣāḥ” coṣyapeyalehyādayaḥ | “prakārayet” pācayet | “nityaṃ vā” yadi tv annaviśeṣāṇāṃ dāne na samarthas tadā nityam eva yadgṛhe pacyate tad eva viśeṣapūjāpūrvakaṃ dadyāt | “madhyataḥ” sādhāraṇena satkāreṇa | “avaidye” vidyāsaṃbandharahite | “viparīte” vidyāhīne | “anabhyāso” anatikramaḥ |
manuḥ [3.105–107] |
apraṇodyo ‘tithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryoḍho gṛhamedhinā |
kāle prāptas tv akāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset ||
na vai svayaṃ tad aśnīyād atithiṃ yan na bhojayet |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ cātithipūjanam ||
āsanāvasathau śayyām anuvrajyām upāsanam |
uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryād dhīne hīnaṃ same samam ||
“apraṇodyaḥ” anirākāryaḥ | “sūryoḍhaḥ” sūryeṇāstaṃ gacchatā prāpitaḥ | uttamādhama-madhyameṣv āgateṣu na samaṃ [188] sarveṣām āsanādi, kiṃ tu “uttameṣūttamam” iti | “kāle” vaiśvadevānte |
atithim āśayed ity abhidhāya, viṣṇuḥ [67.31–32] |
yathā varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prabhur yathā strīṇāṃ bhartā tathā gṛhasthasyātithiḥ | tatpūjāyāṃ svargam āpnoti |
tathā [67.44] |
svādhyāyenāgnihotreṇa yajñena tapasā tathā |
na cāpnoti gṛhī lokān yathā tv atithipūjanāt ||
tathā |
brahmacārī yatir bhikṣur jīvanty ete gṛhāśramāt |
tasmād abhyāgatam atithiṃ gṛhastho nāvamānayet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
viśeṣato ‘nnam atithaye dadyāt | putraṃ jātam udīkṣante śrotriyaṃ bhojayiṣyatīti pitaraḥ pitāmahāḥ prapitāmahāś ca | [189] svāgatenāgnir āsanenendraḥ pitaraḥ pādyenānnādyena prajāpatiḥ | yo vā ekaṃ brahmeti vyākhyātaṃ jānāti | rājanyo vātithir bhavati neti vibhāṣā | bhavatīty eva | yasyaivaṃ vidvān gṛham ito bhavati tasya yathā priyaṃ tathā kurvīta | vrātya yā te priyam iti bhavati tasya priyāṃś cāpriyāṃś ca devān manasā matvā tāṃs tān paricaret | tasya vrātyo vrātyabruva iti havir bhavati | kṣīraprabhṛtayo yajñā vyākhyātāḥ | saṃvatsaro vātithiḥ | tasyāṅgāni sarvā devatāḥ | samānam arthaṃ pariyanti devā iti vijñāyate | sarvān saṃvatsaram avarundhīta | na tv eva bhūtiṃ kāṅkṣet | yathāśakti tān tān paricared ity āha | ambuśākaphalādīn dadyāt tāny avicārayan | aninditāni pratipādayan ṛṣīṇāṃ yāti salokatām iti | tṛṇāni bhūmir udakaṃ kalyāṇī vāk ceti satāṃ nagare (?) na kṣīyante | evaṃvṛttāv anantaloko bhavati | dampatī śayanīyābhyañjanasnāneṣu viśeṣaṃ kuryātām | svargalokaṃ ca gacchataḥ | tadahar gṛhitvaṃ bhavati | viśiṣṭaṃ hutaṃ cāgnibhyaś ca ||
“viśeṣato ‘nnaṃ” mṛṣṭam annam | “yo vā ekaṃ brahmeti” upaniṣatsu “vyākhyātaṃ” brahma “jānāti” taṃ bhojayiṣyatīty [190] arthaḥ | “vibhāṣā” vikalpaḥ | sa ca nāvyavasthitaḥ, kiṃ tu guṇvān so ‘pi bhavaty evātithiḥ | “bhūtiṃ” saṃpadam, atithipūjanārtham iti śeṣaḥ | “viśeṣam” utkarṣarūpam | “svargalokaṃ gacchato” dampatīty anvayaḥ | “tadahaḥ” yasminn ahany atithiḥ pūjyate | “gṛhitvam” atithipūjanārtham iti śeṣaḥ | “viśiṣṭaṃ hutaṃ ca” viśiṣṭaṃ bhavati tad ahar ity arthaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.109] |
mahokṣaṃ vā mahājaṃ vā śrotriyāyopakalpayet |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [4.8] |
athāpi brāhmaṇāya vā rājanyāya vābhyāgatāya mahokṣāṇaṃ vā mahājaṃ vā paced evam asyātithyaṃ kurvantīti |
atra yady api gṛhāgataśrotriyatṛptyarthaṃ govadhaḥ kartavya iti pratīyate, tathāpi kaliyuge nāyaṃ dharmaḥ | kiṃ tu yugāntare |
tad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
dīrghakālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dhāraṇaṃ ca kamaṇḍaloḥ |
gotrān mātṛsapiṇḍād vā vivāho govadhas tathā ||
narāśvamedhau madyaṃ ca kalau varjyaṃ dvijātibhiḥ || iti |
[191] baudhāyanaḥ [2.5.11–13] |
sāyaṃ prātar yadannaḥ syāt tenānnena vaisvadevaṃ balim upahṛtya brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviṭśūdrān abhyāgatān yathāśakti pūjayet | yadi bahūnāṃ na śaknuyād ekasmai guṇavate dadyāt | yo vā prathamam āgataḥ syāt |
“sāyaṃ prātar yadannaḥ” sāyaṃ prātar yadannabhug bhavati tenānnena | “vaiśvadevaṃ” viśve aneke devatā yatra taṃ “balim” ity arthaḥ | “bahūnāṃ na śaknuyāt” yadā bahūnāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ dātuṃ na śaknuyāt tadā “ekasmai guṇavate dadyāt” | yugapadāgamane “guṇavate” dadyāt | kramāgamane tu “prathamam āgatāya” iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.4.16–21] |
brāhmaṇāyānadhīyānāyāsanam annam udakam iti deyam | na pratyuttiṣṭhet | abhivādanāyaivopatiṣṭhed abhivādyaś cet | rājanyavaiśyau ca | śūdram abhyagataṃ karmaṇi niyuñjyāt | athāsmai [192] dadyāt | dāsā vā rājakulād āhṛtyātithivac (?) chūdraṃ pūjayeyuḥ |
“abhivādyaḥ” vayo’dhikatvādinā | “rājanyavaiśyau ca” prati nottiṣṭhed iti saṃbandho yogyatvāt | yasya hṛhiṇaḥ śūdro ‘tithir abhyāgatas tasya dāsās taṃ śūdram atithidharmeṇ kuryur ity anvayaḥ | “rājakulāt” nijasvāmikulāt |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nābrāhmaṇo ‘tithir brāhmaṇasya | śrotriyāya guṇavata ātithyam | rājanyavaiśyābhyāṃ mitravat | śūdrāyānṛśaṃsyārtham |
“ātithyaṃ” yathāvat kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | anyeṣāṃ tu “mitravad” annamātraṃ deyam ity arthaḥ | “ānṛśaṃsyārtham” akrūratvārtham |
gautamaḥ [5.42–45] |
kuśalānāmayārogyāṇām anupraśno ‘ntyaṃ śūdrasya | brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāhmaṇo ‘yajñe saṃvṛtaś cet | bhojanaṃ tu kṣatriyasyordhvaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ | anyān bhṛtyaiḥ sahānṛśaṃsyārtham |
“anupraśnaḥ” ānupūrvyeṇa vacanaḥ | “antyam” ārogyam | “abrāhmaṇaḥ” kṣatriyādiḥ | “saṃvṛtaḥ” saṃkucito [193] dhanādyabhāvena, daridra iti yāvat | etac ca viśeṣaṇaṃ gṛhasthasya | “anyān” vaiśyaśūdrān, bhojayed iti śeṣaḥ |
śātātapaḥ |
janmanaiva mahābhāgo brāhmaṇo nāma jāyate |
namasyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ janmaśreṣṭhaḥ pitā guruḥ ||
nāsty eṣāṃ pūjanīyo ‘nyas triṣu lokeṣu kaścana |
tapovidyāviśeṣeṇa pūjayanti parasparam ||
anyonyaṃ guravo viprā anyonyātithayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
anyonyam upakurvantas tārayanti taranti ca ||
yo hi yāṃ devatām icched ārādhayituṃ kaścana |
sarvopāyaprayatnena saṃtoṣayati brāhmaṇān ||
devatā dravyabhūteṣu kaścit kāścit pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
brāhmaṇā devatāḥ sarvā atas tān pūjayet sadā ||
āsanāśanaśayyābhir adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
nāsya kaścid vased gehe śaktito nārcito ‘tithiḥ ||
pākhaṇḍino vikarmasthān baiḍālavratikān śaṭhān |
haitukān bakavṛttīṃś ca vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet ||
“pākhaṇḍinaḥ” vedabāhyāgamānuṣṭhātāraḥ | “vikarmasthāḥ” āśramasthā evānāpadi svāśramadharmānuṣṭhānarahitāḥ | [194] “baiḍālavratikaḥ” “dharmadhvajī sadā lubdhaḥ” ityādinā manunoktaḥ | “śaṭhaḥ” karmakaraṇasamartho ‘pi vyājena dharmānanuṣṭhāyī | “haitukāḥ” vedaviruddhatarkavyavahāriṇaḥ | “cakravṛttayaḥ” “adhodṛṣṭiḥ” ityādinā kathitāḥ | “vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayed” iti sarvaprakāram arcanaṃ niṣidhyate | annadānamātraṃ tu na niṣidhyate |
śātātapaḥ pārāśaraś ca prathame |
priyo vā yadi vā dveṣyo mūrkhaḥ paṇḍita eva vā |
vaiśvadeve tu saṃprāpte so ‘tithiḥ svargasaṃkramaḥ ||
bhikṣāṃ vā puṣkalaṃ vāpi hantakāram athāpi vā |
asaṃbhave sadā dadyād udapātram athāpi vā ||
grāsamātrā bhaved bhikṣā puṣkalaṃ tu caturguṇam |
puṣkalāni ca catvāri hantakāraṃ vidur budhāḥ ||
“priyaḥ” jāmātādiḥ | “dveṣyaḥ” śatruḥ | “mūrkhaḥ” adhyayanarahitaḥ | “paṇḍitaḥ” yathāsvarūpaṃ pramāṇato jñātvā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphaladātṛkarmānuṣṭhātā (?) | “svargasaṃkramaḥ” nadīsaṃkramavat prāptihetuḥ |
[195] dakṣaḥ [3.5–8] |
sudhāvastūni vakṣyāmi viśiṣṭe gṛham āgate |
manaś cakṣur mukhaṃ vācaṃ saumyaṃ dadyāc catuṣṭayam ||
abhyutthānam ihāgaccha pūrvālāpaḥ priyānvitaḥ |
upāsanam anuvrajyā kāryāṇy etāni yatnataḥ ||
īṣadvastūni cānyāni bhūmir āpas tṛṇāni ca |
pādaśaucaṃ tathābhyaṅgam āśrayaḥ śayanaṃ tathā ||
kiṃcid rātrau yathāśakti nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset |
mṛjjalaṃ cārthine deyam etāny api sadā gṛhe ||
“sudhāvastūni” anāyāsasaṃpādyāni | “upāsanaṃ” samīpe sthitiḥ | “īśadvastūni kāryāṇi” iti saṃbandhaḥ | īṣadvastutvam alpāyāsasādhyatvāt | “śayanaṃ kiṃcid” iti saṃbandhaḥ |
yatyadhikāre punar dakṣaḥ [7.39–43] |
tapojapaiḥ kṛśībhūto vyādhināvasathārhakaḥ |
vṛddho grahagṛhītaś ca yaś cānyo vikalendriyaḥ ||
nīrujaś ca yuvā caiva bhikṣur nāvasathārhakaḥ |
[196]        sa dūṣayati tat sthānaṃ vṛddhaḥ pīḍayatīti ca ||
nīrujaś ca yuvā caiva brahmacaryād vinaśyati |
brahmacaryād vinaṣṭas tu kulaṃ gotraṃ ca nāśayet ||
vasann āvasathe bhikṣur maithunaṃ yadi sevate |
tasyāvasathanāthasya mūlāny api nikṛntati ||
āśrame tu yatir yasya viśrāmyati muhūrtakam |
kiṃ tasyānyena dharmeṇa kṛtakṛtyo hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
janmaprabhṛti yat pāpaṃ gṛhasthena tu saṃcitam |
nirmārjayati tat sarvam ekarātroṣito yatiḥ ||
“āvasathārhako” gṛhe vāsayituṃ yogyaḥ | “vṛddha” ity asamarthopalakṣaṇam | “grahagṛhītaḥ” bhūtābhibhūtaḥ |
yamaḥ |
nāgnihotreṇa dānena nopavāsopalepanaiḥ |
devatāḥ parituṣyanti yathā cātithipūjanaiḥ ||
atithiḥ pūjito yat tu dhyāyeta manasā śubham |
na tat kratuśatair vāpi tulyam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
cakṣur dadyān mano dadyād vācaṃ dadyāt tu sūnṛtām |
utthāya cāsanaṃ dadyāt sa dharmaḥ pañcalakṣaṇaḥ ||
āsanaṃ pādaśaucaṃ ca dīpam annaṃ pratiśrayam |
[197]        dadyād etāni yaḥ pañca sa yajñaḥ sarvadakṣiṇaḥ ||
annadaḥ prāṇado jñeyo dīpado vastradaḥ smṛtaḥ |
sa hi sarvaprado nāma yo dadāti pratiśrayam ||
gṛhasthair api yad bhuktaṃ snātakair vedapāragaiḥ |
yatinā vāpi yad bhuktaṃ tac chreyo netarais tathā ||
yat kiṃcit kurute dhyāyī dātur ardhaṃ prayacchati |
tasmān mahatphalaṃ dānaṃ dhyāyine snātake tathā ||
aśvamedhasahasreṇa rājasūyaśatena ca |
puṇḍarīkasahasreṇa yogiṣv āvasatho varaḥ ||
śrāntāyādṛṣṭapūrvāya priyāyādhvani vartine |
dadāti yo ‘nnam akliṣṭaṃ sa yajñaḥ sarvadakṣiṇaḥ ||
“pratiśrayaḥ” vāsārthaṃ gṛham | “śreyaḥ” atipraśastam | “itaraiḥ” yathoktagṛhasthād anyaiḥ | “puṇḍarīko” yajñaviśeṣaḥ | “akliṣṭaṃ” satkṛtam |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
āgatasyāsanaṃ dadyāc chrāntasya śayanaṃ tathā ||
tṛṣārtasya tu pānīyaṃ kṣudhitasya tu bhojanam |
[198]        cakṣur dadyān mano dadyād vācaṃ dadyāc ca sūnṛtām ||
eṣa sādhāraṇo dharmaś cāturvarṇyo ‘bravīn manuḥ |
prīyate svāgatenāgnir āsanena śatakratuḥ ||
pitaraḥ pādaśaucena bhojanena prajāpatiḥ |
viprapādodakaklinnā yāvat tiṣṭhati medinī ||
tāvat puṣkarapātreṇa pibanti pitaro jalam |
yasyādhvakhinnāḥ pathikā gṛham āyānti karṣitāḥ ||
pūjyante tṛṇabhūmyādyaiḥ pitaras tena putriṇaḥ |
“karṣitāḥ” pīḍitāḥ |
“atithaye dadyād” ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ [67.46] |
pratiśrayaṃ tathā śayyāṃ pādābhyaṅgaṃ sadīpakam |
pratyekadānenāpnoti gopradānasamaṃ phalam ||
yamaḥ |
yeṣām anaśnann atithir viprāṇāṃ pravrajed gṛhāt |
te vai kharatvam uṣṭratvam aśvatvam upapedire ||
yasya caiva vased vipro gṛhe kaścid abhojitaḥ |
na tasya devāḥ pitaro havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca bhuñjate ||
atithir yasya bhagnāśo gṛhāt pratinivartate |
[199]        sukṛtaṃ tasya yat kiṃcit sarvam ādāya gacchati ||
atithir yatra vai grāme bhikṣamāṇaḥ prayatnataḥ |
śete niraśanas tatra brahmahatyā vidhīyate ||
api śākaṃ pacānasya śiloñchenāpi jīvataḥ |
svadeśe paradeśe vā nātithir vimanā bhavet ||
agnayo ‘tithim icchanti so ‘tithiḥ svargam icchati |
svargo ‘pīcchati dātāraṃ samyak sukṛtakāriṇam ||
parāśaraḥ [1.45] |
atithir yasya bhagnāśo gṛhāt pratinivartate |
na tasya pitaro ‘śnanti daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca ||
agnihotraṃ jaratkanyā dvāre ca paśubandhanam |
atithir vaiśvadevānte nirdahanty avamānitāḥ ||
“jarat” jarāyuktā | “dvāre paśubandhanam” kāle yavasodakādyadāne sati |
devalaḥ |
na pṛcched gotracaraṇaṃ svādhāyaṃ deśajanmanī |
bhikṣito brāhmaṇair annaṃ dadyād evāvicārayan ||
atithir gṛham abhyetya yasya pratinivartate |
[200]        asaṃskṛto nirāśaś ca sadyo hanti ca tatkulam ||
yamaḥ |
atithiṃ pūjayed yas tu śrāntaṃ cādṛṣṭapūrvakam |
savṛṣaṃ gośataṃ tena dattaṃ syād iti me matiḥ ||
na pṛcched gotracaraṇaṃ deśaṃ nāma kulaṃ śrutam |
adhvano ‘bhyāgataṃ vipraṃ bhojanārtham upasthitam ||
deśaṃ nāma kulaṃ vidyāṃ pṛṣṭvā yo ‘nnaṃ prayacchati |
na sa tatphalam āpnoti datvā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
parāśara-śātātapau |
na pṛcched gotracaraṇaṃ svādhyāyaṃ janma caiva hi |
cittaṃ hi bhāvayet tasmin vyāsaḥ svayam upāgataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
deśaṃ gotraṃ kulaṃ vidyām annārthe yo nivedayet |
vaivasvateṣu dharmeṣu vāntāśī sa nirucyate ||
manuḥ [3.108] |
vaiśvadeve tu nirvṛtte yady anyo ‘tithir āvrajet |
[201]        tasmād annaṃ yathāśakti pradadyān na baliṃ haret ||
“vaiśvadeve” sarvārthapāke | “nirvṛtte” kṛtaprayojane, niḥśeṣa iti yāvat | “atithiḥ” atithilakṣaṇopetaḥ | “baliṃ hared” iti manuṣyayajñavyatiriktapākayajñopalakṣaṇam |
atha yājñavalkyaḥ [1.111] |
adhvanīno ‘tithir jñeyaḥ śrotriyo vedapāragaḥ |
mānyāv etau gṛhasthasya brahmalokam abhīpsataḥ ||
“adhvanīnaḥ” satatam adhvagāmī | “śrotriyaḥ” ekaśākhādhyāyy api |
tathā ca baudhayanaḥ |
ekāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati | iti |
manur viṣṇuḥ śātātapaś ca dvitīye |
ekarātraṃ tu nivasann atithir brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate ||
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ vipraṃ sāṅgatikaṃ tathā |
[202]         upasthitaṃ gṛhe vidyād bhāryā yaatrāgnayo ‘pi vā ||
ayam arthaḥ — ekāṃ rātriṃ vasato ‘tithitvam, na tataḥ param | ekarātrer api na kṣatriyādeḥ | “ekagrāmīṇaḥ” ekagrāmavāsī | “sāṅgatikaḥ” yaś citraparihāsakathādibhiḥ sarvaiḥ saha saṃgacchet | etau ca vaiśvadevakālopasthitāv api nātithī | “gṛhe” nityanivāsasthāne | na kevalaṃ gṛha evātithiḥ | kiṃ tu bhāryādinā yoṣitasya grāmantare vane vā, bhāryā agnayaś ca yatra bhavanti tatrāpy upasthitam atithiṃ vijānīyād iti medhātithiḥ |
yamaḥ |
tithiparvotsavāḥ sarve tyaktā yena mahātmanā |
so ‘tithiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ śeṣān abhyāgatān viduḥ ||
vrati yatir ekarātraṃ nivasann ucyate ‘tithiḥ |
yasmān nityaṃ ca vasati tasmāt tam atithiṃ viduḥ ||
“parvāṇi” amāvāsyādīni | “utsavāḥ” vivāhādayaḥ | “tithiḥ” tadvyatiriktā dīpamālikāpratipadādiḥ | ete dharmapuraskāreṇānnaviśeṣaprāptihetavo “yena tyaktāḥ” “so ‘tithiḥ” | “dharmapuraskāro nānyaprayojanaḥ so ‘tithiḥ” ity āpastambavacanāt [2.3.5] |
manur viṣṇuḥ śātātapaś ca dvitīye |
ekarātraṃ tu nivasann atithir brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate ||
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ vipraṃ sāṅgatikaṃ tathā |
upasthitaṃ gṛhe vidyād bhāryā yaatrāgnayo ‘pi vā ||
[203]  gautamaḥ [5.41] |
asamānagrāmo vātithir ekarātriko ‘dhivṛkṣasūryopasthāyī |
“ekarātrikaḥ” ekarātravasanaśīlaḥ | adhivṛkṣasūryaśabdaḥ sāyaṃkālapara iti maskari-bhartṛyajñau |
parāśaraḥ śātātapaś ca prathame |
acintitam anāhūtaṃ deśakāla upasthitam |
atithiṃ taṃ vijānīyān nātithiḥ pūrvam āgataḥ ||
kṣudhārtas tṛṣitaḥ śrānto dūrād adhvana āgataḥ |
taṃ pūjayet prayatnena so ‘tithiḥ svargasaṃkramaḥ ||
“deśo” bhāryāgnisaṃnihitaḥ | “kālo” vaiśvadevāntādiḥ |
manu-viṣṇū |
itarān api sakhyādīn saṃprītyā gṛham āgatān |
prakṛtyānnaṃ yathāśakti bhojayet saha bhāryayā ||
“sakhyādīn”ityādiśabdena saṃbandhibāndhavānām upādānam | “prakṛtya” prakarṣeṇa satkṛtya | “saha bhāryayā” [204] bhāryāsahito gṛhasthas tān “bhojayet” | bhāryāpi pariveṣaṇādi kuryād ity arthaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.108] |
bhojayec cāgatān kāle sakhisaṃbandhibāndhavān |
tathā [1.113] |
ahaḥśeṣaṃ sahāsīta śiṣṭair iṣṭaiś ca bandhubhiḥ |
“kāle” bhojanakāle |
āpastambaḥ [2.5.4–11] |
samāvṛttaṃ ced ācāryo ‘bhyāgacchet tam abhimukho ‘bhyāgamya tasyopasaṃgṛhya na bībhatsamāna udakam upaspṛśet puraskṛtyopasthāpya yathopadeśaṃ pūjayet | āsane śayane bhakṣye bhojye vāsasi vā saṃnihite nihīnataravṛttiḥ syāt | tiṣṭhan savyena pāṇinānugṛhyācāryam ācāmayet | anyaṃ vā samudetam | sthānāsanacaṅkramaṇasmiteṣv anucikīrṣan | saṃnihite mūtrapurīṣavātakarmoccairbhāṣāhāsaniṣṭhīvanadantaskhalananiśṛṅkhaṇa-bhrukṣepaṇatālananiṣṭhyānīti | dāre prajāyāṃ copasparśanabhāṣā visrambhapūrvāḥ parivarjayet | vākyena vākyasya pratighātam ācāryāya varjayet | śreyasāṃ ca |
[205] “tasyopasaṃgṛhya” pādāv iti śeṣaḥ | “na bībhatsamāna udakam upaspṛśet” ācāryasya caraṇaprakṣālane sparśe ghṛṇāṃ kurvan hastaprakṣālanaṃ na kuryāt | “puraskṛtya” caraṇaprakṣālanānulepanādinātithyaṃ kṛtvā | “upasthāpya” sarvam etad dhanaśarīrādi bhavadīyam iti nivedya | "yathopadeśaṃ" yathāśāstram | "saṃn”hite" gurau | “tiṣṭhan ācāryam ācāmayet” nopaviṣṭaḥ | “savyena pāṇinānugṛhya” dakṣiṇena pāṇinā gṛhītaṃ jalapātraṃ paścād vāmenāpi gṛhītvā | “anyaṃ vā samudetam” ācāmayed ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | tac cācamanam ācāryavad eva samudetasyāpy uktasatkārādipūrvakaṃ boddhavyam, tathāvidhasyaiva prakṛtatvāt | “anucikīrṣan” snānādikriyāsu vartamāne ārye svayam api paścāt tathā | “saṃnihite” ācārya iti śeṣaḥ | “dantaskhalanaṃ” dantadhāvanam | “niśṛṅkhaṇaṃ” nāsāmalotsarjanam | “tālanaṃ” hastavādyam | “niṣṭhyaṃ” viṭśleṣaḥ | ādiśabdena nṛtyagītavādyādyupasaṃgrahaḥ | “prajāyām” apatye | “upasparśanam” aṅgapariṣvaṅgaḥ | “bhāṣā” sambhāṣaṇam, etat praṇayapūrvakaṃ varjayet | “śreyasāṃ” śreṣṭhapuruṣāṇām apy anyeṣām, “pratighātaṃ varjayed” ity anvayaḥ |
gautamaḥ [5.27–32]
ācāryapitṛsakhīnāṃ tu nivedya vacanakriyā | [206] ṛtvigācāryaśvaśurapitṛvya-mātulānām upasthāne madhuparkaḥ | saṃvatsare punaḥ | yajñavivāhayor arvāk | rājñaś ca śrotriyasya | aśrotriyasyāsanodake |
“ācāryapitṛsakhīnām” gṛhāgatānām | “nivedya” siddham annādi | “vacanakriyā” yat te bruvate tat karaṇam | ṛtvigādīnāṃ tu “upasthāne” gṛhāgamane sati madhuparkeṇa pūjā kartavyā | “saṃvatsare punaḥ” “upasthāne madhuparkaḥ” ity anvayaḥ | “yajñavivāhayor arvāg” iti, yajñavivāhayor upasthāne saṃvatsarād arvāg api madhuparko deya ity arthaḥ | “rājñaḥ” iti, śrotriyasya sato rājñaḥ saṃvatsarād arvāg api madhuparko deyaḥ | “aśrotriyasya” rājña āsanodake eva deye na madhuparkaḥ |
manuḥ [3.119–120] |
rājartviksnātakagurūn priyaśvaśuramātulān |
arhayen madhuparkeṇa pratisaṃvatsarāt punaḥ ||
rājā ca śrotriyaś caiva yajñakarmaṇy upasthitau |
madhuparkeṇa saṃpūjyo na tv ayajña iti sthitiḥ ||
[207] yājñavalkyaḥ [1.110] |
