pratisaṃvatsaraṃ tv arghyāḥ snātakācāryapārthivāḥ |
priyo vivāhyaś ca tathā yajñaṃ praty ṛtvijaḥ punaḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.35–37] |
pūjyān pūjayet |
ṛṣividvannṛpavaramātulaśvaśurartvijaḥ |
ete ‘rghyāḥ śāstravihitāḥ smṛtāḥ kālavibhāgaśaḥ ||
ṛṣividvannṛpāḥ prāptāḥ kriyārambhe varartvijau |
mātulaśvaśurau pūjyau saṃvatsaragāv iti ||
“ṛṣiḥ” mantradṛk | “vidvān” vedārthavit | “nṛpaḥ” abhiṣiktaḥ kṣatriyo dharmeṇa prajāpālakaḥ | “prāptāḥ” gṛham iti śeṣaḥ | “kriyārambheṣu” yajñavivāhādiṣu |
āpastambaḥ [2.8.5–9] |
gomadhuparkārho vedādhyāyaḥ | ācārya ṛtvik snātako rājā vā dharmayuktaḥ | ācāryāyartvije śvaśurāya rājña iti parisaṃvatsarād upatiṣṭhadbhyo gaur madhuparkaś ca | dadhi madhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ madhuparkaḥ payo vā madhusaṃsṛṣṭam | abhāva udakam |
[208] “vedādhyāyāḥ” adhītavedaḥ | “parisaṃvatsarāt” saṃvatsar saṃvatsare |
punar āpastambaḥ [2.9.5–6] |
sarvān vaiśvadevabhāginaḥ kurvītāśvacāṇḍalebhyaḥ | nānarhadbhyo dadyād ity eke |
“sarvān” annārthinaḥ | “vaiśvadevabhāginaḥ” vaiśvadevānnasaṃvibhāginaḥ kuryāt | “ā śvacāṇḍālebhyaḥ” ity āṅabhividhau | “anarhadbhyaḥ” śvapāṣaṇdacaṇḍālādibhyaḥ | anarhatām adāne nāsti doṣaḥ, dāne tu puṇyam iti vyavasthā |
āpastamba-baudhāyanau |
ye nityā bhaktikāḥ syus teṣām anurodhena saṃvibhāgo vihitaḥ | kāmam ātmānaṃ bhāryāṃ putraṃ coparundhyān na tv eva dāsakarmakaram | tathā cātmano ‘nuparodhaṃ ca kuryād yathā karmasu samarthaḥ syāt |
“bhaktikāḥ” bhaktārhāḥ |
manuḥ [4.32] |
śaktito ‘pacamānebhyo dātavyaṃ gṛhamedhinām |
saṃvibhāgaś ca bhūtānāṃ kartavyo ‘nuparodhataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
apacantam atikramya pacantaṃ yas tu bhojayet |
[209]        śauṇḍikānāṃ vrajed yoniṃ varṣāṇām adhikaṃ śatam ||
vasiṣtho manuś ca dvitīye śloke |
mahayed yathāśakti cānnena sarvabhūtāni |
gṛhastha eva yajate gṛhasthas tapyate tapaḥ |
caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ tu gṛhasthaś ca viśiṣyate ||
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃsthitim |
evam āśramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim ||
yathā mātaram āśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ |
evaṃ gṛhastham āśritya sarve jīvanti bhikṣukāḥ ||
“mahayet” pūjayet |
manuḥ [3.78–79] |
yasmāt trayo ‘py āśramiṇo jñānenānnena cānvaham |
gṛhasthair eva dhāryante tasmāc chreṣṭho gṛhāśramī ||
sa saṃdhāryaḥ prayatnena svargam akṣayam icchatā |
sukhaṃ cehecchatātyantaṃ yo ‘dhāryo durbalendriyaiḥ ||
“saḥ” gṛhāśramaḥ | “durbalendriyaiḥ” asaṃyatendriyaiḥ, “adhāryo” dhārayitum aśakyaḥ | asaṃyatendriyāṇāṃ svādhīna[210]viṣayalābhe sati niyatdharmānuṣṭhānāsaṃbhavāt | tasmāc chreṣṭho gṛhāśramī |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
āśramāṇāṃ samutpattir vardhanaṃ pālanaṃ tathā |
gṛhasthāj jāyate samyak tena so ‘bhyadhikaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
catvāro hy āśramā brahmacārī gṛhastho vānaprastho bhikṣukaś ceti | sarve te gṛhasthamūlās tadupajīvinaḥ |
dakṣaḥ [2.57–60] |
devaiś caiva manuṣyaiś ca tiryagbhiś copajīvyate |
gṛhasthaḥ pratyahaṃ yasmāt tasmāc chreṣṭho gṛhāśramī ||
trayāṇām āśramāṇāṃ ca gṛhastho yonir ucyate |
sīdamānena tenaiva sīdantīhetare trayaḥ ||
mūlaṃ prāṇo bhavet skandhaḥ skandhāc chākhās ca pallavāḥ |
mūlenaiva vinaṣṭena sarvam eva vinaśyati ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena rakṣaṇīyo gṛhī sadā |
rājñā cānyais tribhiḥ pūjyo mānanīyaś ca sarvadā ||
[211] atha gautamaḥ [5.26] |
bhojayet pūrvam atithikumāravyādhitagarbhiṇīsvavāsinīsthavirān jaghanyāṃś ca |
ātmanaḥ “pūrvam” etān “bhajayet” | “kumārāḥ” bālāḥ | “svavāsinyaḥ” ūḍhā duhitaraḥ | “jaghanyāḥ” bhṛtyāḥ |
budhaḥ |
atithiṃ pūjayet | bhṛtyān bandhūn poṣyavargaṃ ca | śvavāyasādibhyo bhūmau datvā brāhmaṇān bhojayet pitrarthān |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.105] |
bālasvavāsinīvṛddhagarbhiṇyāturakanyakāḥ |
saṃbhojyātithibhṛtyāṃś ca dampatyoḥ śeṣabhojanam ||
devalaḥ |
garbhiṇyāturavṛddheṣu bālaśrāntātithiṣv api |
aprāśiteṣu tat pūrvam anivedya ca nāśnīyāt ||
manu-viṣṇū [3.114] |
suvāsinīḥ kumārāṃś ca rogiṇo garbhiṇīs tathā |
[212]        atithibhyo ‘gra evaitān bhojayed avicārayan ||
adatvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sa bhuñjāno na jānāti śvagṛdhrair jagdhim ātmanaḥ ||
“agre” prathamam | atra ca yad gautamādismṛtiṣv atithibhojanasya bālādibhojanāt prāthamyapratipādanam, tad atither abhyarhitatvapratipādanārtham | manāv apy atithibālādīnāṃ bhojane parasparaṃ paurvāparyam avivakṣitam | kiṃ tu dampatyos taccheṣabhojanam abhimatam |
adatvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ity ekavākyatayaiteṣu bhuktavatsu svayaṃ bhuñjītety atra tātparyam |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.13.5] |
agre bhojayed atithīn antarvatnīr anantaram |
bālān vṛddhāṃs tathā dīnān vyādhitāṃś ca viśeṣataḥ ||
“dīnāḥ” duḥkhitāḥ |
yamaḥ |
vidvān agniparo nityam arcayet pitṛdevatāḥ |
gurūn atithibālāṃś ca tarpayet pūrvam eva tu ||
ātmānaṃ tarpayet paścān niyato vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
[213]        strīśūdraṃ tarpayet paścād eṣa dhamaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
“guravaḥ” pitrādayaḥ |
bhojayed ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.5–9] |
svagṛhyānāṃ kumārībālavṛddhataruṇaprajātās tato ‘parān gṛhyān | śvacāṇḍāla-patitavāyasebhyo bhūmau baliṃ nirvapet | śūdrāyocchiṣṭam anucchiṣṭaṃ vā dadyāt | śeṣaṃ dampatī bhuñjīyātām | sarvopayoge tu punaḥ kākaḥ kāryaḥ |
“taruṇaprajātāḥ” navaprasūtā nāryaḥ | “gṛhyāḥ” gṛhabhavāḥ paricārakā iti yāvat | ayaṃ cocchiṣṭānucchiṣṭadānavikalpa āśritānāśritaśūdraviṣayaḥ sadasacchūdraviṣayo vā | “sarvopayoge” sarvasyānnasyātithibhojane kṛte yadānyat prayojanam asti tadā tadarthaṃ “punaḥ pākaḥ” kartavyaḥ | na punar vaiśvadevādi kāryam |
manuḥ [3.116–118] |
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi |
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścād avaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī ||
devān ṛṣīn manuṣyāṃś ca pitṝn gṛhyāś ca devatāḥ |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścād gṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhug bhavet ||
[214]        aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacaty ātmakāraṇāt |
yajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ hy eva satām annaṃ vidhīyate ||
ṛṣipūjā brahmayajñena |
tathā [3.285] |
vidhasāśī bhaven nityaṃ cāmṛtabhojanaḥ |
vighaso bhṛtyaśeṣaṃ tu yajñaśeṣam athāmṛtam ||
“bhṛtyaḥ” avaśyapoṣaṇīyaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.13.7] |
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ mātāpitros tathā guroḥ |
maunī vighasam aśnīyād evaṃ dharmo na hīyate ||
yamaḥ |
amṛtāśī bhaven nityaṃ vighasāśī tu vā punaḥ |
amṛtaṃ yajñaśeṣaṃ tu haviṣyaṃ bhojanaṃ smṛtam |
bhṛtyaśeṣaṃ tu yo ‘śnīyāt tam āhur vighasāśinam ||
devalaḥ |
snātvā prakṣālya pādau ca sraggandhālaṃkṛto śuciḥ |
pañcayajñāvaśiṣṭaṃ ca yo bhuṅkte so ‘mṛtāśanaḥ ||
[215] hārītaḥ |
hutaṃ nivāpyāhutaprahutprāśitāvaśiṣṭam amṛtaṃ prāhur yam aśnuvanto gṛhasthavānaprasthād eva sāyujyam āpnuvanti |
yama-paiṭhīnasī |
nātmārthaṃ pācayed annaṃ nātmārthaṃ pācayet paśūn |
devārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā pacamāno na lipyate ||
yamaḥ |
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacaty ātmakāraṇāt |
indriyaprītijananaṃ vṛthāpākaṃ vivarjayet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
devān bhṛtyātithīṃś caiva pitṝn ātmānam eva ca |
prakṛtān nāvamanyeta teṣāṃ śiṣṭaṃ samaśnuyāt ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [29.39–40] |
kuṭumbino bhojanīyāḥ svasamaṃ vibhave sati |
śrīmantaṃ jñātim āsādya yo jñātir avasīdati ||
sīdatā yat kṛtaṃ tena tat pāpaṃ sa samaśnute |
“svasamam” ātmabhojyatulyabhojyaṃ tān api bhojayed ity arthaḥ |
[216] bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
akṛtvā vaiśvadevaṃ tu ye bhuñjanty abudhāḥ khaga |
tathā vinā tu sākṣyeṇa mṛgayoniṃ vrajanti te ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.5.17–18] |
na caiva kvacid adatvā bhuñjīta | athāpy atrānnagītau ślokāv udāharanti |
yo mām adatvā pitṛdevatābhyo
bhṛtyātithīnāṃ svasuhṛjjanasya |
saṃpannam aśnan viṣam atti mohāt
tam admy ahaṃ tasya ca mṛtyur asmi ||
hutāgnihotraḥ kṛtavaiśvadevo
bhuktātithir bhṛtyanāvaśiṣṭam |
tuṣṭaḥ śuciḥ śraddadhad atti yo māṃ
tasyāmṛtaṃ syāṃ sa ca māṃ bhunakti || iti |
[217] dakṣaḥ [2.61–62] |
gṛhastho ‘pi kriyāyukto na gṛheṇa gṛhī bhavet |
na caiva putradāreṇa svakarmaparivarjitaḥ ||
asnātvā cāpy ahutvā cāpy adatvā ye tu bhuñjate |
devādīnām ṛṇī bhūtvā narakaṃ te vrajanty adhaḥ ||
tathā [2.65–66]
eko hi bhuṅkte hy aśanam aparo ‘nnena bhujyate |
na bhujyate sa evaiko yo bhuṅkte ca sasākṣikam ||
samvibhāgaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhug bhavet |
“sākṣiṇaḥ” atithibhṛtyādayaḥ |
yama-śātātapau |
adatvā vaiśvadevaṃ tu bhuñjate ye dvijādhamāḥ |
vṛthā tenānnapākena kākayoniṃ vrajanti te ||
āpastambaḥ [2.15.13] |
yasyāgnau na kriyate yasya cāgraṃ na dīyate na tad bhoktavyam |
“yasya” annasya |
jābāliḥ |
asnātāśī malaṃ bhuṅkte ajapī pūyaśoṇitam |
[218]        ahutvā tu kṛmīn bhuṅkte adatvā viṣabhojanam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.121] |
bhāryāratiḥ śucir bhṛtyabhartā śrāddhakriyārataḥ |
namaskāreṇa mantreṇa pañcayajñān na hāpayet ||
parāśaraḥ [1.38] |
ṣaṭkarmābhirato nityaṃ devatātithipūjakaḥ |
hutaśeṣaṃ tu bhuñjāno brāhmaṇo nāvasīdati ||
ṣaṭkarmāṇi yajanādīni vakṣyamāṇāni |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakāṇḍe ‘tithipūjā ||
[219]
7
atha bhojanavidhiḥ
tatra dakṣaḥ [2.56, 67] |
pañcame ca tathā bhāge saṃvibhāgo yathārhataḥ |
devapitṛmanuṣyāṇāṃ kīṭānāṃ copadiśyate ||
saṃvibhāgaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhug bhavet |
“saṃvibhāgaḥ” saṃvibhajya pratipādanam |
devalaḥ |
snātvā prakṣālya pādau ca sraggandhālaṃkṛtaḥ śuciḥ |
pañcayajñāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu bhuṅkte yaḥ so ‘mṛtāśanaḥ ||
upalipte śucau deśe pādau prakṣālya vāgyataḥ |
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta śuciḥ pīṭham adhiṣṭhitaḥ ||
[220] baudhāyanaḥ [2.5.21; 2.6.1–2] |
suprakṣālitapāṇipādo ‘pa ācamya śucau saṃvṛte deśe ‘nnm upahṛtam upasaṃgṛhya kāmakrodhadrohalobhamohān apahatya sarvābhir aṅgulībhiḥ śabdam akurvan prāśnīyāt | na piṇḍaśeṣaṃ pātryām utsṛjet | māṃsamatsyatila-madhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ prāśyāpa upaspṛśyāgnim abhispṛśet |
“saṃvṛte” vastrādinā pariveṣṭite, upaviśyeti śeṣaḥ | “upahṛtam” upanītam | “upasaṃgṛhya” samīpasthe pātre prītipūrvakaṃ samyag gṛhītvā | “śabdam akurvan” bhakṣaṇe kriyamāṇe sītkārādidhvanim akurvan | “piṇḍaśeṣam” aśituṃ gṛhītasya śeṣam |
brahmapurāṇe |
viśed bhojanabhūmiṃ tu suprakṣālitapāṇimān |
āsanasthas tu yo darpāt paṅktimadhya upaspṛśet ||
sa sarveṣāṃ tu yat pāpaṃ tad gṛhṇāti narādhamaḥ |
[221]        tasmād balir upaspṛśya svācāntaḥ praviśed gṛham ||
upalipte same sthāne śucau ślakṣṇāsanānvite |
caturasraṃ trikoṇaṃ tu vartulaṃ cārdhacandrakam ||
kartavyam ānupūrvyeṇa brāhmaṇādiṣu maṇḍalam |
brahmarudrendracandrārkā vasavo maṇḍalāntarāt ||
niveditaṃ narair annaṃ yasmād gṛhṇanti nānyathā |
akṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ ye tu bhuñjate ‘dhamayonayaḥ ||
teṣāṃ tu yakṣarakṣāṃsi haranty annasya tadbalam ||
manuḥ [2.52] |
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte hy udaṅmukhaḥ ||
“āyuṣyam” āyuṣe hitam | “yaśasyam” yaśase hitam | “śriyam” “ṛtam” ity atrobhayatra kāmayamāna ity adhyāhāreṇa parisamāptiḥ | “ṛtam” satyaṃ yajñaṃ vā |
hārītaḥ |
prāṅmukha āyuḥkāmo ‘śnīyāt | yaśo’rthī dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ | śrīkāmaḥ pratyaṅmukhaḥ | nodaṅmukhaḥ | nādhiśayane [222] nāsane vāśnīyāt | na kārṣṇāyāse | na mṛtpātre | na bhinnāvakīrṇe |
“adhiśayane” upariśayane khaṭvādau | “āsane” pīṭhādau, annapātraṃ nidhāyeti śeṣaḥ | “kārṣṇāyse” lohapātre | “mṛtpātre” śarāvādau | “bhinne” sphuṭite | “avakīrṇe” durbrāhmaṇādibhojanādināpavitrīkṛte |
vāmanapurāṇe |
bhuñjīta naiveha ca dakṣiṇāmukho
na ca pratīcīm abhibhojanīyam |
ayaṃ ca pratyaṅmukhodaṅmukhaniṣedho niṣkāmaviṣayaḥ | sakāmasya,
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte hy udaṅmukhaḥ |
iti mukhadvayabhojanasya vidhānāt | dakṣiṇāmukhabhojananiṣedho jīvanmātṛkaviṣayaḥ |
tathā ca puṣṭikāmādhikāre āpastambaḥ [2.19.1–2] |
vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukho bhuñjīta | anāyuṣyaṃ tv evaṃmukhasya bhojanaṃ mātur upadiśanti | iti |
[223] viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.79–80] |
naikavastradharo nārdrapāṇipādo nareśvara |
viśuddhavadanaḥ prīto bhuñjīta na vidiṅmukhaḥ ||
prāṅmukhodaṅmukho vāpi na caivānyamanā naraḥ |
“vidiṅmukhaḥ” nāgneyādikoṇābhimukhaḥ | “prāṅmukho udaṅmukho vā” ity atra bhuñjītety anvayaḥ |
yamaḥ |
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta pañcārdro vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
bhuñjīta hy ārdrapādas tu nārdrapādaḥ svapen niśi ||
ārdrapādas tu bhuñjānaḥ śatavarṣāṇi jīvati |
“pañcārdraḥ” karacaraṇavadanārdraḥ | “pañcārdraḥ” ity abhidhāya “ārdrapādaḥ” iti punar abhidhānam ārdrapādatāyā ādarātiśayārtham |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
snāto yathāvat kṛtvā ca devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
praśastaratnapāṇis tu bhuñjīta prayato gṛhī ||
kṛtavāpo hute cāgnau śuddhavastradharo nṛpa |
“praśastaratnāni” gāruḍādīni | “kṛtavāpaḥ” devādyarthe kṛtanirvāpaḥ |
[224] manuḥ |
sāyaṃprātar manuṣyāṇām aśanaṃ devanirmitam |
nāntarā bhojanaṃ kāryam agnihotrasamo vidhiḥ ||
sāyaṃprātaḥśabdāv atra rātridivasaparau |
tathā ca chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [2.33] |
munibhir dviraśanam uktaṃ
viprāṇāṃ martyalokavāsināṃ nityam |
ahani ca tathā tamasvinyāṃ
sārdhapraharayāmāntaḥ ||
atrāpy ahaḥśabdo dakṣānusārāt pañcamāharbhāgaparaḥ | “tamasvinī” rātriḥ | “sardhapraharayāmāntaḥ” sārdhaprathamapraharamadhye |
brahmapurāṇe |
pātreṣv arthānurūpeṣu putrabhṛtyātmajaiḥ saha |
bhūmau pātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya maunenānnaṃ tu bhojayet ||
pavitrapāṇiḥ puṇyaṃ ca labhet tatrāghamarṣaṇam |
“arthānurūpeṣu” svavibhavānusāreṇa sauvarṇarājatādiṣu | “aghamarṣaṇam” aghamarṣaṇajanyaṃ puṇyam |
[225] manuḥ [2.54–55] |
pūjayed aśanaṃ nityam adyāc caitad akutsayan |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyet prasīdec cāpy abhinandec ca sarvaśaḥ ||
pūjitaṃ hy aśanaṃ nityaṃ balam ūrjaṃ ca yacchati |
apūjitaṃ tu tad bhuktam ubhayaṃ nāśayed idam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
annaṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇamyādau prāñjaliḥ kathayet tataḥ |
asmākaṃ nityam astv etad iti bhaktyā ca vandayet ||
annaṃ viṣṇuṃ svayaṃ prāha nityam eva janārdanaḥ |
yo māṃ dadāti prāṇāya bhuñjānaḥ śāstravid dvijaḥ ||
prāṇārthaṃ māṃ sadā dhyāyet sa māṃ saṃpūjayet sadā |
vāmena pāṇinā pātraṃ gṛhītvā bhuvi saṃsthitam ||
tejo ‘sīti japaṃs tv annaṃ praṇamed amṛtaṃ ca tat |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.28] |
upaghātād ṛte doṣaṃ nānnasyodīrayed budhaḥ |
[226] upaghātaḥ śvakākādisparśaḥ |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
paridhānam apośānaṃ pūrvam ācchādanaṃ param |
bhavaty annam anagnaṃ hi sottarīyaṃ tathāmṛtam ||
pūrvaṃ yad “apośanaṃ” tat “paridhānam”, param “apośānam” “ācchādanam” |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.106] |
apośānenopariṣṭād adhastād aśnatā tathā |
anagnam amṛtaṃ caiva kāryam annaṃ dvijanmanā ||
brahmapurāṇe |
apośānaṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt sarvatīrthamayaṃ ca tat |
amṛtopastaraṇam asīti viṣṇor annamayasya ca ||
hastena laṅghayen nānnaṃ sodakena kadācan |
dambhādyo laṅghayed bhuñjaṃs tenānnaṃ nihitaṃ bhavet ||
hataṃ cānnam abhakṣyatvaṃ tasya yāni durātmanaḥ |
pañcagrāsāṃs tu bhuktvādau kvacid veśmani saṅkaṭe ||
[227]         pātram uddhṛtya śeṣaṃ tu bhakṣayet sāṃkarād bhayāt |
pitrye karmaṇi bhuñjāno bhūmau pātraṃ na cālayet ||
tathā |
prāṇebhyas tv atha pañcabhyaḥ svāhāpraṇavasaṃyutāḥ |
pañcāhutīs tu juhuyāt pralayāgninibheṣu ca ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.89] |
pañcagrāsān mahāmaunaṃ prāṇādyāpyāyanāya tat |
mahāmaunagrahaṇāt sarvathā dhvaninivṛttir abhimatāh |
brahmapurāṇe |
prāṇe tṛpte tu tṛpyante nityaṃ svargārkacakṣuṣaḥ |
vyāne tṛpte tu tṛpyante śrotre tvakkakubhas tathā ||
apāne tṛpte tṛpyante vahnivāgbhūmayaḥ sadā |
samāne tṛpte tṛpyante manaḥparjanyavidyutaḥ ||
udāne tṛpte tṛpyante vāyvākāśau ca sarvadā |
tṛpteṣv eteṣu tṛptaṃ syāj jagat sadasadātmakam ||
prajāḍhyo brahmacārī syāt paśvannair vardhate ca saḥ |
prāṇāgnihotravid vipro dātā ca syāt samṛddhimān ||
tathā |
bhuktvāmṛtāpidhānārthaṃ pibet toyaṃ sakṛt sakṛt |
[228]        yenānnaṃ na bhaven nagnaṃ jīvabhūtaṃ jagattraye ||
evaṃ bhuñjan sadā vipro jñātiprādhānyam āpnuyāt |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
śrutismṛtisamāpannaṃ devadravyaguṇānvitam |
devatoyair yathākḷptaṃ prokṣitaṃ śiṣṭabhojanam ||
“śrutismṛtisamāpannam” śrutismṛtibhyām avagatam | “devadravyaguṇānvitam” yajñiyam | “devatoyair yathākḷptam” yena prakāreṇa kḷptaṃ tatprakāram eva bhojanam |
hārītaḥ |
atha brāhmaṇānāṃ bhojanavidhiṃ pravakṣyāmy aham | snāto ‘hatavāsāḥ pavitrātmā prāṅmukha āsīnaḥ pāṇī prakṣālya daśahotāraṃ nigadyendraṃ gaccha svāheti dhyāyed avyaktaṃ puruṣam | oṃ tejo ‘sīty annādyam āhriyamāṇam abhimantrayet | dyaus tvā dadātv iti dīyamānam | bhūs tvā pratigṛhṇātv iti pratigṛhya |
agnir asmi janmanā jātavedā
ghṛtaṃ me cakṣur amṛtaṃ ma āsan |
[229]        arkas tridhātū rajaso vimāno
‘jasro gharmo havir asmi nāma ||
aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya
pūrvaṃ devebhyo amṛtasya nābhiḥ |
yo mā dadāti sa i devam āvāḥ
aham annam annam adantam admi ||”
ity annam abhimantrayet |
“antaś carasi bhūteṣu guhāyāṃ viśvatomukhaḥ |
tvaṃ yajñas tvaṃ vaṣaṭkāras tvaṃ viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ||
āpo jyotī raso ‘mṛtaṃ brahma bhūr buvaḥ svar om |”
amṛtopastaraṇam asīty apaḥ prāśya prāṇāya svāhety ekaikayāhutyā sarvān prāṇān prīṇāti | samastā nigiret | atha maitrāyaṇer matam | āyuṣe svāheti daśaitāḥ prāṇāhutīr [230] āyuṣkāmo bhuñjītety evaṃ hy āha | yathākāmaṃ samaśnute | antaś carasīty abhidhāyedam amṛtayonau satye jyotiṣi paramātmani juhomi svāheti prīṇāty ātmānam | tena prāṇān āpyāyasveti hṛdayadeśam ālabhya japati | prāṇānāṃ granthir asīti prāṇadeśam | viṣṇor jaṭharam asīti nābhideśam | “yo devānām asi śreṣṭha ugra tanti caro vṛṣāmṛtatvam asmabhyaṃ rudraitad astu hutaṃ tava svāhā” iti punar api hṛdayadeśam ālabhya japati | sāvitrīṃ cānubhāṣya trir apo vasānā bahūnāṃ rasabhuk samastānnapākakarmeṣṭiḥ prīyatāṃ viśvabhug iti dhyāyed avyaktaṃ avyaktaṃ puruṣam | mūtrapurīṣe rathyām ākramya śmaśānaṃ vācāntaḥ punar ācāmet | evaṃ yo brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte saḥ puṇyatamo bhavati | paṅktipāvano bhavati | ubhayataḥ [231] pakṣāṇi tarpayati | devāṃś ca tarpayati | pitṝṃś ca tarpayati | manuṣyāṃś ca tarpayati | abhojyasyānnaṃ punāti | brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchatīty evaṃ hy āheti |
“daśahotā” “cittiḥ sruk cittam ājyam” ityādiko mantraḥ | “indraṃ gaccha svāhā” iti mantrāntena “cittiḥ sruk” ityādi daśahotṛmantrapradarśanam, evaṃ mantram uccārya | “avyaktaṃ puruṣam” jagatkāraṇam | “prāṇān” iti prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānānāṃ grahaṇam | āhitigrahaṇam anāmikāṅguṣṭhamātragrāhyānnagrahaṇārtham | tathā chandogopaniṣadi vākyakāraḥ: “samāṃ prathamāhutim ity āhutidharmaprajñaptiḥ stokena gṛhītaiḥ” iti | etac ca vṛddhadraviḍavivaraṇakāreṇa vyākhyātam | “stokatā nirdiśyate, anāmikāṅguṣṭhena ca yāvad gṛhyate tāvat | kutas tadgṛhītaiḥ | tathā hy āhutir gṛhyamāṇā dṛśyate” iti jayasvāmī | kṣiptām āhutiṃ “nigiret” na dantabhinnāṃ kuryāt | aparā  daśāhutayaḥ “āyuṣe svāhā” ityādayaḥ kāmyāḥ | “yathākāmam” ity anenaitad uktam | śeṣāhutiṣu nigaraṇe niyamo nāsti | “antaś carasīty abhiprāyaḥ” “antaś [232] carasti” ityādinā “amṛtāpidhānam asi” ity ūhitāntenābhidhāya, juhoty apa iti śeṣaḥ | “sāvitrīṃ” “triḥ” uccārya “apo vasānā” ityādimantreṇa “avyaktaṃ puruṣaṃ dhyāyet” | evaṃbhūtetikartavyatākabhojane vaiśvānaravidyāvido ‘dhikāraḥ | ayaṃ cārthaḥ puṇyatammetyādyante ‘nnam ity anuvādena sūcitaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.12.1–4, 7–16; 2.13.1–4] |
atha śālīnayāyāvarāṇām ātmayājināṃ prāṇāhutīr vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | sarvāvaśyakāvasāne saṃmṛṣṭopalipte deśe prāṅmukha upviśyoddhṛtam āhriyamāṇaṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar om ity upasthāya vācaṃ yacchet | nyastaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ pradakṣiṇam annam udakaṃ praiṣicya savyena pāṇināvimuñcann amṛtopastaraṇam asīti purastād apaḥ pītvā pañcānnena prāṇāhutīr juhoti | [233] “prāṇe niviṣṭo ‘mṛtaṃ juhomi śivo māviśāpradāhāya prāṇāya svāhā | apāne niviṣṭo ‘mṛtaṃ juhomi śivo māviśāpradāhāyāpānāya svāhā | vyāne niviṣṭo ‘mṛtaṃ juhomi śivo māviśāpradāhāya vyānāya svāhā | udāne niviṣto ‘mṛtaṃ juhomi śivo māviśāpradāhāyodānāya svāhā | samāne niviṣṭo ‘mṛtaṃ juhomi śivo māviśāpradāhāya samānāya svāhā |” iti pañcānnena prāṇāhutīr hutvā tūṣṇīṃbhūto bhūyo vratayet prajāpatiṃ manasā dhyāyan | athāpy udāharanti |
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho ‘śnīyād vāgyato ‘nnam akutsayan |
askandayaṃs tanmanāś ca bhuktvā cāgnim upaspṛśet || iti |
sarvabhakṣyāpūpakandamūlaphalamāṃsādīni dantair nāvadyet | [234] nātisuhitaḥ | amṛtāpidhānam asīty upariṣṭād apaḥ pītvācānto hṛdayadeśam abhimṛśati “prāṇānāṃ granthir asi rudro mā viśāntakas tenānnenāpyāyasva” iti | punar ācamya dakṣiṇe pādāṅguṣṭhe pāṇī niḥsrāvayati –
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo aṅguṣṭhaṃ ca samāśritaḥ |
īśaḥ sarvasya jagataḥ prabhuḥ prīṇāti viśvabhuk || iti |
hutānumantraṇam ūrdhvahastaś ca samācaret | “śraddhāyāṃ prāṇe niviśyāmṛtaṃ hutaṃ śivo māviśa | prāṇam annenāpyāyasva | śraddhāyām apāne niviśyāmṛtaṃ hutaṃ śivo māviśa | apānam annenāpyāyasva | śraddhāyāṃ vyāne niviṣyāmṛtaṃ hutaṃ śivo māviśa | vyānam annenāpyāyasva | śraddhāyām udāne niviśyāmṛtaṃ hutaṃ śivo māviśa | udānam annenāpyāyasva | śraddhāyāṃ samāne niviśyāmṛtaṃ hutaṃ śivo māviśa | samānam annenāpyāyasva |” iti pañcabhiḥ | [235] “brahmaṇi ma ātmāmṛtatvāya” ity akṣareṇa cātmānaṃ yojayet | sarvakratuyājinām ātmayājī viśiṣyate | athāpy udāharanti |
yathā hi tūlam aiṣīkam agnau protaṃ pradīpyate |
tadvat sarvāṇi pāpāni dahyante hy ātmayājinaḥ ||
kevalāgho bhavanti kevalādī | mogham annaṃ vindata iti | sa evam eva vāhar ahaḥ sāyaṃprātar juhuyāt | adbhir vā sāyam |
“śālīnāḥ” śālānivāsaśīlāḥ | “yāyāvarāś” ca sarvadāṭanaśīlāḥ sāgnayo gṛhasthaviśeṣāḥ | “ātmayājī” yo ‘gnim eva sasādhanaṃ paramātmabudhyā upāsate | “āvaśyakam” snānādipañcayajñāntaram | “saṃsṛṭaṃ” saṃmārjanyādinā śodhitam | “upalipte” gomayodakābhyām | “uddhṛtam” annam iti śeṣaḥ | [236] “upasthānam” tatsaṃmukhatvena namrībhāvaḥ | “nyastam” pātre nihitam | “pradakṣiṇam annam udakam” iti, tasyāyam arthaḥ — udakaṃ gṛhītvā sarvatodikkaṃ pradakṣiṇam udakena secanam annasya tatra kuryād iti | “pañca prāṇāhutīḥ” ity anvayaḥ | “vācaṃ yacched” ity abhidhāya punas tūṣṇīṃgrahaṇaṃ vāgayamasya pañcāhutimātraviṣayatā-nivāraṇārtham | “askandayan” annam avikiran | eteṣāṃ śālīnādīnāṃ tilādisaṃsṛṣṭānnabhakṣaṇaṃ vināpi bhojanānte ‘gner upasparśanaṃ nityavad aṅgam ity uktam “agnim upaspṛśed” iti | “kandāni” sūraṇādīni | “mūlāni” mūlakādīni | sarvabhakṣyagrahaṇād evāpūpādiprāptes tadgrahaṇaṃ doṣātiśayārtham | “dantair nāvadyet” dantaiḥ khaṇḍayitvā śeṣā na punar bhakṣaṇīyā ity arthaḥ | “pañcabhiḥ” uktamantrair iti śeṣaḥ | “brahmaṇi ma ātmāmṛtatvāya” ity anena mantreṇa “akṣareṇa” parabrahmaṇā “ātmānaṃ” jīvaṃ “yojayed” ekatayā cintayed ity arthaḥ | “adbhir vā sāyam” iti, sāyaṃbhojanāsaṃbhave ‘dbhir eva prāṇāhutyādikaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpādanīyam |
āpastambaḥ [2.1.1–3] |
pāṇigrahaṇād adhi gṛhamedhinor vratam | kālayor bhojanam | atṛptiś cānnasya |
“pāṇigrahaṇād adhi” vivāhād ūrdhvam | “gṛhamedhinoḥ” [237] gṛhasthayor dampatyoḥ | “kālayoḥ” sāyaṃprātaḥkālayoḥ “bhojanam” | “annasya” annenety arthaḥ | suhitārthayoge ṣaṣṭhī |
gautamaḥ [9.59] |
sāyaṃ prātas tv annam abhipūjitam anindan bhuñjīta |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.13.10–14] |
gṛhastho brahmacārī vā yo ‘naśnaṃs tu tapaś caret |
prāṇāgnihotralopena hy avakīrṇī bhavet tu saḥ ||
anyatra prāyaścittāt prāyaścitte tad eva vidhānam | athāpy udāharanti |
antarā prātarāśaṃ ca sāyamāśaṃ tathaiva ca |
sadopavāsī bhavati yo na bhuṅkte kadācana ||
prāṇāgnihotramantrāṃs tu niruddhe bhojane japet |
tretāgnihotramantrān vai dravyālābhe yathā japet || iti |
sa evam evācaraṃs tu brahmabhūyāya kalpate |
“gṛhasthaḥ” atrāhitāgniḥ | “anaśnan” upavāsaṃ kurvan | “avakīrṇī” avakīrṇivihitaprāyaścittārhaḥ | “anyatra [238] prāyaścittāt” upavāsayuktāt prāyaścittād anyatra | atraiva hetuḥ — “prāyaścitte tad eva vidhānam” anaśanam eva viśeṣavihitam | anenedam uktaṃ bhavati — yatrāhitāgnyuddeśena viśeṣavihita upavāsavidhis tatraivopavāsakarmaṇi tasyādhikāro nānyatreti | evaṃ brahmacāriṇo ‘pi | “antarā prātarāśam” ityādi — “antarā” madhye aśanam “āśo” bhojanam | “upavāsī” upavāsaphalabhāk | niruddhe bhojanārhatve saty annādyasaṃbhavād eva bhojananivṛttau | trayo ‘gnayaḥ “tretā” gārhapatyādayaḥ | “dravyālābhe” agnihotrahavanīyadravyālābhe |
puṣṭikāmādhikāre āpastambaḥ [2.19.3–12] |
audumbaraś camasaḥ suvarṇanābhaḥ praśastaḥ | na cānyenāpi bhoktavyam | yāvadgrāsaṃ na sannayann askanadyan nāpajihītāpajihīta vā kṛtsnaṃ grāsaṃ graset sadāṅguṣṭham | na ca mukhaśabdaṃ kuryāt | pāṇiṃ ca nāvadhunuyāt | ācamyordhvau pāṇī dhārayed ā prodakībhāvāt | tato ‘gnim upaspṛśet | divā ca na bhuñjītānyan mūlaphalebhyaḥ | sthālīpākānuddeśyāni ca varjayet | sottarācchādanaś caiva yajñopavītī bhuñjīta |
“audumbarḥ” tāmramayaḥ | “na cānyenāpi” ityādinā tatputreṇāpi tatra pātre na bhoktavyam ity uktam | “yāvadgrāsam” [239] yāvad grasituṃ śakyam ity arthaḥ | “nāpajihīta” savyena pāṇinā pātraṃ na vimuñcet, atha vā “apajihīta” | ayaṃ ca pātradhāraṇādhāraṇavikalpaḥ ṣoḍaśigrahaṇāgrahaṇavad vyavasthitaḥ | yadi prathamaṃ pātradhāraṇapūrvakaṃ bhojanam upakrāntaṃ tadā dhāraṇam eva | yadi prathamam adhṛtvaiva bhojanam upakrāntaṃ tadādhāraṇam eveti kapardī | “prodakībhāvaḥ” pracurodakasaṃbandhitvam | etac cāgnyupasparśanaṃ (?) tilamāṃsādisaṃsṛṣṭānnabhojanaviṣayam | “divā na bhuñjīta” punar iti śeṣaḥ | “sthālīpākaḥ” pakṣādicaruḥ | “anuddeśyāni” devapitrādyuddeśenāsaṃkalpitāni | svayam eva “yajñopavītī” “sottarāchādanaḥ” soparivastra eva “bhuñjīta” | nottarīyaṃ vāsovyatirekeṇa bhojanakāle sūtrādinā kāryam iti kapardibhāṣyam |
vasiṣthaḥ [12.15–17] |
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta | tūṣṇīṃ sāṅguṣṭhaṃ kṛtsnaṃ grāsaṃ graset | na ca mukhaśabdaṃ kuryāt |
annagrahaṇāt tāmbūlādau prāṅmukhatvādiniyamo nāstīti sūcitam | tūṣṇīṃgrahaṇenaiva maune prāpte punar mukhaśabdaniṣedho bhāṣaṇavytiriktasītkārādidhvaninivṛttyarthaḥ |
[240] manuḥ [4.62] |
na bhuñjītoddhṛtasnehaṃ nātisauhityam ācaret |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prātarāśitaḥ ||
“uddhṛtasneham” piṇyākādi | “atiprage” acirodita eva sūrye | “atisāyaṃ” sūryāstamanasamīpe | “prātarāśitaḥ” prātastṛptaḥ “sāyaṃ” na bhuñjīta |
viṣṇuḥ [68.48] |
na tṛtīyam athāśnīyān nāpathyaṃ ca kadācana |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prātarāśitaḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.87] |
aśnīyāt tanmanā bhūtvā pūrvaṃ tu madhuraṃ rasam |
lavaṇāmlau tathā madhye kaṭutiktādikāṃs tathā ||
prāgdravaṃ puruṣo ‘śnīyān madhye tu kaṭhināśanaḥ |
ante punar dravāśī ca balārogye na muñcati ||
brahmapurāṇe |
sarvāṅgulībhir aśnīyān nāvadhūyet karaṃ kvacit |
kuryāt kṣīrānnnam āhāraṃ na ca paścāt pibed dadhi ||
[241]        jaṭharaṃ pūrayed ardham annair bhāgaṃ jalena ca |
vāyoḥ saṃcāraṇārthāya caturtham avaśeṣayet ||
devalaḥ |
na bhuñjītāghṛtaṃ nityaṃ gṛhastho bhojanaṃ svayam |
pavitram atha hṛdyaṃ ca sarpir āhur aghāpaham ||
manuḥ [4.55] |
nāśnīyāt saṃdhivelāyām |
āpastambaḥ [1.17.6–8] |
na nāvi bhuñjīta tathā prāsāde kṛtabhūmau ca bhuñjīta |
“nāvi” āsīna iti śeṣaḥ | “prāsāde” prāsādasthoparīty arthaḥ | “kṛtabhūmau” mṛttikāprakṣepeṇa saṃpāditabhūmau |
brahmapurāṇe |
yas tu pāṇitale bhuṅkte yas tu phūtkārasaṃyutam |
prasṛtāṅgulibhir yaś ca tasya gomāṃsavac ca tat ||
nājīrṇe bhojanaṃ kuryāt syāc ca nātibubhukṣitaḥ |
hastyaśvarathayānoṣṭraprāsādastho na bhakṣayet ||
[242]        śmaśānāntargato vāpi devālayagato ‘pi vā |
śayanastho na bhuñjīta na pāṇisthaṃ na cāsane ||
na saṃdhyayor na madhyāhne nārdharātre kadācana |
nārdravāsā nārdraśirā na cāyajñopavītavān ||
na prasāritapādas tu pādāropitapāṇimān |
nāvasakthikasaṃsthaś ca na ca paryaṅkikāsthitaḥ ||
na veṣṭitaśirāś cāpi notsaṅgakṛtabhājanaḥ |
naikavastro duṣṭamadhye sopānatkaḥ sapādukaḥ ||
na carmoparisaṃsthaś ca carmaveṣṭitapārśvavān |
annasya janmakāluṣyaṃ duṣpaṅktiṃ ca na kutsayet ||
grāsaśeṣaṃ tu nāśnīyāt pītaśeṣaṃ piben na tu |
śākamūlaphalekṣvādi dantacchedair na bhakṣayet ||
saṃcayen nānnam annena vikṣiptaṃ pātrasaṃsthitam |
bahūnāṃ bhuñjatāṃ madhye na cāśnīyāt tvarānvitaḥ ||
vṛthā na vikired annaṃ nocchiṣṭaḥ kutracid vrajet |
na spṛśet svaśiro vipraḥ socchiṣṭenaiva pāṇinā ||
tilakalkaṃ jalaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhikṣaudraghṛtāni tu |
na tyajed ardhajagdhāni saktṝṃś cātha kadācana ||
“prasṛtāṅgulibhiḥ” asaṅkucitābhir aṅgulībhiḥ | “syāc ca nātibubhukṣitaḥ” atiśayena bubhukṣayātmānaṃ na [243] pīḍayed ity arthaḥ | “yānaṃ” dolādi | “paryaṅkikā” mañcikā | “duṣṭāḥ patitādayaḥ | “carmaveṣṭitapārśvavān” kṛtasannāhādiḥ | “janmakāluṣyaṃ duṣpaṅktiṃ ca na kutsayet” “janmakutsanaṃ” ninditadeśotpattyā, “kāluṣyaṃ” malinatvam, “duṣpaṅktikaḥ” ninditapākaḥ | “pītaśeṣaṃ” pānapātrāvaśiṣṭam eva | “dantacchedaiḥ” dantaiś chitvā | “annena” apūpādinā pātre vikīrṇam annaṃ na rāśīkuryād ity arthaḥ | “ucchiṣṭena pāṇinā” ucchiṣṭānnaliptena pāṇinā, “svaśiro na spṛśet” ity arthaḥ | “ardhajagdhāni” kiṃcidbhuktāni |
mahābhārate |
pānīyaṃ pāyasaṃ sarpir madhuśeṣaṃ dadīty api |
nirasyaṃ śeṣam eteṣāṃ na pradeyaṃ tu kasyacit ||
“nirasyam” atyājyam |
brahmapurāṇe |
apy ekapaṅktyāṃ nāśnīyāt saṃyutaḥ svajanair api|
ko hi jānāti kiṃ kasya pracchannaṃ pātakaṃ bhavet ||
bhasmastambhajaladvāramārgaiḥ paṅktiṃ ca bhedayet |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.83, 86] |
na cāsandīsthite pātre nādeśe ca nareśvara |
[244]        nākāle nātisaṃkīrṇe ‘datvāgraṃ na naro graset ||
nāśeṣaṃ puruṣo ‘śnīyād anyatra jagatīpate |
maṇḍāmbudadhisarpirbhyaḥ saktubhyaś ca vivekavān ||
“āsandī” vetrādinirmitā | “adeśe” rathyādau | “akāle” saṃdhyādau | “maṇḍaḥ” —
saktavaḥ sarpiṣābhyaktāḥ śītavāripariplutāḥ |
nātidravā nātisāndrā maṇḍa ity abhidhīyate ||
iti carakaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na bhojanārthaṃ pākaśālāṃ praviśet | na vṛthāpākaṃ pacet | na vṛthāpākam aśnīyāt |
hārītaḥ |
na kruddho nānyamanā nābhibhāṣamāṇo ‘śnīyāt | na śiśūn bhartsayan | nāpradāya prekṣamāṇebhyaḥ | sa ca tad aśnīyād yena devapitṛmanuṣyārthaṃ na kuryāt | evaṃ hy āha –
ātmārthaṃ bhojanaṃ yasya ratyarthaṃ yasya maithunam |
vṛttyarthaṃ yasya cādhītaṃ niṣphalaṃ tasya jīvitam ||
[245]                yad bhuṅkte veṣṭitaśirā yac ca bhuṅkta udaṅmukhaḥ |
sopānatkaś ca yad bhuṅkte sarvaṃ vidyāt tad āsuram ||
“prekṣamāṇebhyaḥ” prakarṣeṇa sābhilāṣam īkṣamāṇebhyo bālād anyebhyaḥ | “devapitṛmanuṣyārtham” iti pañcayajñopalakṣaṇam | bhujyata iti “bhojanaṃ” bhojyam annam |
yamaḥ |
aśnāti yo bhṛtyajanasya madhye
miṣṭānnam eko rasagṛdhnubuddhiḥ |
dīnaiḥ kaṭākṣair api vīkṣyamāṇo
vyaktaṃ viṣaṃ hālahalaṃ sa bhuṅkte ||
“rasagṛdhnubuddhiḥ” rase gṛdhnur buddhir yasya, yasya rasakāṅkṣiṇī matir ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [68.4–47] |
na gobrāhmaṇoparāge cāśnīyāt | na rājanyavyasane | pravasitaś cāgnihotrī yadāgnihotraṃ hutaṃ manyeta tadāśnīyāt | yadā kṛtaṃ manyeta vaiśvadevam api | parvaṇi ca parma yadā kṛtaṃ [246] manyeta | nāśnīyāc cājīrṇe | nārdharātre | na madhyāhne | na saṃdhyayoḥ | nārdravāsāḥ | naikavāsāḥ | na nagnaḥ | nodake | notkuṭukaḥ | na ca bhinnāsanagataḥ | na ca śayanagataḥ | na bhinnabhājane | notsaṅge | na bhuvi | na pāṇau | lavaṇaṃ ca yan na dadyān na tad aśnīyāt | na bālakaṃ nirbhartsayan | naiko mṛṣṭam | noddhṛtasneham | na divā dhānāḥ | na rātrau tilasaṃbandham | na dadhisaktūn | na kovidāravaṭapippalaśaṇaśākam | nādatvā | nāhutvā | nānārdrapādaḥ | nānārdrakaramukhaś ca | nocchiṣṭo ghṛtam ādadyāt | na candrārkatārakā nirīkṣeta | na mūrdhānaṃ spṛśet | na brahma kīrtayet | prāṅmukho ‘śnīyāt | dakṣiṇābhimukho vā | abhinandyānnam | sumnāḥ sragvy anuliptaś ca | na niḥśeṣakṛt syāt | anyatra dadhimadhusarpiḥpayaḥsaktu-palalodanebhyaḥ |
nāśnīyād bhāryayā sākaṃ nākāśe na tathotthitaḥ |
bahūnāṃ prekṣamāṇānāṃ naikasmin bahavas tathā ||
[247]                śūnyālaye vahnigṛhe devāgāre kathaṃcana |
piben nāñjalinā toyaṃ nātisauhityam ācaret ||
“gobrāhmaṇoparāge” gobrāhmaṇayor vyasane | “pravasitaḥ” agnihotradeśaṃ tyaktvā grāmāntaraṃ gataḥ | “notkuṭukaḥ” notkaṭāsanaḥ | “bhinnaṃ” sphuṭitam | “na bhuvi” kṛtvety śeṣaḥ | “pāṇau” sthitam iti śeṣaḥ | “dadyād” devādibhya iti śeṣaḥ | “nādatvā” ity anena brāhmaṇādibhyo dānam akṛtvā nāśnīyād ity uktam | “yan na dadyād” ity anenānyajātīyaṃ datvāpy anyn nāśnīyād iti |”"niḥśeṣakṛt” sarvabhojī | ākāśe nirādhāre bhojanāsaṃbhavād āsandikādau pratiṣedho ‘nākāsa” iti | “ekasmin” prekṣamāṇa iti śeṣaḥ |
tathā [68.49] |
na bhāvaduṣṭam aśnīyān na bhāṇḍe bhāvadūṣite |
śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaś [?] ca kṛtvā caivāvasakthikām ||
“bhāvaduṣṭam” aśucitvena śaṅkitam |
manuḥ |
ekavastro na bhuñjīta kapāṭam apidhāya ca |
bhūmau cānanarīkṛtya bhinnapātre na cāśanam ||
[248] tathā |
svagrāme grāmato vāpi saṃnikṛṣṭe mṛte sati |
na bhuñjītāśanaṃ dhīmān adharmyaṃ śokakāraṇāt ||
“bhinnapātre n cāśanam” ity atra na kuryād iti śeṣaḥ | “svagrāme” svagrāmīṇe | “grāmataḥ saṃnikṛṣṭe” saṃnikṛṣṭagrāmīṇe | “adharmyam” adharmasādhanam |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
na spṛśed vāmahastena bhuñjāno ‘nnaṃ kadācana |
na pādau na śiro vastiṃ na padā bhājanaṃ spṛśet ||
bhuñjītety anuvṛttau uśanā |
nānācāntaḥ khaṭvārūḍho na cottānaḥ kadācana |
gautamaḥ [9.56–57] |
na bhakṣyān utsaṅge bhakṣayet | na rātrau preṣyāhṛtam |
“rātrau” “preṣyeṇa” dāsādinā ānītaṃ kiṃcid api na bhakṣayitavyam iti maskarī |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.131] |
na bhāryādarśane ‘śnīyān naikavāsā na saṃsthitaḥ |
[249] “bhāryādarśane” paśyantyāṃ bhāyāyām |
manuḥ [4.43] |
nāśnīyād bhāryāyā sārdhaṃ nainām īkṣeta nāśnatīm |
“aśnatīm” bhuñjānām |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.29] |
bhāryayā saha nāśnīyād avīryavad apatyaṃ bhavatīti vājasaneyake vijñāyate |
brāhmṇyā saha yo ‘śnīyād ucchiṣṭaṃ vā kadācana |
na tasya doṣaṃ manyante nityam eva manīṣiṇaḥ ||
ucchiṣṭam itarastrīṇāṃ yo ‘śnīyād brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
prāyaścittī sa vijñeyaḥ saṃkīrṇo mūḍhacetanaḥ ||
brāhmaṇīgrahaṇaṃ bhartṛsavarṇopalakṣaṇārtham | brāhmaṇyāpi saha kadācid bhojanam adhvany eva |
tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
brāhmaṇyā bhāryayā sārdhaṃ kvacid bhuñjīta vādhvani |
asavarṇastriyā sārdhaṃ bhuktvā patati tatkṣaṇāt || iti |
[250] brahmapurāṇe |
vipras tv evam upaspṛśya pādāṅguṣṭhe ca dakṣiṇe |
hastābhyāṃ mantravad dadyād vidhivac cāvanejanam ||
saṃmārjya bāhū jānū ca gopradānaphalaṃ labhet |
bhuktvā caiva pratiṣṭheta na cāpy ārdreṇa pāṇinā ||
ācānto ‘py śucis tāvad yāvat pātram anuddhṛtam |
uddhṛte ‘py aśucis tāvad yāvan nonmṛjyate mahī ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.90–95] |
bhuktvā śamyag athācamya prāṅmukhodaṅmukho ‘thavā |
ācāntaḥ punar ācāmet pāṇī prakṣālya mūlataḥ ||
svasthaḥ praśāntacittas tu kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ |
abhīṣṭadevatānāṃ tu kurvīta smaraṇaṃ tataḥ ||
agnir āpyāyatāṃ dhātūn pārthivān pavaneritaḥ |
dattāvakāśo nabhasā jarayatv astu me sukham ||
annaṃ balāya me bhūmer apām agnyanilasya ca |
bhavatv etat pariṇatau mamāstv avyāhataṃ sukham ||
prāṇāpānasamānānām udānavyānayos tathā |
annaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ cāstu mamāstv avyāhataṃ sukham ||
[251]        agastir agnir vaḍavānalaś ca
bhuktaṃ mayānnaṃ jarayatv aśeṣam |
sukhaṃ ca me tatpariṇāmasaṃbhavaṃ
yacchatv arogaṃ mama cāstu dehe ||
viṣṇuḥ samastendriyadehadehī
pradhānabhūto bhagavān yathaikaḥ |
satyena tenānnam aśeṣam etad
ārogyadaṃ me pariṇāmam etu ||
viṣṇur attā tathaivāntaṃ pariṇāmaś ca vai yathā |
satyena tena yad bhuktaṃ jīryatv annam idaṃ tathā ||
ity uccārya svahastena parimārjya tathodaram |
anāyāsapradāyīni kuryāt karmāṇy atandritaḥ ||
āpastambaḥ [2.4.24] |
yatra bhujyate deśe tat samūhya nirhṛtyāvokṣya taṃ deśam amatrebhyo lepāt saṃkṛṣyādbhiḥ saṃmṛjyottarataḥ śucau deśe rudrāya ninayet | evaṃ vāstu śivaṃ bhavati |
“samūhanam” vardhanyādinā śodhanam | “nirharaṇam” samūhasyocchiṣṭasyāpanayanam | “avokṣya” prasicya, samācārād gomayodakena | “amatrāṇi” pākabhāṇḍāny atra | “uttarato” gṛhāt | “vāstu” gṛham | “śivam” bhadram |
hārītaḥ |
paścāt patnī śeṣaṃ baliṃ haret |
[252] etac ca pākabhāṇḍaśiṣṭenānnena raudrabaliharaṇaṃ kartavyam iti jayasvāmī |
atha bhojyābhojyāni
tatra gautamaḥ [17.1] |
praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta |
“praśastānāṃ” samyak svakarmaniṣṭhatvena stutiviṣayāṇām | “dvijātīnāṃ” traivarṇikāṇām |
yamaḥ, hārīta-jamadagnī prathamadvitīyayoḥ, paiṭhīnasiś ca tṛtīye |
brāhmaṇasya sadāśnīyāt kṣatriyasya ca parvasu |
prakṛteṣu ca vaiśyasya śūdrasya na kadācana ||
amṛtaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ kṣatriyasya payaḥ smṛtam |
brāhmaṇānnaṃ kṣatriyānnaṃ vaiśyānāṃ śaudram eva ca ||
tāṃ tāṃ yoniṃ vrajed vipro bhuktvānnaṃ yasya vai mṛtaḥ |
“parvsu” paurṇamāsyādiṣu | “prakṛteṣu” prakramaviśeṣeṣu gomaṅgalādiṣu | “yasya” brāhmaṇādeḥ | śūdrānnaniṣedhaś cāsacchūdrānnaviṣayaḥ, gavādiprāptyasaṃbhave sacchūdrānnaviṣayaś ca |
tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
[253]        rājñāṃ parvaṇi vaiśyānām aśnīyān maṅgale gavām |
gobhūmiratnahemārthaṃ sacchūdrasya gṛhe tathā ||
“aśnīyād” ity anvayaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.18.9–13] |
trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ kṣatriyaprabhṛtīnāṃ samāvṛttena na bhoktavyam | prakṛtyā brāḥmaṇasya bhoktavyaṃ kāraṇād abhojyam | yatrāprāyaścittaṃ karmāsevane prāyaścittavati | caritanirveṣasya bhoktavyam | sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharme vartamānānāṃ bhoktavyaṃ śūdravarjam ity eke |
samāvṛttagrahaṇād brahmacāriṇaḥ sarvavarṇeṣu bhikṣācaraṇaṃ na niṣiddham | “prakṛtyā” svābhāvikyā, svadharmaparatayā sthitasyeti śeṣaḥ | “kāraṇād” upapātakādiyogāt | “aprāyaścittaṃ” prāyaścittād itaran nityanaimittikaṃ karma | idaṃ ca prakṛtyety anenaivoktam | “prāyaścittavati” prāyaścittahetupāpasadbhāve | “caritanirveṣasya” kṛtaprāyaścittasya | idaṃ ca dharmaniṣṭhakṣatriyavaiśyānnabhojanānujñānaṃ yamavacanānusārāt parvamaṅgalaviṣayam |
[254] manuḥ [4.223] |
nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān aśrāddhino dvijaḥ |
ādadītāmam evāsmād avṛttāv ekarātrikam ||
“aśrāddhinaḥ” nityaśrāddham akurvataḥ | “avṛttau” āpadi | “ekarātrikam” ekadivasanirvāhayogyam annam |
hārītaḥ |
śūdrānnena tu bhuktena jaṭharasthena yo mṛtaḥ |
sa vai kharatvam uṣṭratvaṃ śūdratvaṃ copagacchati ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [6.26, 25, 27] |
śūdrānnenodarasthena yaḥ kaścin mriyate dvijaḥ |
sa bhavet sūkaro grāmyas tasya vā jālate kule ||
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgo hy adhīyāno ‘pi nityaśaḥ |
juhvan vāpi japan vāpi gatim ūrdhvāṃ na gacchati ||
śūdrānnena tu bhuktena maithunaṃ yo ‘dhigacchati |
yasyānnaṃ tasya vai putrā na ca svargārhako bhavet ||
yamaḥ |
śūdrānnenodarasthena brāhmaṇo yas tyajet tanum |
[255]        āhitāgnis tathā yajvā sa śūdragatim āpnuyāt ||
yaś cāhitāgnir vipras tu śūdrānnān na nivartate |
pañca tasya praṇaśyanti ātmā brahma trayo ‘gnayaḥ ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ṣaṇmāsān yo dvijo bhuṅkte śūdrasyānnaṃ vigarhitam |
sa ca jīvan bhavac chūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gṛhamedhī na śūdrānnam aśnīyād āyur balaṃ teja ity apakrāmati |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
samyak prokṣya tu gṛhṇīyāc chūdrānnaṃ gṛham āgatam |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
upakṣepaṇadharmeṇa śūdrānnaṃ yaḥ paced dvijaḥ |
abhojyaṃ tad bhaved annaṃ sa ca vipraḥ purohitaḥ ||
abhojyaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ vṛṣalena nimantritam |
tathaiva vṛṣalasyānnaṃ brāhmaṇena nimantritam ||
[256]        śūdrānnaṃ śūdrasaṃparkaḥ śūdreṇa ca sahāsanam |
śūdrāj jñānāgamaḥ kaścij jvalantam api pātayet ||
“upakṣapaṇadharmaḥ” śūdrasvāmikasyānnasya pākārthaṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe samarpaṇam | yat tu,
yathā yatas tato ‘py āpaḥ śuddhiṃ yānti nadīṃ gatāḥ |
śūdrād vipragṛheṣv annaṃ praviṣṭaṃ ca sadā suci ||
ity aṅgirovacanam, tac chūdrād brahmaṇena pratigrahādinā yal labdham annaṃ tadviṣayam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.166] |
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiva yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
“gopālaḥ” yasya gāḥ pālayati tasyaiva bhojyānna iti bhartṛyajñaḥ | “kulamitram” kulakramād āyātā yena saha maitrī | “ardhasīrī” militvā karṣakaḥ | “nāpitaḥ” kṣurakarmopajīvī, so ‘pi svakīya eva | “ātmanivedanam” kulataḥ śīlato dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaprayojanenopacāraviśeṣeṇa ca svāmiprāyakathanapūrvakaṃ tadāśrayaṇam |
tathā ca manuḥ [4.254] |
yādṛśo ‘sya bhaved ātmā yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam |
yathā copacared enaṃ tathātmānaṃ nivedayet || iti |
[257] devalaḥ |
svadāso nāpito gopaḥ kumbhakāraḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ |
brāhmaṇair api bhojyānnāḥ pañcaite śūdrayonayaḥ ||
“kṛṣīvalaḥ” atra yājñavalkyavacanānusārād ardhasīrī | teṣām āmam annam anāpady api grāhyam | āpadi tv eteṣāṃ pakvānnam api grāhyam |
śūdrādhikāre gautamaḥ [17.6–7] |
paśupālakṣetrakarṣakakulasaṃgatakārayitṛpraticārakā bhojyānnāḥ | vaṇik cāśilpī |
“kārayitā” nāpita iti maskaribhāṣyam | yaḥ śūdro “vaṇik” sa ced “aśilpī” takṣādi na bhavati tadā bhojyānnaḥ |
śūdrādhikāre āpastambaḥ [1.18.6–8] |
etasyāpi dharmopanatasya suvarṇaṃ datvā paśuṃ vā bhuñjīta | nātyantam anvavasyet | vṛttiṃ prāpya viramet |
“suvarṇaṃ vā datvā” “paśuṃ vā datvā” dharmeṇopanatasya śūdrasyāpi bhuñjītety arthaḥ | “nātyantam anvavasyet” vṛttyantarālābhe ‘py etadīyānnabhojanaratir na syād ity arthaḥ |
[258]        gorasaṃ caiva saktūṃś ca tailaṃ piṇyākam eva ca |
apūpān bhakṣayec chūdrād yac cānyat payasā kṛtam ||
“payasā kṛtam” kilāṭādi |
hārītaḥ |
kandupakvaṃ snehapakvaṃ pāyasaṃ dadhi saktavaḥ |
etāny aśudrānnabhujo bhojyāni manur abravīt ||
parāśaraḥ |
tāvad bhavati śūdrānnaṃ yāvan na spṛśati dvijaḥ |
dvijāgrakarasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ tad dhavir ucyate ||
yamaḥ |
yathā jalaṃ nirgamaneṣv apeyaṃ
nadīgataṃ tat punar eva peyam |
tathānnapānaṃ vidhipūrvam āgataṃ
dvijātipātrāntaritaṃ na duṣyati ||
[259] “nirgamanāni” rathyādipatitavāripravāhapradeśāḥ |
manuḥ [4.205–208] |
nāśrotriyatate yajñe gāmayājihute tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit ||
aślīkam etat sādhūnāṃ yatra huhvaty amī haviḥ |
pratīpam etad devānāṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet ||
mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
“aśrotriyaḥ” adhyayanarahitaḥ, ṛtvig yajamāno vā | “tate” vitate prārabdha ity arthaḥ | “grāmayājī” bahūnāṃ yājayitā | “yaś copanayate bahūn” iti vākyaparyālocanayā bahūnām upaneteti viśvarūpācāryaḥ | sa yasya homakartā tasyānnaṃ na bhoktavyam | “striyā hute” striyā yatra prātar vaiśvadevādi kṛtam | “aślīkam” aśrīkaram | “pratīpam” pratikūlam |
tathā [4.209–217] |
gavā cānnam upāghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣā ca jugupsitam ||
stenagāyanayoś caiva takṣṇo vārdhuṣikasya ca |
dīkṣitasya kadaryasya baddhasya nigaḍasya ca ||
[260]        abhiśastasya ṣaṇḍhasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭam eva ca ||
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ |
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryācantam anirdaśam ||
samāsamābhyāṃ viprābhyāṃ viṣamaṃ samam eva ca |
pūjānte dīyamānaṃ ca na grāhyaṃ deyam eva ca ||
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ |
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ patitānnam avakṣutam ||
piśunānṛtinoś cānnaṃ kratuvikrayikasya ca |
śaulūṣaatunnavāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnam eva ca ||
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatarakasya ca |
suvarṇakartur veṇasya śastravikrayiṇas tathā ||
śvavatāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca |
rajakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
mṛṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strījitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
anidaśaṃ ca pretānnam atuṣṭikaram eva ca ||
“ghuṣṭānnam” bhujyatām annaṃ ye kecid bhojanārthinaḥ ityādyāghuṣya yad annaṃ dīyate | “gaṇaḥ” saṅghaḥ | “gaṇikā” viśyā | “viduṣā” vedārthavidā madhyasthena | “jugupsitam” ninditam | jugupsāta evaa tatrādṛṣṭadoṣānumānam | “gāyano” gāyanena yo jīvati | “kadaryaḥ” kṛpaṇaḥ | “baddhasya” rajjvādinā [261] yantritasya | “nigaḍasya” nigaḍavataḥ, matvarthalakṣaṇayā | atha vā nigaḍena baddhasyety arthaḥ | tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | “puṃścalī” vyabhicāriṇī | “śuktam” yad anyarasaṃ dravyāntarasaṃbandhād amlatāṃ gatam | tac cātyamlaṃ varjanīyam | “atyamlaṃ śuktam ākhyātam” iti vṛddhabṛhaspativacanānusārāt | “śūdrocchiṣṭam” śūdraṃ bhojayitvā yad uddhṛtam | “krūro” anṛjuprakṛtiḥ | “ucchiṣṭabhojī” niṣiddhocchiṣṭabhojī | “ugraḥ” dāruṇakarmā | “ugro rājā” iti mañjarīkāraḥ | “sūtikānnam” sūtikām uddiśya yat kriyate tad annaṃ tatkulair api na bhoktavyam | “paryācāntam” itarān ekapaṅktisthān paribhūya yatrānne bhujyamāne kenāpy ācamyate tad annaṃ paryācāntam | “anirdaśam” sūtakyannam | vidyādinā tulyayor viprayor viṣamapūjāyāṃ dīyamānam annam abhojyam | evaṃ vidyādinā viṣamayoḥ samapūjayāṃ dīyamānam aannam abhojyam | dātrāpi na tathā dātaavyam ity arthaḥ | “anarcitam” arcārhasya yad avajñayā dīyate | “vṛthāmāṃsam” devapitrādyuddeśena yan na kṛtam | “avīrā” patiputrarahitā | “nagarī” nagarasvāmī | “piśunaḥ” parokṣe parāpavādabhāṣaṇaparaḥ | “anṛtī” atiśayenānṛtavādī kūṭasākṣyādiḥ | “kratuvikrayikaḥ” madīyayāgāt tava phalam astv ity abhidhāya yo dhanaṃ gṛhṇāti | “śailūṣo” naṭaḥ | “tunnavāyaḥ” saucikaḥ | “kṛtaghnaḥ” kṛtaṃ yo na manyate | “karmakāro” lohakāraḥ | “niṣādo” jātitaḥ | “taṅgāvatarakaḥ” naṭagāyanādyatirikto raṅgāvataraṇakarmajīvī | “veṇo” veṇucchedanena yo jīvati | “baruḍa” iti viśvarūpaḥ | “śvavataḥ” ākheṭakārthaṃ [262] paśūnāṃ poṣakaḥ | “śauṇḍiko” madyavikretā | “nṛśaṃso” nirdayaḥ | “strījitānām” bhāryāvaśavartinām | “sarvaśaḥ” sarvavyavahāreṣu | “anirdaśaṃ” daśāhābhyantare pretam uddiśya yad dīyate tad asūtakino ‘py annaṃ na bhoktavyam iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.162] |
krūrograpatitavrātyadāmbhikocchiṣṭabhojinām |
“vrātyaḥ” patitasāvitrīkaḥ |
tathaaiva punar yājñavalkyaḥ [1.164] |
nṛśaṃsarājarajakakṛtaghnavadhajīvinām |
“vadhajīvī” prāṇivadhena yo jīvati |
tathā [1.165] |
piśunānṛtinoaś caiva tathā cakrikabandinām |
teṣām annaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ somavikrayiṇas tathā ||
“cakrikaḥ” śakaṭopajīvī |
yamaḥ |
naṭanartakatakṣāṇaś carmakāraḥ suvarṇakṛt |
sthāṇukāṣaṇḍhagaṇikā abhojyānnāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
[263]        gāndharvo lohakāraś ca sūcikas tantuvāyakaḥ |
cakropajīvī rajakaḥ kitavas taskaras tathā ||
dhvajī mānopajīvī ca śūdrādhyāpakayājakau |
kulālaś citrakarmā ca vārdhuṣī carmavikrayī ||
“kulālaḥ” atrāsvakīyaḥ, “śūdrasyāsvabhūtasya” iti vasiṣṭhavacanānusārāt | yat tu devalena kumbhakāro bhojyānna ity uktaḥ saḥ svakīyo boddhavyaḥ |
sumantuḥ |
abhiśastapatitapaunarbhavabhrūṇahapuṃścalyastraviśastrakāratailikacakrika-dhvajisuvarṇakāralekhakaṣaṇḍhabandhakīgaṇakagaṇikānnāni cābhojyāni | saukarikavyādhaniṣpacarajakaraṅgakārabaruḍacarmakārā abhojyānnā apratigrāhyāḥ |
“paunarbhavaḥ” punarbhūputraḥ | “astram” dhanuḥ | “viśastram” vividhaprakāraṃ khaḍgādi | “dhvajī” madyavikrayī | “lekhakaḥ” citrakāraḥ | “ṣaṇḍhakaḥ” napuṃsakaḥ | “bandhakī” abhisāriṇī | “saukarikaḥ” saukaropajīvī | “niṣpaco” yaḥ pākaṃ na karoti, yatir brahmacārī ca |
[264] annaṃ nādyam ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.4–11] |
śūdrasya vāsvabhūtasya | upapater yaś copapatiṃ manyate | yaś ca gṛhān dahet | yaś ca vadhārheṇopahanyāt | ko bhojyata iti cābhikruṣṭam | gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca | athāpy udāharanti |
nāśnanti śvavato devā nāśnanti vṛṣalīpateḥ |
bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
“vadhārheṇa” viṣādinā |
gautamaḥ [17.15–17] |
utsṛṣṭapuṃścalyabhiśastānapadeśyadāṇḍikatakṣakadaryabandhanikacikitsaka-mṛgayvaniṣucāryucchiṣṭabhojigaṇavidviṣāṇānām | apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālāt | vṛthānnācamanotthānavyapetāni |
“utsṛṣṭam” asvāmikam | “anapadeśyaḥ” avijñātakulācāraḥ | “dāṇḍikaḥ” pāpakṛnnigrahādhikṛtaḥ | “bandhanikaḥ” kārāgārādhyakṣaḥ | “cikitsakaḥ” cikitsā jīvikā yasya | “mṛgayuḥ” lubdhakaḥ | sa ced “aniṣucārī” bhavati | “aniṣucārī” pāśikaḥ | “prāg durvālāt” prāg ye śrāddhaprakaraṇe [265] apāṅkteyā uktāḥ stenādayas tyaktātmaparyantās teṣām akṛtaprāyaścittānām annam abhojyam ity arthaḥ | “vṛthānnam” yad devādyuddeśena kevalam ātmārthaṃ pacyate | “ācamanavyapetam” yatrārdhabhukta ācamanaṃ kṛtam | “utthānavyapetam” yatrārdhabhukta uttānaṃ kṛtam |
annam abhojyam ity anuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.18.18–33; 1.19.1] |
sarveṣāṃ ca śilpajīvinām | ye ca śastram ājīvanti | ye cādhim | bhiṣak | vārdhuṣikaḥ | dīkṣito ‘krītarājakaḥ | agnīṣomīyasaṃsthāyām eva | hutāyāṃ vā vapāyāṃ dīkṣitasya bhoktavyam | yajñārthe vā nirdiṣṭe śeṣād bhuñjīrann iti hi brāhmaṇam | klībaḥ | rājñāṃ praiṣakaraḥ | ahaviryājī | cārī | avidhinā ca pravrajitaḥ | yaś cāgnīn apāsyati | yaś ca sarvān varjayate sarvānnī ca śrotriyo nirākṛtir vṛṣalīpatiḥ | matta unmatto baddha ṛṇikaḥ pratyupaviṣṭo yaś ca pratyupaveśayate tāvantaṃ kālam |
[266] “ādhim” bandhakam, ājīvantīty anuṣaṅgaḥ | “vārdhuṣikaḥ,”
samarghaṃ dhānyam ādāya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
ityādinābhihitaḥ | “akrītarājakaḥ” akrītasomaḥ | “agnīṣomīyasaṃsthā” agnīṣomīyapaśuyāgasamāptiḥ | “hutāyāṃ vā vapāyāṃ” agnīṣomīyapaśor eva | “nirdiṣṭam” uddiṣṭam | preṣaṇam eva “praiṣam” | “ahaviryājī” nararudhirādinābhicārādikarmasu yo juhoti | “cārī” caraḥ | “avidhinā” śāstroktavidhim antareṇa | “apāsyati” avidhineti śeṣaḥ | “sarvān” anindyān api | “nirākṛtiḥ” adhītavedo ‘py adhyāpanabrahmayajñādirahitaḥ | “ṛṇikaḥ” uttamarṇaḥ | sa cet “pratyupaviṣṭo” adhamarṇaṃ prati dhanagrahaṇārtham anaśnann upaviṣṭo bhavati tāvatkālam abhojyānnaḥ | “yaś ca pratyupaveśayate” yaś cādhamarṇa uttamarṇam adānena pratyupaveśayet |
punar āpastambaḥ [1.19.9–10] |
puṇyasyepsato bhoktavyam | puṇyasyāpy anīpsato na bhoktavyam |
“puṇyasya” dhārmikasya | “īpsato” yācamānasya |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
bhītaruditākranditāvakruṣṭakṣutaparibhuktavismitonmattāvadhūta-rājapurohitānnāni varjayet |
[267] “bhītaḥ” trastaḥ | “ruditaḥ” aśrupātavān | “ākranditaḥ” ākrando duḥkhitayā saṃtataśabdakaraṇam, tadvān | “avakruṣṭaḥ” jugupsitaḥ | “kṣutaḥ” chikkāvān | “paribhuktaḥ” sarvatobhāvena bhuktaṃ śeṣīkṛtam annaṃ yena | “vismito” vismitavān | “avadhūtaḥ” sādhubhir bahiṣkṛtaḥ | sarvatrādikarmaṇi ktaḥ | tena bhayādayo vartamānā eva nimittam iti boddhavyam | “rājā” janapadeśvaraḥ | “purohitaḥ” yasya kasyacit | eteṣām annaṃ na bhoktavyam |
punaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
vidviṣāṇasya nāśnīyād brahmacchitpāpakāriṇām |
śrāddhasūtagaṇānnāni paribhūtāni yāni ca ||
“vidviṣāṇaḥ” vidveṣaśīlaḥ | “śrāddham” pretaśrāddhāśī | “sūtaḥ” brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāj jātaḥ | “paribhūtāni” tiraskṛtāni |
devalaḥ |
patitānnam abhojyānnam apāṅkteyānnam eva ca |
śūdrānnaṃ kutsitānnaṃ ca dūṣitaṃ parivarjayet ||
“abhojyāḥ” puṃścalyabhiśastādayaḥ smṛtyantaraprasiddhāḥ | “dūṣitaṃ” keśakīṭādibhiḥ |
[268] vasiṣṭhaḥ [4.31–32] |
aśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake vāpi bhuktavān |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
anirdaśāhe paraśave niyogād bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
kṛmir bhūtvā sa dehānte tāṃ viṣṭhāṃ samupāśnute ||
“paraśave” yadīyam āśaucaṃ yasya nāsti tasya saḥ paraḥ, parasya “śave” mṛtakāśaucābhyantare |
yamaḥ |
yas tu prāṇān vimuñceta bhuktvā śrāddhaṃ navaṃ dvijaḥ |
ayājyāsu ca ghorāsu tiryagyoniṣu jāyate ||
yas tu prajāyate garbho bhuktvā śrāddhaṃ navaṃ dvijaḥ |
na sa vidyām avāpnoti kṣīṇāyuś caiva jāyate ||
“ayājyāḥ” yājyās chāgādayas tebhyo ‘nye |
śaṅkhaḥ [15.24] |
parāśauce ca yo bhuṅkte kṛmiyonau prajāyate |
bhuktvānnaṃ mriyate yasya tasya jātau prajāyate ||
āpastambaḥ [1.16.18–20] |
yasya kule mriyeta na tatrānirdaśe bhoktavyam | tathānutthitāyāṃ sūtikāyām | antaḥśave ca |
[269] “antaḥśave” aśaucānadhikāriṇo ‘pi yas gṛhamadhye śavas tadgṛhe yad annam |
aṅgirāḥ |
janmaprabhṛtisaṃskāre bālasyānnasya bhojane |
asapiṇḍair na bhoktavyaṃ śmaśānānte viśeṣataḥ ||
“bālasya” “janmaprabhṛtisaṃskāra” ity anvayaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
yo ‘gṛhītvā vivāhāgniṃ gṛhastha iti manyate |
annaṃ tasya na bhoktavyaṃ vṛthāpāko hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
yatra nāśnanti vai devāḥ pitaraś ca tathātithiḥ |
vṛthāpākaḥ sa vijñeyo na tasyādyāt kathaṃcana ||
aṅgirāḥ |
aprajānāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ nāśnīyāj jātu tadgṛhe |
mohād vā yas tu bhuñjīta sa pūyanarakaṃ vrajet ||
“aprajāḥ” anapatyāḥ |
yamaḥ |
adhītya caturo vedān sāṅgopāṅgān viśeṣataḥ |
[270]        narendrasya gṛhe bhuktvā kṛmiyonau prajāyate ||
rājānnaṃ harate tejaḥ śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
vaiśyāānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati ||
tathā rājabhṛtasyānnaṃ caurasyānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
sūtake mṛtake cānnaṃ svargastham api pātayet ||
hārītaḥ |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokān api nikṛntati ||
ya icchec chuddham ātmānam iṣṭāṃl lokāṃś ca veditum |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||
“veditum” anubhavitum |
manuḥ [4.218–221] |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmavikartinaḥ ||
kārukānnaṃ prajāṃ hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasya ca |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati ||
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ puṃścalyās tv annam indriyam |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śalyavikrayiṇāṃ malam ||
ya ebhyo ‘nye tv abhojyānnāḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
[271] “nirṇejakaḥ” dhāvakaḥ |
yamaḥ |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ rudhiraṃ brahmaghātinaḥ |
malaṃ hi rajakasyānnaṃ carmakārasya kilbiṣam ||
pāpmā tathānnaṃ śauṇḍasya ghāṇṭikasya tathaiva ca |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca duṣkṛtam | vārdhuṣer viṣṭhā | sāṃvatsaraghāṇṭika-grāmakūṭānnaṃ viṣam | bandhakīnāṃ retaḥ | bhiṣakśalyakṛtaḥ pūyam | parivittiparivevidānaviddhaprajananavṛṣalīpatididhiṣūpatipunarbhūputrāṇāṃ rudhiram | patitānnaṃ ca |
“sāṃvatsaro” jyautiṣikaḥ | “grāmakūṭakaḥ” grāme kapaṭavyavahāraśīlaḥ | “parivevidānaḥ” parivettā | “viddhaprajananaḥ” chinnaśiśnacarmā |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.14] |
śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ caurasyāpi viśeṣataḥ |
na tv evaṃ bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn ||
“bahuyājyo” bahūnāṃ yājakaḥ |
manuḥ [4.224–225] |
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan ||
[272]        tān prajāpatir ity āha mā kṛdhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddhayetarat ||
“vadānyaḥ” priyavāg dānaśīlaś ca |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.10.5] |
śucer aśraddadhānasya śraddadhānasya cāśuceḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan ||
tān prajāpatir ity āha kṛdhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
hatam aśraddadhānasya śraddhāpūtaṃ viśiṣyate ||
yamaḥ |
avadhūtam avijñātaṃ saroṣaṃ vismayānvitam |
guror api na bhoktavyam annaṃ satkāravarjitam ||
avadhūtaṃ yad ucchiṣṭaṃ vāgduṣṭam api yad bhavet |
aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattam abhojyaṃ tad dvijātibhiḥ ||
avaliptasya mūrkhasya durvṛttasya ca durmateḥ |
annam aśraddadhānasya yo bhuṅkte bhrūṇahā tathā ||
asutānnaṃ ca yo ‘śnīyāt sa bhuṅkte pṛthavīmalam |
nṛṇām āhur malaṃ cānnaṃ sarvam anne pratiṣṭhitam ||
duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyasya annam āśritya tiṣṭhati |
yo yasyānnam ihāśnāti sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam ||
“avadhūtam” upahatam | “vāgduṣṭaṃ” bhakṣyam apy abhakṣyābhidhāyiśabdenoktam | “avalipto” garvitaḥ |
[273] ādityapurāṇe |
viṣṇuṃ jāmātaraṃ manyet tasya manyuṃ na kārayet |
aprajāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ nāśnīyāt tasya vai gṛhe ||
brahmadeye viśeṣeṇa daive bhojyaṃ sadaiva hi |
gāndharve caiva rājanye kuryād vai gamanāgamam ||
brahmadeyāṃ na vai kanyāṃ datvā bhuṅkte kadācana |
atha bhuñjīta mohātmā sa pūyanarakaṃ vrajet ||
āpastambaḥ [2.6.19–20] |
dviṣan dviṣato vā nānnam aśnīyād doṣeṇa vā mīmāṃsamānasya mīmāṃsitasya vā | pāpmānaṃ hi sa tasya bhakṣayatīti vijñāyate |
manu-yama-vasiṣṭhāḥ |
annāde bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī |
gurau śiṣyaś ca yājyaś ca steno rājani kilbiṣam ||
[274] “kilbiṣam” ity annādibhiḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | “mārṣṭi” saṃyojayati |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.14–15] |
na mṛgayor iṣucāriṇaḥ parivaryam annam | vijñāyate hy agastyo varṣasāhasrike satre mṛgayāṃ cakāra | tasyāsaṃstarasamayāḥ puroḍāśā mṛgapakṣiṇāṃ praśastānām |
atra “iṣucārī” dvijātir eva vṛttikarśito mṛgayopajīvī |
śātātapaḥ |
vanaspatigate some parānnaṃ ye tu bhuñjate |
teṣāṃ māsakṛto homo dātāram adhigacchati ||
“vanaspatigate some” amāvāsyāyām ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ [3.104] |
upāsate gṛhasthā ye parapākam abuddhayaḥ |
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajanty annādyadāyinām ||
“annādyam” annam eva |
yamaḥ |
svapāke vartamāne yaḥ parapākaṃ niṣevate |
[275]        so ‘śvatvaṃ sūkaratvaṃ ca gaardabhatvaṃ ca gacchati ||
parapākena puṣṭasya dvijasya gṛhamedhinaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ dattaṃ tapo ‘dhītaṃ yasyānnaṃ tasya tad bhavet ||
yasyānnena tu bhuktena bhāryāṃ samadhigacchati |
yasyānnaṃ tasya te putrā annād retaḥ pravartate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.112] |
parapākarucir na syād anindyāmantraṇād ṛte |
hārīta-jamadagnī |
brāhmaṇānnena dāridryaṃ kṣatriyānnena preṣyatām |
vaiśyānnena ca śūdratvaṃ śūdrānnair narakaṃ vrajet ||
athābhakṣñāni
tatra kathaṃ mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsatīti ṛṣipraśnānantaram manuḥ [5.4–6] |
anabhyāsena vedānām ācārasya ca varjanāt |
[276]        ālasyād annadoṣāc ca mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsati ||
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnām amedhyaprabhavāni ca ||
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
śeluṃ gavyaṃ ca peyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet ||
“ālasyaṃ” sāmarthye say avaśyakartavyakaraṇānutsāhaḥ | “annadoṣāt” jātyādibhiḥ |
tathā ca bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
jātiduṣṭaṃ kriyāduṣṭaṃ kālāśrayavidūṣitam |
saṃsargāśrayaduṣṭaṃ ca sahṛllekhaṃ svabhāvataḥ ||
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
vārtākuṃ nālikālābū upeyāj jātidūṣitam ||
na bhakṣayet kriyāduṣṭaṃ yad dṛṣṭaṃ patitaiḥ pṛthak |
kāladuṣṭaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ hyastanaṃ cirasaṃsthitam ||
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ vikārāś ca madhuvarjaṃ tad iṣyate |
surālaśunasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ peyūṣādisamanvitam ||
saṃsargaduṣṭam etad dhi śūdrocchiṣṭavad ācaret |
[277]        śūdrocchiṣṭaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ pūrvaṃ śūdre pradarśitam ||
vicikitsā tu hṛdaye anne yasmin prajāyate |
sahṛllekhaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ purīṣaṃ tu svabhāvataḥ ||
rasaduṣṭaṃ vikārād dhi rasasyeti pradarśitam |
pāyasakṣīrapūpāni tasminn eva dine tathā ||
“kāladuṣṭam” ityāder ayam arthaḥ — “hyastanam” paryuṣitaṃ sarvam evābhakṣyam | “dadhi” “bhakṣyam” “vikārāś ca” yavagodhūmādiprabhavāḥ paryuṣiā api ye bhakṣyatvenābhyanujñātās te ‘pi “cirasaṃsthitā” ativikṛtarasagandhāḥ santo na bhakṣaṇīyā ity arthaḥ | “madhu” punaś cirasaṃsthitam api bhakṣyam | tad uktam “madhuvarjam” iti | “gṛñjanaḥ” laśunasadṛśaḥ kandaviśeṣa uttarapathe prasiddhaḥ | “kavakam” chatrākasadṛśaṃ kumudakalikākṛti |
tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
madhukaiṭabhavṛtrāṇāṃ triśīrṣasyāsurasya ca |
viṣṇunā hanyamānānāṃ yan medaḥ patitaṃ bhuvi ||
piṇḍopamaṃ tu khuḍkhaṇḍaṃ kavakaṃ caityasaṃnibham |
chatrākaṃ chatrasadṛśaṃ daityadehasamudbhavam ||
“amedhyaprabhavāni” sākṣādviṣṭhājātāni taṇḍulīyakādīni | “viḍjāni” iti yājñavalkya-vacanānusārāt | ye tu [278] sākṣād viḍjā na bhavanty amedhyākrāntabhūprabhavā vṛkṣās teṣāṃ puṣpaphalāny aduṣṭāny eva |
tad uktaṃ baudhāyanena [1.9.4] |
amedhyeṣu ca ye vṛkṣā uptāḥ puṣpaphalopagāḥ |
teṣām api na duṣyanti puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca ||
atra vṛkṣagrahaṇāc chākādīnām evaṃvidhānām pratiṣedha eva | sākṣād amedhyajāteṣu vṛkṣeṣu yāni puṣpaphalāni tāni pratiṣiddhāny eva | “vṛkṣaniryāsaḥ” vṛkṣān nirgato rasaḥ kaṭhinatāṃ gataḥ | “vraścanaṃ” chedanam | “vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā” tathā vraścanaprabhavān alohitān api varjayed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca taittirīyaśrutiḥ “atho khalu ya eva lohito yo vāvraścanān niryeṣati tasya nāśaṃ kāmam anyasya” iti | tena karpūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | “śeluḥ” bahuvārakaḥ | “peyūṣaṃ” navaprasūtāyāḥ kṣīraṃ yad agnisaṃyogāt kaṭhitaṃ bhavati tac ced gavyaṃ tadā varjyam |
yamaḥ |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva vilayaṃ sumukhaṃ tathā |
kavakāni palāṇḍuṃ ca varjayet sarvadā budhaḥ ||
varaṃ svayaṃ viśasyāpi sarvamāṃsāni bhakṣayet |
na ca chatrākam aśnīyād dvijātyapasado ‘pi san ||
[279]        bhūmijaṃ vṛkṣajaṃ vāpi chatrākaṃ bhakṣayanti ye |
brahmaghnāṃs tān vijānīyād brahmavādiṣu garhitān ||
“vilayo” ghṛtamalam | “viśasya” mārayitvā | “apasado” apakṛṣṭaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ tathā |
bhakṣayan vai pated vipro yadi syāt sarvavedavit ||
devalaḥ |
śleṣmātako vajraphalī kaustumbhaṃ nālamastakān |
gṛñjanaṃ ceti śākānām abhakṣyāṇi pracakṣate ||
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ śuktaṃ niryāsaṃ ceti sarvaśaḥ |
kucundaṃ śvetavṛntākaṃ kumbhāṇḍaṃ ca vivarjayet ||
“nālaṃ” kalambikā | “śvetavṛntākaṃ” śvetavārtākuḥ | “kumbhāṇḍaṃ” dāḍimasadṛśaḥ phalaviśeṣaḥ kumbhālu iti prasiddhaḥ |
niyamabhojyā gautamaḥ [17.30–31] |
kisalayakvākulaśunaniryāsalohitā vraścanāś ca |
[280] “kisalayo” navapllavaḥ | “kvākuḥ” ahicchatrākaḥ |
abhojyaprakaraṇe āpastambaḥ [1.17.25–28] |
tathā kīlālauṣadhīnāṃ ca | kalañjapalāṇḍuparārīkāḥ | yac cānyat paricakṣate | kyākv abhojyam iti hi brāhmaṇam |
“kīlālaṃ” surā, tadarthaṃ sthāpitā yā “oṣadhayo” vrīhyādayaḥ, teṣāṃ vikāra it śeṣaḥ | “kalañjaṃ” laśunaviśeṣaḥ | “parārīkaḥ” palāṇḍuviśeṣaḥ | tathā ca smṛtimañjarīkāralekhitāyurvedaślokaḥ |
rasono dīrghapatraś ca picchagandho mahauṣadham |
hiraṇyaś ca palāṇḍuś ca navatarkaḥ parārikā ||
gṛñjano yavaneṣṭaś ca palāṇḍor daśajātayaḥ |
“yac cānyad” evaṃvidhaṃ kovidārādi śiṣṭāḥ “paricakṣate” parivarjayanti tad api na bhojyam iti śeṣaḥ |
uśanā |
kusumbhaṃ nālikāśākaṃ vṛntākaṃ pautikaṃ tathā |
bhakṣayan patitas tu syād api vedāntago dvijaḥ ||
“vṛntākaṃ” śvetam atra | “śvetavṛntākam” iti devalavacanāt |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
vṛntākanālikāpotakusumbhāśmantakāś ceti śākaphalānām abhojyāḥ |
[281] “aśmantako” vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ |
“varjayed” ity anuvṛttau yājñavalkyaḥ [1.171] |
devatārthaṃ haviḥ śigruṃ lohitān vraścanāṃs tathā |
anupākṛtamāṃsāni viḍjāni kavakāni ca ||
“devatārthaṃ” balyupahārādi | “haviḥ” homārthaṃ yac carupuroḍāśādi | “śigruḥ” śobhāñjanaḥ | “anupākṛtaṃ” paśuyāgādau mantravat tṛṇena paśoḥ sparśanam upākaraṇam, tadrahitam |
tathā [1.173] |
vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ |
“vṛthā” devatādyanuddeśena kevalam ātmārthaṃ yat pacyate, “na paced annam ātmārtham” iti vacanāt | vṛthānnapratiṣedhe siddhe kṛsarādīnāṃ punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣārthaḥ | “kṛsaraḥ’ tilamudgābhyāṃ saha siddha odanaḥ | “saṃyāvaḥ” ghṛtakṣīragodhūmacūrṇasiddhaḥ, utkariketi prasiddhaḥ | “śaṣkulī” mudgādicūrṇasiddhā satilā snehapakvā |
hārītaḥ |
paramānnakṛsaraśaṣkulīmāṃsayāvakāpūpān na pācayed ātmārtham | na vaṭaplakṣodumbaradadhitthanīpamātuluṅkāni vā bhakṣayet |
“parmānnaṃ” pāyasam | “apūpāḥ” pūrikā iti prasiddhāḥ | “nīpaḥ” kadambaḥ | “mātuluṅgaḥ” bījapūraḥ |
[282] yamaḥ |
yavāgūṃ kṛsaraṃ caivāpūpapāyasaśaṣkulīḥ |
ṛjīṣapakvaṃ māṃsaṃ ca matsyān apy anupākṛtān ||
varjayet sarvaśuktāni devānnāni havīṃśi ca |
snehena tu samāyuktaṃ naiva sarvaṃ prayojayet ||
“ṛjīṣapakvaṃ” bhaumoṣmapakvam |
gautamaḥ [9.58] |
uddhṛtasnehavialayanapiṇyākamathitaprabhṛtīni cāttavīryāṇi nāśnīyāt |
“āttavīryāṇi” uddhṛtasārāṇi |
“aśnīyād” ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ [68.27–31] |
noddhatasneham | na divā dhānāḥ | na rātrau tilasaṃbandham | na dadhisaktūn | na kovidāravaṭapippalaśāṇaśākam |
devalaḥ |
na bījāny upabhuñjīa rogāpattim ṛte budhaḥ |
phalāny eṣām anantāni bījānāṃ hi vināśayet ||
nāśnīyāt payasā naktaṃ bhuktaṃ cen na svapen niśi |
na kṣīram utsṛjet prāptaṃ pavitraṃ hi payaḥ smṛtam ||
[283] “bījāni” kūṣmāṇḍabījāni |
yamaḥ |
bhinnabhāṇḍe na bhuñjīta na rātrau dadhisaktukān |
divā dadhitthadhānāsu rātrau ca dadhisaktuṣu ||
kovidāre ca rajake taskare sūtake tathā |
śleṣmātake tathālakṣmīr nityam eva kṛtālayā ||
“sūtake” jananamaraṇāśauce |
brahmapurāṇe |
rājamāṣāḥ sthūlamudgās tathā vṛṣakavāsakau |
masūrāḥ śatapuṣpāś ca kusumbhaṃ śrīniketanam ||
sasyāny etāny abhakṣyāṇi na ca deyāni kasyacit |
āpastambaḥ [2.18.1–4] |
vilayanaṃ mathitaṃ piṇyākaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ ca varjayet |
kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ ye cānye ‘nāśyasaṃmitāḥ ||
ahaviṣyam anṛtaṃ krodhaṃ yena ca krodhayet param |
smṛtim icchan medhāṃ puṣṭiṃ dvādaśaitāni varjayet ||
[284] “kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ” kaliṅgakādi | “anāśyam” abhakṣyaṃ maṇḍūkādi, taiḥ “saṃmitāḥ” tulyatvena matāḥ | yena ca vyāpāreṇ parasya krodho jāyate taṃ vyāpāraṃ svayam akruddho ‘pi varjayet |
viśvāmitraḥ |
jātikriyābhāvaduṣṭam asatsaṃsargaduṣṭaṃ sahṛllekhaṃ ca na bhakṣayet |
“bhāvaduṣṭam” abhakṣyapadārthasadṛśatvena buddhisvīkṛtam | “anāśyasaṃmitā” ity āpastambavacanānusārāt |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.20–22] |
ucchiṣṭam aguror abhojyam | svam ucchiṣṭam ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca | vasanakeśakīṭopahataṃ ca |
“svam ucchiṣṭaṃ” svayam eva kiṃcid bhuktvā yat parityaktam | “vasanam” atra parihitaṃ vāsaḥ |
sumantuḥ |
keśakīṭakṣutavaco’bhihataṃ śvabhir āghrātaṃ prekṣitaṃ cādadhi paryuṣitaṃ punaḥsiddhaṃ caṇḍālāvekṣitam annam abhojyam anyatra hiraṇyodakaiḥ spṛṣṭān |
[285]  “vaco’bhihataṃ” yasyānnasyābhimukhena vāguccāraṇaṃ kṛtam | “prekṣitaṃ” śvabhir eva | “adadhi” dadhivyatiriktam | “paryuṣitaṃ” siddham annaṃ rātryantaritam | “punaḥsiddhaṃ” dviḥpakvam | “hiraṇyodakaṃ” hiraṇyaspṛṣṭam udakam |
manuḥ [4.207–208] |
mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ ||
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃ cāpy udakyayā |
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭam eva ca ||
“patatriṇā” pakṣiṇā | “avalīḍham” āsvāditam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.167–168] |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitocchiṣṭaṃ śvaspṛṣṭaṃ patitekṣitam |
udakyāspṛṣṭasaṃghuṣṭaṃ paryāyānnaṃ ca varjayet ||
“paryāyānnam” anyasaṃbandhi yad annam anyavyapadeśena dīyete | yathā –
brāhmaṇānnaṃ dadac chūdraḥ śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo dadat |
[286]        ubhāv evāv abhojyānnau bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
yamaḥ |
kākakukkuṭsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ vā kṛmisaṃyutam |
abhojyaṃ tad vijānīyād dharmarājavaco yathā ||
āpastambaḥ [1.16.21–33; 1.17.1–5] |
aprayatopahatam annam aprayataṃ na tv abhojyam | aprayatena śūdreṇopahatam abhojyam | yasmiṃś cānne keśaḥ syāt | anyad vāmedhyam | amedhyair avamṛṣṭam | kīṭo vāmedhyasevī | mūṣakalāṅgaṃ ca | padā copahatam | sicā vā | śunā vāpapātreṇa vā dṛṣṭam | sicā copahṛtam | dāsyā vā naktam āhṛtam | bhuñjānaṃ vā yatra śūdra upaspṛśet | anarhadbhir vā samānapaṅktau | bhuñjāneṣu vā yatrānūtthāyocciṣṭaṃ prayacched ācāmed vā | kutsayitvā vā yatrānnaṃ dadyuḥ | manuṣyair avaghrātam anyair vāmedhyaiḥ |
“aprayatopahatam” “aprayatena” aśucinā “upahatam” spṛṣṭam | “aprayatam” aśuci | “na tv abhojyam” kiṃ tv agnāv adhisaṃśrayaṇādinā śuddhiṃ kṛtvā bhojyaṃ bhavati | “aprayatena” śūdreṇa sprṣṭaṃ na kathaṃcanāpi bhojyam | “amedhyam” ūṣara[287]mṛttikādi | “amedhyaiḥ” kalañjapalāṇḍvādibhiḥ | “avamṛṣṭaṃ” spṛṣṭam | “kīṭo” “amedhyasevī” yasminn anne tad abhojyam iti vākyaparisamāptiḥ | “mūṣakalā” mūṣikāviṣṭhā | “aṅgaṃ” mūṣikāpucchapadādi | “padā upahatam” padā spṛṣṭam | “sicā” spṛṣṭaṃ, vastravātena prakṣālitenāpi spṛṣṭam | “śunā” kukkureṇāpi spṛṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ca | “apapātreṇa” patitādinā | “upahṛtam” ānītam | “dāsī” iti strīliṅgam avivakṣitam | “upaspṛśed” iti atra tad annam ardhabhuktam apy abhojyam iti śeṣaḥ | “anarhadbhiḥ” abhijanavidyācāraśūnaiḥ, “samānapaṅktau” annam abhojyam | arhadbhir vā samānapaṅktau yatrārdhabhukteṣu kaścid “anūtthāya” bhṛtyādeḥ “ucchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet” tatretareṣām ardhabhuktam annam abhojyam | etena “ācāmed” ity api vyākhyātam | “kutsayitvā” “ehi viṣaṃ bhuṅkṣva” ityādy uktvā | “manuṣyair avaghrātaṃ” prayatnata it śeṣaḥ | “amedhyaiḥ mārjāragardabhādibhiḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tatrāpeyāny abhakṣyāṇi ca varjayet | amedhyapatita[288]cāṇḍālapulkasa-rajasvalākuṇapakuṣṭhisaṃspṛṣṭāni |
“pulkasaḥ” mlecchajātiviśeṣaḥ | “kuṇapaḥ” śavaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra 71; śrāddha 86a] |
nādyāc chāstraniṣiddhaṃ tu bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ dvijaḥ |
māṃsaṃ vigarhitaṃ caiva śuktaṃ bahuvidhaṃ tathā ||
atyamlaṃ śuktam ākhyātaṃ ninditaṃ brahmavādibhiḥ |
“vigarhitaṃ” śiṣṭair ninditam |
devalaḥ |
abhojyaṃ prāhur āhāraṃ śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ sadā |
anyatra madhusaktubhyāṃ bhakṣyebhyaḥ sarpiṣo guḍāt ||
avalīḍhaṃ śvamārjāradhvāṅkṣakukkuṭamūṣikaiḥ |
bhojane nopayuñjīta tad amedhyaṃ hi dharmataḥ ||
viśuddham api cāhāraṃ makṣikākṛmitantubhiḥ |
keśalomanakhair vāpi dūṣitaṃ parivarjayet ||
“bhakṣyā” laḍḍukādayaḥ |
[289] aśnīyād ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
na rajasvalayā dattaṃ na puṃścalyā na kruddhayā | na malavadvāsasā | nāparayā dvārā dāpitam | na dviḥ pakvam | na śuktam | na paryuṣitam | anyatra guḍapiṣṭasaktusnehagorasatailādiṣupakvāt | na tailaṃ dadhyanupānam | nāvakṣutānnam | na jugupsitam |
“malavadvāsā” amedhyaliptavastrā | “aparayā dvārā” mukhyadvārātiriktadvāreṇa | “tailādiṣu” ityādiśabdād ghṛtasarṣapasnehagrahaṇam | “na tailaṃ dadhyanupānam” bhojanānte tailaṃ dadhī vā yathā bhavati tathā nāśnīyāt |
“abhojyam” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [17.14] |
paryuṣitam aśākabhakṣyasnehamāṃsamadhuni |
śākādīni paryuṣitāni vihāyety arthaḥ
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt | na dviḥ pakvam | na śuktam | na paryuṣitam | anyatra rāgacukraṣāḍavadadhiguḍagodhūmayavapiṣṭavikārebhyaḥ |
“rāgāḥ” mudgadāḍimamāṃsādyā aṃśukagālitāḥ pralehāḥ | “ṣāḍavāḥ” svādvamlakaṭukādyāḥ pralehāḥ eva |
[290] vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.28–29] |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaaṃ sakṛllekhaṃ punaḥsiddham āmam ṛjīṣapakvam | kāmaṃ tu dadhnā ghṛtenābhighāritam upabhuñjīta |
“āmaṃ” taṇḍulādi | “kāmaṃ dadhnā ghṛtenābhighāritam upabhuñjīta” ity anena bhojyāntarāsaṃbhava evaṃ bhuñjītety uktam |
āpastambaḥ [1.17.14–20] |
nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt | tathā rasānām amāṃsamadhulavaṇānīti parihāpya | tailasarpiṣī tūpayojayed udake ‘vadhāya | kṛtānnaṃ paryuṣitam akhādyāpeyānādyam | śuktaṃ ca | phāṇitapṛthukataṇḍulakarambhabharuja-saktuśākamāṃsapiṣṭakṣīravikārauṣadhivanaspatimūlaphalavarjam | suktaṃ cāparayogam |
“āpaṇīyaṃ” haṭṭād upānatam | “rasāḥ” guḍādayaḥ | “udake ‘vadhāya” udakapātre maṇikādau vidhāya | “akhādyāpeyānādyaṃ”, “khādyaṃ” kharaviśadaṃ “peyaṃ” dravam, “ādyaṃ” mṛduviśadam odanādi | “śuktaṃ ca” paryuṣitam api nopayojyam [291] ity arthaḥ | “phāṇitam” ikṣurasavikārapākajanyo dravaḥ | “pṛthukataṇḍulāḥ” bhraṣṭadhānyakuṭṭanān niṣpāditās taṇḍulāś cipiṭakā iti prasiddhāḥ | “karambhāḥ” dadhimiśrāḥ saktavaḥ | “bharujāḥ” dhānāḥ | “śuktaṃ cāparayoga” iti, pareṇānnena yogo yasyeti gamakatvād vaiyadhikaraṇye bahuvrīhiḥ | yac ca śuktam apeyam akhādyam uktaṃ tad dravyāntarāsaṃyuktam | “śuktaṃ tu kevalam” iti gautamavacanāt |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācār. 80–81] |
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ śuktam api yac caiva dadhisaṃbhavam |
kandamūlaphalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śastaiḥ śuktāsavaṃ tu tat ||
avikāri bhaven medhyam abhakṣyaṃ tad vikārakṛt |
“kandamūlaphalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ” ityāder ayam arthaḥ — yac chuktaṃ kandādibhiḥ kṛtasaṃdhānaṃ tad yadi mohādivikārakaraṃ na bhavati tadā “bhakṣyaṃ” bhakṣaṇīyam |
yamaḥ |
śuktāni hi dvijo ‘nnāni na bhuñjīta kadācana |
prakṣālitāni nirdoṣāṇy āpadharmo yadā bhavet ||
[292]        masūramāṣasaṃyuktaṃ tathā paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
tat tu prakṣālitaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīta hy abhighāritam ||
yat “śuktaṃ” yat “paryuṣitaṃ ca masūramāṣasaṃyuktaṃ” tat prakṣālya “abhighāritaṃ kṛtvā” āpadi bhuñjītety arthaḥ |
manuḥ [5.24–25] |
yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||
cirasthitam api tv ādyam asnehāktaṃ dvijātibhiḥ |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ payasaś caiva vikriyā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.169] |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ snehāktaṃ cirasaṃsthitam |
asnehā api godhūmayavagorasavikriyāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
apūpāś ca karambhāś ca dhānā vaṭakasaktavaḥ |
śākaṃ māṃsam apūpaṃ ca sūpaṃ kṛsaram eva ca ||
yavāgūṃ pāyasaṃ caiva yac cānyat snehasaṃyutam |
sarvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ śuktaṃ ca parivarjayet ||
[293] vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.37–38] |
apūpadhānākarambhasaktuvaṭakatailapāyasaśākāni ca śuktāni varjayet | anyāṃś ca kṣīrapiṣṭavikārān |
devīpurāṇe |
vṛthakṛsarapūpāni pāyasaṃ madhusarpiṣī |
vṛthāmāṃsaṃ ca nāśnīyāt pitṛdevavivarjitam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
caṇḍālapatitāmedhyakuṇapaiḥ kuṣṭhinā tathā |
brahmaghnasūtikodakyākauleyakakuṭumbibhiḥ ||
iṣṭaṃ vā keśakīṭāktaṃ mṛdbhasmakanakāmbubhiḥ |
śuddham adyāt sahṛllekhaṃ prabhūtaṃ coṣṇam eva ca ||
“kauleyakaḥ” śvā |
tathā |
ucchiṣṭena tu śūdreṇa saṃspṛṣṭaḥ pariveṣakaḥ |
dravyahastas tu yat kiṃcid dadyāt tv annaṃ na bhakṣayet ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
bhinnabhāṇḍagataṃ tadvan mukhavātopamārjitam |
[294]        tad ūṣmapakvam asvinnam avalīḍham asaṃskṛtam ||
piṣṭaśākekṣupayasāṃ vikārā nṛpanandana |
tathā māṃsavikārāś ca varjyāś caiva ciroṣitāḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
śūdrabhuktāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu nādyād bhāṇḍasthitaṃ tv api |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
āyasena tu pātreṇa yad annam upanīyate |
bhoktā viṣṭhāsamaṃ bhuṅkte dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
aṅgulyā dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ ca yat |
mṛttikābhakṣaṇaṃ caiva tulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaiḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
eken pāṇinā dattaṃ śūdrādattaṃ na bhakṣayet |
ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ pānīyāṃ pāyasaṃ tathā |
bhikṣā ca hatadattā yā na grāhyā kutracit kvacit ||
atha dugdhāni
tatra manuḥ [5.8–9] |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram auṣṭram ekaśaphaṃ tathā |
[295]        āvikaṃ sandhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ payaḥ ||
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ māhiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktāni caiva hi ||
“anidaśāyāḥ” yasyāḥ prasūtāyā daśadināny anapakrāntāni | “ekasaphāḥ” ekakhurā aśvādayaḥ | “āvikaṃ” meṣīkṣīram | “sandhinī” yā ṛtumatī vṛṣabham icchati | “sandhinī vṛṣasyantī tasyāḥ payo na pibed ṛtumad bhavati” iti hārītavacanāt | “vivatsā” vatsarahitā | “āraṇyā mṛgāḥ” rurumahiṣapṛṣatādayaḥ |
gautamaḥ [17.20–23] |
goś ca kṣīram anirdaśāyāḥ sūtake | ajāmahiṣyoś ca | nityam āvikam apeyam auṣṭram ekaśaphaṃ ca | syandinīyamasūsandhinīnāṃ ca |
“syandinī” vatsaṃ vinaiva prasravatstanī | “yamasūḥ” yamalāpatyā |
[296] baudhāyanaḥ [1.12.9–10] |
anirdaśāhāsandhinīkṣīram apeyam | vivatsānyavatsayoś ca |
“anyavatsā” anyasyā vatsena yuktā |
āpastambaḥ [1.17.21–24] |
apeyam | tathailakaṃ payaḥ | uṣṭrīkṣīramṛgīkṣīrasandhinīkṣīrayamasūkṣīrāṇīti | dhenoś cānirdaśāyāḥ |
“payo na pibet” ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
na vivatsāyāḥ steyayogāt | na hatavatsāyāḥ śokābhibhūtatvāt | na nirṇikāyā asattvāt | nānyasyācchidyātmanāśnīyāt | evaṃ na navasūtāyāḥ sarajastvāt | saptarātrād ity eke | daśarātrād ity apare | māsena peyūṣaṃ bhavatīty apare | evaṃ hy āha |
dvau māsau pāyayed vatsaṃ tṛtīye dvistanaṃ duhet |
caturthe tristhanaṃ duhyād yathānyāyaṃ yathābalam ||
[297] “vivatsā” atra viprakṛṣṭavatsā | “nirṇiktāyāḥ” niḥśeṣeṇa dugdhāyāḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
ghṛtāt phenaṃ ghṛtān maṇḍaṃ peyūṣam atha vāpi goḥ |
saguḍaṃ maricāktaṃ tu tathā paryuṣitaṃ dadhi ||
dīrṇaṃ takram apeyaṃ ca naṣṭasvādaṃ ca phenavat |
pramādād bhakṣitair ebhir vane pakṣaṃ vrataṃ caret ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
kapilāṃ yaḥ pibec chūdro narake sa vipacyate |
hutaśeṣaṃ pibed vipro vipraḥ syād anyathā paśuḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
api prayāṇasamaye rātrau na prāśayed dadhi |
madhuparkapradānaṃ ca varjayitvā tu kāmataḥ ||
divā dhānāsu vasati rātrau ca dadhisaktuṣu |
alakṣmīḥ kovidāreṣu kapittheṣu sadā sthitā ||
[298]
atha pakṣiṇaḥ
tatra manuḥ [5.11–16] |
kravyādāñ chakunīn sarvān tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśapāṃṣ ṭiṭṭibhaṃ ca vivarjayet ||
kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
sārasaṃ rajjudālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike ||
pratudān jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān |
nimajjataś ca matsyādān saunaṃ vallūram eva ca ||
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakam |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃś ca matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ ||
“kravyādāḥ” māṃsāśino gṛdhrādayaḥ | “grāmanivāsinaḥ” pārāvatādayaḥ | atrobhayatra śakunīti saṃbadhyate | “anidirṣṭān” śrutau bhakṣyatvenākathitān | śrutau hi kecit kvacit bhakṣyatvenaikaśaphā nirdiṣṭāḥ | yathā “tvāṣṭraṃ vāḍavam ālabheta tasya ca māṃsam aśnīyāt” iti | ato ye kvacid yajñe śrutyā bhakṣayatvenoktās te taddarśanenānyatrāpi na bhakṣaṇīyāḥ | teṣām ekaśaphānāṃ anyatrāpi bhakṣyatvenānirdeśāt | “kalaviṅkaḥ” [299] grāmacaṭakaḥ | tasya ca grāmanivāsitvena niṣedhe siddhe punar grāmavacanaṃ grāmāranyobhayanivāsitvād āraṇyasyāpi niṣedhārtham | “plavaḥ” plavanenaiva ya āhāraṃ prāpnoti | “rajjudālaḥ” kāṣṭhakuṭṭakaḥ | “dātyūhaḥ” kālakaṇṭhaḥ, ḍāḍaka iti prasiddhaḥ | “pratudāḥ” ye cañcvā pratudya bhakṣayanti | “jālapādāḥ” āṭyādayaḥ | “koyaṣṭiḥ” pakṣiviśeṣaḥ | “nakhaviṣkirāḥ” nakhair vikīrya ye bhakṣayanti | “nimajyato matsyādāḥ” madguprabhṛtayaḥ | “saunaṃ” sūnāsthānasaṃbhavaṃ māṃsam | “vallūraṃ” śuṣkamāṃsam | “kākolo” droṇakākaḥ | “matsyādāḥ” nakrādayaḥ | “sarvaśaḥ” sarvaprakāreṇa |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.172–173] |
kravyādapakṣidātyūhaśukapratudaṭiṭṭibhān |
sārasaikaśaphān haṃsān sarvāṃś ca grāmavāsinaḥ ||
koyaṣṭiplavacakrāhvabalākābakaviṣkirān |
tathā [1.174–175] |
kalaviṅkaṃ sakākolaṃ kuraraṃ rajjudālakam |
[300]        jālapādān khañjarīṭān ajñātāṃś ca mṛgadvijān ||
cāṣāṃś ca raktapādāṃś ca saunaṃ vallūram eva ca |
matsyāṃś ca kāmato jagdhvā sopavāsas tryahaṃ vaset ||
“kuraraḥ” kuralaḥ | “cāṣaḥ” kikīdiviḥ | “raktapādāḥ” kadambādayaḥ |
devalaḥ |
balākāhaṃsadātyūhabhṛṅgarājakacitrakāḥ |
ulūkakuraraśyenagṛdhrakukkuṭavāyasāḥ ||
cakoraḥ kokilo rajjuḥ kuṭilaś cāṣamadgukau |
kaṅkaḥ sāravaṇo bhāsaḥ śatapatraplavaṅgamāḥ ||
utkrośo barhiṇaḥ krauñcaś cakravākaḥ śilīmukhaḥ |
pārāvatakapotau cābhakṣyāḥ pakṣigaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
“bhrṅgarājo” dhūmrāṭaḥ, dhanacchuā iti prasiddhaḥ | “citrakaḥ” citrakapotaḥ | “kaṅkaḥ” lohitapatraḥ |
“abhojyam” ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.48] |
[301]
śakunānāṃ vidhunaviṣkirajālapādāḥ kalaviṅkaplavahaṃsacakravākabhāsa-vāyasapārāvatāḥ kukkuṭasāraṅgāḥ pāṇḍukapotakrauñcakrakarabakabalākā-madguṭiṭṭibhamāndhālanaktaṃcarā dārvāghāṭakacātakatailātakahārītaka-khañjarīṭakagrāmyakukkuṭaśukasārikākokilakravyādo grāmopacāriṇaś ca |
vidhunotīti “vidhunaḥ” | “bhāsaḥ” tallacaro gṛdhraviśeṣaḥ | “sāraṅgo” mṛgaḥ | “krakaraḥ” kṛkalāsaḥ | “māndhālo” vāggudaḥ | “naktaṃcarā” ulūkādayaḥ | “dārvāghāṭaḥ” kāṣṭhakuṭṭakaḥ | “hārītakaḥ” haritapakṣaḥ kapotākṛtiḥ | “grāmopacāriṇaḥ” grāma eva ye sadā caranti |
atra “abhakṣyā” iti bhāvini gautamaḥ [17.26–27] |
kalaviṅkaplavacakravākahaṃsāḥ | kākakaṅkagṛdhraśyenā jalajā raktapādatuṇḍā grāmyakukkuṭasūkarāḥ |
[302] abhakṣyā ity anuvṛttau yamaḥ |
kalaviṅkaplavacakravākahaṃsabhāsasārasā ye cānye pratudā viṣkirā jālapādā raktapādā koyaṣṭikāḥ |
punar yamaḥ |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca jālapādāṃś ca kukkuṭān |
bhakṣayitvā pated vipro yo ‘pi syāt sarvavedavit ||
atrābhakṣyaprakaraṇe āpastambaḥ [1.17.32–36] |
kukkuṭo viṣkirāṇām | plavaḥ pratudām | kravyādaḥ | haṃsasārasacakravāka-suparṇāś ca | kruñcakrauñcavārdhrāṇasalakṣmaṇavarjyam |
gautamaḥ [17.32–33] |
nicudārubakabalākāśukamadguṭiṭṭibhamāndhālanaktaṃcarā abhakṣyāḥ | bhakṣyāḥ pratudaviṣkirājālapādāḥ |
[303] bhakṣyā ity anuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ [1.12.7] |
pakṣiṇas tittirikapotakapiñjalavārdhrāṇasatṛṇamayūravāraṇā vāraṇavarjāḥ pañcaviṣkirāḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ [17.27] |
tittiriṃ ca mayūraṃ ca lāvakaṃ ca kapiñjalam |
vārdhrāṇasaṃ vartakaṃ ca bhakṣyān āha yamaḥ sadā ||
atha paśavaḥ
tatra devalaḥ |
abhakṣyāḥ paśujātīnāṃ gokharoṣṭrāśvakuñjarāḥ |
siṃhavyāghrarkṣaśarabhāḥ sarpājagarajālakāḥ ||
ākhumūṣikamārjāranakulagrāmasūkarāḥ |
śvaśṛgālamṛgadvīpigolāṅgūlanṛmarkaṭāḥ ||
[304] “ṛkṣaḥ” bhallūkaḥ | “śarabho” aṣṭapadaḥ | “ajagaraḥ” sarpaviśeṣaḥ | “ākhuḥ” mahānunduruḥ | “mūṣiko” atra svalpamūṣikaḥ | “dvīpī” citrakaḥ | “golāṅgūlaaḥ” kṛṣṇamukho vānaraḥ | “nā” manuṣyaḥ | “markaṭo” vānaraḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mṛgapakṣiṇāṃ kravyādāṃś ca varjayet | sarvamāṃsādānāṃ yāvantas tathoktās tāvanto ‘bhakṣyāḥ |
yamaḥ |
gavāśvaṃ gardabhoṣṭraṃ ca śvaśṛgālaṃ tathaiva ca |
viṣkirān pratudān bhuktvā sadyaḥ patati vai dvijaḥ ||
“nityam abhojyam” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [17.26] |
ubhayatodantakeśyalomaikaśaphakalaviṅkaplavacakravākahaṃsāḥ |
“ubhayatodantaḥ” manuṣyaḥ | “keśī” camarī | “alomāḥ” sarpādayaḥ |
punar gautamaḥ [17.29] |
apannadadavasannavṛthāmāṃsāni |
[305] “apannadat” yāvat svabhāvato dantā na patanti tāvad bhakṣyo ‘py abhakṣyaḥ | “avasanno” vyādhiglānaḥ | “vṛthāmāṃsaṃ” yad devapitrādyuddeśena na pacyate |
“abhojyā” ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.43–47] |
gauragavayaśarabhājāś ca | anuddiṣṭās tathā | dhenvanaḍvāhāvapannadantāś ca | bhakṣyau dhenvanaḍvāhau medhyau vājasaneyake vijñāyate | khaḍge tu vivadanty agrāmyasūkare ca |
“gauraḥ” aśvasadṛśa āraṇyapaśuḥ | “medhyau” medho yajñas tadaṅgabhūtau | “khaḍge ca vivadanty agrāmyasūkare ca” iti, khaḍgagrāmyasūkarayoḥ kecin munayo bhakṣyatvaṃ vadanti, kecic cābhakṣyatvam | tataś ca vikalpaḥ | sa ca śrāddhaniyuktāniyuktatayā vyavasthitaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
paśoś ca māryamāṇasya na māṃsaṃ grāhayed dvijaḥ |
pṛṣṭhamāṃsaṃ garbhaśayyāṃ śuṣkamāṃsam athāpi vā ||
bhūmer antaritaṃ kṛtvā mṛdbhiś cācchāditaṃ ca yat |
pakvamāṃsam ṛjīṣaṃ ca prayatnāt tan na bhakṣayet ||
[306] manuḥ [5.17] |
na bhakṣayed ekacarān ajñātāṃś ca mṛgadvijān |
bhakṣyeṣv api samuddiṣṭān sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā ||
“ekacarāḥ” ekākinaḥ prāyeṇa svabhāvato ye caranti sarpādayaḥ | “ajñātāḥ” nāmataḥ svabhāvato rasavīryavipākataś cābhiyuktair api ye | sāmānyato viśeṣataś ca pratiṣedhānākrāntatvena bhakṣyavargamadhyapātād “bhakṣyeṣv api samuddiṣṭān” ity uktam |
abhakṣyaprakaraṇe āpastambaḥ [1.17.37] |
pañcanakhānāṃ godhākacchapaśvāvicchalyakakhaḍgaśaśapūtikhaṣavarjam |
“śvāvit” sedhā | “śalyakaḥ” śalyasahitaḥ sedhāsadṛśāḥ prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | “pūtikhaṣaḥ” himavati prasiddha iti kapardī |
[307] manuḥ [5.18] |
śvāvidhaṃ śalyakaṃ godhāṃ khaḍgakūrmaśaśāṃs tathā|
bhakṣyān pañcanakheṣv āhur anuṣṭrāṃś caikatodataḥ ||
“ekatodataḥ” ekatodantān |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.12.5] |
bhakṣyāḥ śvāvidgodhāśaśakaśalyakakacchapakhaḍgāḥ khaḍgavarjāḥ pañca pañcanakhāḥ |
mahābhārate |
ājaṃ gavyaṃ ca yan māṃsaṃ māyūraṃ ca vivarjayet |
hārītaḥ |
grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnām aśnanti yathājameṣamahiṣahariṇakhaḍgaruru-pṛṣatarśyanyaṅkumahāraṇyavāsinaś ca varāhān | tathā śaśakaśalyakasedhā-godhākūrmavartakatittirimayūravārdhrāṇasalāvakakrakurakapiñjalān | saśalkāṃś ca matsyān nyāyopapannān bhakṣayet |
[308] “ruruḥ” bahuśākhaśṛṅgo mṛgaḥ | “pṛṣato” binducitrito mṛgaḥ | “ṛśyaḥ” mṛduśṛṅgah, rojha it prasiddhaḥ | “nyaṅku” śabarasadṛśaḥ śṛṅgarahitaḥ | “sedhā” śalalavatī |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
grāmyāraṇyāś caturdaśa | gaur avir ajo ‘śvo ‘śvataro gardabho manuṣyaś ceti sapta grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ | pakṣivānaramahiṣasarīsṛparurupṛṣatamṛgās ceti saptāraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ |
atha matsyāḥ
tatra manu-yamau |
yo yasya māṃsam aśnāti sa tanmāṃsāda ucyate |
matsyādaḥ sarvamāṃsādas tasmān matsyān vivarjayet ||
āpastambaḥ [1.17.38–39] |
abhakṣyaś ceṭo matsyānām | sarpaśīrṣo mṛduraḥ kravyādo ye cāne vikṛtāsyā yathā manuṣyaśirasaḥ |
“sarpaśīrṣaḥ” sarpasyeva śiro yasya | “mṛduraḥ” makaraḥ | “kravyādaḥ” ye prāyeṇ māṃsam aśnanti nakrādayaḥ |
[309] yamaḥ |
abhakṣyā makarasarpasarīsṛpamadgumayūravarmikanakrakarkaṭaśiśumārāḥ | ye cānye hayakarṇikāḥ | ye cānye ‘śalkā matsyā ubhayakāsyāḥ |
“sarpaḥ” atra jalasarpo ḍuṇḍubhaḥ | “sarīsṛpaḥ” atra jalaukāḥ | “madguḥ” jalakākaḥ | “mayūraḥ” atra jalacaro mayūrasadṛśaḥ pakṣī | “varmikaḥ” svalpasarpasadṛśo matsyaḥ | “ubhayakāsyāḥ” ubhayatomukhāḥ |
punar yamaḥ |
matsyān aśalkān sarvān vedādhyāyī vivarjayet |
“vedādhyāyī” traivarṇiko ‘tra |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kulīravārtākapṛtanajalānartakṣipragāś cety abhakṣyāḥ | sarpaśīrṣā
vikṛtamukhāś ca |
“abhakṣyāḥ” ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.41–42] |
matsyānāṃ veṭagavayaśiśumāranakrakulīrāḥ | vikṛtarūpāḥ sarpaśīrṣāś ca |
[310] devalaḥ |
śambūkaḥ śuktir nakhaśuktiḥ śiśumāraḥ plavaṅgamaḥ |
matsyāś ca vikṛtākārā naiva bhakṣyā jalaukasām ||
“nakhaśuktiḥ” dīrghaśuktikā | “plavaṅgamo” maṇḍūkaḥ |
manuḥ [5.16] |
pāṭhīnarohitāv ādyau niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ |
rājīvān siṃhatuṇḍāṃś ca saśalkāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ ||
“niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ” śrāddhādyarthaṃ viniyuktau | “ādyau” adanīyau |
śaṅkhaḥ [17.25] |
rājīvāḥ siṃhtuṇḍāś ca saśalkāś ca tathaiva ca |
pāṭhīnarohitau vāpi bhakṣyā matsyeṣu kīrtitāḥ ||
“bhakṣyā” ity anuvṛttau baudhayanaḥ [1.12.8] |
matsyāḥ sahasradaṃṣṭraś cilicimo varmī bṛhacchiro romaśakarirohitarājīvāḥ |
[311] hārītaḥ |
saśalkāṃś ca matsyān nyāyopapannān bhakṣayet |
atra “bhakṣyā” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [17.34] |
matsyāś cāvikṛtāḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.177–178] |
bhakṣyāḥ pañcanakhā sedhāgodhākacchapaśalyakāḥ |
śaśaś c matsyeṣv api tu siṃhatuṇḍakarohitāḥ ||
tathā pāṭhīnarājīvasaśalkāś ca dvijātibhiḥ |
ataḥ śṛṇudhvaṃ māṃsasya vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇavarjane ||
atha māṃsabhakṣaṇavarjanavidhiḥ
tatra manuḥ [5.26–32] |
etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyam aśeṣataḥ |
māṃsasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇavarjane ||
[312]        prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyuktaś ca prāṇānām eva cātyaye ||
prāṇasyānnam idaṃ sarvaṃ prajāpatir akalpayat |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ caiva sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam ||
