carāṇām annam acarā daṃṣṭriṇāṃ cāpy adaṃṣṭriṇaḥ |
ahastāś ca sahastānāṃ śūrāṇāṃ caiva bhīravaḥ ||
nāttā duṣyaty adann ādyān prāṇino ‘hany ahany api |
dhātraiva sṛṣṭā hy ādyāś ca prāṇino ‘ttāra eva ca ||
yajñāya jagdhir māṃsasyety eṣa daivo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
ato ‘nyathā pravṛttis tu rākṣaso vidhir ucyate ||
krītvā svayaṃ vāpy utpādya paropahṛtam eva vā |
devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati ||
“etad uktaṃ dvijātīnām” iti, pūrvoktabhakṣyābhakṣyayor brāhmaṇādivarṇatrayādhikārakathanaṃ śūdravyudāsārtham | tena laśunādibhakṣaṇe tasyādoṣaḥ | tanmadhyapaṭhitakākādibhakṣaṇaṃ tu mahājanavigānād doṣāvaham | ato vakṣyamāṇamāṃsavarjanavidhānaṃ cāturvarṇyasādhāraṇam | varjane tu vidhiḥ sūryānī[313]kṣaṇavat saṃkalparūpaḥ | “prokṣitam” atra yajñārthamantraiḥ saṃskṛtam, “asaṃkṛtān paśūn mantraiḥ” ityādivākyaśeṣāt | “brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā” iti, yadā brāhmaṇāḥ kecit pratyekaṃ kāmayante tvayā māṃsaṃ bhoktavyam iti, tadā teṣām icchayaikavāraṃ māṃsaṃ bhakṣayato na doṣaḥ, “sakṛd brāhmaṇakāmyayā” iti yamavacanāt | “prāṇānām eva cātytye” iti, rogeṇānnābhāvena vā yadā māṃsabhakṣaṇavyatirekeṇa prāṇātyayaḥ saṃbhāvyate, tadā māṃsaṃ bhakṣyam ity arthaḥ | eṣām eva pūrvoktānām arthavādāḥ “prāṇasyānna” ityādayaḥ | “carāḥ” hariṇādayaḥ | “acarāḥ” tṛṇādayaḥ | “daṃṣṭriṇo” vyāghrādayaḥ | “adaṃṣṭriṇo” hariṇādayaḥ | “sahastāḥ” manuṣyādayaḥ | “ahastāḥ” matsyādayaḥ | “śūrāḥ” yuddhādyutsāhitāḥ | “bhīravaḥ” kātarāḥ | “yajñāya” yajñārtham | “jagdhiḥ” bhakṣaṇam | “svayam utpādya” iti kṣatriyaviṣayam |
tathā ca mahābhārate |
kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu yo dṛṣṭo vidhis tam api me śṛṇu |
vīryeṇopārjitaṃ māṃsaṃ yathā khādan na duṣyati ||
āraṇyāḥ sarvadaivatyāḥ prokṣitāḥ sarvaśo mṛgāḥ |
[314]        agastyena purā rājan mṛgayā yena pūjyate ||
nātmānam aparityajya mṛgayā nāma vidyate |
samatām upasaṃgamya bhūtaṃ hanyeta mānavaḥ ||
ato rājarṣayaḥ sarve mṛgayāṃ yānti bhārata |
lipyante na ca doṣeṇa na caitat pātakaṃ viduḥ || iti |
“krītvā svayaṃ vā” ity anena ca devapitrarcanapūvake māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣābhāvaḥ pradarśitaḥ | yat tu māṃsavarjanāt phalaśravaṇaṃ tad apy etat prakāraprāptamāṃsabhakṣaṇavarjanaviṣayaṃ, na niyuktādiviṣayam | tathākaraṇe doṣaśruteḥ |
yājñavalkya-viśvāmitrau |
prāṇātyaye tathā śrāddhe prokṣitaṃ dvijakāmyayā |
devān pitṝn samabhyarcya khādan māṃsaṃ na doṣabhāk ||
devalaḥ |
bhakṣayann api māṃsāni śeṣabhojī na lipyate |
auṣadhārtham aśaktau ca niyogād yajñakāraṇāt ||
[315] “śeṣabhojī” devapitrādyarcanaśeṣasya bhoktā | “auṣadhārtham” auṣadhaprayojanena | “aśaktau” vyādhinābhibhave sati | tena yatra māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ vinā rogāpanayo na bhavatīti vaidyakaśāstravidāṃ niścayas tatra bhakṣyam ity arthaḥ | “niyogaḥ” śrāddhe nimantraṇam | “yajñakāraṇāt” yajñasiddhyartham | “bhakṣayan na lipayata” ity anvayaḥ |
yamaḥ |
bhakṣayet proṣitaṃ māṃsaṃ sakṛd brāhmaṇakāmyayā |
daive niyuktaḥ śrāddhe vā niyame tu vivarjayet ||
“sakṛd” ekavāraḥ | asya ca “brāhmaṇakāmyayā” ity anena saṃbandhaḥ | “niyame” māṃsavarjanavrate kṛte sati | etac c prokṣitātiriktaiḥ sarvair eva saṃbadhyate | prokṣite tu na saṃbadhyate, pratyakṣaśrutivirodhāt | evam anyasminn api māṃsabhakṣaṇābhyanujñāne smṛtyantarokte balavat pramāṇaviṣayaṃ vihāya niyame tu varjayed iti saṃbandhanīyam |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācār. 84, 81] |
rogī niyukto vidhinā hutaṃ vipravṛtas tathā |
māṃsam adyāc caturdhaiṣā parisaṃkhyā prakīrtitā ||
[316]        ato ‘nyathā tu yo ‘śnīyād vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvat prāpnoti māraṇam ||
“rogī” cātra māṃsenaiva yasya rogāpanayanaṃ bhavati | “vidhinā” śāstroktaprakāreṇa | “hutaṃ” hutāvaśiṣṭam | “vipravṛtaḥ” vipreṇa prārthitaḥ | “parisaṃkhyā” atra parigaṇanam | smṛtyantare
devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na doṣabhāk |
ityādiprakārāntareṇāpi māṃsabhakṣaṇābhyanujñānāt |
hārītaḥ |
vṛthamāṃsaṃ na bhakṣyaṃ tu prāsya kṛcchraṃ cared budhaḥ |
kāmyayā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu yathākāmaṃ samaśnuyāt ||
manu-viṣṇū |
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kathaṃcana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyāc chāśvataṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ ||
yat “prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsam” iti pūrvam uktam, tasyaiva viṣayam āha “mantrais tu saṃskṛtān” iti | “śāśvataḥ” nityavedapratipāditatvāt | “āsthitaḥ” āsritaḥ |
[317] yamaḥ |
yajuṣā saṃskṛtaṃ māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet tu yathāvidhi |
na bhakṣayed vṛthā māṃsaṃ pṛṣṭhamāṃsaṃ vivarjayet ||
“pṛṣṭhamāṃsam” pṛṣṭhasaṃbandhimāṃsam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śrāddhe tu māṃsam aśnīyāt tathātithinimittake |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam ṛcchati ||
“śrāddhe” nimantrita iti śeṣaḥ | “atithinimittake” atithyarthaṃ yad upāttaṃ tad atithinā bhakṣyam ity arthaḥ |
mahābhārate |
havir yat prokṣitaṃ mantraiḥ prokṣitābhyukṣitaṃ śuci |
vedoktena prakāreṇa pitṝṇāṃ prakriyāsu ca ||
ato ‘nyathā vṛthāmāṃsam abhakṣyaṃ manur avravīt |
tathā |
prokṣitābhyukṣitaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā brāhmaṇakāmyayā |
alpadoṣam iha jñeyaṃ viparīte tu lipyate ||
tathā |
pitṛdaivatayajñeṣu prokṣitaṃ havir ucyate |
vidhinā vedadṛṣṭena tad bhuktveha na duṣyati ||
[318] manuḥ [5.35] |
niyuktas tu yathānyāyaṃ yo māṃsaṃ nātti mānavaḥ |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavān ekaviṃśatim ||
yamaḥ |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe daive vā māṃsam utsṛjet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam aśnute ||
hārīta-śātātapau |
niyuktas tu yathā śrāddhe yas tu māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam ṛcchati ||
kṣatriyais tu mṛgavyena vidhinā samupārjitam |
śrāddhakāle praśaṃsanti siṃhavyāghrahataṃ ca yat ||
viṣachadmahataṃ caiva vyādhatiryagghataṃ ca yat |
na praśaṃsanti vai śrāddhe yac ca mantravivarjitam ||
“mṛgavyena” ākheṭakena | “vidhinā” viṣaghadmādirahitaśauryeṇa | “niryañco” atra siṃhāder anye paśavaḥ |
gautamaḥ [17.36] |
vyālahatādṛṣṭadoṣavākpraśastāny abhyukṣyopayuñjīta |
[319] “vyālāḥ” siṃhavyāghrādayaḥ smṛtyantaroktaḥ | “adṛṣṭadoṣam” aniścitadoṣam | “vākapraśastam” atra śucyaśuci veti yat saṃdigdhaṃ tad yadānyena vācā śobhanam iti praśastaṃ bhavati tadābhyukṣyopayuñjītety arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.16.16] |
hiṃsārthenāsinā chinnaṃ māṃsam abhojyam |
“hiṃsārthena” prāṇivadhārtham eva yan niyataṃ tena yat “chinnaṃ” pākādyarthaṃ kṛtam | asigrahaṇam atropalakṣaṇam |
manuḥ viṣṇuś ca dvitīya |
nādyād avidhinā māṃsaṃ vidhijño ‘nāpadi dvijaḥ |
jagdhvā hy avidhinā māṃsaṃ pretya tair adyate ‘vaśaḥ ||
na tādṛśaṃ bhavaty eno mṛgahantur dhanārthinaḥ |
yādṛśaṃ bhavati pretya vṛthāmāṃsāni khādataḥ ||
māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsam ihādmy aham |
[320]        etan māṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitum icchati |
anabhyarcya pitṝn devān na tato ‘nyo ‘sty apuṇyakṛt ||
yamaḥ |
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitum icchati |
yatra tatrābhijāyeta sa baved vyādhipīṣitaḥ ||
bhuktvā tu mohān māṃsāni naraḥ puṣṭyartham ātmanaḥ |
akṛtvehātmanaḥ śuddhiṃ tiryagyoniṣu pacyate ||
“ātmanaḥ śuddhim” prāyaścittam |
hārītaḥ |
mātṛkāt paitṛkāc caiva kilbiṣāj jantusaṃbhavaḥ |
yo yasya bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam ||
bhakṣayitvā tu yo māṃsam adbhiḥ śaucaṃ samācaret |
hasanti devatās tasya hy aśuceḥ śucidarśinaḥ ||
“kilbiṣam” atra śukraśoṇite | “tasya kilbiṣam” tanmāṃsaṃ tanmayatvāt kilbiṣam ucyate |
manuḥ [5.51] |
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaś ceti ghātakāḥ ||
[321] devalaḥ |
ātmārthaṃ svādukāmitvāj jīvajātaṃ na ghātayet |
kaṣṭaṃ hi vyāladharmitvāj jīvamāṃsopajīvanam ||
“kaṣṭam” pāpahetutvāt |
yamaḥ |
yas tu khādati māṃsāni prāṇināṃ jīvitaiṣiṇām |
hatānāṃ ca mṛtānāṃ ca yathā hantā tathaiva saḥ ||
anumantā viśastā ca nihantā krayavikrayī |
ghātakāḥ sarva evaite saṃskartā ṣaṣṭha ucyate ||
nirdeśenānumantā ca viśastā śāsanāt tathā |
hananena tathā hantā dhanena krayikas tathā ||
vikrayī ca dhanādānāt saṃskartā tatpravartanāt |
dhanena copabhogena vadhabandhena cāpy atha ||
trividhas tu vadho jñeyo bhoktā tatrātiricyate |
ghātakāḥ ṣaṭ samākhyātā bhoktā tatra tu saptamaḥ ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ teṣāṃ sakāśāt tu hy upabhoktātiricyate |
kretāraṃ bhajate pādaḥ pādo bhoktāram ṛcchati ||
[322]        ghātakaṃ bhajate pādaḥ pādam ṛcchanty atas trayaḥ |
yadi tatkhādako na syād ghātako na tathā bhavet ||
khādako ghātakaḥ kretā trayas tulyā na saṃśayaḥ |
na bhūmer jāyate māṃsaṃ na vṛkṣās tu prarohati ||
ghoraṃ prāṇivadhaṃ kṛtvā tasmān māṃsaṃ vivarjayet |
yas tu khādati māṃsāni brāhmaṇo vedavittamaḥ ||
sa pacyate nirālambe narake tena karmaṇā |
manuḥ [5.50] |
yas tu bhakṣayate māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
sa loke ‘priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiś caiva pīḍyate ||
yamaḥ |
bhakṣyāṇy api ca māṃsāni yas tu tāni na bhakṣayet |
abhakṣaṇena māṃsānāṃ sākṣād dharmaś ca tuṣyati ||
sarveṣām eva māṃsānāṃ mahān doṣas tu bhakṣaṇe |
abhakṣaṇe tu dharmaḥ syād viśiṣṭa iti naḥ smṛtam ||
manuḥ [5.53–54] |
varṣe varṣe ‘śvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ |
[323]        māṃsāni tu na khāded yas tayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam ||
phalamūlāśanair medhyair munyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
na tat phalam avāpnoti yan māṃsaparivarjanāt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.181] |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti hayamedhaphalaṃ tathā |
gṛhe ‘pi nivasan vipro munir māṃsavivarjanāt ||
nandipurāṇe |
kledi durgandhi vikṛtaṃ jugupsāspadam eva ca |
māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayed vidvān na svādu paramaṃ hitam ||
yaś ca samyak svadharmastho guruśuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ |
gacchel lokaṃ śubhaṃ martyo divyaṃ devaniṣevitam ||
tam eva divasaṃ māṃsād virato lokam āpnuyāt |
yo māsaṃ varjayen māṃsaṃ puruṣaḥ śubhamānasaḥ ||
sa yāti svargaṃ atulaṃ divyabhogasamṛddhimān |
yat tapoghorasaṃkalpaṃ puṣkareṣu śataṃ samāḥ ||
taptvā phalam avāpnoti tat phalaṃ māṃsavarjanāt |
candrasūryagrahe yas tu dadyāt pṛthvīṃ carācarām ||
[324]        gayāyāṃ tu tapo ghoraṃ yaś cābdaśatam ācaret |
evaṃvidho yaḥ paramāṃl lokān saṃprāpnuyān naraḥ ||
lokān imān samāpnoti sarvadā māṃsavarjanāt |
yaś copadeśaṃ kurute parasya tu mahātmanaḥ ||
māṃsasya varjanaphalaṃ so ‘māṃsād aphalaṃ labhet |
mahābhārate |
māsi māsy aśvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ |
na khādati ca yo māṃsaṃ samam etad yudhiṣṭhira ||
tathā |
yas tu varṣaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ tapas tapyet sudāruṇam |
yaś caikaṃ varjayen māṃsaṃ samaṃ vā syān na vā samam ||
kaumudaṃ tu viśeṣeṇa śuklapakṣaṃ narādhipa |
varjayet sarvamāṃsāni dharmo hy atra vidhīyate ||
caturo vārṣikān māsān yo māṃsam api varjayet |
catvāri bhadrāṇy āpnoti kīrtitam āyur yaśo balam ||
atha vā māsam apy ekaṃ sarvamāṃsāny abhakṣayan |
atītya sarvaduḥkhāni sukhaṃ jīven nirāmayaḥ ||
ye varjayanti māṃsāni māsaṃ pakṣam athāpi vā |
[325]        teṣāṃ hiṃsānivṛttānāṃ brahmaloko vidhīyate ||
māsaṃ tu kaumudaṃ pakṣaṃ varjitaṃ sarvarājabhiḥ |
sarvabhūtātmabhūtais tair vijñātārthaparāparaiḥ ||
nābhāgenāmbarīṣeṇa gayena ca mahātmanā |
āyuṣā cānaraṇyena dilīparaghusūnubhiḥ ||
kārtavīryāniruddhābhyāṃ nahuṣeṇa yayātinā |
nṛpeṇa viṣvaksenena tathaiva śatabindunā ||
yuvanāśvena ca tathā śibinauśīnareṇa ca |
mucukundena māndhātrā hariścandreṇa vā vibho ||
satyaṃ vadata māsatyaṃ satyaṃ dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ |
hariścandraś carati vai divi satyena candravat ||
śyenacitreṇa rājanya somakena vṛkeṇa ca |
devatrā rantidevena vasunā sañjayena ca ||
duṣyantena karūṣeṇa rāmālarkanalais tathā |
virūpāśvena niminā janakena ca dhīmatā ||
nīlena pṛthunā caiva vīrasenena caiva hi |
ikṣvākuṇā śaṃbhunā ca śvetena sagareṇa ca ||
ajena dhundhunā caiva tathaiva ca subāhunā |
haryaśvena ca rājendra kṛpeṇa bharatena ca ||
etaiś cānyaiś ca rājendraiḥ purā māṃsaṃ na bhakṣitam |
śāradaṃ kaumudaṃ māṃsaṃ tatas te svargam āpnuvan ||
brahmaloke ca tiṣṭhanti jvalamānāḥ śriyā vṛtāḥ |
[326]        upāsyamānā gandharvaiḥ strīsahastrasamanvitaiḥ ||
tad evam uttamaṃ dharmam ahiṃsālakṣaṇaṃ śubham |
ye caranti mahātmāno nākapṛṣṭhe vasanti te ||
madhu māṃsaṃ ca te nityaṃ varjayantīha dhārmikāḥ |
janmaprabhṛti madyaṃ ca sarve te munayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācār. 85, 82] |
rogārto ‘bhyarcito vāpi yo māṃsaṃ nātty alolupaḥ |
phalaṃ prāpnoty ayatnena so ‘śvamedhaśatasya tu ||
madyaṃ māṃsaṃ maithunaṃ ca bhūtnāṃ lalanaṃ smṛtam |
tad eva vidhinā kurvan svargam āpnoti mānavaḥ ||
manuḥ [5.56] |
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā ||
“nivṛttiḥ” varjanasaṃkalpaḥ | “mahāphalā” svargādiphalā, bhakṣaṇajanyatṛptyapekṣayā svargāder mahāphalatvāt |
atha paśuhiṃsāvidhipratiṣedhau
tatra manuḥ [5.22–23] |
yajñārthaṃ brāhmaṇair vadhyāḥ praśastā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
[327]        bhṛtyānāṃ caiva vṛttyartham agastyo hy ācarat purā ||
babhūvur hi puroḍāśā bhakṣyāṇāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām |
purāṇeṣv api yajñeṣu brahmakṣātrasaveṣu ca ||
“yajñārtham” iti dṛṣṭāntartham | “praśastāḥ” bhakṣyatvena prasiddhāḥ | “purāṇāḥ” ṛṣiyajñāḥ ṣaḍviṃśadvatsarādayaḥ | tatra hi mṛgavadhaḥ śrūyate —
saṃsthite ‘hani gṛhapatir mṛgayāṃ yāti | sa tatra yān yān mṛgān hanti teṣāṃ tarasāḥ puroḍāśā bhavanti | iti |
“saṃsthite” samāpte | “tarasāḥ” māṃsamayāḥ |
manur viṣṇuś ca [5.39–42, 44] |
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā |
yajño ‘sya bhūtyai sarvasya tasmād yajñe vadho ‘vadhaḥ ||
oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣās tiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇas tathā |
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvanty ucchritīḥ parāḥ ||
madhuparke ca some ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi |
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatrety abravīn manuḥ ||
[328]        eṣv artheṣu paśūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthavid dvijaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ca paśūṃś caiva gamayaty uttamāṃ gatim ||
yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatāsmiṃś carācare |
ahiṃsām eva tāṃ vidyād vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [4.5, 8] |
pitṛdevatātithipūjāyām apy eva paśuṃ hiṃsyād iti mānavam | athāpi brāhmaṇāya rājanyāya cābhyāgatāya mahokṣaṃ mahājaṃ vā paced evam asmā ātithyaṃ kurvanti |
“pitṛdaivatam” aṣṭakākarma |
yama-paiṭhīnasī |
nātmārthaṃ pācayed annaṃ nātmārthaṃ ghātayet paśūn |
devārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā pacamāno na lipyate ||
manuḥ, viṣṇuś ca tṛtīye |
gṛhe gurāv araṇye vā nivasann ātmavān dvijaḥ |
nāvedavihitāṃ hiṃsām āpady api samācaret ||
[329]        kuryād ghṛtapaśuṃ saṅge kuryāt piṣṭapaśuṃ tathā |
na tv eva tu vṛthā hantuṃ paśum icchet kathaṃcana ||
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvatkṛtveha māraṇam |
vṛthā paśughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya janmani janmani ||
yo ‘hiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinasty ātmasukhecchayā |
sa jīvaṃś ca mṛtaś caiva na kvacit sukham aśnute ||
“gurau vasan” brahmacārī | “gṛhe vasan” gṛhasthaḥ | “araṇye vasan” vānaprasthaḥ | “avedavihitām” avihitām | “āpadi” kṣudhādikleśe | prāṇātyayasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ tu vihitatvāt kartavyaiva hiṃsā | “ghṛtapaśuṃ” paśukārye ghṛtam | “saṅge” lokācāraprāptasītāyajñādau paśuvadhasaṃprayoge, tatra ghṛtaṃ piṣṭaṃ vā paśuṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.180] |
vaset sa narake ghore dināni paśuromabhiḥ |
saṃmitāni durācāro yo hanty avidhinā paśūn ||
manuḥ [5.46–47] |
yo bandhanavadhakleśaṃ prāṇināṃ na cikīrṣati |
sa sarvasya hitaprepsuḥ sukham atyantam aśnute ||
[330]        yad dhyāyati yat kurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca |
tat tad āpnoty ayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃcana ||
hārītaḥ |
yathātmanas tathānyeṣāṃ yo vidvān svasti cecchati |
sa sarvalokapravare brahmaloke mahīyate ||
atha madyāni
tatra yamaḥ |
māṃsaṃ samalam annānāṃ māṃsānāṃ ca malaṃ surā |
malaṃ hi sarvapāpānāṃ surā dharmeṣu kathyate ||
tasmād dvijair na peyaiṣā neṣā grāhyā kathaṃcana |
“samalaṃ” malam | “dharmeṣu” dharmaśāstreṣu |
manuḥ, hārītaś ca caturthavarjam, yamaś ca prathamacaturthayoḥ |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet ||
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavam |
[331]        tad brāhmaṇena nāttavyaṃ devānām aśnatā haviḥ ||
amedhye vā paten matto vaidikaṃ vāpy udāharet |
akāryam anyat kuryād vā brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ ||
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt |
tasya vyapaiti brāhmaṇyaṃ śūdratvaṃ ca sa gacchati ||
“gauḍī” guḍakāryaṃ madyam | “paiṣṭī” piṣyakāryam | “mādhvī” madhukāryam | “surā” mukhyā paiṣṭī | “yathaivaikā” iti, yathā ekā paiṣṭī tribhir api dvijātibhir na pātavyā tathā “dvijottamaiḥ” brāhmaṇaiḥ “sarvā eva na pātavyā” | hārīte tu “dvijātibhir” iti pāṭhaḥ | atra ca dvijātiśabdo brāhmaṇaparaḥ, “tad brāhmaṇena nāttavyam” iti vākyaśeṣāt,
kāmād api hi rājanyo vaiśyo vā susamāhitaḥ |
pibetāṃ madhu tāvat tu na doṣaṃ kaṃcid ṛcchataḥ ||
iti yamavacanāt | “yakṣarakṣa” iti, yasmād yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānām etad annaṃ yan madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavaṃ tasmād brāhmaṇena na bhoktavyam |
yamaḥ |
madyaṃ pītvā tu yo vipro brahma vyāharate kvacit |
ghṛtāhutisahasreṇa na sa kiṃcid upāśnute ||
tathā |
brāhmaṇyam īhate yas tu yajamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
[332]        na tena madyaṃ pātavyaṃ dātavyaṃ na ca kasyacit ||
uśanā |
madyam apeyam adeyam anirgrāhyam |
“anirgrāhyam” asvīkāryam |
“varjayed” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [2.26] |
madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra. 75] |
sautrāmaṇyāṃ tathā madyaṃ śrutau bhakṣyam udāhṛtam |
viṣṇuḥ [22.82–84] |
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na spraṣṭavyā dvijātibhiḥ ||
mādhūkam aikṣavaṃ ṭāṅkaṃ kaulaṃ khārjūrapānase |
mṛdvīkārasamādhvīke maireyaṃ nālikerajam ||
amedhyāni daśaitāni madyāni brāhmaṇasya tu |
rājanyaś caiva vaiśyaś ca spṛṣṭvaitāni na duṣyataḥ ||
“dvijātibhiḥ” brāhmaṇaiḥ | “mādhūkaṃ” madhupuṣpabhavam | “aikṣavam” ikṣurasabhavam | “ṭāṅkaṃ” ṭaṅkaḥ kapitthaviśeṣaḥ, tadbhavam | “kaulam” koliphalajam | “khārjūram” kharjūraphalajam | [333] “pānasam” kaṇṭakiphalajam | “mṛdvīkārasam” drākṣādravajam | “maireyam” dhātupuṣpaṃ guḍadhānyāmbusahitam iti śabdārṇave vācaspatiḥ |
bhakṣyābhakṣyaprakaraṇe devalaḥ |
ayātayāmaṃ rasavat pratyagraṃ supariśrutam |
dravyaṃ dravyeṣu dharmajñair etan medhyatamaṃ smṛtam ||
surām ariṣṭaṃ maireyaṃ madhu mādhvīkam āsavam |
dhānyāmbu śuktaṃ varjaṃ tu na peyaṃ brāhmaṇair viduḥ ||
“ayātayāmam” aparibhuktam | “rasavat” agalitarasam | “pariśrutam” vastrapūtam | itthaṃbhūtam apariśrutam abhojyam | “ariṣṭam” madyākārādikadravyaṃ khādirādibhir vinirmitam | “āsavam” saṃdhānakam | “dhānyāmbu śuktam” dhānyāmbu śuktaṃ jātam |
atha bhojyottarakarma
tatra dakṣaḥ [2.68–69] |
bhuktvā tu sukham āsthāya tad annaṃ pariṇāmayet |
itihāsapurāṇādaiḥ ṣaṣṭhasaptamakau nayet ||
aṣṭame lokayātrā tu bahiḥsaṃdhyā tataḥ punaḥ ||
“sukham āsthāya” atra pariṇāmānukūlāṃ sukhāvasthitiṃ kuryāt |
[334] yājñavalkyaḥ [1.114] |
upāsya paścimāṃ saṃdhyāṃ hutvāgnīṃs tān upāsya ca |
bhṛtyaiḥ parivṛto bhuktvā nātitṛpto ‘tha saṃviśet ||
“tān” agnīn | “upāsya” ārādhya | “saṃviśet” śayīta |
dakṣaḥ [2.70–71, 74] |
homo bhojanakāsaś ca yac cānyad gṛhakṛtyakam |
kṛtvā caiva tataḥ paścāt svādhyāyaṃ kiṃcid ācaret ||
pradoṣapaścimau yāmau vedābhyāsaratir nayet |
yāmadvayaṃ śayānas tu brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||
asminn eva prayuñjāno hy asminn eva ca līyate |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kartavyaṃ sukham icchatā ||
śarvaryā madhyamau yāmau hutaśeṣaṃ haviś ca yat |
bhuñjānaś ca śayānaś ca brāhmaṇo nāvasīdati ||
“asmin” iti pradoṣacaturthayāmadvaye | “prayuñjāno” vedaṃ paṭhan | “asmin” brahmaṇi” “krtavyam” vedādhyayanam iti śeṣaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [2.12.29–34] |
śuśumāras tu yaḥ proktaḥ sa dhruvo yatra tiṣṭhati |
[335]        saṃniveśaṃ ca tasyāpi śṛṇuṣva munisattama ||
yad ahnā kurute pāpaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ niśi mucyate |
yāvatyaś ceha tārāś ca śuśumārāśritā divi ||
tāvanty eva ca varṣāṇi jīvanty abhyadhikāni tu |
uttānapādas tasyātha vijñeyo hy uttaro hanuḥ ||
yajño ‘dharaś ca vijñeyo dharmo mūrdhānam āśritaḥ |
hṛdi nārāyaṇaś cāste aśvinau pūrvapādayoḥ ||
varuṇaś cāryamā caiva paścime tasya sakthinī |
śiśnaḥ saṃvatsaras tasya mitro ‘pānaṃ samāśritaḥ ||
pucche ‘gniś ca mahendraś ca kaśyapo ‘the tato dhruvaḥ |
tārakāśuśumārasya nāstam eti catuṣṭayam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
trayāṇām api varṇānāṃ pūrvarātreṣu jāgaraḥ |
[336]
8
atha rātrikṛtyam
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.108–110] |
kṛtapādādiśaucas tu bhuktvā sāyaṃ tato gṛhī |
gacched asphuṭitāṃ śayyām api dārumayīṃ nṛpa ||
nāviśālāṃ na vā bhagnāṃ nāsamāṃ malināṃ na ca |
na ca jantumayīṃ śayyām adhitiṣṭhed anāstṛtām ||
prācyāṃ diśi śiraḥ śastaṃ yāmyāyām atha vā nṛpa |
sadaiva svapataḥ puṃso viparītaṃ tu rogadam ||
viṣṇuḥ [70.1–17] |
ārdrapādo na supyāt | nottarāparaśirāḥ | na nagnaḥ | nānuvaṃśam | nākāśe | na pālāśe śayane | na pañcadārukṛte | na gajabhagnakṛte | na vidyuddagdhakṛte | na bhinne | nāgipluṣṭe | na ghaṭasiktajale | na śmaśānaśūnyālayadevatālayeṣu | na capalamadhye | na nārīmadhye | na dhānyagoguruhutāśanasurāṇām upari | [337] nocchiṣṭaḥ | na divā svapet | na saṃdhyayoḥ | na bhasmani | deśe na cāśucau | na cārdre | na parvatamastake |
“aparaḥ” paścimaḥ | “anuvaṃśam” vaṃśadairghyānugataḥ | vaṃśaḥ gṛhamūrghany adīrghakāṣṭham | “ākāśe” anāvṛtadeśe | “pañcadārukṛte” pañcajātīyadārukṛte | “capalaḥ” atra vyasanī | “ārdre” deśa ity anuvṛttaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
na saṃdhivelāyāṃ śayīta | nānyapūrve | nānuvaṃśāstīrṇe | nāśmapīṭhopadhāne | na tiryagudakpratyakśirāḥ | na nagnaḥ | nāśuciḥ | nāsane | nocchiṣṭaḥ | no bhagne | na prage | noccair niśāyāṃ bhāṣeta |
“anyapūrve” pūrvopabhukte | “prage” prātaḥ |
“supyāt pratyakśirāḥ” ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
na viśīrṇakhaṭvāyām | nānyavarṇopasevitāyām anabhyukṣya | na bhūtayakṣagraahāyataneṣu | na śmaśānavṛkṣacchāyāsu | na parvaṇi rabhasotsave vā |
“parva” pratipatpañcadaśyoḥ saṃdhiḥ | “rabhasotsave” harṣotpādakaputrajanmādyutsave |
[338] manuḥ [4.57] |
naikaḥ supyāc chūnyagṛhe |
gautamaḥ [9.60] |
na kadācid rātrau nagnaḥ |
supyād ity śeṣa iti bhartṛyajñaḥ |
uśanā |
na tailenābhyakaśirāḥ svapet |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
na śīrṇāyāṃ khatvāyām upaviśet | nādvāreṇa praviśet | tathā nādīkṣitaḥ kṛṣṇacarmaṇi śayīta |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.4.24] |
na divāsvapnaśīlena na ca sarvānnabhojinā |
kāmaṃ śakyaṃ nabho gantum ārūḍhapatitena vā ||
āpastambaḥ [1.32.6] |
sadā niśāyāṃ dārān praty alaṃkurvīta |
[339] atha manuḥ [3.45–50] |
ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt svadāranirataḥ sadā |
parvavarjaṃ vrajed enāṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā ||
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir itaraiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ ||
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu ninditaikādaśī ca yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣāḥ syuḥ praśastāḥ daśa rātrayaḥ ||
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu rātriṣu |
tasmād yugmāsu putrārthī saṃviśed ārtave striyam ||
pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukre strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
same ‘pumān puṃstriyo vā kṣīṇe ‘lpe ca viparyayaḥ ||
nindyāsu cānyāsv aṣṭāsu striyo rātriṣu varjayet |
brahmacāry eva bhavati yatra tatrāśrame vasan ||
“ṛtuḥ” strīṇāṃ garbhagrahaṇayogyāvasthā, tadupalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ sa “ṛtukālaḥ” | tatrābhigamanam eva vrataṃ yasya sa “ṛtukālābhigāmī” | “vrate” iti ṇiniḥ | “parvavarjam” iti, ṛtāv anṛtau ca | “tadvrataḥ” bhāryāratikāmyayā gamanam eva vrataṃ yasya sa tathā | etac ca gamanaṃ sakāmāyā rakṣaṇārtham | svābhāvikagrahaṇād rogādito ‘nyathāpi bhavatīti sūcitam | ekādaśyāditrātrayo ‘tra ṛtūpakramāpekṣayā na tithyapekṣayā | “ayugmāsu” viṣamāsu pañcamyādiṣu rātriṣu | “saṃviśet” abhigacchet | “same” iti, same tulye śukre [340] strīpuṃsayoḥ | “apumān” napuṃsakaḥ | “yugapad yatra vartete vidyāt tatra napuṃsakam” iti devalavacanāt | “puṃstriyau vā” iti, dvidhābhūte tu sama eva strīpuruṣaśukre strīpuṃsayugalaṃ jāyate | “kṣīṇe” niḥsāre | “alpe” parimāṇataḥ |”"viparyayaḥ” atra garbhānutpattiḥ | “nindyāsu” ṣaṭsu, ādyāś catasra ekādaśī trayodaśī cety etāsu | “aṣṭāsu cānyāsu” anindyāsv api yāsu kāsucid rātriṣu striyo varjayet | rātridvitaya eva ca striyo ‘bhigacchan “brahmacāry eva bhavati” brahmacaryaphalaṃ prāpnotīty arthaḥ | “yatra tatrāśrame” iti, gṛhastho ‘pi san |
yamaḥ |
ṛtus tu ṣoḍaśāhāni rajanyo ‘ṣṭau vininditāḥ |
tāsām ādyās catasras tu saptamy ekādaśī ca yā ||
trayodaśī pañcadaśī navamī pañcamī tathā |
yugmāsu putrā jāyante hy ayugmāsu ca yoṣitaḥ ||
yugapad yatra vartete vidyāt tatra napuṃsakam |
puruṣo hīyate yatra yoṣit tatrābhijāyate ||
puruṣo hy adhiko yatra pumāṃs tatrābhijāyate |
yadi saṃyogakāle tu puruṣo rāgamohitaḥ ||
dvidhā samutsṛjec chukraṃ yamalaṃ tatra jāyate |
śukraśoṇitavaiṣamyāj jāyate kṛṣṇapiṅgalaḥ ||
evam utpāditāḥ putrā mriyante vāgatāyuṣaḥ |
“vartete” pravartete | etac cālpaśukratvopalakṣaṇam | “hīyate” śukrataḥ | “adhiko” api śukrata eva | “vaiṣamyaṃ” vaikṛtam | [341] “kṛṣṇapiṅgala” ity anena varṇopalakṣaṇam | “evam utpāditāḥ” aninditarātrijanitagarbhādhānajanitāḥ | “agatāyuṣaḥ” saṃpūrṇāyuṣaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.79–81] |
ṣoḍaśartur niśāḥ strīṇāṃ tasmin yugmāsu saṃviśet |
brahmacāry eva parvāṇy ādyāś catasras tu varjayet ||
evaṃ gacchan striyaṃ kṣāmāṃ maghāṃ mūlaṃ ca varjayet |
sustha indau sakṛt putraṃ lakṣaṇyaṃ janayet pumān ||
yathākāmī bhaved vāpi strīṇāṃ varam anusmaran |
svadāranirataś cāpi striyo rakṣyā yataḥ smṛtāḥ ||
“parvāṇi” paurṇamāsyāmāvasyāṣṭamīcaturdaśīsaṃkrāntayaḥ | “kṣāmā” alpabalā | kṣāmatā ca rajasvalāvratair yadi na bhavati tadāvṛtyā laghvāhārādinā kartavyā | “sustha indau” balavati candre | indugrahaṇaṃ śubhagrahāntaropalakṣaṇārtham | “sakṛt” ekasyāṃ rātrāv ekavāraṃ na dvir gacched ity arthaḥ | “lakṣaṇyam” śubhalakṣaṇayuktam |
hārītaḥ |
caturthe ‘hani snātāyāṃ yugmāsu garbhādhānādyupeto brahmagarbhaṃ saṃdadhāti |
[342] mahābhārate |
snātāṃ caturthe divase rātrau gacched vicakṣaṇaḥ |
etadvacanāc ca “tāsām ādyāś catasra” iti mānavīyo “na caturthe āhvayed” iti ca paiṭḥīnasīyo niṣedho ‘patyagatālpāyuṣṭvādidoṣārtha iti tātparyam | “caturthīprabhṛtyāṣoḍaśīm uttarottarāṃ prajāniḥśreyasārtham” ity āpastambagṛhyavacanāt |
devalaḥ |
brāhmaṇyā dvādaśāhaṃ syād vedoktam ṛtudhāraṇam |
daśāṣṭau ṣaṭ ca śeṣāṇāṃ vidhīyante ‘nupūrvaśaḥ ||
eteṣv api tryahaṃ pūrvaṃ varjayitvā rajasvalām |
śuddhāṃ snātāṃ tato bhāryāṃ sameyuḥ putrakāraṇāt ||
idaṃ tu brāhmaṇādīnāṃ dvādaśāhādiṛtudhāraṇavacanaṃ gamyatvābhiprāyaṃ na ṛtukālābhiprāyam,
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ smṛtaḥ |
iti manuvacanavirodhāt | “śuddhām” kālataḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃ. 30, 34–35, 33]
ṛtukālābhigamanaṃ kuryāt parvāṇi varjayet |
striyāḥ śukre ‘dhike strī syāt pumān puṃso ‘dhike bhavet ||
tasmāc chukravivṛddhyarthaṃ vṛṣyaṃ snigdhaṃ ca bhakṣayet |
laghvāhārāṃ striyaṃ kuryād evaṃ saṃjanayet sutam ||
[343]        ṛtukāle ‘bhigamanaṃ puṃsā kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ |
sadaiva vā parvavarjaṃ strīṇām abhimataṃ hi tat ||
gautamaḥ [5.1–2] |
ṛtāv upeyāt | sarvatra vā pratiṣiddhavarjam |
devalaḥ |
atīrthagamanāt puṃsas tīrthasaṃgūhanāt striyāḥ |
ubhayor dharmalopaḥ syād dveṣeṇa tu viśeṣataḥ ||
yaugapadye tu tīrthānāṃ vivāhakramaśo vrajet |
rakṣaṇārtham aputrāṃ vā grahaṇakramaśo ‘pi vā ||
“tīrtham” ṛtuḥ | “saṃgūhanam” pracchādanam | “aputrām” iti, itarāsu putravatīṣu madhye ṛtuyaugapadye 'pi tām eva prathamato gacchet | "grahaṇakramaśaḥ" ṛtugrahaṇakrameṇa |
āpastambaḥ [2.1.17–23; 2.2.1] |
ṛtau ca saṃnipāte dārāṇām anuvratam | antarāle ‘pi dāra eva | brāhmaṇavacanāc ca saṃveśanam | strīvāsasaiva [344] saṃnipātaḥ syāt | yāvat saṃnipātaṃ ca saha śayyā | tato nānā | udakopasparśanam | api vā lepān prakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānām |
“saṃnipātaḥ” saṃyogaḥ | “anuvratam” parvādivarjanarūpaṃ vratam anugamyety arthaḥ | “antarāle” anṛtau | “brāhmaṇam” atra vedabhāgaḥ, “abruvan varaṃ vṛṇāmaha” ityādi taittirīye | “nānā” pṛthak śayitārau syātām | “udakopasparśanam” snānam | etad ṛtau | anṛtau ca “prokṣaṇam” | yata āha vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
ṛtau tu garbhaśāṅkitvāt snānam eva vidhīyate |
anṛtau tu sadā kāryaṃ śaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat || iti |
āpastambaḥ [1.32.2] |
mithunībhūya ca na tayā saha sarvāṃ rātriṃ śayīta |
[345] vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
dvāv etāv aśucī syātāṃ dampatī śayanaṃ gatau |
śayanād utthitā nārī śuciḥ syād aśuciḥ pumān ||
“śucitvam” snānaprokṣaṇavyatirekeṇāpi |
gautamaḥ [9.26] |
na mithunībhūya śaucaṃ prati vilambet |
parāśaraḥ [4.15] |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ brahmaheti tathocyate ||
yamaḥ |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
ghorāyāṃ brahmahatyāyāṃ pacyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
bhāryām ṛtumukhe yas tu saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ tasmin retasi śerate ||
devalaḥ |
yaḥ svadārān ṛtusnātān susthaś cen nopagacchati |
[346]        bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoti garbhaṃ prāpya vināsya saḥ ||
“susthaḥ” rogādirahitaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [4.1.19, 21] |
trīṇi varṣāṇy ṛtumatīṃ yo bhāryāṃ nopagacchati |
sa tulyaṃ brahmahatyāyā doṣam ṛcchaty asaṃśayam ||
ṛtau nopaiti yo bhāryām anṛtau yaś ca gacchati |
tulyam āhus tayor doṣam ayonau yaś ca siñcati ||
atha manuḥ [4.40–42] |
nopagacchet pramatto ‘pi striyam ārtavadarśane |
samānaśayane caiva na śayīta tayā saha ||
rajasābhiplutāṃ nārīṃ narasya hy upagacchataḥ |
prajñā tejo balaṃ cakṣur āyuś caiva prahīyate ||
tāṃ visarjayatas tasya rajasā samabhiplutām |
prajñā lakṣmīr yaśaś cakṣur āyuś caiva pravardhate ||
[347] devalaḥ |
aśuddhadivaseṣv etāṃ maithunārthaṃ yadi vrajet |
āyuḥ prajñā ca dharmaś ca tasya puṃsaḥ prahīyate ||
tasmāt trirātraṃ cāṇḍālīṃ puṣipitāṃ parivarjayet |
avekṣeta ca tāṃ nityaṃ rathyābhasmaniṣevaṇaiḥ ||
“avekṣeta” rakṣeta | “rathyābhasmaniṣevaṇaiḥ” ity atrādhyāhṛtotsukaśabdayoge tṛtīyā | tena tatautsukyaṃ nivārayed ity arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.5–7] |
na malinavāsasā saha saṃviśet | na rajasvalayā | nāyogyayā |
“ayogyā” aprāptayauvanayā |
gautamaḥ [9.29–32] |
nākalyāṃ nārīm abhiramayet | na rajasvalām | na caināṃ śliśyet | na kanyām |
“akalyāṃ” rogiṇīm | “na kanyāṃ śliṣyet” na ramayed ity arthaḥ |
[348] śrāddhānantaraṃ śaṅkhalikhitau |
ṛtusnātāṃ tadahorātraṃ pariharet | nārtave | na divā maithunaṃ vrajet | klībā alpavīryāś ca divā prasūyante ‘lpāyuṣaś ca | tasmād etad varjayet prajākāmaḥ | pitṝṇāṃ neha vai tantuṃ vicchindyāt | prayatenācchedāya yenāpratiṣṭhaḥ patati | tasmāt pratiṣṭhākāmaḥ prajayā pratiṣṭhet |
“tantuṃ” saṃtānam | “acchedāya” avicchedāya saṃtānasya | “yena” yasmāt | nośabdena niṣedhe | “apratiṣṭhaḥ” prajānutpattyāprāptapratiṣṭhaḥ san “patati” | tasmād utpattyarthaṃ yatitavyam |
viṣṇuḥ [69.1–17] |
nāṣṭamīcaturdaśīṣu striyam upeyāt | na śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā | na datvā | nāmantritaḥ śrāddhe | na vratī | na dīkṣitaś ca | na devāyatane | na śmaśānaśūnyālayeṣu | na vṛkṣamūleṣu | na divā | na saṃdhyayoḥ | na malinām | nābhyaktām | nābhyaktaḥ | na rogārtām | na rogārtaḥ |
na hīnāṅgīṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ tathaiva ca vayodhikām |
[349]                nopeyād garbhiṇīṃ nārīṃ dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ ||
“dīkṣitaḥ” dīkṣākhyasaṃskāravān avabhṛteṣṭiṃ yāvat, tatra dīkṣonmocanāt | “rogārtāṃ” vyādhipīḍitām, striyaḥ puruṣasya vopagamanajanyo tatsaṃvardhanīyo vā rogo vivakṣitaḥ | “vayodhikā” atroparatarajaskā |
manuḥ [4.128] |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.21–24] |
ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt parvavarjaṃ svadāreṣu | nātīrtham upeyāt |
“tīrtham” atra yoniḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
yas tu pāṇigṛhītāyā āsye kurvīta maithunam |
bhavanti pitaras tasya tanmāsaṃ retaso bhujaḥ ||
yā syād anaticāreṇa ratiḥ sā dharmam āśritā |
“anaticāreṇa” yonyanatikrameṇa | [350]
api ca kāṭake pravacane vijñāyate | api naḥ śvo vijaniṣyamāṇāḥ patibhiḥ saha śarīrann iti strīṇām indradatto vara iti |
baudhāyanaḥ |
yas tu pāṇigṛhītāyā mukhe gacchati maithunam |
tasyeha niṣkṛtir nāstīty evam āha prajāpatiḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiaḥ |
ayugmenāvapet strī jāyate yugme pumān bhavati | saṃvṛte deśe maithunārtham āhvayet | na prathame | na dvitīye | na tṛtīye | na caturtha āhvayet |
atha rajasvalādhārmāḥ
tatra hārītaḥ |
rajaḥ prāptā ced adhaḥ śayīta | na devakāryaṃ kurvīta | bhūmau kārṣṇāyase pāṇyāṃ mṛnmaye vāśnīyāt |
“rajaḥ prāptā” rajaḥ prāptavatī | “adhaḥ” bhūmau | aspṛśyatvād eva tasyāḥ sākṣād devakāryakaraṇaniṣedhe siddhe “na devakāryaṃ kuryād” ity abhisaṃdhānaṃ tadannaprekṣaṇaṃ taddravyapuṣpanaivedyādyavekṣaṇam api na kuryād ity etadartham | “kārṣṇāyasam”
ayaḥpātram |
[351] vasiṣṭhaḥ [5.6–10] |
trirātraṃ rajasvalāśucir bhavati | sā nāñjyāt | nābhyañjyāt | nāpsu snāyāt | adhaḥ śayīta | na divā supyāt | nāgniṃ spṛśet | na rajjuṃ visṛjet | na dantān dhāvayet | na māṃsam aśnīyāt | na grahān nirīkṣeta | na haset | na kiṃcid ācaret | nāñjalinā pibet | na kharveṇa pibet | na lohitāyasena vā | vijñāyate hi | indras triśīrṣāṇaṃ tvāṣṭraṃ hatvā pāpmagṛhīto mahattamādharmasaṃbaddho ‘ham ity evam ātmānam amanyata | taṃ sarvāṇi bhūtāny abhyākrośān bhrūṇahann iti | sa striya upādhāvad asmai me bhrūṇahatyai tṛtīyaṃ pratigṛhṇāteti | tā abruvan kiṃ no bhūyuād iti | so ‘bravīd varaṃ vṛṇīṣvam iti | tā abruvan ṛtau prajāṃ vindāmaha iti kāmam ā vijanitoḥ saṃbhavāmeti | tatheti tāḥ pratijagṛhuḥ | saiṣā brahmahatyā māsi māsy āvir bhavati | tasmād rajasvalāyā annaṃ nśnīyāt | bhrūṇahatyāyā evaiṣā rūpaṃ pratimucyāste | tad āhur añjanābhyañjanam evāsyā na pratigrāhyaṃ tad dhi striyā annam iti | tasmāt tasyā annaṃ na bībhatsante maivaṃ medham upāgād iti |
udakyānvāsate yeṣāṃ ye ca kecid anagnayaḥ |
kulaṃ cāśrotriyaṃ yeṣāṃ sarve te śūdradharmiṇaḥ ||
“grahān” ādityādīn | “kiṃcit” gṛhalepanādy api | “kharvo” vāmahastaḥ | “lohitāyasena” tāmrapātreṇa | “pāpmagṛhītam amanyata” pāpagṛhīto ‘ham iti matiṃ kṛtavān | “ā vijanitoḥ [352] saṃbhavāma” iti, “ā vijananād” ā prasavāt “saṃbhavāma” mithunībhavāmety arthaḥ | “ā vijanitor” iti “bhāvalakṣaṇa” ityādinā tosunpratyayaḥ | “pratimucya” ābandhya | “añjanam abhyañjanaṃ ca” “añjanaṃ” maithunaṃ snehadravyalakṣaṇasāmyāt, “abhyañjanam” atrāliṅganam | añjanam abhi tasya vṛtteḥ | “na caināṃ śliśyed” iti gautamavacanānusārāt | “aśnīyād” upabhuñjīta | “bhrūṇahatyā” atra brahmahatyā | yatas tayāñjanābhyañjanam evānnam, tasmāt tasyā odanādirūpam annaṃ “na bībhatsante” na nindanti | yady añjanābhyañjanamātre sāśucis tadā yajñacaryāyāṃ sā śuciḥ syād ity āha “maivaṃ medham upāgād” iti | “medho” yajñaḥ | maivaṃvidhā sati yajñaṃ gacched ity arthaḥ | “anvāsate” upāsanāṃ kurute |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
atha rajasvalāṃ pariharet | tvāṣṭraṃ viśvarūpaṃ jaghāna vajreṇendraḥ | taṃ devā bhrūṇahann ity evam avadan | sa striya upādhāvad asyai me bhrūṇahatyāyai tṛtīyaṃ bhāgaṃ pratigṛhṇīta varaṃ dāsyāmīti | tato ‘syāṃśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ brahmahatyāyāḥ striyaḥ prapadyante | evaṃ bhūmir vanaspatayaś caikaikam | tāsāṃ varān tuṣṭaḥ prāyacchat | bhūmeḥ sthiratvaṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ chinnaprarohaṇaṃ strīṇāṃ sarvakāle saṃbhogaḥ | tasmād rajasvalāṃ nopāsīta | brahmahatyāprayuktāṃ tāṃ catūrātreṇa snātāṃ sarvakarmāṇi kārayet |
“sarvakarmāṇi” devapitṛvyatiriktāni |
[353] punaḥ paiṭhīnasiḥ |
trirātraṃ malavadvāso dhārayamāṇā na snāyāt | nābhyañjyāt | na nakhān nikṛntet | nāpamārjayet | na rajjuṃ spṛśet | na dantān dhāvayet | na srajaṃ dhārayet | na gandhān seveta | na kṛṇīyāt | na kharveṇa pibet | na parṇaiḥ | na prapāṇiḥ | na gopīthe | na gavām iva jalapānam | atraiva taittirīyaśrutiḥ — yāṃ malavadvāsasaṃ saṃbhavanti yas tato jāyate so ‘bhiśasto yām araṇye tasyai steno yāṃ parācīṃ tasyai hrītamukhyapragalbho yā snāti tasyā apsu māruko yābhyaṅkte tasyai duścarmā yā pralikhate tasyai khalatir apamārī yāṅkte tasyai kāṇo yā datto dhāvate tasyai śyāvadan yā nakhāni nikṛntate tasyai kunakhī yā kṛṇatti tasyai klībau yā rajjuṃ sṛjati tasyā udbandhuko yā parṇena pibati tasyā unmāduko yā kharveṇa pibati tasyai kharvas tisro rātrīr vrataṃ cared añjalinā vā pibed akharveṇa vā pātreṇa prajāyai gopīthāya | iti |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakāṇḍe ‘horātrakṛtyāni ||
[354]
9
atha parvakṛtyāni
tatra manuḥ [4.150] |
sāvitrān śāntihomāṃś ca kuryāt parvasu nityaśaḥ |
pitṝṃś caivāṣṭakāsv arcen nityam anvaṣṭakāsu ca ||
“sāvitrāḥ” savitṛdevatākāḥ | te ca gāyatryākṣatadravyeṇa kāryāḥ,
śāntikāmas tu juhuyād gāyatryā cākṣataiḥ śuciḥ |
iti śaṅkhavacanāt | dharmadvārā aihikāniṣṭanivṛttiḥ “śāntiḥ” | “parvasu”  caturdaśyādiṣu | nityagrahaṇāt parvasv avaśyakartavyatā pratīyate |
viṣṇuḥ [71.86–89] |
parvasu ca śāntihomān kuryāt | na tṛṇam api chindyāt | alaṃkṛtas tiṣṭhet | evam ācārasevī syāt |
“chindyāt” hiṃsyāt |
[355] āpastambaḥ [1.31.21–22] |
divādityaḥ sattvāni gopāyati naktaṃ candramāḥ | tasmād amāvāsyāyāṃ niśāyāṃ svādhīya ātmano guptim icchet prāyatyabrahmacaryakālecaryayā ca | saha hy etāṃ rātriṃ sūryācandramasau vasataḥ |
“svādhīyaḥ” bhṛśataram | “guptiḥ” rakṣā | “prāyatyena” aprāyatyanimittadṛṣṭārtha-rathyopasarpaṇavarjanādinā samyak śaucācamananiṣpādanena | evaṃ “brahmacaryeṇa” | “kālecaryayā” mukhyakāla eva prātarādau tatkarmakaraṇena |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
paurṇamāsīm amāvāsyām adhaḥsvāpo vidhīyate |
anāhitāgner apy eṣa paścād agner vidhīyate ||
“anāhitāgniḥ” atra smārtāgnimān |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.21.19–21] |
parvasu ca nādhīyīta | na māṃsam aśnīyāt | na striyam upeyāt | parvasu hi rakṣaḥpiśācā vyabhicāravanto bhavanti |
[356] paiṭhīnasiḥ |
na parvasu tailaṃ kṣuraṃ maithunaṃ māṃsam ity upeyāt | nāmāvāsyāyāṃ haritāni chindyāt |
“upeyāt” upabhuñjīta |
viṣṇupurāṇe [2.12.10] |
chinatti vīrudho yas tu vīrutsaṃsthe divākare |
patraṃ vā pātayaty ekaṃ brahmahatyāṃ sa vindati ||
“vīrutsaṃsthe” vanaspatisthite |
yamaḥ |
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ hanti śiro ‘bhyaṅge caturdaśī |
māṃsāśane pañcadaśī kāmadharme athāṣṭamī ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
dantakāṣṭham amāvāsyā maithunaṃ ca caturdaśī |
hanti saptakulaṃ tasya tailagrahaṇam aṣṭamī ||
ṣaṣṭhy aṣṭamī pañcadaśī ubhau pakṣau caturdaśī |
[357]        atra saṃnihitaṃ pāpaṃ taile māṃse kṣure bhage ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tailam anāyuṣyam aṣṭamyāṃ piśitaṃ tathā |
kṣurakarma caturdaśyāṃ tathā parvaṇi maithunam ||
sarvaparvasu ṣaṣṭhyāṃ ca sāmānyenaiva sarvatailādiniṣedhe siddhe tithiviśeṣasaṃbandhena tailādiviśeṣaniṣedho doṣaviśeṣārthaḥ |
devalaḥ |
pañcadaśyāṃ caturdaśyām aṣṭamyāṃ ca viśāradaḥ |
tailaṃ māṃsaṃ vyavāyaṃ ca kṣuraṃ ca parivarjayet ||
atra ṣaṣṭhyādiśabdena tithivācinā candragatyavacchinnakālaviśeṣapratipādanād yāvat ṣaṣṭhyāditithikālam eva māṃsādiniṣedhaḥ, na tu tadupalakṣaṇato ‘horātre | upalakṣaṇaparatve pramāṇābhāvāt | ata eva devapitṛkṛtyayoḥ pūrvāhṇāparāhṇayor vihitatvāt
tatkālavyāpitithyādaraḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.118–120] |
caturdaśy aṣṭamī caiva amāvāsyā ca pūrṇimā |
parvāṇy etāni rājendra ravisaṃkrāntir eva ca ||
tailastrīmāṃsasaṃbhogī parvasv eteṣu vai pumān |
viṇmūtrabhojanaṃ nāma prayāti narakaṃ nṛpa ||
[358]        aśeṣaparvasv eteṣu tasmāt saṃyamibhir naraiḥ |
bhāvyaṃ sacchāstradevejyādhyānajapyaparaiḥ sadā ||
varāhapurāṇe |
cāṇḍālaśapatheṣu —
ṣaṣṭhy aṣṭamy amāvāsyā ubhau pakṣau caturdaśī |
asnātānāṃ gatiṃ yāsye yady ahaṃ nāgame punaḥ ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
saptamyāṃ spṛśatas tailam iṣṭā bhāryā vinaśyati |
vāmanapurāṇe |
nandāsu nābhyaṅgam upācareta
kṣauraṃ ca riktāsu jayāsu māṃsam |
pūrṇāsu yoṣit parivarjanīyā
bhadrāsu sarvāṇi samācaret ||
nābhyaṅgam arke na ca bhūmiputre
kṣauraṃ ca śukre ‘tha kuje ca māṃsam |
budhe tu yoṣāṃ na samācareta
śeṣeṣu sarvāṇi sadaiva kuryāt ||
citrāsu haste śravaṇe ca tailaṃ
kṣauraṃ viśākhāpratipatsu varjam |
mūle mṛge bhādrapadāsu māṃsaṃ
yoṣin maghākṛttikasottarāsu ||
[359] varāhapurāṇe |
vaiṣṇavaṃ prakṛtya varāha uvāca —
ṣaṣṭhy aṣṭamī cāmāvāsyā ubhe pakṣe caturdaśī |
maithunaṃ naiva seveta dvādaśīṃ ca mama priyām ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ ṣaṣthyāṃ ca dvādaśīṃ tathā |
amāvāsyāṃ caturthyāṃ ca maithunaṃ yo ‘dhigacchati ||
tiryagyoniṃ sa gacchec ca mama lokaṃ na gacchati |
manuḥ [4.128] |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ ||
nandipurāṇe |
yadi vā na samarthaḥ syāt sadā māṃsavivarjane |
varjayed ayane mukhye kṛtasvargamatir naraḥ ||
caturthī cāṣṭamī caiva dvādaśī ca caturdaśī |
tathā pañcadaśī varjyā ṣaḍaśītimukhāni ca ||
[360]        saṃkramaṃ vāpi sūryasya viṣuvau cāpi vārṣikau |
māṃsāt tu virato nityaṃ svargaṃ yāti dinatrayam ||
atha vāśvayujaṃ māsaṃ varjayen māṃsabhakṣaṇam |
māsaṣaṭkaṃ kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ labhetāśvayujān naraḥ ||
māṃsabhojanasaṃtyāgāt puruṣaḥ śuddhamānasaḥ |
yo naraḥ kārtikaṃ māsaṃ māṃsaṃ tu parivarjayet |
saṃvatsarasya labhate puṇyaaṃ māṃsavivarjanāt ||
atha saṃkrāntinirṇayaḥ
tatra devīpurāṇe |
dvādaśaiva samākhyātāḥ samāḥ saṃkrāntikalpanāḥ |
saptadhā sāpi boddhavyā ekaikaṃ ca yathā śṛṇu ||
mandā mandākinī dhvāṅkṣī ghorā caiva mahodarī |
rākṣasī miśritā proktā saṃkrāntiḥ saptadhā nṛpa ||
mandā dhruveṣu vijñeyā mṛdau mandākinī tathā |
kṣiprair dhvāṅkṣīṃ vijānīyād ugrair ghorā prakīrtitā ||
carair mahodarī jñeyā krūrair ṛkṣais tu rākṣasī |
miśritā caiva nirdiṣṭā miśritarkṣais tu saṃkrame ||
tricatuḥpañcasaptāṣtanavadvādaśa eva ca |
krameṇa ghaṭikā hy etās tatpuṇyaṃ pāramārthikam ||
atītānāgato bhogo nāḍyaḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ |
sāṃnidhyaṃ tu bhavet tatra grahāṇāṃ saṃkrame raveḥ ||
[361]        vyavahāro bhavel loke candrasūryopalakṣitaḥ |
kāle vikalate sarvaṃ brahmādyaṃ sacarācaram ||
puṇyapāpavibhāgena phalaṃ devī prayacchati |
ekadhāpi kṛtaṃ yasmin koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ bhavet ||
dharmād vivardhate hy āyū rājyaputrasukhādayaḥ |
adharmād vyādhiśokādi viṣuvāyanasaṃnidhau ||
viṣuveṣu ca yaj japtaṃ dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam |
evaṃ viṣṇupade caiva ṣaḍaśītimukheṣu ca ||
“kalpanāḥ” bhedāḥ | “samāḥ” tulyāḥ | saṃkrāntimātrarūpatayā ceṣṭāniṣṭaphalajanakatāmātra-sāmyena vakṣyamāṇās tu viśeṣā ayanādiviśeṣarūpatayā | yady api kadācin malamāsena saha trayodaśa māsā bhavanti tathāpi saṃkrāntayo dvādaśa, na trayodaśety evakāreṇa sūcitam | “dhruvāṇi” sthirāṇi trīṇy uttarāṇi rohiṇī ca | “mṛdūni” anurādhācitrārevatīmṛgaśīrṣāṇi | “kṣiprāḥ” laghavaḥ hastāśvinīpuṣyāḥ | “ugrāḥ” pūrvātrayaṃ bharaṇī maghā ca | “carāḥ” punarvasu-śravaṇadhaniṣṭhā  svātīśatabhiṣāḥ | “krūrāḥ” tīkṣṇāḥ, te ca mūlājyeṣṭhāśleṣārdrāḥ | “miśrā” kṛttikā viśākhā ca | “krameṇa” yathāsaṃkhyena | “pāramārthikaṃ” mukhyaṃ phalam | sūkṣmasaṃkrāntikāle vasor dhārādiprayogānuṣṭhānajanyaṃ yat puṇyaṃ tad etāsv api nāḍikāsv ity arthaḥ | “tāḥ” ghaṭikāḥ | puṇyahetutvāt “puṇyam” | “bhogo” vyāptiḥ | sūkṣmasaṃkramaṇakālasaṃnidhāne ca puṇyatamatvam iti yāvat | “sāṃnidhyam” ityādikais tasyaiva kālasya stutiḥ |
śātātapaḥ |
arvāk ṣoḍaśa vijñeyā nāḍyaḥ paścāt tu ṣoḍaśa |
[362]        kālaḥ puṇyo ‘rkasaṃkrāntau vidvadbhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
ity ubhayataḥ ṣoḍaśanāḍīnāṃ puṇyatām āha |
tathā jābālir api |
saṃkrānteḥ puṇyakālas tu ṣoḍaśobhayataḥ kalāḥ | iti |
etadvākyadarśanāc ca pañcadaśanāḍībhogo ‘py ubhayata eva boddhavyaḥ | “vikalate” svabhāvāc cyavate | “devī” durgā, saṃkrāntyupakrame devīpurāṇa eva |
samāyanaṛtumāsapakṣāhādikrameṇa tu |
sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgena devī sarvagatā vibho ||
ity abhidhānāt | “saṃnidhau” bhogakāle | viṣṇupadīṣaḍaśītimukhasvarūpam uktaṃ jyotiḥśāstre yathā |
dhanur mithunakanyāsu mīne ca ṣaḍaśītayaḥ |
vṛṣavṛścikakumbheṣu siṃhe viṣṇupadī smṛtaḥ || iti |
[punar devīpurāṇe] |
ayaneṣu vikalpo yas tan me nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
yāvad viṃśatkalā bhuktā tatpuṇyaṃ cottarāyaṇe ||
niraṃśe bhāskare dṛṣṭe dinānte dakṣiṇāyane |
ardharātre tv asaṃpūrṇe divāpuṇyam anāgatam ||
saṃpūrṇe cārdharātre ca udayāstamaye ‘pi vā |
mānārdhaṃ bhāskare puṇyam apūrṇe śarvarīdale ||
[363]        saṃpūrṇe tūbhayor deyam atireke pare ‘hani |
ṣaḍaśītimukhe ‘tīte vṛtte vā viṣuvadvaye ||
bhaviṣyaty ayane puṇyam atīte cottarāyaṇe |
ādau puṇyaṃ vijānīyād yady abhinnā tithir bhavet ||
ardharātre vyatīte tu vijñeyaṃ cāpare ‘hani |
“viṃśatkalā” viṃśatitamī kalā saṃkrāntikṣaṇād ūrdhvaṃ yāvad bhavati tāvat puṇyam | “niraṃśe” aṃśakaśūnye, saṃkrāntikāle hi bhāskaro ‘ṃśakarahito bhavati | “dinaṃ” ca ravisaṃskārasaṃskṛtās triṃśannāḍikā iti jyotirvidaḥ, “tadante” puṇyam iti | “divā puṇyam anāgataṃ” saṃkramapuṇyaṃ yad divā tena saṃkrāntikālād arvāg eva divābhāge saṃkrāntikṛtyaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | “saṃpūrṇe ardharātre” udayopakramam astamayāntam | “mānārdhaṃ puṇyaṃ” “mānaṃ” dinamānam, “tadardhaṃ” bhāskaropalakṣite kāle | “dalam” ardham | atrāpi mānārdhm ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “saṃpūrṇe ardharātre” saṃkrāntau satyām “ubhayoḥ” pūrvāparadinayoḥ “mānārdhaṃ” “deyaṃ” yojyam ity arthaḥ | “atireke pare ‘hani” atrāpi mānārdhaṃ deyam ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “vṛtte” atīte | evaṃ ca rātrau dvādaśasv api ravisaṃkrāntiṣv ardharātrād arvāk saṃkramaṇe pūrvadinottarārdhaṃ puṇyam | saṃpūrṇe tv ardharātre pūrvadinasyottarārdham uttaradinasya pūrvam ardhaṃ puṇyam | ardharātrād anantaraṃ tūttaradinasya pūrvam ardhaṃ puṇyam | “ṣaḍaśītimukhe ‘tīte” ityādi tu pāriśeṣyād dinasaṃkrāntiviṣayam | ubhayataḥ pañcadaśaṣoḍaśanāḍīnāṃ puṇyatvam api dinasaṃbandhi-viṣṇupadīmātraviṣayaṃ pāriśeṣyāt | “ādau” [364] ityādi, “ādau” pūrvadina eva | “abhinnā” pūrvadine saṃkrāntikāle caikaiva | etenārdharātrisaṃkramaṇe yad ubhayataḥ puṇyakathanaṃ tad bhinnatithiviṣayam | “ardharātre vyatīte tu” pūrveṇāhṇā tithyabhede ‘pi parasyeva pūrvam ardhaṃ puṇyam iti tātparyārthaḥ |
tathā |
mandā viprajane śastā mandākiny api rājani |
dhvāṅkṣī vaiśyeṣu vijñeyā ghorā śūdre śubhapradā ||
mahodarī tu corāṇāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ jayāvahā |
cāṇḍālapulkasānāṃ tu ye cānye krūrakarmiṇaḥ ||
sarveṣāṃ kārukāṇāṃ tu miśritā bhūtivardhinī |
nṛpāḥ pīḍyante pūrvāhṇe madhyāhne tu dvijottamāḥ ||
aparāhṇe tathā vaiśyāḥ śūdrās cāstamaye ravau |
piśācās tu pradoṣe tu ardharātre tu rākṣasāḥ ||
ardharātre vyatīte tu pīḍyante naṭanartakāḥ |
uṣaḥkāle tu saṃkrāntau hanti gosvāmino janān ||
hanti pravrajitān sarvān saṃdhyākāle tu saṃkramaḥ |
eṣa sthūlavibhāgas tu bhuktikāmasya kīrtitaḥ ||
parmārthena yā saṃkhyā kathayāmi nṛpottama |
svasthe nare sukhāsīne yāvat spandati locanam ||
[365]        tasya triṃśattamo bhāgas tatparaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
tatparāc chatmo bhāgas truṭir ity abhidhīyate ||
truṭeḥ sahasrabhāgo yaḥ sa kālo ravisaṃkrame |
tatkāle tu dravībhūtaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ||
vyatīpāte tu caivaṃ syād bhavet puṇyaṃ tato ‘dhikam |
atra brahmāpi saṃdigdham uvāca surasattama ||
dānādhyayanajapyādi viśiṣṭaṃ vahnihomataḥ |
vasor dhārāsu labhate tv anyathā na kathaṃcana ||
śātātapaḥ |
ayanādau sadā deyaṃ dravyam iṣṭaṃ gṛhe tu yat |
ṣaḍaśītimukhe caiva vimokṣe candrasūryayoḥ ||
arvāk ṣoḍaśa vijñeyā nāḍyaḥ paścāc ca ṣoḍaśa |
kālaḥ puṇyo ‘rkasaṃkrānter vidvadbhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
saṃkrāntau yāni deyāni havyakavyāni kartṛbhiḥ |
tāni nityaṃ dadāty arkaḥ punar janmani janmani ||
brahmapurāṇe |
tulāṃ pratyāgate sūrye viṣuvad divase sati |
[366]        brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradeyāni sasyāny abhimatāni ca ||
meṣāditrayasaṃkrāntau caṇḍāṃśus tūttarāyaṇe |
brahmalokaṃ tu gacchanti devāḥ sarve savāsavāḥ ||
tasmān māsatrayaṃ teṣāṃ pratimāḥ śailasaṃbhavāḥ |
ācchādya ghṛtavastrābhyāṃ tathā rakṣyāś ca nityaśaḥ ||
puṣpair vastraiḥ sakāmais tu gṛhītavyās tu sarvadā |
pūjyāḥ pratidinaṃ tāś ca tathā viprās tu bhojanaiḥ ||
tarpaṇīyās tathā gāvas tṛṇena lavaṇāmbubhiḥ |
vahnir nityaṃ ca prajvālyo nṛṇāṃ kāyāgnivardhanaḥ ||
“meṣo” atra makaraḥ | “śailasaṃbhavāḥ” pāṣāṇanirmitāḥ |
tathā |
meṣaṃ jigamiṣau sūrye śaiśiraṃ ghṛtakambalam |
apāsya dehād devebhyaḥ pūjā kāryā prayatnataḥ ||
kālikāpurāṇe |
kanakaṃ kuliśaṃ nīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca mauktikam |
etāni pañcaratnāni vinyasel liṅgamūrdhani ||
ratnānāṃ cāpy abhāve tu karṣaṃ karṣārdham eva vā |
suvarṇaṃ yojayitvā ca tasminn evottarāyaṇe ||
vidhivac ca tathābyarcya gavyenājyena bhūriṇā |
[367]        prakṣālya mardayitvāpi dadyāc ca ghṛtakambalam ||
datvā copaskaraṃ bhūyo brāhmaṇān yatibhiḥ saha |
saṃbhojya dakṣayitvā tu kalpayed anivāritam ||
upoṣya sarvam etad vai kuryād bhaktipuraḥsaram |
pañcagavyaṃ tilair yuktaṃ pītvā vai pārayet svayam ||
tilaiḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta tair evodvartanaṃ budhaḥ |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca ubhābhyāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā ||
homaṃ taiś ca prakurvīta sarvadā cottarāyaṇe |
tān vai devāya viprebhyo hāṭakena samaṃ dadet ||
sakṛd evaṃ karoty evaṃ cittaṃ śambhau niveśya yaḥ |
uttarāyaṇam āsādya naraḥ kasmāt sa śocate ||
“kuliśaṃ” hīrakam | “dakṣayitvā” dakṣiṇayā saṃpūjya | “kalpayet” bhojanam iti śeṣaḥ | “pārayet” pāraṇaṃ kuryāt | tilasnānapāraṇayor nātra kramo vivakṣitaḥ | “ubhābhyām” iti tādarthye caturthī |
brahmapurāṇe |
śuklapakṣe tu saptamyāṃ yadā saṃkramate raviḥ |
mahājayāā tadā syād vai saptamī bhāskarapriyā ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ pitṛdevābhipūjanam |
[368]        sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ proktaṃ tapanena mahaujasā ||
yas tv asyāṃ mānavo bhaktyā ghṛtena snāpayed ravim |
so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya tataḥ sūryapuraṃ vrajet ||
payasā snāpayed yas tu bhāskaraṃ bhaktimān naraḥ |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo yāti sūryasalokatām ||
karbureṇa vimānena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā |
tejasā ravisaṃkāśaḥ kāntyātreyasamo bhavet ||
sthitvā tatra ciraṃ kālaṃ rājā bhavati kiṃjayaḥ |
mahājayaiṣā kathitā saptamī tripurāntaka ||
yām upoṣya naro bhaktyā hy acalām abjajāṃ labhet |
tato yāti paraṃ brahma yatra gatvā na śocati ||
atha grhaṇanirṇayaḥ
tatra jābāliḥ |
saṃkrānteḥ puṇyakālas tu ṣoḍaśobhayataḥ kalāāḥ |
candrasūryoparāge ca yāvad darśanagocaraḥ ||
candrasūryoparāgasya nimittatvapratipādanāj jñātasyaiva ca nimittatvāj jñānamātre prāpte “yāvad darśanagocaraḥ” iti [369] “rāhudarśana” iti vacanāc ca cākṣuṣajñānaviṣayasyaiva nimitttatā | cākṣuṣajñāne darśanapadasya mukhyatvāt | tena na meghādicchanne snānādikaṃ kartavyam | evaṃ grastāstamite pare ‘hani yan muktadarśanaṃ tac cākṣuṣam eva |
viṣṇuḥ [68.1, 3] |
candrārkoparāge nāśnīyāt | amuktayor astaṃgatayor dṛṣṭvā snātvā pare ‘hani |
śātātapaḥ |
ahorātraṃ tu nāśnīyāc candrasūryagraho yadā |
muktiṃ dṛṣṭvā tu bhuñjīta snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhanataḥ ||
sūryācandramasor lokān akṣayān yāti mānavaḥ |
dhautapāpmā viśuddhātmā modate tatra devavat ||
ayane viṣuve caiva candrasūryagrahe tathā |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātaḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
“ahorātroṣitaḥ” pūrvasmin dine kṛtopavāsaḥ | ahorātrabhojanaṃ tu phalartham iti
bhāullasmṛtiḥ |
tathā |
nādyāt sūryagrahāt pūrvam ahni sāyaṃ śaśigrahāt |
[370]        grahakāle ca nāśnīyāt snātvāśnīyāc ca muktayoḥ ||
mukte śaśini bhuñjīta yadi na syān mahāniśā |
snātvā dṛṣṭvāpare ‘hny adyād grastāstamitayos tayoḥ ||
“muktayoḥ” sūryacandrayoḥ | “mahāniśā” rātreḥ prathamārdhapraharād ūrdhvaṃ muhūrtacatuṣṭayam |
brahmapurāṇe |
nāśnīyād atha tatkāle grastayoś candrasūryayoḥ |
muktayoś ca kṛtasnānaḥ paścāt kuryāt svaveśmani ||
“kuryād” iti, aśanam iti vipariṇatam anuṣajyate |
tathā |
nityaṃ dvayor ayanayos tathā viṣuvator dvayoḥ |
candrārkayor grahaṇayor vyatīpāteṣu parvasu ||
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātaḥ śrāddhaṃ dānaṃ tathā japam |
yaḥ karoti prasannātmā tasya syād akṣayaṃ ca tat ||
devīpurāṇe |
kārtike grahaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
mārge tu grahaṇaṃ proktaṃ devikāyāṃ mahāmune ||
[371]        pauṣe tu narmadā puṇyā māghe saṃnihitā śubhā |
phālgune varuṇā puṇyā caitre puṇyā sarasvatī ||
vaiśākhe tu mahāpuṇyā candrabhāgā sarid varā |
jyeṣṭhe tu kauśikī puṇyā āṣāḍhe tāpikā nadī ||
śrāvaṇe sindhunāmā tu tathā bhādre ca gaṇḍakī |
āśvine sarayūḥ śreṣṭhā bhūyaḥ puṇyā tu narmadā ||
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā candre rāhusamanvite |
sūrye ca rāhuṇā graste tamobhūte mahāmune ||
narmadātoyasaṃsparśāt kṛtakṛtyā bhavanti te |
ye sūrye saiṃhikeyena graste revājalaṃ narāḥ ||
spṛśanti cavagāhanti na te prakṛtimānuṣāḥ |
smṛtvā śatakratuphalaṃ dṛṣṭvā godānajaṃ phalam ||
spṛṣṭvā gomedhatulyaṃ tu pītvā sautrāmaṇiṃ labhet |
snāptvā vājimakhaṃ puṇyaṃ prāpnuyād avicārataḥ ||
ravicandroparāge tu ayane cottare tathā |
evaṃ gaṅgāpi draṣṭavyā tadvad eva sarasvatī ||
śivādityaphalaṃ yac ca maṇḍale samudāhṛtam |
sagrahe maṇḍale yoge tad api prāpnuyān naraḥ ||
ūṣarāraṇyakṣetreṣu puṇyaṃ yat samudāhṛtam |
tad atra kālam āhātmyād uparāge ‘dhikaṃ bhavet ||
[372]
[yugādayo yugāntāś ca]
brahmapurāṇe |
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyāyāṃ kṛtaṃ yugam |
kārtike śuklapakṣe tu tretā tu navame ‘hani ||
atha bhādrapade kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ tu dvāparam |
māghe ca paurṇamāsyāṃ tu ghoraṃ kaliguyaṃ tathā ||
yugārambhās tu tithayo yugādyās tena viśrutāḥ |
sūryasya siṃhasaṃkrāntyām antaḥ kṛtayugasya tu ||
atha vṛścikasaṃkrāntyām antas tretāyugasya tu |
jñeyaś ca vṛṣasaṃkrāntyāṃ dvāparāntaś ca saṃjñayā ||
tathā ca kumbhasaṃkrāntyām antaḥ kaliyugasya ca |
yugādyeṣu yugānteṣu śrāddham akṣayam ucyate ||
[mahākārtikyādi]
tathā |
puṇyā mahākārtikī syāj jīvendvoḥ kṛttikāsthayoḥ |
maghāsthayoś ca jīvendvor mahāmāghīti kathyate ||
tathā |
tiladvādaśisāmjñā ca māghe kṛṣṇacaturdaśī |
tathā |
āgneyaṃ tu yadā ṛkṣaṃ kārtikyāṃ bhavati kvacit |
[373]        mahatī sā tithir jñeyā snānadāneṣu cottamā ||
yadā yāmyaṃ ca bhavati ṛkṣaṃ tasyāṃ tithau kvacit |
tithiḥ sāpi mahāpuṇyā munibhiḥ parikīrtitā ||
prājāpatyaṃ tathā ṛkṣaṃ tithau tasyāṃ narādhipa |
sā mahākārtikī proktā devānām api durlabhā ||
“āgneyaṃ” kṛttikā | “yāmyaṃ” bharaṇī | “prājāpatyaṃ” rohiṇī | etannakṣatrayogenāpi mahatvapratipādanāt kṛttikānte bṛhaspaticandrayoge yan mahatvābhidhānaṃ tan mahatvātiśayārtham |
mande cārke gurau vāpi vāreṣv eteṣu caitrikī |
atrāśvamedhikaṃ puṇyaṃ snānataś ca labhen naraḥ ||
dānam akṣatāṃ yāti pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā |
"mandaḥ" śanaiśvaraḥ |
viśābhāsu yadā bhānuḥ kṛttikāsu ca candramāḥ |
sa yogaḥ padmako nāma puṣkare hy atidurlabhaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [50.9–10] |
dṛśyete sahitau yasyāṃ divi candrabṛhaspatī |
paurṇamāsī tu mahatī proktā saṃvatsare tu sā ||
tasyāṃ dānopavāsādyam akṣayaṃ parikīrtitam |
tathaiva dvādaśī śuklā yānyā śravaṇasaṃyutā ||
[374] “yasyāṃ” paurṇamāsyāṃ māsasaṃjñāpravṛttinimitte nakṣatre candrabṛhaspatī bhavataḥ, “sā” sarvā “mahatī” mahāphalety arthaḥ | mahākārtikīmahāmāghyor māsasaṃjñaikanakṣatra-sthatvena mahatvasyokter anyatrāpi māsasaṃjñaikanakṣatrasthatvena mahatvam |
[manvatarādayaḥ]
matsyapurane [17.6] |
āśvayukśuklanavamī dvādaśī kārtikī tathā |
tṛtīyā caitramāsasya tathā bhādrapadasya ca ||
phālgunasya tv amāvāsyā puṣyasyaikādaśī tathā |
āṣāḍhasyāpi daśamī māghamāsasya saptamī ||
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathā ṣaṣṭhī ca pūrṇimā |
kārtikī phālgunī caitrī jyeṣṭhī pañcadaśī tathā ||
manvantarādayaś caitā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ |
amāvāsyāśrāvaṇāṣṭamīvyatiriktāḥ sarvās tithayaḥ śuklāḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
mahādeva uvāca |
puṣye māsi yadā devi śuklāṣṭamyāṃ budho bhavet |
tadā tu sā mahāpuṇyā mahābhadreti kīrtitā ||
tasyāṃ snānaṃ japo homas tarpaṇaṃ viprabhojanam |
matprītaye kṛtaṃ devi śatasāhasrikaṃ bhavet ||
[375]        tasmāt tasyāṃ mahādevi pūjyo ‘haṃ vidhivad budhaiḥ |
gandhapuṣpopahāraiś ca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇaiḥ ||
rohiṇī ca yadā kṛṣṇe pakṣe ‘ṣṭamyāṃ dvijottama |
jayantī nāma sā proktā sarvapāpaharā tithiḥ ||
yad bālye yac ca kaumāre yaivane vārdhike ca yat |
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu ||
tat kṣālayati bhūteśaṃ tasyām abhyarcya bhaktitaḥ |
homajapyādidānānāṃ phalaṃ ca śatasaṃmitam ||
sa prāpnoti na saṃdeho yac cānyan manasepsitam |
upavāsas tu tatrokto mahāpāpakanāśanaḥ ||
pauṣe māse yadā devi aṣṭamyāṃ nagaje śubhe |
nakṣatraṃ jāyate puṇyaṃ yal loke raudram ucyate ||
“raudram” ārdram |
tadā tu sā mahāpuṇyā jayantī cāṣṭamī śubhā |
tasyāṃ snānaṃ tathā dānaṃ japo homaś ca tarpaṇam ||
tat sarvaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ hutaṃ bhavati kṛtsnaśaḥ |
tasmān matpūjanaṃ devi kartavyaṃ vidhivan naraiḥ ||
pauṣe māsy aṣṭamī puṇyā caitre māsi trayodaśī |
kārtike paurṇamāsī ca dvādaśī kārtikottare ||
“kārtikottare” mārgaśīrṣe |
[376]        yeyaṃ bhādrapade māsi ṣaṣṭhī syād bharatarṣabha |
puṇyā ceyaṃ pāpaharā śivā śāntā śubhā nṛpa ||
snānadānādikaṃ kiṃcit sarvam akṣayam ucyate |
yo ‘syāṃ paśyati gāṅgeyaṃ dakṣiṇāpathavāsinam ||
brahmahatyādipāpais tu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
tasmāt tasyāṃ sadā paśyet kārtikeyaṃ sadaiva hi ||
triḥkṛtvā dakṣiṇām āśāṃ gatvā yaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ |
pūjayed devadeveśaṃ sa gacchec chāntimandiram ||
puṇyā bhādrapade proktā pañcamī nāgapañcamī |
“gāṅgeyaṃ” kārtikeyam | “śāntimandiram” śivamandiram | “triḥkṛtvā dakṣiṇām āśāṃ gavā” ity ukter vindhyottarato gacchatām etat phalam |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
śrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakāṇḍe parvakṛtyāni ||
[377]
10
atha tithikṛtyāni
brahmapurāṇe |
caitre māsi jagad brahmā sasarja prathame ‘hani |
śuklapakṣe samagraṃ tu yadā sūryodaye sati ||
tataḥ prabhṛti yo dharmaḥ pūrvaiḥ pūrvataraiḥ kṛtaḥ |
adyāpi gūḍhaḥ sutarāṃ kartavyo ‘sau prayatnataḥ ||
“gūḍho” guptaḥ |
tatra kāryā mahāśāntiḥ sarvakalmaṣanāśinī |
sarvotpātapraśamanī kaliduḥsvapnanāśinī ||
āyuḥpradā puṣṭikarī dhanasaubhāgyavardhinī |
maṅgalyā ca pavitrā ca lokadvayasukhāvahā ||
yasyām ādau ca saṃpūjyo brahmā kamalasaṃbhavaḥ |
pādyārghyadhūpapuṣpaiś ca vastrālaṃkārabhojanaiḥ ||
homair balyupahāraiś ca tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
tathā krameṇa devebhyaḥ pūjā kāryā pṛthak pṛthak ||
[378] “devebhyo” vakṣyamāṇebhyaḥ kālādibhyaḥ |
kṛtvoṃkāraṃ namas te ‘stu kuśodakatilākṣataiḥ |
puṣpadhūpapradīpaiś ca bhojanaiś ca yathākramam ||
mantraṃ saṃpūjanārthaṃ tu bahurūpaṃ paṭhet tataḥ |
oṃ namo brahmaṇe tubhyaṃ kālāya ca mahātmane ||
namas te ‘stu maheśāya truṭaye ca namo namaḥ |
bhavāya ca namas tubhyaṃ namas te ‘stu kṣaṇāya ca ||
namao namas te kāṣṭhayai kalāyai cātha sarvadā |
nāḍikāyai suṣumnāyai muhūrtāya namo namaḥ ||
namo niśābhyaḥ puṇyābhyo divasebhyaś ca nityaśaḥ |
pākṣābhyāṃ cātha māsebhya ṛtubhyaḥ ṣaḍbhya eva ca ||
ayanābhyāṃ ca pañcabhyo vatsarebhyaś ca sarvadā |
namaḥ kṛtayugādibhyo grahebhyaś ca namo namaḥ ||
aṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyebhyo nakṣatrebhyo namo namaḥ |
“aṣṭāviṃśatiḥ” nakṣatrāṇām abhijitā saha |
rāśibhyaḥ karaṇebhyaś ca vyatīpātebhya eva ca ||
prativarṣādhipebhyaś ca vijñānebhyo namaḥ sadā |
namo ‘stu kulanāgebhyaḥ sānuyātṛbhya eva ca ||
“sānuyātṛbhyaḥ” sānucarebhyaḥ |
[379]        namaś caturdaśabhyas tu manubhyaś ca punaḥ punaḥ |
namaḥ puraṃdarebhyaś ca tatsaṃkhyebhyo namo namaḥ ||
“tatsaṃkhyebhyaḥ” caturdaśasaṃkhyebhyaḥ |
pañcāśate namo nityaṃ dakṣakanyābhya eva ca |
namo ‘dityai suvṛttāyai jayāyai nātha sarvadā ||
kṛśāśvāya namas tubhyaṃ sarvāstrajanakāya ca |
namo ‘stu bahuputrāya patnībhiḥ sahitāya ca ||
namo vṛdhyai tathā ṛdhyai nidrāyai dhanadāya ca |
nalakūbarayakṣāya guhykasvāmine namaḥ ||
namo ‘stu śaṅkhapadmābhyāṃ nidhibhyām api nityaśaḥ |
bhadrakālyai namo nityaṃ surabhyai ca namo namaḥ ||
vedavedāṅgavedāntavidyāsthānebhya eva ca |
nāgayakṣasuparṇebhyo namo ‘stu garuḍāya ca ||
aruṇāya namas tubhyaṃ saptadvīpebhya eva ca |
saptabhyaś ca samudrebhyaḥ sāgarebhyaś ca sarvadā ||
uttarebhyaḥ kurubhyaś ca namo hairaṇmayāya ca |
bhadrāśvaketumālābhyāṃ namaḥ sarvatra sarvadā ||
iḍāvṛttāya ca namo harivarṣāya caiva hi |
namaḥ kiṃpuruṣebhyaś ca bhāratāya namo namaḥ ||
[380]        namo bhāratadevebhyo nāgebhyaś cātha sarvadā |
pātālebhyaś ca saptabhyo narakebhyo namo namaḥ ||
kālāgnirudraśeṣābhyāṃ haraye kroḍarūpiṇe |
saptabhyas tv atha lokebhyo mahābhūtebhya eva ca ||
tamase buddhaye caiva namaḥ prakṛtaye tathā |
puruṣāyābhimānāya namo ‘stv avyaktamūrtaye ||
himavatpuruṣebhyaś ca parvatebhyo namaḥ sadā |
purāṇībhyaś ca gaṅgābhyaḥ saptabhyaś ca namo namaḥ ||
namo ‘stv adyatanībhyaś ca saptabhyaś cātha sarvadā |
namo ‘stu puṣkarādibhyas tīrthebhyaś ca punaḥ punaḥ ||
nimnagābhyo namo nityaṃ vitastādibhya eva ca |
caturdaśabhyo devebhyo dhāraṇebhyo namo namaḥ ||
namo dhātre vidhātre ca chandobhyaś ca namo namaḥ |
sureśairāvaṇābhyāṃ ca namo bhūyo namo namaḥ ||
apsarobhyaḥ somapebhyo devebhyaś ca namo namaḥ |
namas tathoccaiḥśravase dhruvāya ca namo namaḥ ||
namo ‘stu dhanvantaraye śastrāstrābhyāṃ namaḥ sadā |
vināyakakumārābhyāṃ vighnebhyaś ca namaḥ sadā ||
śikhāya ca viśākhāya naigamāya namo namaḥ |
namaḥ skandagrahebhyaś ca skandamātṛbhya eva ca ||
jvarāya rogapataye bhasmapraharaṇāya ca |
[381]        ṛṣibhyo vālakhilyebhyaḥ kaśyapāya namaḥ sadā ||
agastaye nāradāya vyāsādibhyo namo namaḥ |
asomapebhyaś ca namas tuṣitebhyo namaḥ sadā ||
ādityebhyo namo nityaṃ dvādaśabhyaś ca sarvaśaḥ |
ekādaśabhyo rudrebhyaḥ sagaṇebhyo namo namaḥ ||
daśabhyo viśvedevebhyaḥ puṇyebhyaś ca namaḥ sadā |
namo vasubhyas tvaṣṭabhyo divyayogibhya eva ca ||
dvādaśabhyo bhṛgubhyaś ca namaḥ sarvatra sarvadā |
daśabhyas tv aṅgirobhyaś ca tapasvibhyo namo namaḥ ||
namo nāsatyadasrābhyām aśvibhyāṃ nityam eva hi |
sādhyebhyo dvādaśabhyaś ca purāṇebhyo namaḥ sadā ||
ekonapañcāśadbhyo ‘tha marudbhyaś ca namo namaḥ |
śilpācāryāya devāya namas te viśvakarmaṇi ||
aṣṭabhyo lokapālebhyaḥ sāyudhebhyaś ca sarvadā |
āyudhebhyo vāhanebhyaḥ purāṇebhyo namaḥ sadā ||
daityarākṣasagandharvapiśācebhyaś ca nityaśaḥ |
pitṛbhyaḥ saptabhedebhyaḥ pretebhyaś ca namaḥ sadā ||
supūjyebhyaś ca devebhyo bhāgavatebhya eva ca |
namas te bahurūpāya viṣṇave paramātmane ||
atha kiṃ bahunoktena mantreṇānena cārcayet |
prāṅmukhodaṅmukho vāpi devān uddiśya pūrvavat ||
[382]        arghaiḥ puṣpaiś ca dhūpaiś ca vastrair mālyaiś ca hṛṣṭavān |
dhanadhānyānnavibhavair dakṣiṇābhiś ca sarvadā ||
itihāsapurāṇānāṃ pravaktṝṃś ca dvijottamān |
kālajñān kālabhedajñān bhṛtyān saṃbandhibāndhavān ||
anenaiva tu mantreṇa svāhāntena pṛthak pṛthak |
yaviṣṭāyāgnaye homaḥ kartavyaḥ sarvatṛptaye ||
vedavac cāhutiṃ datvā sthāne prādhānike sati |
“vedavac cāhutiḥ” iti vedetikartavyatāyuktā | “sthāne prādhānike” pradhānāhutisthāne, ājyabhāgānta iti yāvat |
ātmaśobhā tataḥ kāryā maṅgalālambhanaṃ tathā ||
bhojayitvā dvijān sarvān munīn saṃbandhibāndhavān |
“munayo” atra niyatātmānaḥ |
viśeṣeṇa tu bhoktavyaṃ kāryaś cāpi mahotsavaḥ ||
navasaṃvatsarārambhe sarvasiddhipravartakaḥ |
śuklāyām atha pañcamyāṃ caitre māsi śubhānana ||
śrīr brahmalokān mānuṣyaṃ saṃprāptā keśavājñayā |
tatas tāṃ pūjayet tatra yas taṃ lakṣmīr na muñcati ||
eṣā śrīpañcamī proktā viṣnulokagatipradā |
amāvāsyāyāṃ saṃbhūtaḥ skandaḥ pūrvaṃ hutāśanāt ||
jātaḥ parvaṇi ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu śuklāyāṃ caitranāmani |
“caitranāmani” māse |
[383]        senāpatyābhiṣiktas tu devānāṃ brahmaṇā svayam ||
jitavān tārakaṃ daityaṃ krauñcaṃ śaktyā vibhedanāt |
tasmāt sarvatra vidhinā skando jātīsugandhibhiḥ ||
dīpālaṃkāravastrānnakukkuṭaiḥ pūjya eva hi |
“kukkuṭaḥ” krīḍārthaṃ dātavyaḥ na tu māraṇīyaḥ |
abjaiḥ krīḍanakair anyair ghaṇṭācāmaradarpaṇaiḥ ||
sarvāsu śuklaṣaṣṭhīṣu pūjyo vā śraddhayā naraiḥ |
ārogyakāmair bālānāṃ putravadbhir viśeṣataḥ ||
caitre navabhyāṃ śuklāyāṃ bhadrakālī mahābalā |
yoginīnāṃ tu sarvāsām ādhipatye ‘bhiṣecitā ||
tasmāt tāṃ pūjayet tatra sopavāso jitendriyaḥ |
vicitrair balibhir bhaktyā sarvāsu navamīṣu ca ||
ekādaśyāṃ tataḥ strībhir devī pūjyā ca rukmiṇī |
paurāṇī yoginī kācic chaktir nārāyaṇāṅganā ||
puṣpālaṃkāradhūpānnaśākaiś ca vividhair api |
apūpair vividhākārair vahnibrāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
tatrāparāhṇe vāstuś ca veśmano vaṃśadhārakaḥ |
arghair mālaiś ca vastraiś ca pūjyo raṅgaiḥ sucitritaiḥ ||
pradoṣasamaye tatra sopavāso jitendriyaḥ |
tilasiddhārthakair yuktaṃ gṛhītvā veśmano bahiḥ ||
deyaṃ dikṣu ca paryukṣed duṣṭaprāṇinivāraṇam |
[384] ‘paryukṣet” tad eva pañcagavyaṃ pratidiśaṃ kṣipet |
kṛtopavāso dvādaśyāṃ tato viṣṇuṃ ca pūjayet ||
aparāhṇe tu tatraiva kāmadevaṃ prapūjayet |
paṭasthaṃ vividhair mālyair gandhair uccāvacaiś tathā ||
tatas tu śītalaṃ toyaṃ puṇyam ādāya vāgyataḥ |
kāmadevāgrataḥ sthāpyaṃ puṣpacchanne mahāghaṭe ||
arghādipūjitaṃ samyak praśastair vṛkṣapallavaiḥ |
tasyāṃ rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ tato guptatare gṛhe ||
anarkābhydite kāle snāpyāḥ syus tena vāriṇā |
“sthāpyāḥ” vakṣyamāṇā dayitā eva |
trayodaśyāṃ tu dayitāḥ svayaṃ bhartrā priyeṇa ca ||
ātmapūjā ca kartavyā pūjanīyā gṛhe striyaḥ |
trayodaśīṣu sarvāsu kāmaḥ pūjyo ‘tha sarvadā ||
yātrotsavaś ca vidhivat kartavyaś cātha viṣṇave |
upoṣya ca caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ hariṃ vrajet ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ nikumbhaś ca piśācaiḥ sahito balī |
yāti yoddhuṃ piśācāṃś ca sikatādvīpavāsinaḥ ||
tadarthaṃ gacchatāṃ teṣāṃ madhyāhne tu gṛhe gṛhe |
pūjā kāryā prayatnena nityaṃ śaktyā yathākramam ||
piśācaṃ mṛnmayaṃ kṛtvā ramyaṃ tṛṇamayaṃ ca vā |
gandhair mālyais tathā vastrair alaṃkārair manoharaiḥ ||
[385]        bhakṣyair ullepikāpūpair māṃsair divyaiś ca pānakaiḥ |
svajātivihitaiḥ puṣpair naivedyaiś ca pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
āyudhair vividhākāraiś chatropānahayaṣṭibhiḥ |
śūkānnapūrikāyuktaiḥ sthirair bhakṣyaiś ca bhastrayā ||
“sūkānnaṃ” yavādi | “bhastrā” carmapuṭaḥ |
śikyakuddālapiṭakair bandhanaddhaiś ca carmaṇā |
“bandhanaddhaiś ca carmaṇā” carmabandhena baddhaiḥ |
tatrīvādyair manojñaiś ca tathā pānthopayogibhiḥ ||
“pānthopayogibhiḥ” chatropānatprabhṛtibhiḥ |
madhyāhne taṃ tu saṃpūjya prāpte candrodaye punaḥ |
pūrvavat pūjayet taṃ tu vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ||
tataḥ kṛtasvastyayanān brāhmaṇāṃs tu visarjayet |
tam anuvrajya niyataṃ dvitīyaṃ divase sati ||
gṛhād adūre yo yasya parvatas tam athāruhet |
punar gṛhaṃ praviśyaiva kartavyaḥ sumahotsavaḥ ||
gītavāditranirghoṣair janakolāhalais tathā |
kṛtvā tṛṇamayaiḥ sarpaṃ dṛḍhaiḥ kāṣṭhais tu veṣṭitam ||
krīḍitavyaṃ punar grāme nagareṣu ca sarvadā |
saṃtrāso duṣṭasarpāṇāṃ tatkṣaṇādyair na jāyate ||
tribhiś caturbhir divasaiḥ kartavyaṃ khaṇḍakhaṇḍaśaḥ |
[386]        sarvāpasmāraśamanaṃ tac ca khaṇḍaṃ gṛhe gṛhe ||
pūjitavyaṃ sugupte tu rakṣitavyaṃ ca vatsaram |
irā nāmāpsarāḥ pūrvaṃ saktā viśvāvasor yadā ||
nṛtyagītais tathā devān nāstuvan mandacetanā |
vāsavena tataḥ śaptā jātā ramye himācale ||
manojñapuṣpatāṃ prāptā kalpe vaivasvate sati |
atha phullāṃ tu tāṃ dṛṣṭvā vaiśākhe māsi mādhave ||
strīputramitrabhṛtyaiś ca sahitaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
suvāsāḥ svanuliptāṅgaḥ parituṣṭena cetasā ||
samīpastho vidhānena tām ādau yajate kramāt |
arghaiḥ puṣpair bhakṣyabhojyair dīpadhūpaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā divyām apsarasaṃ ca tām |
gṛhītvā parayā bhaktyā brahmaṇe ca nivedayet ||
tatpuṣpāṇāṃ sahasreṇa viṣṇave ca nivedayet |
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya divyām apsarasaṃ ca tām ||
iha loke ca puṣṭyarthaṃ svargāyānte ca sarvadā |
atha rudrendracandrārkadevanāgebhya eva ca ||
lakṣmyai kṣīrodakanyāyai durgāyai kaśyapāya ca |
nīlāya nāgapataye brāhmaṇebhyas tataḥ kramāt ||
strīputramitrabhṛtyebho jyeṣṭhaputrakrameṇa vā |
[387]         raktasūtreṇa suprotaṃ srajaṃ ca paramātmane ||
nivedayitvā tāṃ devīṃ sugandhāṃ sarvavallabhām |
tatraiva bhojanaṃ bhuṅkte sabhṛtyajñātibāndhavaḥ ||
tat puṣpāḍhyaṃ pibet pānaṃ svajātivihitaṃ śuciḥ |
śṛṇvaṃś ca gītavādyādi paśyaṃś ca naṭanartakān ||
vaiśākhe māsi śuklāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ janārdanaḥ |
yavān utpādayāmāsa yugaṃ cārabdhavān kṛtam ||
brahmalokatripathagāṃ pṛthivyām avatārayat |
tasyāṃ kāryo yavair homo yavair viṣṇuāṃ samarcayet ||
yavān dadyād dvijātibhyaḥ prayataḥ prāśayed yavān |
pūjayec chaṅkaraṃ gaṅgāṃ kailāsaṃ tu himācalam ||
bhagīrathaṃ ca nṛpatiṃ sāgarāṇāṃ sukhāvaham |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tataḥ śrāddhaṃ japahomādikaṃ ca yat ||
śraddhayā kriyate tatra tad ānantyāya kalpate |
sindhos tīre viśeṣeṇa sarvam akṣayam ucyate ||
vaiśākhe śuklasaptamyāṃ jāhnavī jahnunā purā |
krodhāt pitā punas tyaktā karṇarandhrāt tu darkṣiṇāt ||
tāṃ tu pūjayate devīṃ gaṅgāṃ gaganamekhalām |
[388]        aṣṭāviṃśatime prāpte viṣṇuḥ kaliyuge sati ||
śākyān vinaṣṭadharmāṃś ca buddho bhūtvā pravartayat |
tatra pūjyo bhaviṣyo ‘sau puṣpādyair divasatrayam ||
sarvauṣadhaiḥ sarvagandhaiḥ sarvaratnaiś ca sarvadā |
buddhārcāsnāpanaṃ kāryaṃ śāstroktair vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
suvicitrāṇi kāryāṇi caityadevagṛhāṇi ca |
pūjyāḥ śākyāś ca yatayaś cīvarāhārapustakaiḥ ||
puṣpavastrānnadānaṃ ca deyaṃ dīnajanasya ca |
tridinaṃ cotsavaḥ kāryo naṭanartakasaṃkulaḥ ||
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu sṛṣṭāḥ sakalayoninā |
tilāḥ kṛṣṇāś ca gaurāś ca tṛptaye sarvadehinām ||
tasmāt kāryaṃ tilaiḥ snānaṃ tatrāgnau juhuyāt tilān |
nivedayec ca vidhivat tilapātraṃ ca viṣṇave ||
tilatailena dīpāś cadeyā devebhya eva ca |
sodakaiś ca tilaiḥ sārdhaṃ kartavyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
[389]        tilaiḥ samadhubhir yuktaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaś ca bhojanam |
dātavyā dakṣiṇā cāpi tilair madhuyutaiḥ saha ||
mantraṃ japec ca paurāṇaṃ pāramparyakramāgatam |
“oṃ tilāḥ somadevatyāḥ suraiḥ sṛṣṭāś ca gosave ||
svargapradāḥ svatantrāś ca te māṃ rakṣantu nityaśaḥ |”
dadyād anena mantreṇa tilapātrāṇi tatra ca ||
saptabhyas tv atha pañcabhyo brāhmaṇebhyas tu kīrtayet |
prīyatāṃ dharmarājaś ca devāṃś cānyāṃs tathāpi vā ||
evaṃ kṛte sa muktaḥ syāt pāpair janmaśatārjitaiḥ |
“prīyatāṃ dharmarāja” iti mantraṃ “kīrtayet” | “devāṃś cānyān” prīyatāṃ vāsudeva ityādi kīrtayet |
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ [90.10] |
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu brāhmaṇasaptakaṃ kṣaudrayuktais tilaiḥ saṃtarpya dharmarājānaṃ prīṇayitvā pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati | iti |
jyeṣṭhakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇā tu revatī ||
jātā jagati tasyāṃ tāṃ kṛṣṇaiḥ puṣpaiś ca pūjayet |
jyeṣṭhaśuklacaturhyāṃ ca jātā pūrvam umā satī ||
tasmāt sā tatra saṃpūjyā strībhiḥ saubhāgyavṛddhaye |
upahāraiś ca vividhair gītavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ ||
[390]               homaiḥ payobhir vastraiś ca kundapuṣpaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ |
śuklāṣṭamyāṃ (?) purā jātā śuklā devī mahāśaniḥ ||
vadhāya dānavendrāṇāṃ śuklapakṣe tato yajet |
dvādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣe tu viśokā viṣṇunā purā ||
pratiṣṭhitā śubhair mālyais tasmāt tāṃ tatra pūjayet |
śāstreṣu vedāḥ saṃbhūtāḥ purā jyeṣṭhyāṃ yadā bhuvi ||
tadā tān pūjayet tatra vittaśaktyā prayatnataḥ |
navair yavānnaiḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha devaiḥ prapūjya ca ||
tato 'ṅgaliptaḥ sragvī ca navavastropaśobhitaḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvādau kṛtasvastyayanān śucīn ||
adveṣarāgibhir yuktaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ prāśayed yavān |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ tathāṣāḍhe gāṇapatye vināyakaḥ ||
kṛtaḥ pūjyaś ca tatraiva sagaṇo modakotkaraiḥ |
bhakṣyair mālyais tathā gandhaiḥ kulmāṣeṇa tu bhūriṇā ||
gītavādaiḥ sumadhurais tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīṣu sarvāsu vighneśaṃ pūjayet tu tam ||
āṣāḍhe śuklasaptamyāṃ vivasvān nāma bhāskaraḥ |
jātaḥ pūrvāsu tasmāt taṃ tatropoṣya yajet sadā ||
'yajet sadā' ity abhidhānaṃ tasyāṃ tathau tatpūjāvaśyakartavyatābodhanārtham ||
[391]        rathacakrākṛtau ramye maṇḍale sarvakāmadam |
bhakṣyair bhojyaiś tathā peyaiḥ puṣpair dhūpavilepanaiḥ ||
āṣāḍhaśuklapakṣe tu svātīyoge mahābalaḥ |
jāto vāus tathā pūjyo gandhadīpair dvijārcanaiḥ ||
bhūriṇā paramānnena saktubhir vividhais tathā |
“paramānnena” pāyasena | “vividhaiḥ” vividhaprakṛtadravyaiḥ |
sumanobhir vicitraiś ca kusumaiḥ śraddhayā tathā ||
ekādaśyāṃ tu suklāyām āṣāḍhe bhagavān hariḥ |
bhujaṅgaśayane śete yadā kṣīrārṇave sadā ||
tadā tatpratimā kāryā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutā |
suptā tu śeṣaparyaṅke śailamṛdbhiḥ sudārubhiḥ ||
tāmrārakūṭarajatais tathā citrapaṭeṣu vā |
lakṣmyā utsaṅgahasteṣu vinyastacaraṇāmbunā ||
nānāvidhopakaraṇaiḥ pūjyā ca vidhipūrvakam |
upavāsaś ca kartavyo rātrijāgaraṇaṃ tathā ||
tasyāṃ rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ dvādaśyāṃ pūjayet tathā |
trayodaśyāṃ tato nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ vādyaṃ nivedayet ||
vaiṣṇave raṅgajīvibhyo dhanaṃ dadyāc ca śaktitaḥ |
“raṅgajīvino” naṭādayaḥ |
caturdaśyām upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ yajed dharim ||
yamaḥ |
kṣīrābdhau śeṣaparyaṅke āṣāḍhyāṃ saṃviśed dhariḥ |
[392]        nidrāṃ tyajati kārtikyāṃ tayos taṃ pūjayet sadā ||
brahmahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ kṣipram eva vyapohati |
hiṃsātmakais tu kiṃ tasya yajñaiḥ kāryaṃ mahātmanaḥ ||
suṣvāpi ca prabodhe ca pūjito yena keśavaḥ |
āṣāḍhasyeyam “āṣāḍhī”, kārtikayeyam “kārtikī” | “āṣāḍīyā” kārtikīyā iti prāpte kvacid apavādaviṣaye ‘py utsargapravṛtter asyāpi viṣayatā bhavati | yathā śvadaṃṣṭrīya iti prāpte śvādaṃṣṭro maṇir iti | sā caikādaśyām abhipretā,
ekādaśyāṃ tu śuklāyāṃ kārtike māsi keśavam |
prasuptaṃ bodhayed rātrau śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ ||
iti brahmapurāṇavacanāt |
varāhapurāṇe |
bhagavān uvāca |
anyat tava pravakṣyāmi karma saṃsāramokṣaṇam |
kadambaḥ kuṭajaiś caiva dhanako ‘rjunakas tathā ||
ebhir abhyarcanaṃ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
tataḥ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā mama mantravidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
“dhanakaḥ” kapittha iti śabdārṇave vācaspatiḥ | “saṃsthāpanam” anantaroktakarma-samāpanam |
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā idaṃ mantram udīrayet |
[393]        paśyanti meghāny api meghaśyāmaṃ
kramāgataṃ sicyamānaṃ mahīm imām |
nidrāṃ bhagavān gṛhṇātu lokanātha
varṣāsv imaṃ gṛhṇātu meghavṛndam ||
jñātvā svayaṃ paśya ca devanātha
māsaṃ catvāri vaikuṇṭhasya tu paśya nātha ||
āṣāḍhamāse dvādaśyāṃ sarvaśāntikaraṃ śivam |
ya etena vidhānena bhūmir me karma kārayet ||
sa pumān na praṇaśyeta saṃhāreṣu yuge yuge |
brahmapurāṇe |
āṣāḍhānte vaiśvadeve nakṣatre sati śobhane |
daśa tān pūjayet tatra viśvedevān mahābalān ||
“āṣāḍhānte” āṣāḍhapaurṇamāsyām | “vaiśvadevanakṣatraṃ” uttarāṣāḍhā | “daśa viśvedevāḥ” kratudakṣādayaḥ |
yadā tu karkaṭaṃ yāti bhagavān caṇḍadīdhitiḥ |
tadā prabhṛti kālaḥ syād dakṣiṇāyanasaṃjñakaḥ ||
[394]        tatra prāvṛṭsu saṃpūjyāḥ phalapuṣpāmbubhiḥ sadā |
gorasāktān tathā saktūn brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet ||
himaṃ ca śarkarāṃ caiva śākamūlaphalāni ca |
upānacchatramālyāni jaladhenuṃ ca śāstravat ||
“himam” atra karpūram |
śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇapakṣe tu śaṅkaraḥ prathame ‘hani |
triparvaṇā triśalyena trimukhena śareṇa tu ||
“śalyaṃ” lohaśaṅkuḥ |
mukhāni trīṇi cicheda yajñasya mṛgarūpiṇaḥ |
taiḥ śirobhis tapas taptaṃ varāḥ prāptāś ca śaṅkarāt ||
strībhiḥ pūjyāni tānīti na manuṣyaiḥ kathaṃcana |
mṛgaśīrṣaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā liṅgākāraṃ tu mṛnmayam ||
kṣīreṇa snapanīyaṃ tu pūjitavyaṃ yathāvidhi |
arghaiḥ puṣpaiś ca dhūpaiś ca naivedyair vividhair api ||
śākaiḥ sauvarcalābhiś ca bhakṣyaiḥ piṣṭamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
“suvarcalā” oṣadhiviśeṣaḥ |
kāṃsyabhajanavādyaiś ca paścāt karyaṃ ca bhojanam ||
[395]        śrāvaṇe rohinīyoge kaśyapaś ca prajāpatiḥ |
jātas tatrātha pūjyo ‘sau devasyātha pravartakaḥ ||
gandhair malyaiś ca naivedyais tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
pūjyā gāvaś ca rohiṇyas tathā nāryaḥ pativratāḥ ||
śrāvaṇyāṃ śrāvaṇe caiva pūrvaṃ hayaśirā hariḥ |
jagāda sāmavedaṃ tu sarvakilbiṣanāśanam ||
sindhur nadī vitastāyāṃ praviṣṭā tatra caiva hi |
ato ‘rthaṃ [ardhaṃ?] śrāvaṇe tatra snānaṃ sarvārthasiddhidam ||
kṛtvā saṃpūjayed viṣṇuṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam |
śrotavyāny atha sāmāni pūjyā viprāś ca sarvadā ||
krīḍitavyaṃ ca bhoktavyaṃ tatraiva svajanaiḥ saha |
jalakrīḍā tu nārībhiḥ kartavyā bhartṛlabdhaye ||
atha bhādrapade māsi kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kalau yuge |
aṣṭāviṃśatime jātaḥ kṛṣṇo ‘sau devakīsutaḥ ||
bhārāvatāraṇārthāya kṣatriyāṇāṃ kṣayāya ca |
tasmāt sa tatra saṃpūjyo yaśodā devakī tathā ||
gandhair malyais tathā dhūpair yavagodhūmasaṃbhavaiḥ |
[396]        sagorasair bhakṣyabhojyaiḥ phalaiś ca vividhair api ||
rātrau prajāgaraḥ kāryo nṛtyagītasamākulaḥ |
aruṇodayavelāyāṃ navamyāṃ ca tataḥ striyaḥ ||
raktavastrāvṛtāḥ sarvāḥ puṣpamālyair alaṃkṛtāḥ |
nayanti pratimā hy eṣāṃ nānāvibhavasaṃbhavāḥ ||
“eṣāṃ” viṣṇor devakyā yaśodāyāś ca |
nadītīraṃ śubhaṃ ramyaṃ viviktaṃ vā mahat saraḥ |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvanti snapayanti ca tās tataḥ ||
punaḥ praviśya ca gṛhaṃ yavānnaṃ bhuñjate ca tāḥ |
yuktam ikṣuvikāraiś ca madhvājyamaricaiḥ saha ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ pitrye tu nakṣatre sati saṃyutāḥ |
saptaprakārāḥ pitaro jātāḥ kamalasaṃbhavāt ||
tebhyaḥ pūjā ca kartavyā tatra sarvātmanā budhaiḥ |
piṇḍo deyas tathāṣṭāṅgaḥ śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ svasaṃpadā ||
pañcamyāṃ śuklapakṣe tu nīlo nāgapatiḥ purā |
satīdehaṃ praviṣṭaś ca tasmāt taṃ tatra pūjayet ||
mālyair vastropahāraiś ca devāgnigurutarpaṇaiḥ |
nṛtyagītais tathā vādyaiḥ puṣpadhūpānnasaṃpadā ||
[397]        sthānanāgaṃ ca vidhinā pūjayec cāpi sarvadā |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu mādhavaḥ kīrtiṃ labdhavān devakīsutaḥ ||
sthānanāgaḥ kumāraś ca gāvo viprāś ca bhūtale |
tasmāt te tatra saṃpūjyāḥ śarkarāsaṃbhavaiḥ phalaiḥ ||
madhvājyamaricopetaiḥ paripakvair yathākramam |
bhṛtyamitrakalatrebhyo datvā bhuñjīta tāny api ||
janārdanaś ca durgā ca dhanado varuṇas tathā |
vanaspatiś ca pañcaite navamyāṃ tu kṛte yuge ||
utpādayeyur godhūmāṃs tṛptyartham iha dehinām |
tasmāt tān pūjayet tatra bhakṣyair godhūmasaṃbhavaiḥ ||
guḍājyamaricopetaiḥ puṣpadhūpair yathākramam |
godhūmais tarpayed vahniṃ godhūmaiś cārcayed dharim ||
tanniveditaśiṣṭāni bhakṣyāṇi prayatātmavān |
datvā viprāya viprebhyo dadyāt tenaiva dakṣiṇām ||
susaṃskṛtāyāṃ nadyāṃ ca supuṇye ca jalāśaye |
ārāme vṛkṣabahule paścād bhuñjīta tāny api ||
vijayadvādaśīṃ prāpya pūjayet tu janārdanam |
māsi bhādrapade śuklā dvādaśī śravaṇe budhaḥ ||
praśastaḥ sodayaḥ kālaḥ siṃhasaṃstho divākaraḥ |
siṃhāt tṛtīyo drekkāṇaḥ prāvṛṇṇadyoś ca saṃgamaḥ ||
“drekkāṇaḥ” rāśes tṛtīyo bhāgaḥ |
[398]        brāhmaṇo vedapūrṇaś ca pātraṃ hṛdayanandanaḥ |
jitaṃ ta iti mantraś ca svadharmopārjitaṃ dhanam ||
yugapad dvādaśaitāni durlabhāni surair api |
dvādaśātmā hṛṣīkeśo yeṣāṃ daivatam ucyate ||
siṃhasthite tu mārtaṇḍe śravaṇasthe niśākare |
sabudhā dvādaśī śuklā na syād bhādrapadād ṛte ||
sudurlabhā ca sā puṇyā vijayā dvādaśī śubhā |
anugrahārthaṃ martyānāṃ kadācit syān na nityaśaḥ ||
ākasmikīṃ tu tāṃ labdhvā sarvasaṃbhārasaṃbhṛtāḥ |
ekādaśyām upoṣyādau dvādaśyāṃ susamāhitaḥ ||
prayāgādiṣu tīrtheṣu nadīnāṃ saṃgameṣu ca |
kṛtvā snānaṃ ca vidhinā hṛṣīkeśaṃ prapūjayet ||
sthale jale ‘mbare mūrtau kumbhe vā kamalopari |
vahnau vipre gavi gurau pitary atha ca mātari ||
āhūyāsanapuṇyāhasvāgatair atha vistaraiḥ |
“āhūya” āvāhanaṃ kṛtvā |
pādyārghapuṣpadhūpaiś ca dīpālaṃkāracāmaraiḥ ||
aṅgarāgaiḥ sugandhaiś ca payasā pāyasena vā |
bhakṣyair mūlaphalaiḥ śākair māṃsaiḥ suddhair mano’nugaiḥ ||
[399]        madhuparkadadhikṣīramadhubhiḥ sākṣatais tilaiḥ |
mātrayā ca tathā lājair manojñair deśasaṃbhavaiḥ ||
“mātrā” parcchado vastrādiḥ |
pavitraiḥ pānakair hṛdyair gītanṛtyaiḥ savāditaiḥ |
strotrair anekaiḥ paurāṇair vedamantraiḥ susaṃskṛtaiḥ ||
“susaṃkṛtaiḥ” adhyayanasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
pauruṣeṇātha sūktena sūktair anyaiś ca vaiṣṇavaiḥ |
hṛṣīkeśaṃ samabhyarcya dadyāj jalamayīṃ ca gām ||
“jalamayī gauḥ” jaladhenuḥ |
yathāśaktyā tu viprebhyaḥ śraddhāpūtena cetasā |
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ tāmrīṃ raṅgajāṃ mṛnmayīṃ ca tām ||
śatamānāṃ tu maṇikāṃ jalapūrṇāṃ suśobhanām |
“śatamānāṃ” śatapalamānām |
tasyāṃ tu jalapūrṇāyāṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ ca vā ||
tāmraraṅgamayaṃ pātraṃ mṛnmayaṃ vā suśobhanam |
saṃpūrṇaṃ paramānnena dadhisarpirguḍais tathā ||
śarkarākṣīramadhubhis tilacāmīkarāmbubhiḥ |
sevitaṃ mantrapūtaṃ ca dadyād ācchādanaṃ śubham ||
jaladhenuṃ sapātrāṃ tāṃ navena sadaśena tu |
sapavitreṇa śuddhena vastreṇācchādya yatnataḥ ||
[400]        chatreṇa ca suśuddhena sarvam ācchādya śāstravit |
viyaccarakṛtān doṣān rakṣed bhaumāṃś ca vighnadān ||
jitaṃ ta iti mantreṇa hṛśīkeśaṃ smaranti ca |
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ parāṃ śraddhām āśritya susamāhitaḥ ||
dadyād upānahau tadvat pāduke tadanantaram |
rajataṃ gāṃ tathā bhūmiṃ suvarṇaṃ ratnam eva vā ||
hastyaśvarathayānāni gṛhāṇi śayanāni ca |
āsanāny atha bhāṇḍāni vastrāṇy ābharaṇāni ca ||
uddyśya sarvāṃś ca pitṝn paitṛkān mātṛkāṃs tathā |
bhāryāmitragurūṇāṃ tu ye kecit pitaraś ca tān ||
gotranāmasvadhākāraiḥ pitṛyajñavidhānavat |
pitṛbhyaś cānnam akṣayyam idam astv iti saṃjapet ||
athānnadānād bhagavān prīto bhavati keśavaḥ |
guḍakṣīraghṛtair vastraiḥ prīto bhavati candramāḥ ||
jaladānāc ca varuṇaḥ suprasanno bhavet sadā |
rajatena ca dattena prītaḥ syāt tu maheśvaraḥ ||
suvarṇena ca dattena vahnir nityaṃ prasīdati |
hastyaśvarathayānaiś ca maghavā vyajnais tathā ||
śayanair veśmabhir dharmaś chatreṇārkaḥ prasīdati |
upānadbhyāṃ yamo gobhiḥ saurabheyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
[401]        pādukābhyām pradattābhyāṃ padmayoniś ca tuṣyati |
alaṃkārais tathā mālyair jaganmātā ca pārvatī ||
dānaṃ homo japaḥ pūjā tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇam |
nadīnāṃ saṃgame snānaṃ tathā vipreṣu dakṣiṇā ||
sahasraguṇitaṃ tatra sarvam akṣayam ucyate |
yaḥ prīṇāti hṛṣīkeśaṃ tatra śraddhāsamanvitaḥ ||
sa sarvalokān jayati yāti viṣṇoḥ padaṃ ca tat |
tārayet sa pitṝn sarvān daśa pūrvān daśāparān ||
tasmin kāle tu saṃprāpya vitastāṃ sindhusaṃyutām |
suśītalena puṇyena jalenāloḍya mṛttikām ||
sugaurīṃ lakṣaṇopetāṃ saṃgamasnānapuṇyadām |
tām ālabheta yatnena snānakāle sadaiva hi ||
nabhasyānte daśamyāṃ tu kaśyapo labdhavān varam |
avatāraṃ vitastāyāḥ satīdehe purātane ||
ekādaśyāṃ tato rudraḥ satīṃ bhāryām adeśayat |
rasātalagatā sā ca dvādaśyāṃ śaṅkarājñayā ||
atha jātā trayodaśyāṃ śūlabhedād rasātalāt |
caturdaśyāṃ punar naṣṭā paurṇamāsyāṃ samutthitā ||
pratipady atha niṣkrāntā phaṇāmārgeṇa sā nadī |
daśamyādiṣu tat snātvā divaseṣv api saptasu ||
paśyet saṃpūjayed dhyāyet pibet snāyāc ca sarvadā |
[402]        gandhair mālyaiś ca naivedyair dīpair dhūpaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ ||
aṅgarāgaiś ca vividhair vastraiḥ puṣpair vilepanaiḥ |
raktasūtraiḥ kaṅkaṇaiś ca strīṇām ābharaṇais tathā ||
phalair mūlais tathā śākair homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
pūjanīyā viśeṣeṇa vitastāsindhusaṃgame ||
mahāyātrotsavaḥ kāryaḥ pūjyāś ca naṭanartakāḥ |
indro bhādrapade māsi śuklapakṣe tu viṣṇunā ||
niyuktaḥ sarvasasyāni vrīhīṃś caiva phalāni ca |
oṣadhīr vajrahastaś ca tāḍayan pātayaty api ||
tadbhayāt tāni sarvāṇi vardhayanty akhilaṃ jagat |
tasmāt sa tatra saṃpūjyaḥ sabhāryaś ca dine dine ||
sagaṇaḥ sāyudhaś caiva sānuyātraḥ savāhanaḥ |
“sānuyātraḥ” sānucaraḥ |
paṭabhittikṛto devo rājñā pūjyo viśeṣataḥ ||
“paṭabhittikṛtaḥ” paṭe bhittau vā likhitaḥ |
bhakṣyair annaiḥ phalair mūlair vastrair mālyaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ |
tam uddiśyārcayed viprān tathā raṅgopajīvinaḥ ||
pūrvabhādrapadāyoge paurṇamāsyāṃ pitāmahaḥ |
śakrāgniyamayātvambhovāyuvitteśaśaṅkarān ||
[403]        lokapālāṃs tu rakṣārthaṃ trailokyaasyābhyaṣecayat |
pṛthivyā dhāraṇārthaṃ ca tathānantaṃ ca bhoginam ||
“yātuḥ” rākṣasaḥ |
tasmāt tatra likhet padmam aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam |
karṇikāyāṃ likhet tatra sānantaṃ kamalodbhavam ||
prācyādiṣu ca patreṣu vāsavādyāṃś ca sāyudhān |
grahān nāgān krameṇaiva yathārūpān samālikhet ||
tataḥ krameṇa tenaiva saṃsthāpya kalaśān śubhān |
paścāt saṃpūjanīyās tu bhaktyā brahmādayaḥ surāḥ ||
arghapādyādibhiḥ samyag dhūpamālyānulepanaiḥ |
vastrai ratnaiḥ phalair bhakṣyair homair brāḥmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
manojñaiś ca guḍaiḥ kṣīraiḥ sarvasasyasamudbhavaiḥ |
nṛtyair gītaiś tathā vādyaiḥ sarvakāmārthasiddhaye ||
viprebhyaś cātha bandhubhyo dhānā deyāś ca śāstravat |
rātrau dhānāḥ svayaṃ bhakṣyāḥ saṃyamasthair vivekibhiḥ ||
kṛṣṇāśvayuktṛtīyāyāṃ pūrvaṃ tu varuṇo hy abhūt |
tasmāt sa tatra saṃpūjyo yathāvibhavavistaraiḥ ||
śakrād dhi śaṅkhapadmau tu nidhī lebhe dhaneśvaraḥ |
[404]        caturthyāṃ tena tatrāsau sampūjyo brāḥmaṇānugaiḥ ||
pañcamyāṃ vasudhā devī pātālād uddhṛtā purā |
varāheṇātha sā tatra pūjyā diggajasaṃyutā ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ kumārāḥ saṃbhūtāḥ paurāṇāḥ kecid eva hi |
tān uddiśya kumārās tu pūjyāḥ putrāḥ svalaṃkṛtāḥ ||
saptamyām abhimānas tu svavīryāj jitavān jagat |
tam uddiśyātmanaḥ pūjā kāryā strībālakeṣu ca ||
aśokā yoginī siddhā kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ purā yadā |
tadā sā tatra saṃpūjyā yoganidrā tu vaiṣnavī ||
viśeṣeṇa kṛtasnānaiḥ suvastraiś ca svayaṃkṛtaiḥ |
sindūrakardamālepair nṛtyavādyapuraḥsaraiḥ ||
“sindūrakardamālepaiḥ” udakādyāloḍitasindūrapaṅkalepaiḥ |
śaktyā śayyāsane tasyai nivedye sottaracchade |
“sottarachade” soparivastre |
puṣpānnadhūpapūrvaṃ tu krīḍitavyaṃ ca sarvadā ||
guḍājyamaricopetaṃ yavānnaṃ bhakṣayet tataḥ |
śuklāśvayuk caturthyāṃ tu jahau dehaṃ jale satī ||
tasmāt sā tatra saṃpūjyā sarvasaubhāgyavardhinī |
[405]        arghaiḥ puṣpaiś ca dhūpaiś ca dīpair mālyaiś ca kaṅkaṇaiḥ ||
kuṅkumāñjanavastraiś ca nitambiny upayogibhiḥ |
lavaṇālaktakābhyāṃ ca guḍārdraphalakais tathā ||
