tām uddiśya ca nāryas tu pūjyāḥ syur jīvabhartṛkāḥ |
bhaginyo mātaraś caiva tathā nāryaḥ pativratāḥ ||
śuklapakṣe navaṃ dhānyaṃ pakvaṃ jñātvā suśobhanam |
sutithau ca sunakṣatre sumuhūrte śubhe sati ||
gacchet kṣetraṃ vidhānena nṛtyavādyapuraḥsaraḥ |
tatra vahniṃ ca prajvālya dhānyaiḥ saṃstīrya śāstravat ||
“śāstravat” svagṛhyoktaprakāreṇa |
kṛtvā homaṃ tataḥ paścān nayed dhānyaṃ ca bhūṣitam |
puṣpair vastraiḥ phalair mūlair hastyaśvarathasaṃyutam ||
tena devān pitṝn bandhūṃs tarpayitvā yathākramam |
vibhajyāś ca yathāśaktyā daivajñāḥ sasyarakṣiṇaḥ ||
navavastrāvṛtaḥ sragvī svanuliptaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ |
sthitaḥ pūrvamukhas tuṣṭo brahmaghoṣapuraḥsaraḥ ||
śṛṇvan vādyavaraṃ hṛṣṭo maṅgalāmbhanādimān |
prāśnīyād dadhisaṃyuktaṃ navaṃ viprābhimantritam ||
kṛtāhāraś ca kurute gītavādyair mahotsavam |
kṣīrodasāgarāt pūrvaṃ mathyamānāt purātanāt ||
śyāmā devī samutpannā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutā |
[406]        nārāyaṇājñayā sā ca sukumārī yaśasvinī ||
satīdehasamutpannā latā paramaśobhanā |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā cakitās tatra tataḥ sarve surāsurāḥ ||
manojñā sumukhī caiṣā tāṃ ca drakṣyāmahe vayam |
evam uktvā vacas tāṃ tu dadṛśuḥ sarva eva ca ||
cakrur nāma ca tasyās te drākṣeti bhuvi viśrutam |
ato ‘rthaṃ sā supakvā hi pūjitavyā prayatnataḥ ||
puṣpadhūpānnabhakṣyādyais tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
dvau bālau dvau tathā vṛddhau saṃpūjyau tadanantaram ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṃś ca samuddiśya kuṭīrake |
strīsahāyena hṛṣṭena bhṛtyamitrasutaiḥ saha ||
anuliptena vidhivat sragviṇā vajravāsasā |
niveditā gurubhyaś ca svayaṃ bhaktyā na cānyathā ||
utsavaś cāpi kartavyo nṛtyagītasamākulaḥ |
āśvayukśuklapakṣe tu svātīyoge suśobhane ||
pūrvam ucchaiḥśravā nāma prathamaṃ sūryam āvahat |
[407]        tasmāt sāśvair narais tatra pūjyo ‘sau śraddhayā sadā ||
pūjanīyāś ca turagā navamīṃ yāvad eva hi |
śāntisvastyayane kārye tadā teṣāṃ dine dine ||
dhānyaṃ bhallātakaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ vacāṃ siddhārthakāṃs tathā |
pañcaraṅgeṇa sūtreṇa kaṇṭhe teṣāṃ tu bandhayet ||
vāyavyair vāruṇaiḥ sauraiḥ śākrair mantraiḥ savaiṣṇavaiḥ |
vaiśvadevais tathāgneyair homaḥ kāryo dine dine ||
turagā rakṣaṇīyāś ca puruṣaiḥ śastrapāṇibhiḥ |
na ca tāḍyāḥ kvacit tatra na ca vāhyāḥ kadācana ||
jyeṣṭhāyoge purā tatra gajāś cāṣṭau mahābalāḥ |
pṛthivīm avahan pūrvaṃ saśailavanakānanām ||
kumudairāvaṇau padmaḥ puṣpadanto ‘tha vāmanaḥ |
supratīko ‘ñjano nīlas tathā tāṃs tatra pūjayet ||
śākrād ṛkṣāt samārabhya navamyantaṃ ca pūjayet |
tatrāṣṭamyāṃ bhadrakālī dakṣayajñavināśinī ||
prādurbhūtā mahāghorā yoginīkoṭibhiḥ saha |
ato ‘rthaṃ [‘rdhaṃ] pūjanīyā sā tasminn ahani mānavaiḥ ||
uṣitair vastradhūpaiś ca mālyai ratnānulepanaiḥ |
dīparatnais tathā bhakṣyaiḥ phalamūlaiś ca dhānyakaiḥ ||
[408]        āmiṣair vividhaiḥ śākair homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
bilvapatraiḥ śrīphalaiś ca candanena ghṛtena ca ||
paśubhiḥ pānakair hṛdyai rātrijāgaraṇena ca |
durgāgṛhe tu śastrāṇi pūjitavyāni paṇḍitaiḥ ||
vādyabhāṇḍāni cihnāni kavacāny āyudhāni ca |
rātrau tu śilpibhiś tāni svāni pūjyāni sarvadā ||
navamyāṃ tu kṛtasnānaiḥ sarvaiḥ pūjyāś ca brāhmaṇāḥ |
bhuktvā tu śāntiḥ kartavyā yoge godhūlināmani ||
yad uktaṃ śālihotre tu jyotiḥśāstre tu yan matam |
atharvavede yat proktaṃ tac ca tatra samācaret ||
chindyāt sakukkuṭaṃ chāgaṃ kṛtrimaṃ dāruveśmani |
śravaṇe cātha saṃdhyāyāṃ nirdahet sagṛhaṃ tataḥ ||
nirdahyamānau tau tatra nirgacchantau vibhāvayet |
diśaṃ śāntāṃ pradīptāṃ vā dagdhāṃ vā tatphalapradām ||
draṣṭavyāś cāttaśastrāś ca rājñā nīrājane janāḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
sumantur uvāca |
navamyāṃ śrīḥ samāyuktā devaiḥ sarvaiḥ supūjitā |
jaghāna mahiṣaṃ duṣṭam avadhyaṃ devatādibhiḥ ||
[409]        labdhvābhiṣekaṃ varadā śukle cāśvayujasya tat |
yasmāt sā tatra saṃpūjyā navamyāṃ caṇḍikā budhaiḥ ||
mahatvaṃ hi tataḥ prāptā yatra devī sarasvatī |
ato ‘rthaṃ [‘rdhaṃ?] mahatī proktā navamīyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ ||
pūjayitvā mahādevīṃ navamyām atra caṇḍikām |
mahatvam āptavān vīro brahmā viṣṇus tathāmarāḥ ||
tasmād iyaṃ mahāpuṇyā navamī pāpanāśinī |
upoṣyā tu prayatnena satataṃ sarvapārthivaiḥ ||
mūlottareṇa ṛkṣeṇa saṃyuktā vidhivan nṛpa |
viśeṣato dhyānajīvyair bālavṛddhaiḥ sayauvanaiḥ ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyair vaiśyaiḥ śudrair anyaiś ca sādhanaiḥ |
atra dānāni deyāni sopavāsair nṛpair nṛpa ||
gosuvarṇādi rājendra vastrāṇi vividhāni ca |
svamāṃsarudhirair dattair devī tuṣyati vai bhṛśam ||
mahiṣacchāgameṣāṇāṃ rudhireṇa tathā nṛpa |
sumekam ekaṃ varadā tṛptā bhavati caṇḍikā ||
“sumekaḥ” saṃvatsaraḥ |
rudhireṇoraṇasyaiṣā tarpitā vidhivat tataḥ |
ajasya daśavarṣāṇi rudhireṇa pratarpitā ||
[410]        māhiṣeṇa śataṃ vīra tṛptā bhavati caṇḍikā |
sahasraṃ tṛptim āyāti svadeharudhireṇa tu ||
tarpitā vidhivad durgā bhittvā bāhū ca jaṅghake |
evaṃ nānāmlecchagaṇaiḥ pūjyate sarvadasyubhiḥ ||
aṅgavaṅgakaliṅgaiś ca kinnarair barbaraiḥ śakaiḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
sopavāsaś ca kartavya ekādaśyāṃ prajāgaraḥ |
dvādaśyāṃ vāsudevaś ca pūjanīyaś ca sarvadā ||
yātrotsavaś ca kartavyas trayodaśyāṃ ca viṣṇave |
upavāsaś caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ hariṃ yajet ||
āśvayujyāṃ purā vipraiḥ kāmyo homaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
mahābhaye tu rakṣārthaṃ dvāropānte hutāśanaḥ ||
yavākṣataghṛtopetais taṇḍulaiś ca sutarpitaḥ |
pūrṇenduḥ pūjitaś cāpi pādyair dhānyaiḥ phalais tathā ||
devadāruvane nagnaḥ stabdhaliṅgo digambaraḥ |
sabhāryaḥ pūjito rudraḥ skandanandipuraḥsaraḥ ||
devair aśvapatiś cāpi revantaḥ pūjitaḥ sadā |
munibhir gaus tu surabhir gomadbhir atha pūjitā ||
chāgarūpaś ca munibhiḥ pūjito ‘tra hutāśanaḥ |
[411]        urabhrarūpī deveṣu pūjitas tu jalādhipaḥ ||
vināyakena sṛṣṭās tu vāraṇādyā mahābalāḥ |
kāntāradīpadānaṃ ca viṣṇunā saṃpravartitam ||
kaśyapena nikumbhasya yuddhād āgamanaṃ kṛtam |
yugapat tatra kartavyā dvādaśeme mahotsavāḥ ||
āśvayujyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ nikumbho vālukārṇavāt |
āyāti senayā sārdhaṃ kṛtvā yuddhaṃ sudāruṇam ||
tasmāt tatra narair mārgāḥ svagehasya samīpagāḥ |
śodhitavyāḥ prayatnena bhūṣitavyāś ca maṇḍalaiḥ ||
puṣpārghaphalamūlānnasarṣapaprakarais tathā |
veśmāni bhūṣitavyāni nānāraṅgair viśeṣataḥ ||
susnātair anuliptaiś ca narair bhāvyaṃ sabālakaiḥ |
divā tatra na bhoktavyaṃ manuṣyaiś ca vivekibhiḥ ||
strīmūrkhavṛddhabālaiś ca bhoktavyaṃ pūjitaiḥ suraiḥ |
pūjyāś ca sphalaiḥ puṣpaiś tathā dvārordhvabhittayaḥ ||
dvāropānte pradīptas tu saṃpūjyo havyavāhanaḥ |
yavākṣataghṛtopetais taṇḍulaiś caiva tarpitaḥ ||
saṃpūjitavyaḥ pūrṇenduḥ payasā pāyasena ca |
rudraḥ sabhāryaḥ skandaś ca tathā nandī sureśvaraḥ ||
gomadbhiḥ surabhiḥ pūjyā chāgavadbhir hutāśanaḥ |
[412]        aurabhravadbhir varuṇo gajavadbhir vināyakaḥ ||
pūjyaḥ sāśvaiś ca revanto yathāvibhavavistaraiḥ |
tataḥ pūjyo nikumbhaś ca samāṣais tilataṇḍulaiḥ ||
sugandhibhir ghṛtopetaiḥ kṛsarākhyaiś ca bhūribhiḥ |
“kṛsarākhyo” mahiṣākhyagugguluḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu bhoktavyaṃ māṃsavarjitam ||
śaṅkhavādyaravonmiśrair gītanṛtyaiś ca sā niśā |
vahnipārśvagatair neyā dṛṣṭvā krīḍāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ ||
tataḥ prabhātasamaye svanuliptaiḥ svalaṃkṛtaiḥ |
maṅgalālambhanaṃ kāryaṃ vahnisaṃpūjanaṃ tathā ||
bhoktavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ krīḍitavyaṃ yathāsukham |
tasyāṃ rātrau vyatītāyāṃ dvitīyāyām anantaram ||
prabhāte kardamāktaiś ca krīḍitavyaṃ piśācavat |
nirlajjaiś cātha sarvatra rudravat sarvaveśmasu ||
yasmāt sa deśaḥ sotkaṇṭhaḥ piśācaiḥ śāntivāhanaiḥ |
labdhas tu kaśyapāt pūrvaṃ sakrodhasya prajāpateḥ ||
ānīya cātha maitrāṇi lepanīyāni kardamaiḥ |
kāmāgnijanakair vākyaiḥ strīpuṃliṅgārthadeśibhiḥ ||
vācā paiśācam ācāraṃ kīrtayadbhir itas tataḥ |
[413]        aślilāny appi jalpadbhir ākrośadbhiś ca saṃmukham ||
tasminn ahani pūrvāhṇe nikumbhasyānuyāyinaḥ |
kāntaveśāḥ piśācāś ca praviśanti narāṃś ca tān ||
aparāhṇe tataḥ snātāṃs tyaktvā gacchanti bandhuvat |
darpābhimānadoṣeṇa tatra yas tv āgatān narān ||
na pūjayati mohena krīḍāṃ na kurute ‘tha ca |
taṃ nirdahanti te krodhāt piśācā raudradarśanāḥ ||
tataḥ snātair naraiḥ pūjyo devo dāmodaro hariḥ |
saṃpūjya viprān bhoktavyaṃ svanuliptaiḥ svalaṃkṛtaiḥ ||
bandhumitrakalatraiś ca sahitasya yathākramam |
tataḥ prabhṛti ṣaṇmāsān sveṣu veśmasu mānavaiḥ ||
agniḥ saṃnihitaḥ kāro rātrau siddhārthakās tilāḥ |
māsaṃ samagraṃ deyaś ca dīpo nityaṃ gṛhād bahiḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
ratāvasānaṃ saṃprāpy niṣkrānte dikpatau patau |
[414]        utthāya śayanād devī śaucaṃ cakre ‘tiśocitā ||
tataḥ spṛśantyāḥ parvatyā vāridhārādharaprabha |
“dhārādharaprabhā” meghaveṇī | vyāsasyaitat saṃbodhanam |
cintā samabhavat tasyā na putro duhiteti vā ||
tasyāś cintayamānāyā hṛdayāmbusamudbhavā |
jajñe kamalapatrākṣī kanyā mṛnmayapaṅkitā ||
nīlavastrair nivasitā raktavastrāvaguṇṭhitā |
sotthāya kardamāt tasmād vikṛtānanamūrdhajā ||
baddhāñjalipuṭā prāha pārvatīṃ bhāvinīṃ satīm |
anubrūhi mayā kāryaṃ kiṃ te dehaprasūtayā ||
girikanyā tu tāṃ kanyāṃ hṛdayāmbusamudbhavām |
uvāca saṃpariṣvajya mūrdhni cāghrāya pārvatī ||
bhūmipaṅkāṅkasaṃbhūte saṃliptā ca yad aṅgane |
[415]        tasmād udakaseveti bhaviṣyati mahotsavaḥ ||
yasminn indramaho loke dine pātam upaiṣyati |
tasmin dine tava janā ārapsyante mahotsavam ||
yaḥ kāmo bhairavaś cāsīd bhagavatyā bhavasya ca |
sa mahābhairavo bhūtvā kanyāṃ gṛhya kare sthitaḥ ||
tato ‘mbikā bhagavatī patnī bhagavataḥ priyā |
pumāṃsam abravīt kas tvaṃ kiṃ ca gṛhṇāsi me sutām ||
tato daṃṣṭrākarālāsy bhairavo bhairavākṛtiḥ |
umāṃ nīcaiḥ sthitaḥ prāha vidyunmatta ivāmbudaḥ ||
yo ‘bhavad bhairavaḥ kāmo bhagavatyā bhavasya ca |
tatsaṃbhūto ‘ham evaiṣā bhāryā mama bhaviṣyati ||
[416]        dampatī tau vikṛtāṅgau kuveṣācchādanau sthitau |
vikṣāṃcakre somabhūṣaḥ kāv imāv iti śaṅkitaḥ ||
atha tau dampatī tasya devatāsuravanditau |
maheśacaraṇau kāntau praṇamya ca tataḥ sthitau ||
tāv ubhāv api bhavo bhavapālaś
candracihnitajaṭas tripurāriḥ |
prāha pādapatitau hi samīpe
kau yuvāṃ vadatha kiṃ ca karomi ||
ambikā bhavam athāha girīśaṃ
sūryakoṭitaḍidagnisavarṇam |
hāsapūrṇavadanā valamānaṃ
līlayā bhagavatī valamānā ||
yo ‘bhavat tava mamaiva hi kāmo
bhairavo bhayakaras tridaśānām |
eṣa te bhuvi pumān kila jātaḥ
strīyam eva tu mayā madanāgneḥ ||
somalakṣmā tadā prokta umayā somabhāvanaḥ |
gopaveṣadharo devo dampatīty abravīd vacaḥ ||
yadaiva hi tvayā dhyāne jāteyaṃ strī varāṅganā |
tadaiva matprabhāveṇa bhairavo hy eṣa me ‘bhavat ||
nāhaṃ tvayā vinā devi tvaṃ cāpi na mayā vinā |
[417]        ata eva mayā te ‘dya datto lambodarāraṇi ||
utsavas te ca bhavitā trayīproktas tu matpriye |
pūrvāgamo ‘sya bhavitā vṛtte kāmamahotsave ||
atas tadātmako lokaḥ sarvaḥ suravarārcite |
sajjante tena cānyonyaṃ narā nāryaś ca pārvati ||
liṅgeṣu hṛdayaṃ strīṇāṃ bhageṣu hṛdayaṃ nṛṇām |
bhagaliṅgāṅkitaṃ sarvaṃ tad idaṃ jagad aṅgane ||
bhagaliṅgasamutkrośaṃ kurvāṇāḥ sāmarā narāḥ |
anyonyaṃ pātayiṣyanti prakrośantaḥ parasparam ||
ārambhe cāvasāne ca bhavitā bhairavotsavaḥ |
śakrapātadine tadvad udasevikayotsavaḥ ||
“ārambhe cāvasāne ca” ity udasevikotsavāpekṣayā, udasevikāyāḥ śeṣaṃ kālaṃ “bhavitā” “utsavaḥ” |
yat puraṃ nagaraṃ grāmaṃ bhairavo ‘yaṃ pravekṣyati |
unmattam iva tat sarvaṃ sastrīvṛddhaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
unmattavad anunmattaṃ cāturvarṇyaṃ gireḥ sute |
bhaviṣyati suronmattaṃ bhairavāgamaharṣitam ||
yathā niyuktāḥ pitryarthe viśante devatā dvijān |
[418]        evaṃ vai bhairavamahe bhairavo viśate janān ||
tato rāsabham ārūḍhāḥ sakṛt kardamalepitāḥ |
kaṭukāñcanavallībhiḥ kṛtaveṣṭanabhūṣaṇāḥ ||
tatphalābaddhavalayāḥ prakaṭotkaṭaniḥsvanāḥ |
bhasmabhūṣitasarvāṅgā viṇmūtramalapaṅkinaḥ ||
talatālair vādyamānaiḥ krūrābaddhavaco’nvitaiḥ |
“abaddham” asaṃbaddham |
sūcyamāne varārohe bhairavo bhāryayā saha ||
pravekṣyati puraṃ hy eṣa utsavaṃ janayan nṛṇām |
praviṣṭe bhairave bhīru puruhūtārcitaṃ puram ||
janasya rocako ghoro bhaviṣyati tadotsavaḥ |
“rocakaḥ” prītiviṣayaḥ | sa eva “ghoraḥ” utkaṭaḥ |
yeṣāṃ varṣaśataṃ bhīru jarayā ye ca jarjarāḥ ||
te ‘pi vatsakavat sarve kariṣyanty utsavaṃ narāḥ |
“vatsakavat” kumāravat |
nānābhūṣaṇapuṣṭāṅgāḥ kuṅkumāgurubhūṣitāḥ ||
pītair anekavarṇaiś ca vāsobhiḥ pariveṣṭitāḥ |
karṇapūraiḥ sumālyaiś ca sadāmālāḥ sacūḍinaḥ ||
“sadāmālāḥ” sasrajaḥ | “cūḍā” bāhuvibhūṣaṇam, tadvantaḥ |
[419]        cūrṇaiś campakapuṣpādyai rūkṣapiṣṭaśiroruhāḥ |
āsphoṭayantaḥ krandantaḥ śrāvayanto ‘priyāṇi ca ||
rathyāsu rājamārgeṣu śrāvayanto yatas tataḥ |
kulaputrāḥ kulastrīṇām anaṅgaprakṛtāni ca ||
vyaṅgāni yāni guhyāni kulācārakṛtāni ca |
teṣāṃ hi sarvasaṃdehaṃ darśayantaḥ pade pade ||
gāyantaś ca pranṛtyantaḥ kurvanto ‘vinayāni ca |
pūrvaṃ salajjībhūtvā ca nirlajjatvam upāgatāḥ ||
lajjanīyān api gurūn ākrośantaḥ parān api |
udasevikayā martyāḥ kariṣyanti yathāmanaḥ ||
na mātur lajjate putrī na putrasya tathāraṇī |
pitur na putraḥ pautrasya na pitā tu pitāmahāt ||
na mātulasya svasrīyaḥ svasvrīyasya na mātulaḥ |
muhūrtenaiva svajanā nirlajjatvam upāgatāḥ ||
anyonyaṃ rūkṣavacanais tarjayanti ca mūḍhavat |
bhasmarūkṣā vacorūkṣāḥ śvetāṅgā mṛdayāpi ca ||
bhakticchedaviliptāṅgā cārvaṅgā cāruceṣṭitāḥ |
sārameyān anaḍuha ārūḍhā gardabheṣu ca ||
ḍombaveṣā gopaveṣā vaṭuveṣadharāḥ pare |
rājaveṣāntyaveśāś ca taruṇā vṛddharūpiṇaḥ ||
nāpitānāṃ ca veṣeṇa mandānām atha cāpare |
palāṇḍusīdhuvarṣaiś ca śvāsakṛn miśritair nṛpa ||
dhūpaṃ saṃcārayiṣyanti ghrāṇavairāgyakāraṇam |
jalecaramalaṃ cānye narā nṝṇām ajānatām ||
nāsikāyāṃ pradāsyanti durgandham aśubhaiḥ samam |
[420]        anye tu puruṣā devi vedaveṣavibhūṣitāḥ ||
kāvyāni śrāvayanto hi te hṛṣyanti yathāmarāḥ |
yaś ca nākruśyate tatra yaś ca nākrośate param ||
bibhyanti tasya pitaro brahmahantur yathā tathā |
rājano hi yathāśvānāṃ kuñjarāṇāṃ yathā narāḥ ||
hitāya jāyate tadvan narāṇām udasevikā |
na tasya devā aśnanti haviḥ pitara eva vā ||
madhyasthabhāvaṃ kurute udasevikayā hi yaḥ |
na tvaṃ nandī na caivāhaṃ tasya tuṣyāmi pārvati ||
virāme ca mahāśokaḥ putrābhāve tvayā kṛtaḥ |
udasevikayā tv evaṃ bhavitā bhairavāgamaḥ ||
atulā harṣasaṃpattiḥ paścād atha tathāvayoḥ |
na bhrājate yathā vedaṃ tvacchokād bhavanaṃ tava ||
udasevikayā hīnaṃ tathā tadbhavitā puram |
narā nāryaś ca girije bhasmanā kardamena ca ||
niṣprabhāṇi kariṣyanti gṛhāṇy āyatanāni ca |
cairair udvāsitam iva puraṃ devi bhaviṣyati ||
mṛtpiṇḍabhasmaviṇmūtrair naraiḥ pretair ivāvṛtam |
bhairavo ‘yaṃ mṛta iti ghoṣayantas tathā narāḥ ||
tṛṇacchannaṃ naraṃ tatra saṃveśyāmbarasaṃvṛtam |
iti vācaḥ prakurvāṇo bhairavo ‘yaṃ jahāti naḥ ||
nirhariṣyanti taṃ viprā mṛtaṃ gurum iva priyam |
taḍāgakūle taṃ nyasya saritkūle tathāpi vā ||
snānanirmuktakaluṣāḥ prayāsyanti tato gṛham |
[421]        saṃsādhya bhairavaṃ snātā utsavotkaṭakheditāḥ ||
munivratā iva narā bhaviṣyanti dhiyā yutāḥ ||
yathevete pārvati bhairavāgame
narāś ca lajjāṃ muhur eva lajjire |
tathaiva saṃbhāvitabhairavāḥ punar
babhūvur ekāntatapovanāvṛtāḥ ||
imaṃ tu yaḥ sundari bhairavotsavaṃ
paṭheta vipro dvijadevasaṃsadi |
sa putrapautraiḥ samam eva tatkṣaṇe
mahāgaṇeṣatvam avāpnuyāc chubham ||
brahmapurāṇe |
amāvāsyāyāṃ tu devā kārtike māsi keśavāt |
abhayaṃ prāpya suptās tu sukhaṃ kṣīrodasānuṣu ||
lakṣmīr daityabhayān muktā sukhaṃ suptāmbujodare |
caturyugasahasraṃ tu brahmā suptas tu paṅkaje ||
ato ‘rthaṃ vidhivat kāryā manuṣyaiḥ sukhasuptikā |
divā tatra na bhoktavyam ṛte bālāturāj janāt ||
pradoṣasamaye lakṣmīṃ pūjayitvā yathākramam |
dīpavṛkṣās tathā kāryāḥ śaktyā devagṛheṣv api ||
[422]        catuṣpathaśmaśāneṣu nadīparvataveśmasu |
vṛkṣamūleṣu goṣṭheṣu catvareṣu gṛheṣu ca ||
vastraiḥ puṣpaiḥ śobhitavyāḥ krayavikrayabhūmayaḥ |
dīpamālāparikṣipte pradeśe tadanantaram ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvādau vibhajya ca bubhukṣitān |
alaṃkṛtena bhoktavyaṃ navavastropaśobhinā ||
snigdhair mugdhair vidagdhaiś ca bāndhavair nirvṛtaiḥ saha |
śaṅkaraś ca purā dyūtaṃ sasarja sumanoharam ||
kārtike śuklapakṣe tu prathame ‘hani satyavat |
jitaś ca śaṅkaras tatra jayaṃ lebhe ca pārvatī ||
ato ‘rthaṃ śaṅkaro duḥkhī gaurī nityaṃ sukhoṣitā |
tasmād dyūtaṃ ca kartavyaṃ prabhāte tatra mānavaiḥ ||
tasmin dyūte jayo yasya tasya saṃvatsaraḥ śubhaḥ |
parājayo viruddhas tu labdhanāśakaro bhavet ||
śrotavyaṃ gītavādyādi svanuliptaiḥ svalaṃkṛtaiḥ |
viśeṣatas tu bhoktavyaṃ praśastair bāndhavaiḥ saha ||
tasyāṃ niśāyāṃ kartavyaṃ śayyāsthānaṃ śuśobhanam |
gandhaiḥ puṣpais tathā vastrai ratnair mālyair alaṃkṛtam ||
dīpamālāparikṣiptaṃ tathā dhūpena dhūpitam |
dayitābhiś ca sahitair neyā sā ca bhaven niśā ||
navair vastraiś ca saṃpūjyā dvijasaṃbandhiabāndhavāḥ |
[423] padmapurāṇe |
kārtike māsy amāvāsyā tasyāṃ dīpapradīpanam |
śālāyāṃ brahmaṇaḥ kuryāt sa gacchet paramaṃ padam ||
yāvad dīpasya saṃkhyā vai ghṛtenāpūrya śaktitaḥ |
tāvad yugasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate ||
pratipadi brāhmaṇāṃś ca guḍamiśraiḥ pradīpakaiḥ |
vāsobhir ahataiḥ pūjya gacched vai brahmaṇaḥ padam ||
gandhapuṣpair navair vastrair ātmānaṃ pūjayet tataḥ |
tasyāṃ pratipadāyāṃ tu sa gacched brahmaṇaḥ padam ||
mahāpuṇyā tithir iyaṃ balir ājyapravartinī |
brahmaṇas tu priyā nityaṃ bāleyī parikīrtitā ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayed yo ‘syām ātmānaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ viṣṇor amitatejasaḥ ||
caitre māsi mahābāho puṇyā pratipadā parā |
tasyāṃ yaḥ śvapacaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ kuryān narottamaḥ ||
na tasya duritaṃ kiṃcin nādhayo vyādhayo nṛpa |
bhavanti kuruśārdūla tasmāt snānaṃ samācaret ||
divyaṃ nīrājanaṃ tad dhi sarvarogavināśanam |
gomahiṣyādi yat kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ bhūṣayen nṛpa ||
celavastrādibhiḥ sarvāṃs toraṇādhas tato nayet |
[424]        brāhmaṇānāṃ tathā bhojyaṃ kuryāt kurukulodvaha ||
tisra etāḥ purā proktās tithayaḥ kurunandana |
kārtike ‘śvayuje māsi caitre māsi tathā nṛpa ||
snānaṃ (?) dānaṃ śataguṇaṃ kārtike yā tithir nṛpa |
balir ājye tu śubhadā yā śubhāśubhanāśinī ||
vāmanapurāṇe |
baliṃ prati trivikvrama uvāca |
tathānyad utsavaṃ puṇyaṃ vṛtte śakramahotsave |
vīrapratipadā nāma tava bhāvī mahotsavaḥ ||
tatra tvāṃ naraśārdūla hṛṣṭā puṣṭās tv alaṃkṛtāḥ |
puṣpadīpapradānena hy arcayiṣyanti yatnataḥ ||
tavotsaveṣu vipulaṃ bhaviṣyanti divāniśam |
yathaiva rājye bhavatas tu saṃprati
tathaiva sā bhāvyatikaumudīti ca ||
brahmapurāṇe |
ṣaḍ garbhān dānavān pūrvaṃ vañcayitvā janārdanaḥ |
tāṃ yoganidrām asṛjad devīṃ rakṣārtham ātmanaḥ ||
ekāṃ niśāṃ bhagavatī siddhidāṃ kāladaivatam |
[425]        śuklapakṣe tu saṃpūjyā kārtike keśavājñayā ||
caturthyām atha vāṣṭamyāṃ navamyāṃ vā susiddhidā |
caturdaśyām atha strībhiḥ susnātābhir yathākramam ||
gṛhād bāhye tu yatra syād ekānte tu phaladrumaḥ |
tatrārghapuṣpadhūpānnasaṃpadā pūjayet tu tām ||
ekaputravatī nārī manovākkāyasaṃyutā |
pūrvopakaraṇair yuktaṃ gṛhītvā grāsam uttamam ||
tato dadāti śyenāya suprītā prītikāminī |
imaṃ grāsaṃ nayasvārya bhagavatyai niveditam ||
ity uktvā svagṛhaṃ yāti tataḥ pūrṇamanorathā |
kṛte yuge prasiddho ‘yaṃ dāsavad bhṛtako yathā ||
yathoparicaro rājā retaḥ parṇapuṭe svakam |
nidhāya pradadau netuṃ śyenāya svāṃ priyāṃ prati ||
yugeṣv anyeṣu mantraṃ vai paṭhantv anala ity api |
jahāti bhūmau taṃ grāsaṃ prāṅmukhī yāti veśma ca ||
āmantraṇaṃ tu yasyāpi pakṣiṇo nirmitaṃ purā |
sa eva pakṣī gṛhṇāti taṃ grāsam iti niścayaḥ ||
ādau gṛhe tato bhuṅkte sā nārī susamāhitā |
paścād gṛhaptir bhuṅkte sabhṛtyajñātibāndhavāḥ ||
gṛhadevīṃ tu tenaiva vidhinā pūjayet patiḥ |
śyenagrāso na deyaś ca na ca vṛkṣaṃ samāśrayet ||
kiṃ tu guptagṛhe bhāryāṃ pūjayet pativratām |
[426]        yugmeṣv anyeṣu sadbhāvo dampatyor no bhaved yadā ||
tadā svakuladharmaṃ tu tāvanmātraṃ karoti saḥ |
varāhapurāṇe [123.8–11] |
varāha uvāca |
kaumudasya tu māsasya yā sitā dvādaśī bhavet |
arcayed yas tu māṃ tatra tasya puṇyaphlaṃ śṛṇu ||
yāval lokā hi vartante yāvat tvaṃ caiva mādhavi |
madbhakto jāyate tāvad anyabhakto na jāyate ||
kṛtvā vai mama karmāṇi dvādaśyāṃ matparo naraḥ |
mamaiva bodhanārthāya cemaṃ mantram udāharet ||
oṃ mantra ucyate |
brahmendrarudrair abhipūjyamāno
bhavān ṛṣir vanditavandanīyaḥ |
prāptā taveyaṃ dvādaśī kaumudākhyā
jāgṛṣva jāgṛṣva ca lokanātha ||
meghā gatā nirmalapūrṇacandraḥ
śāradyapuṣpāṇi ca lokanātha |
ahaṃ dadānīti ca puṇyahetoḥ
prabudhya jāgṛṣva ca lokanātha ||
[427]        evaṃ karmāṇi kurvanti dvādaśyāṃ ye yaśasvini |
mama bhaktyā naraśeṣṭhās te yānti paramāṃ gatim ||
brahmapurāṇe |
ekādaśyāṃ tu śuklāyāṃ kārtike māsi keśavam |
prasuptaṃ bodhayed rātrau śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ ||
nṛtyair gītaiś tathā vādyai ṛgyajuḥsāmamaṅgalaiḥ |
vīṇāpaṇavaśabdaiś ca purāṇaśravaṇena ca ||
vāsudevakathābhiś ca stotrair anyaiś ca viṣṇavaiḥ |
subhāṣitair indrajālair bhūmiśobhābhir eva ca ||
puṣpair dhūpaiś ca naivedyair dīpavṛkṣaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ |
homair bhakṣyair apūpaiś ca phalaiḥ śākaiś ca pāyasaiḥ ||
ikṣor vikārair madhunā drākṣākṣaudraiḥ sadāḍimaiḥ |
kuṭherakasya mañjaryā mālatyā lavaṇena ca ||
hṛdyābhyāṃ śvetaraktābhyāṃ candanābhyāṃ ca sarvadā |
kuṅkumālaktakābhyāṃ ca raktasūtraiḥ sakaṅkaṇaiḥ ||
tathā nānāvidhaiḥ puṣpair dravyair vīrakrayāhṛtaiḥ |
tasyāṃ rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ dvādaśyām aruṇodaye ||
ādau ghṛtena tailena madhunā snāpayet tataḥ |
dadhnā kṣīreṇa ca tato pañcagavyena śāstravat ||
udvartanaṃ māṣacūrṇaṃ masūrāmalakāni ca |
lodhraṃ kāleyakaṃ caiva tagaraṃ varṇasaṃbhavam ||
[428]        sarṣapāś ca priyaṅguś ca mātuluṅgarasas tathā |
sarvauṣadhyaḥ sarvagandhāḥ sarvabījāni kāñcanam ||
maṅgalāni yathākāmaṃ ratnāni ca kuśodakam |
hastidantoddhṛtā mṛc ca vṛṣaśṛṅgoddhṛtā ca mṛt ||
nadītīrād gavāṃ sthānād valmīkāt saṃgamoddhṛtāt |
indrasthānāc ca sarasas tathā parvatamastakāt ||
etābhiḥ śodhayed devaṃ dadyād gorocanaṃ śubham |
tatas tu kalaśā deyā yathāproktāḥ svalaṃkṛtāḥ ||
jātīpallavasaṃyuktāḥ saphalāś ca sakāñcanāḥ |
puṇyāhavedaśabdena vīṇāveṇuraveṇa ca ||
śabdena madhureṇaiva sūtamāgadhavandinām |
evaṃ saṃsnāpya govindaṃ svanuliptaṃ svalaṃkṛtam ||
suvāsasaṃ pūjayec ca sumanobhiḥ sukuṅkumaiḥ |
dīpair dhūpair manojñaiś ca pāyasena ca bhūriṇā ||
mātrayānnapradānaiś ca homaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sudakṣiṇaiḥ |
vāsobhir bhūṣaṇai ratnair gobhir aśvair manoharaiḥ ||
brāhmaṇāḥ pūjanīyāś ca viṣṇor ādyāś ca mūrtayaḥ |
saṃvibhajyāś ca dāyādā vrātyāḥ sātvatajātayaḥ ||
yajñaśiṣṭāmṛtaṃ paścād bhoktavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha |
[429]        saṃvibhajyās tataḥ paścāt trayodaśyāṃ tu nartakāḥ ||
caturdaśyāṃ na bhoktavyaṃ pātavyam atha vā payaḥ |
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu saṃpūjyo bhaktyā dāmodaraḥ sadā ||
tasminn ahani yatena kartavyaṃ naktabhojanam |
dauḥśīlyabhrānticakitais tatra saptarṣibhiḥ purā ||
saṃtyaktāḥ kṛttikāḥ ṣaṭ ca ṛte sādhvīm arundhatīm |
tatra skandaś ca bhagavān kārtikeyatvam āgataḥ ||
khaḍgaś ca rudravacanād vahnimadhyāt samutthitaḥ |
rudrāt pāśaśataṃ lebhe varuṇas tatra dāruṇam ||
agniś ca bhṛguśāpāc ca muktas tatra mahābhayāt |
arhito manunā martyo nītvā tatra payonidhim ||
snātavyaṃ vṛṣabhais tatra prāpta utsarga eva hi |
iṣṭiḥ sāṃgrāmikī tatra kṛtā devair jigīṣubhiḥ ||
tasmād daśātra kartavyāḥ paurāṇā utsavāḥ sakṛt |
tatra candrodaye pūjyās tāpasyaḥ kṛttikāś ca ṣaṭ ||
kārtikeyas tathā khaḍgo varuṇaś ca hutāśanaḥ |
dhanyaiḥ saśūkair dvārordhvaṃ bhūṣitavyaṃ niśāgame ||
mālyair dhūpais tathā gandhair bhakṣyais tūccāvacais tathā |
paramānnaiḥ phalaiḥ śākair vahnibrāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
ikṣūṇāṃ tu vikāreṇa dīpavṛkṣaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ |
[430]        kṛsareṇātha payasā tathā ramyaiś ca gorasaiḥ ||
lepakābhir vicitrābhiḥ svajātyuktaiś ca pānakaiḥ |
evaṃ devāḥ susaṃpūjyā dīpo deyo gṛhād bahiḥ ||
dīpopānte tato gartaś caturasro manoharaḥ |
caturviṃśāṅgulaḥ kāryaḥ siktaś candanavāriṇā ||
gavāṃ kṣīreṇa pūrṇaś ca samantāt parirakṣitaḥ |
tatra haimamayo matsyo muktānetro manoharaḥ ||
saṃkṣeptavyo vidhānena namo ‘stu haraye paṭhet |
brāhmaṇāya manojñāya dadyāt tat kṣīrasāgaram ||
nīlaṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ dadyāc caturvatsatarīyutam |
sarvasasyadhanaṃ ramyaṃ sarvagandhasamanvitam ||
suvāsasaṃ brāhmaṇāya mahākāntāratāraṇam |
yāvanti tasya romāṇi śarīre santi saṃkhyayā ||
tāvad yugasahasrāṇi svarge vasati tatpradaḥ |
pūjayitvā tato viṣṇuṃ raktamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
bhoktavyaṃ gorasaprāyaṃ suptavyaṃ sthaṇḍile tataḥ |
ekādaśyādiṣu tathā tāsu pañcasu rātriṣu ||
dine dine ca snātavyaṃ śītalāsu nadīṣv api |
[431]        pūjanīyo harir devo brāhmaṇāḥ sahutāśanāḥ ||
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dattaṃ pūrvaṃ surāsuraiḥ |
viṣṇuprabodhasamaye divyaṃ yugaśataṃ kramāt ||
yathāvat pālitaṃ tac ca munibhir vākyam uttamam |
devair viṣṇuprabodhādi samagraṃ tūttarāyaṇam ||
pālitaṃ tac ca vacanāt paścāt tyaktaṃ vidher vaśāt |
nityasaṃgrāmaśīlatvād dharmād dhiṃsātmakais tathā ||
devadānavayakṣaiś ca piśācair atha rākṣasaiḥ |
varjitā prāṇihiṃsā ca māsāder dinapañcake ||
yasmān na bhojanaṃ dhātrā teṣāṃ māṃsād ṛte kṛtam |
tataḥ prabhṛti yo yena dharmaś ca svayam āśritaḥ ||
sa tena pālyate nityam adyāpi prathitātmanā |
munivad devavac cātha daityadānavavat tathā ||
deyā ca sarvabhūtebhyo narair abhayadakṣiṇā |
mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya prārambhe pratipady api ||
navasaṃvatsarārambho devaiḥ kṛtayuge kṛtaḥ |
kaśyapena ca kaśmīrā nirmitāś ca suśobhanāḥ ||
tasmāt tatra naraiḥ kāryaḥ sadā puṇyamahotsavaḥ |
puṣpair mālyair gītavādyair maṅgalyair dravyaśobhitaiḥ ||
dānair vastrais tathā ratnair uparyuktair navaiḥ sadā |
[432]        yad viṣṇor dakṣiṇaṃ netraṃ tad evākṛtimat punaḥ ||
aditeḥ kaśyapāj jajñe mitro nāma divākaraḥ |
mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya śuklapakṣe śubhe tithau ||
saptamyāṃ tena sā khyātā loke ‘smin mitrasaptamī |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu snapanaṃ kāryaṃ tasmai mitrāya bhānave ||
yathā viṣṇoḥ prabodhe tu kriyate sarvasaṃpadā |
tatropavāsaḥ kartavyo bhakṣyāṇy atha phalāni vā ||
rātrijāgaraṇaṃ kāṛyaṃ gītavādyapuraskṛtam |
saptamyṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ snātvā yajed ravim ||
nānākusumasaṃbhārair bhakṣyaiḥ piṣṭamayais tathā |
madhunā ca prabhūtena homajapyasamādhibhiḥ ||
brāhmaṇāṃs tarpayet paścād dīnānāthāṃś ca mānavān |
aṣṭamyāṃ saṃvibhajyātha tathā ca naṭanartakān ||
dinadvaye ca bhoktavyaṃ paiṣṭam annaṃ madhuplutam |
vāmaṃ netraṃ tu yad viṣṇos tad eva bhuvanatrayam ||
anasūyātmajaś candras tv amṛtenābhyaṣecayat |
paurṇamāsyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tasmāt taṃ pūjayet sadā ||
[433]        śuklaiḥ puṣpaiś ca payasā śraddhayā parayā yutaḥ |
ratnair dīpaiś ca balibhir homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
gobhyaś ca lavaṇaṃ deyaṃ tasminn ahani sarvadā |
yasmāt tu lavaṇaṃ candrād dakṣaśāpād viniḥsṛtam ||
mātā svasā ca duhitā pūjyāḥ śreṣthakulāṅganāḥ |
raktavastradvayenaiva sadaśena navena ca ||
rūpamātraṃ tu vidhivat kartavyaṃ tatra saddvijaiḥ |
kāntaṃ rūpaṃ bhavet strīṇāṃ pūrṇendoḥ pūjanād iti ||
krīḍanty ākāśagaṅgāyām aṣṭame cātmavartmani |
gajendrāḥ kumudādyāś ca karais taj jāhnavījalam ||
gṛhītvā pratimuñcanti sūtkārair dakṣiṇāmukhaiḥ |
[pluṣṭāḥ pūṣṇo ‘ṃśupātaiś ca sāmīpyāt sūryaraśmibhiḥ ||
samyak svadehaśāntyarthaṃ dāhasya śamanāya ca |]
mahāprapātān nabhasaḥ patamānaṃ tu taj jalam ||
vāyunā dhmāyamānaṃ tu sthānatvam upagacchati |
tat puṇyaṃ himavatsānau patate ca yadā himam ||
prathamaṃ tatra saṃpūjyo himavān śiśiras tathā |
hemantaś ca tathā nāgo nīlo nīlābjasaṃnibhaḥ ||
sthānanāgaś ca saṃpūjyaḥ phalaiḥ patrair namerujaiḥ |
bakapuṣpāṇi deyāni dhūpo guggulusaṃbhavaḥ ||
[434]        madhvājyatilasaṃmiśraṃ pitṛbhyaś ca himaṃ bahu |
yasmin deśe himaṃ na syāt tatra brūyād dhimaṃ himam ||
ghṛtāktaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaś ca deyaṃ māṣodanaṃ tathā |
utsavaś ca tataḥ kāryo gītanṛtyasamākulaḥ ||
viśeṣataś ca bhoktavyaṃ manojñaṃ bhojanaṃ śubham |
śyāmā devī pūjitavyā dhūpapuṣpānulepanaiḥ ||
annair bhakṣyaiḥ phalair mūlaiḥ svanuliptaiḥ svalaṃkṛtaiḥ |
himopari niviṣṭaiś ca guruprāvaraṇāmbaraiḥ ||
mitrabhṛtyātmasaṃbandhisahitaiś ca yathāsukham |
bhojyaṃ viśeṣavat kāryaṃ śrotavyaṃ gītavāditam ||
draṣṭavyaṃ puṃścalīnṛtyaṃ pūjanīyās tathā striyaḥ |
navaṃ ca madyaṃ pātavyaṃ madyapaiś ca yathākramam ||
brahmaloke tapas taptvā pitaro lebhire varam |
pauṣe kṛṣṇāṣṭakāyāṃ tu śākaiḥ śrāddhaṃ subhojanam ||
pauṣamāsasya kṛṣṇāyām ekādaśyām upoṣitaḥ |
dvādaśyāṃ pūjayed viṣṇuṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ harim ||
yātrā kāryā trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyām upoṣitam |
paurṇamāsyāṃ yajed viṣṇuṃ yāvad vibhavavistaram ||
idaṃ jagat purā lakṣmyā tyaktam āsīt tato hariḥ |
puraṃdaraś ca somaś ca tathā pūṣā bṛhaspatiḥ ||
[435]        pañcaite puṣyayogena paurṇamāsyām tapobalāt |
alaṃkṛtaṃ punaś cakruḥ saubhāgyotsāhalakṣmibhiḥ ||
tasmān naraiḥ puṣayoge tatra saubhāgyavṛddhaye |
gaurasarṣapakalkena samālabhya svakāṃ tanum ||
kṛtasnānaiś tataḥ kāryam alakṣmīnāśanaṃ param |
udvartya dehaṃ ca tathā sarvauṣadhiyutair jalaiḥ ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā navaṃ vastraṃ gṛhītvācchādanaṃ tataḥ |
draṣṭavyaṃ maṅgalaśataṃ dhūpasragdhānyaśobhitam ||
tatra nārāyaṇaḥ śakraś candraḥ pūṣā bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃpūjyāḥ puṣpadhūpādyair naivedyaiś ca pṛthak pṛthak ||
praśastair vaidikair mantraiḥ kṛtvāgnihavanaṃ śubham |
dhanair viprāś ca saṃtarpyā navair vastraiś ca śobhitāḥ ||
tataḥ puṣṭikaraṃ hṛdyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ ghṛtapāyasam |
puṣyayoge ca kartavyaṃ rājñā snānaṃ ca sarvadā ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ tu māghasya māṃsaśrāddhaṃ mahāphalam |
pitṛbhiḥ prārthitaṃ pūrvaṃ tasmāt tat tatra kārayet ||
māghe tu kṛṣṇadvādaśyāṃ yamo ‘pi bhagavān purā |
tilān utpādayāmāsa tapaḥ kṛtvā sudāruṇam ||
rājā daśaratho bhūmau tasmāt tān avatārayat |
tilānām ādhipatyaṃ tu viṣṇus tatra kṛtaḥ suraiḥ ||
tasyām upoṣitaḥ snātaḥ tilais tasmād yajed dharim |
tilatailena dīpāś ca deyā devagṛheṣu ca ||
[436]        nivedayet tilān eva hotavyāś ca tilās tathā |
tilān dadyāc ca viprebhyo bhakṣayec ca tathā tilān ||
māghakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ viṣṇor dehān marīcayaḥ |
niścerus tilakākārāḥ śataśo ‘tha sahasraśaḥ ||
suptasya bhogiśayane yoganidrāgatasya ca |
udānapavanoddhūtā romakūpeṣu sarvataḥ ||
tāraṇād yogimukhyānāṃ bhinne vaikārike sati |
vināśinyaḥ śarīre tāḥ siddhāḥ sūkṣmatvam āgatāḥ ||
tilakākṛtayo jātāḥ saṃsthitāś ca sudhātmanaḥ |
tārakās tu mahāghorāt saṃsāragahanārṇavāt ||
taratiḥ plavane dhātus tasmāt tāsāṃ nigadyate |
anarkābhyudite kāle satsu tārāṃśukeṣv api ||
rājā ca tatra saṃpūjyo yamaḥ pralayabhāskaraḥ |
sarvāṅgasandhisaṃbhūtā nadyo viṣṇoś ca nityaśaḥ ||
aruṇodayavelāyām āraṭanti ca sarvadā |
niyuktā viṣṇunā sarvāḥ kasya pāpaṃ punīmahe ||
nimittaṃ pañcatāreyaṃ tatra proktaṃ yato bhuvi |
tadā sā tu niśā jñeyā tārārātrī sudāruṇā ||
[437]        tatropoṣya trayodaśyāṃ saṃprāpte tu niśākṣaye |
vitastāyāṃ viśokāyāṃ candravatyām athāpi vā ||
tathā harṣapathāyāṃ ca trikoṭyāṃ vā yathākramam |
sindhau kanakavāhinyāṃ tīrtheṣv anyeṣu tatra vā ||
snātvā pūjyo jagadbhartā hariḥ pūjyāś ca tārakāḥ |
yamo nadyaś ca tīrthāni devatāḥ pitaras tathā ||
arghaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpair dīpamālyānulepanaiḥ |
naivedyair vividhākāraiḥ kṛsareṇa tu bhūriṇā ||
vahniḥ pūjyaś ca bhagavān ghṛtāktais tilataṇḍulaiḥ |
namaḥpraṇavasaṃyuktān satilāṃś ca jalāñjalīn ||
yamāya sapta vitaret dharmarājāya sapta ca |
mṛtyave sapta deyāś ca tathā saptāntakāya ca ||
vaivasvatāya saptānyāḥ sapta kālāya caiva hi |
sapta deyāś ca vidhivat sarvaprāṇaharāya ca ||
kṛsaraṃ bhojanīyāś ca brāhmaṇās tadanantaram |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pitṛbhyaś ca vimuktaḥ sarvapātakaiḥ ||
tato vibhajya bandhubhyaḥ kṛsaraṃ bhakṣayet svayam |
umācaturthyāṃ māghe tu śuklāyāṃ yoginīgaṇaiḥ ||
prāg bhakṣayitvā sṛṣṭvā ca bhūyaḥ svāṅgāṅgajair guṇaiḥ |
sahitāḥ sasṛjur yasmāt tasmāt proktā hy umā satī ||
[438]        tasmāt sā tatra saṃpūjyā naraiḥ strībhir viśeṣataḥ |
kundapuṣpaiḥ prayatnena sapadmakusumākṣataiḥ ||
kuëkumālaktakābhyāṃ ca raktasūtraiḥ sakaṅkaṇaiḥ |
arghaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpair dīpair balibhir eva ca ||
guḍārdrakābhyāṃ payasā lavaṇenātha pānakaiḥ |
pūjyāḥ striyaś cāvidhavās tathā viprāś ca bhojanaiḥ ||
saubhāgyavṛddhaye cātha bhoktavyaṃ bandhubhiḥ saha |
śuklāyāṃ māghasaptabhyāṃ pūṣā nāma mahāraviḥ ||
adityāṃ kaśyapāj jajñe sahasrakiraṇo mahān |
tasmāt taṃ pūjayet tatra sopavāso jitendriyaḥ ||
arghaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpair dīpair bhakṣyair manoharaiḥ |
nṛtyair gītais tathā vādyair homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca punar bhakṣyaiḥ pūjyo ghṛtamadhuplutaiḥ |
bhakṣyāṇi tāni deyāni bhakṣitavyāni tāni ca ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ maghāyoge vāyasāḥ pañca jajñire |
indrāc ca varuṇād vāyor yamād atha ca nairṛteḥ ||
śrāddhaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ kartavyaṃ tilaiḥ pūjyās tathā kila |
aindravāruṇavāyavyā yāmyā nairṛtikāś ca ye ||
vāyasāḥ puṇyayaśas te me gṛhṇantu bhojanam |
[439]        dadyād anena mantreṇa tebhyo bhoktuṃ madhuplutam ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu bhuñjīran bandhubhiḥ saha |
phālgunasya ca māsasya kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ mahī mitā ||
nāradapreritair viprair dakṣaputraiḥ sisṛkṣubhiḥ |
subudhyā sūkṣmamānena samudragirivarjitā ||
ato ‘rthaṃ tu mahīmānaṃ svalpaṃ tatra gadyate |
pitṛbhiḥ pūrvadevais tu tatra kṛtvā mahat tapaḥ ||
apūpaiḥ prārthitaṃ śrāddhaṃ manuṣyebhyo janārdanāt |
athaa sasyāni pūrṇāni munibhiḥ kamalodbhavāt ||
prajānāṃ vardhanārthāya kadācit tatra sarvadā |
jātā dāśaratheḥ patnī tasminn ahani jānakī ||
upoṣito raghupatiḥ samudrasya taṭe tadā |
sarvasasyaiś carus tasmāt tatra kartavya eva hi ||
sāpūpais tatra saṃpūjyā viprasaṃbandhibāndhavāḥ |
rāmapatnī ca saṃpūjyā sītā janakanandinī ||
tato navamyāṃ lakṣmīś ca brāhmaṇāś ca bahuśrutāḥ |
hṛdyena piṣṭabhojyena madhuyuktena sarvadā ||
bahuprakārair bhojyaiś ca daśamyāṃ mitrabāndhavāḥ |
tarpyas tv ātmā ca saṃpūjyaḥ śrotavyaṃ gītavāditam ||
maṅgalālambhanaṃ kāryaṃ nityam eva dinatrayam |
kṛṣṇāyāṃ phālgune māsi dvādaśyāṃ śravaṇe sati ||
[440]        cakāra bhagavān viṣṇuḥ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ mahat |
pitāmahebhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ tu tilaiś ca kṛtavān purā ||
sopavāso hariṃ tasmāt tatra saṃpūjayet tilaiḥ |
tilaiḥ saṃpūjayed devīṃ tilāṃśca juhuyāt tathā ||
tilatailena dīpāṃś ca dadyād devagṛheṣv api |
tilān dadyāc ca viprebhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca tilodakam ||
tilāṃś ca bhakṣayet tatra dharmavṛddhyartham ātmanaḥ |
atha kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ phālgune māsi śaṅkaraḥ ||
brahmaviṣṇū mohayitvā jvālāliṅgena māyayā |
jagalliṅgasahasraiś ca pūrayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt ||
tataḥ prabhṛti rūḍhā ca liṅgapūjā kapardinaḥ |
gauramṛttikayā liṅgam tasmāt tatra suśobhanam ||
kuryāt pramāṇasaṃyuktaṃ trayodaśyām upoṣitaḥ |
devotthānavidhānoktair dravyais tac ca prapūjayet ||
arghaiḥ sugandhimālyaiś ca ratnavastrānulepanaiḥ |
naivedyair vividhākārair homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
bhuktvā rātrau tataḥ kuryān nṛtyagītaiḥ prajāgaraḥ |
śrotavyāḥ śivadharmāś ca prādurbhāvāś ca śāṅkarāḥ ||
ahiṃsālakṣaṇaṃ dharmaṃ samāśrityātha śaṅkaraḥ |
ramyaiḥ piṣṭamayaiḥ pūjyaḥ paśubhiś ca svalaṃkṛtaiḥ ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ pūjyāś ca rudro ‘gnir brāhmaṇās tathā |
[441]        māṃsaudanair lopikābhiḥ pūjyaś cātmā sabāndhavaḥ ||
phālgune māsi śuklāyām aṣṭamyāṃ keśavaḥ purā |
mahīṃ sṛṣṭvā tu manasā saśailavanakānanām ||
dakṣaṃ prajāpatiṃ prāha sṛṣṭim āpūrayasva me |
tasya tad vacanaṃ śrutvā sa munis tāṃ mahīṃ tataḥ ||
māpayāmāsa vidhivad bṛhanmānena tatkṣaṇāt |
dvīpārṇavamaruccandranadīvarṣasamanvitaiḥ ||
bṛhanmānaṃ samudrasya śatayojanamātrakam |
cakre raghupatis tatra vṛkṣapāṣāṇakardamaiḥ ||
trikūṭaśikharaprāntasaṃsthito bhagavāṃś ca saḥ |
bṛhac ca tanmahīmānaṃ purāṇo vīkṣya vismitaḥ ||
iyaṃ mahī mayā sṛṣṭā padbhyām iti vicintayan |
hatasya kumbhakarṇasya mahāmānaṃ smarann iva ||
bṛhanmānena dīpāś ca sabāhyābhyantare gṛhe |
dattās tatra mahīdānaṃ kṛtaṃ mālyādibhiḥ sadā ||
tasmād bṛhanmahīmānaṃ yathā kāryaṃ tathā śṛṇu |
anannabhugbhir aṣṭamyāṃ susnātaiś ca svalaṃkṛtaiḥ ||
lakṣmīḥ sītā ca pūjyātha gandhamālyādibhis tathā |
tataḥ pradoṣasamaye dīpāḥ śatasahasraśaḥ ||
narair gṛhe gṛhe deyāḥ sarvadevībhya eva ca |
[442]        devebhyaś ca pitṛbhyaś ca tato bāhye ca veśmanaḥ ||
teṣāṃ copari deyāś ca tāvatsaṃkhyāś ca dīpakāḥ |
avaśiṣṭaṃ tato bhojyaṃ bhakṣitavyaṃ svabandhubhiḥ ||
dvitīye ‘hani madhyāhne śobhanair mālyadāmabhiḥ |
gṛhāṇi bhūṣaṇīyāni tathā devagṛhāṇy api ||
sītāṃ saṃpūjya bhoktavyaṃ nṛtyagītasamākulam |
pakvānnaṃ nityadānaṃ ca tatra deyaṃ tu yācate ||
nānyat kiṃcit prārthitavyaṃ labdhaṃ grāhyam ayatnataḥ |
daśamyāṃ vāpi kartavyaṃ balikarma tathātmanaḥ ||
maṅgalālambhanaṃ kāryam utsavaś ca viśeṣataḥ |
brāhmaṇebhyo dhanaṃ deyaṃ bhṛtyebhyaś ca yathākramam ||
dhānyam annaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca vastram ābharaṇaṃ tathā |
gobhūmiratnaveśmāni deyāni prayatātmane ||
saṃprāptān grāhayet samyaṅ na tatra glapayen manaḥ |
na kaścid vimukhas tatra kartavyas tv āgato janaḥ ||
svajātivihitaṃ pānaṃ peyaṃ deyaṃ ca yācate |
śayyāsane ca kartavye gandhapuṣpādivāsite ||
striyaḥ pūjyās tv alaṃkāryāḥ prahṛṣṭāś ca svarakṣitāḥ |
[443]        niśāyāṃ krīḍamānāś ca nṛtyeyuḥ kṛtabhojanāḥ ||
phālgunyām aryamā jātaas tv aditau kaśyapād api |
atreś cāpy anasūyāyāṃ jātaḥ pūrvam ayaṃ śaśī ||
tau tatra pūjyau vidhivat prāpte candrodaye sati |
gītanṛtyais tathā vādyai rātrau kāryaḥ prajāgaraḥ ||
atha caitreṣu kṛṣṇāyāṃ pratipady api sarvadā |
jhallamallanaṭānāṃ tu draṣṭavyaṃ kauśalaṃ mahat ||
pratipat tu samārabhya pañcamīṃ yāvad eva hi |
pūjākarma tataḥ kāryaṃ strījanasyātmanas tathā ||
bhojanaṃ parpaṭaprāyaṃ bhojyaṃ deyaṃ ca yācate |
caitre tu kṛṣṇapañcamyāṃ kaśmīrā tu rajasvalā ||
nityaṃ bhavati tasmāt tāṃ kṛtvā śailamayīṃ striyaḥ |
abhyaṅgavastranaivedyaiḥ pūjayanti dinatrayam ||
puṣpālaṅkāradhūpaiś ca gorasaṃ varjayanti ca |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca tataḥ snāpyās tābhir eva gṛhe gṛhe ||
susnātābhiḥ prakṛṣṭābhir jīvapatnībhir eva ca |
bhūṣitābhiḥ śubhair vastrair mālyair gandhair viśeṣataḥ ||
anantaraṃ dvijaiḥ snāpyāḥ sarvauṣadhiyutair jalaiḥ |
gandhair bījais tathā ratnaiḥ phalaiḥ siddhārthakais tathā ||
snapayitvā ca tāṃ devīṃ gandhair mālyaiś ca pūjayet |
vastrālaṃkārapuṣpaiś ca tathā puṇyaiś ca gorasaiḥ ||
[444]        mudgapiṣṭais trikoṇaiś ca tathā bhakṣyaiś ca śālijaiḥ |
payasā pāyasair hṛdyair homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
tanniveditaśiṣṭaṃ tu prārthitavyaṃ gṛhe gṛhe |
tantrīmanoharaṃ vādyaṃ tataḥ śrotavyam ādarāt ||
ataḥ param ṛtusnātā garbhaṃ gṛhṇāti medinī |
ādityamahiṣī rājñī paurāṇī devapūjitā ||
rājñīsnapanam etasmāt kāraṇāt tatra viśrutam |
brahmā viṣṇuś ca rudraś ca kaśyapaḥ surabhī tathā ||
indraḥ pracetāḥ parjanyaḥ śeṣaś candrārkavahnayaḥ |
baladevo halaṃ bhūmir vṛṣabhau rāmalakṣmaṇau ||
rakṣoghnau jānakī sītā yugaṃ gaganam eva ca |
ete dvāviṃśatiḥ proktāḥ prajānāṃ patayaḥ śūbhāḥ ||
gomaṅgale tu saṃpūjyāḥ kṛṣyārambhe mahotsave |
arghaiḥ puṣpais tu dhūpaiś ca mālyai ratnaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||
pradakṣiṇair vidhānena homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
tataḥ śakunisūktena vādyaśabdena bhūriṇā ||
halena vāhayed bhūmiṃ svayaṃ snātaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ |
pūrvottarāṃ diśaṃ gacchet kramāt pauraṃdarīṃ śubhām ||
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāvartam aiśānyābhimukhau tataḥ |
vimucya vṛṣabhau bījaṃ puruṣo lakṣaṇānvitaḥ ||
tṛptaḥ suvāsāḥ sragvī ca suvarṇajalasevitam |
suvarṇapātrasaṃyuktaṃ prāṅmukho nipated bhuvi ||
uptvā bījaṃ tu tatraiva bhoktavyaṃ bandhubhiḥ saha |
[445]         utsavaś caiva kartavyo gītanṛtyasamākulaḥ ||
pūrvaṃ tretāyuge kaścin madhyadeśodbhavo dvijaḥ |
tīrthayātrāprasaṅgena cacāra pṛthivīm imām ||
vihāya karma gārhasthyaṃ tasmin gehād viniḥsṛte |
prasaktā brāhmaṇī śūdre śmaśrukarmakare rahaḥ ||
tasyāḥ kadācit putras tu tasmāj jāto vidher vaśāt |
śālikīrtir vivṛddhaś ca savivekaś ca mātaram ||
papraccha jātasaṃdehaḥ pitur janmātmanas tathā |
vakroktyā sa tayā prokto giritaḥ saritas tv imāḥ ||
nimnaṃ prayātā nimneṣu jātāḥ puṇyāś ca śālayaḥ |
sa tu tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā sūryaṃ ca durmanāḥ ||
tapaś cacāra sumahanmaraṇe kṛtaniścayaḥ |
prayāṇakāle brāhmaṇam apaśyat purataḥ sthitam ||
durlabhaṃ brāhmaṇatvaṃ ca na ca tasmāt sa labdhavān |
mūḍhagrāhaḥ svam ātmānaṃ pīḍayitvā vicetanaḥ ||
niṣphalaṃ tu tatas tyāgaṃ parapīḍārtham ācaret |
ekādaśyāṃ tu caitrasya kṛṣṇāyāṃ nirjane mṛtaḥ ||
yakṣāt piśācyāṃ mattāyāṃ lebhe janmātha tāmasam |
jaghanyāṃ yakṣajātiṃ ca gauhyakīṃ tāṃ samāśritaḥ ||
adyāpi tat phalaṃ bhuṅkte tāvanmātraṃ tu tāmasam |
tasmāt sa tatra saṃpūjyaḥ strībhiś ca prātar eva hi ||
pāṣāṇe vartulaprāye mṛnmaye vāpi citritaḥ |
arghaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpaiḥ kuṅkumena sugandhinā ||
aurabhralomabhir matsyair bhakṣyair uccāvacair api |
[446]        tatra viprair anadhyāyaḥ kartavyo vāci saṃyamaḥ ||
tato maadhyāhnasamaye mṛnmaye ca vicitritaḥ |
kṣiptvā dvāreṇa niṣkāsya strībhir eva subhāṣitaiḥ ||
kṣiptvā gavākṣarandhreṇa praveśyaś ca punar gṛhe |
caitrakṛṣṇe caturdaśyāṃ nikumbhaḥ śaṅkaraṃ sadā ||
pūjayati ca dharmātmā piśācaiḥ sahito balī |
tasyāṃ niśāyāṃ kartavyo narair nityaṃ prajāgaraḥ ||
śambhuḥ pūjyo nikumbhaś ca piśācebhyo baliṃ haret |
dīpikās tilatailasya kalkenoktāḥ suśobhanāḥ ||
matsyamāṃsāni bhūrīṇi tathā māṣodanaṃ śubham |
bhakṣyāṇi paramānnāni pānakāni śubhāni ca ||
vṛkṣamūleṣu goṣṭheṣu tathā śūnyagṛheṣu ca |
catuṣpatheṣu rathyāsu catvareṣu nadīṣu ca ||
devālayeṣu puṇyeṣu vṛkṣakoṭarakeṣu ca |
aṭṭālakaśmaśāneṣu rājamārgeṣu sarvadā ||
tasyāṃ niśāyāṃ rakṣyās tu piśācebhyaś ca bālakāḥ |
draṣṭavyaṃ puṃścalīnṛtyaṃ na kuryāt strīniṣevaṇam ||
amāvāsyāṃ tu caitre sārameyaiś ca kāśyapaiḥ |
vāsudevād varo labdhaḥ prabhūtaṃ bhojanaṃ tv iti ||
vaivasvatakule jātau dvau śyāvaśabalau śubhau |
varaṃ yasmāc ca lebhāte yamadvāranivāsanam ||
tasmāt tatra pitṝn devān tarpayitvā dvijaiḥ saha |
śunām annaṃ prabhūtaṃ tu paścād deyaṃ yathepsitam ||
[447]        pratimāsādyadivase sarvaiḥ kāryo mahotsavaḥ |
atyantaduṣṭe saurāgne maṅgalālambhanaṃ tathā ||
sarvaiś ca janmadivase snātair maṅgalapāṇibhiḥ |
gurudevāgniviprāś ca pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
svanakṣatraṃ ca pitarau tathā devaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ yasmāt kartavyas tu mahotsavaḥ ||
iti rājādhirājaśrīmadgovindcandradevamahāsāndhivigrahikeṇa bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajena bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdhareṇa
raviracite kṛtyakalpatarau niyatakālakalpakāṇḍe tithikṛtyāni ||
[448]
11
atha kāntāradīpadānādividhiḥ
athāgastyāder arghadānavidhiḥ
matsyapurāṇe |
nāradovāca |
katham arghapradānādi kartavyaṃ tasya vai vibho |
vidhānaṃ yad agastyasya pūjane tad vadasva me ||
īśvarovāca |
pratyūṣasamaye vidvān kuryāt tasyodaye niśi |
snānaṃ śuklatilais tadvac chuklamālyāmbaro gṛhī ||
sthāpayed avraṇaṃ kumbhaṃ mālyavastravibhūṣitam |
pañcaratnasamāyuktaṃ ghṛtapātreṇa saṃyutam ||
nānābhakṣyaphalair yuktaṃ tāmrapātrasamanvitam |
aṅguṣṭhamātraṃ puruṣaṃ tathaiva
sauvarṇamatyāyatabāhudaṇḍam |
caturmukhaṃ kumbhamukhe nidhāya
dhānyāni saptāmbarasaṃyutāni ||
[449]        sakāṃsyapātrākṣataśuktiyuktaṃ
mantreṇa dadyād dvijapuṃgavāya |
utkṣipya lambodaradīrghabāhum
ananyacetā yamadiṅmukhasthaḥ ||
śvetāṃ ca dadyād yadi śaktir asti
raupyaiḥ khurair hemamayīṃ savatsām |
dhenuṃ naraḥ kṣīravatīṃ praṇamya
vastraghaṇṭābharaṇāṃ dvijāya ||
ā saptarātrād udaye ‘rgham asya
dātavyam etat sakalaṃ nareṇa |
yāvat samāḥ sapta daśātha vā syur
athordhvam apy eva vadanti kecit ||
kāśapuṣpapratīkāśa agnimārutasaṃnibha |
mitrāvaruṇayoḥ putra kumbhayone namo ‘stu te ||
pratyabdaṃ ca phalatyāgam evaṃ kurvan na sīdati |
homaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścād varjayen mānavaḥ phalam ||
anena vidhinā yas tu pumān arghaṃ nivedayet |
indralokam avāpnoti rūpārogyasamanvitaḥ ||
saptaiva lokān āpnoti saptārghyān yaḥ prayacchati |
yāvad āyuś ca yaḥ kuryāt paraṃ brahmādhigacchati ||
iti paṭhati śṛṇoti vā ya etad
vasuyugalaprabhavasya saṃpradānam |
[450]        sakṛd api ca dadāti so ‘pi viṣṇor
bhavanagataḥ paripūjyate naraughaiḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
agastyo dakṣiṇām āśām āśritya nabhasi sthitaḥ |
varuṇasyātmajo yogī vindhyapādavimardanaḥ ||
kanyāṃśebhyaḥ paścimebhyaḥ ṣaḍbhyaḥ prārabhya saṃkhyayā |
aṃśān dvāsaptatiṃ yāvad bhuṅkte sūryas tu rāśiṣu ||
udeti tāvad bhagavān agastyo vyomni toyabhuk |
ṛkṣāṃśebhyaḥ paścimebhyaḥ ṣaḍbhyaḥ prārabhya pūrvavat ||
ṣaṭtriṃśatiṃ ca yāvac ca bhuṅkte bhānur yathākramam |
tāvac chrāntas tu pātālaṃ prayāty astam upaiti ca ||
kṛtopavāsaḥ saṃpaśyed agastyam uditaṃ munim |
sarvakāmapradaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvabhāgyapravardhanam ||
arcitavyaś ca bhagavān śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ |
pūrṇakumbhaiḥ sukūṣmāṇḍair yavair dhānyair ghṛtena ca ||
jātīpadmotpalaiḥ śuddhaiś candanena śubhena ca |
gavyair ghṛtais tathā vastrai ratnaiḥ sāgarasaṃbhavaiḥ ||
upānacchatradaṇḍaiś ca pādukājinavalkalaiḥ |
bhūriṇā paramānnena phalaiḥ puṣpaiś ca śobhanaiḥ ||
annaprakārair bhakṣyaiś ca homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ |
āśāsya ca śubhaṃ kāmam uddiśya ca manogatam ||
[451]        yady ahaṃ prāpnuyāṃ kāmaṃ bhagavan manasepsitam |
tvatprasādād avighnena bhūyas tvāṃ pūjayāmy aham ||
ity uktvā pūjayet paścād daivajñāṃś ca tathā gurūn |
brāḥmaṇān bhojayitvā tu tato bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe [67.12] |
yadāgastyodaye prāpte tadā saptasu rātriṣu |
agastyāya dadāty arghaṃ mahāpuṇyavivṛddhaye ||
śaṅkhe toyaṃ vinikṣipya sitagandhākṣatair yutam |
mantreṇānena vai dadyāc chvetapuṣpasamanvitam ||
“kāśapuṣpa” ityādimantraḥ paṭhanīyaḥ “ātāpī bhakṣita” iti ca |
evaṃ tu yo dadāty argham agastyāyā mahāmune |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sadā tarati durgatim ||
matsyapurāṇe [73.1–11] |
pippalādovāca |
athātaḥ śṛṇu bhūpāla pratiśukraṃ praśāntaye |
yātrārambhe ‘vasāne ca tathā śukrodayeṣv iha ||
[452]        rājate vātha sauvarṇe kāṃsye pātre ‘tha vā punaḥ |
śuklapuṣpāmbarayute sitataṇḍulapūrite ||
nidhāya rājataṃ śukraṃ śucimuktāphalānvitam |
mahāśvetagavā yuktaṃ sāmagāya nivedayet ||
namas te sarvalokeśa namas te bhṛgunandana |
mayi sarvārthasiddhyarthaṃ gṛhāṇārghyaṃ namo ‘stu te ||
yaḥ sa śukrodaye kuryād yātrādiṣu ca bhārata |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti viṣṇuloke mahīyate ||
tadvad vācaspateḥ pūjāṃ pravakṣyāmi yudhiṣṭhira |
suvarṇapātre sauvarṇam amareśapurohitam ||
pītapuṣpāmbarayutaṃ kṛtvā snātvā ca sarṣapaiḥ |
palāśāśvatthabhṛṅgena pañcagavyatilena ca ||
pītāṅgarāgavat sarvaṃ ghṛtahomaṃ tu kārayet |
praṇamya ca gavā sārdhaṃ brāhmaṇāya nivedayet ||
namas te ‘ṅgirasāṃ nātha vākpate ‘tha bṛhaspate |
krūragrahaiḥ pīḍitānām amṛtaugha namo namaḥ ||
saṃkrāntāv api kaunteya yātrāsv abhydayeṣu ca |
kurvaṇ bṛhaspateḥ pūjāṃ sarvān kāmān samaśnute ||
atha kāntāradīpadānavidhiḥ
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
pūrvam āśvayuje māsi paurṇamāsyāṃ samāhitaḥ |
prathame ca niśārambhe manovākkāyasaṃyutaḥ ||
namaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ pretebhyo namo dharmāya viṣṇave |
[453]        namo dharmāya rudrāya kāntārapataye namaḥ ||
dadyād anena mantreṇa dīpaṃ śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
yaḥ kārtikaṃ samagraṃ tu vardhate tasya saṃpadaḥ ||
divākare ‘stācalamaulibhūte
gṛhād adūre puruṣapramāṇam |
pūṣākṛtiṃ yajñiyavṛkṣadārum
āropya bhūmāv atha tasya mūrdhni ||
pañcāṅgulā madhyakendreṇa yuktā
dvihastadīrghā atha paṭṭikās tu |
kṛtvā catasro ‘ṣṭadalākṛtīś ca
yābhir bhaved aṣṭamārgānusāraḥ ||
tatkarṇikāyāṃ tu mahāprakāśā
dīpās tu deyā dalagās tathāṣṭau |
udaṅmukhān dīpavarāṃś ca taila-
ghṛtārdhayuktāṃś ca yathopalabdhān ||
anaṅgalagnaṃ tv atha vastrakhaṇḍaṃ
naavaṃ suraktaṃ tv atha vā suśuklam |
vartyā prayojyaṃ caṣakaṃ ca hṛdyaṃ
snigdhaṃ tv akhaṇḍendusamaṃ praśastam ||
tac chālipiṣṭopari saṃnidhāya
yathā na naśyen na ca kampayeta vā |
sarvaṃ prakuryāt triguṇapramāṇaṃ
madhye sthitasyāpy atha dīparājñaḥ ||
daleṣu śobhārtham atīva kuryān
manorathaprāptyupalabdhaye ca |
[454]        ghaṇṭāṣṭakaṃ lambitapuṣpadāma-
vastropaśobhānvitam atra paścāt ||
saṃyojya bhūmiṃ tv atha gomayena
sacandanāktena jalena liptvā |
anekavarṇair atha maṇḍalaṃ tu
kṛtvāṣṭapatraṃ kamalapramāṇam ||
phalāni mūlāni tathaikṣavaṃ ca
lājā dadhi kṣīram athānnapānam |
nānāvidhaṃ bhakṣyaviśeṣakaṃ ca
sanṛtyagītaṃ madhuraṃ ca vādyam ||
nivedya dharmāya harāya bhūmau
dāmodarāyāpy atha dharmarājñe |
prajāpatibhyas tv atha satpitṛbhyo
pretebhya evānumataḥ sthitebhyaḥ ||
nairṛtyakoṇād atha dakṣiṇāntaṃ
dharmādibhyaḥ pretaparyantakebhyaḥ |
tato jalaṃ śītalamānayitvā
sarpiḥsamāyuktam atīva hṛdyam ||
āpūrya cāṣṭau kalaśāṃś ca tena
nairṛtyakoṇād atha saṃnidhāya |
homādipātraṃ tilapūrṇam eva
dadyāt pidhnaṃ ca sa dakṣiṇāṃca ||
gobhūhiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ ca vastraṃ
phalāni mūlāniyavāni dhānyam |
gṛhaṃ rathaṃ śayanaṃ vāhanaṃ ca
yac cātha kiṃcid dhṛdaye manojñam ||
[455]        nivedayed brāhmaṇasattamebhyo
nairṛtyakoṇād atha saṃsthitebhyaḥ |
ekaikaśaḥ prīṇanaṃ cātra kuryād
dharmādibhyaḥ pretaparyantakebhyaḥ ||
etat samagraṃ vidhivac ca kuryāt
svaśaktim ādau svadhanaṃ vicārya |
dīpān samagrān atha varjayitvā
sarvaṃ nayeyus tv atha vipramukhyāḥ ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya ciraṃ gatāś ca
tato bhavet saṃyatanaktabhojī |
itīdam idṛgvyavahārayuktaṃ
niśāmukhe pratyaham eva kuryāt ||
māsaṃ samagraṃ parayā ca bhaktyā
samāpyate kārtikapaurṇamāsyām |
evaṃ kṛte nākalokād viśiṣṭaṃ
sukhaṃ bhavet pretalokasthitebhyaḥ ||
athedṛśaṃ cāśvayujyāṃ sakṛd vā
punas tv amāyāṃ divase ‘tha paitrye |
kuryān nataḥ kārtikapaurṇamāsyāṃ
dinatrayaṃ dīpamahotsavaṃ ca ||
eko ‘tha vā dīpavaraś ca deyo
rātryāgame kārtikapaurṇamāsyām |
daridraveśmasv atha gokuleṣu
śmaśānadevāyataneṣu caitye ||
nadītaṭe citraguhāntare vāpy
athaikaliṅge ‘py atha caityavṛkṣe |
[456]        aṣṭādhikaṃ tena sahasramātraṃ
vidhāya pātre suśubhe śataṃ vā ||
palair athārdhair atha vā tadardhaiḥ
pramāpya riktas tv atha māṣakair vā |
hastārdhamātraiś ca caturdaśākhyaiḥ
pramāpya vastraṃ tv atha sūtrakaṃ vā ||
prajvālayet tatra nirūpya dhīmān
strīṇām alaṃkāraśataiḥ prapūjya |
deyā mahāvartir atīva hṛdyā
puṇyā ca sā syād bhuvanaprakāśā ||
etan na kuryād atha yas tu mandas
tasyāndhakārasya kutaḥ praśāntiḥ |
[457]
12
atha goparicaryā
tatra viṣṇuḥ [23.57–61] |
gāvaḥ pavitraṃ maṅgalyaṃ goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
gāvo vitanvate yajñaṃ gāvaḥ sarvāghasūdakāḥ ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ dadhi ca rocanā |
ṣaḍaṅgam etat paramaṃ māṅgalyaṃ sarvadā gavām ||
śṛṅgodakaṃ gavāṃ puṇyaṃ sarvāghavinisūdanam |
gavāṃ kaṇḍūyanaṃ caiva sarvakalmaṣanāśanam ||
gavāṃ grāsapradānena svargaloke mahīyate |
gavāṃ hi tīrthe vasatīha gaṅgā
puṣṭis tathāsāṃ rajasi prasiddhā |
lakṣmīḥ karīṣe praṇatau ca dharmas
tāsāṃ praṇāmaṃ satataṃ ca kuryāt ||
varāhapurāṇe |
prasnave yo gavāṃ snāyād rohiṇyāṃ yo naro dvijaḥ |
sa sarvapāpajātāni dahaty āśu na saṃśayaḥ ||
[458]        lāṅgūlenoddhṛtaṃ toyaṃ mūrdhnā gṛhṇāti yo naraḥ |
saritsv anuprayāntīnāṃ sa pāpebhyaḥ pramucyate ||
dhenustanād viniṣkrāntāṃ dhārāṃ kṣīrasya yaḥ pibet |
śirasā pratigṛhṇāti sa pāpebhyaḥ pramucyate ||
brahmapurāṇe |
gāvaḥ stutyāś ca pūjyāś ca gāvaḥ sevyāś ca nityaśaḥ |
gavāṃ goṣṭhasthitānāṃ tu yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇam ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtaṃ tena jagat sadasadātmakam |
tathā |
yajñopavītaṃ tad vālaiḥ pucchotthair munibhiḥ kṛtam ||
yuddhe bhagnaṃ tu yac chṛṅgaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi |
nirmāṃsaṃ prāpya vidhivat pūrayitvā jalena ca ||
snānaṃ kṛtaṃ ca munibhir devaiḥ siddhaiś ca sarvadā |
alakṣmīnāśanaṃ dhanyaṃ sarvasaubhāgyavardhanam ||
oṃ tṛṇodakādyeṣu vaneṣu mattāḥ
krīḍantu gāvaḥ savṛṣāḥ savatsāḥ |
kṣīraṃ pramuñcantu sukhaṃ svapantu
śītātapavyādhibhayair vimuktāḥ ||
imaṃ mantraṃ suśuddhātmā japen nityaṃ samāhitaḥ |
gacchan tiṣṭhan svapañ jighran bhuñjan krīḍan samucchvasan ||
pañcaviṃśo mahāmantraḥ paṭhitas tu surāsuraiḥ |
śruto ‘numodito dyātaḥ kīrtitaḥ pūjitaḥ kramāt ||
[459]        tadā rasātalād gāvaḥ pañca puṇyāḥ samutthitāḥ |
nandā sunandā surabhī suśīlā sumanā tathā ||
ratnāny anekāny ādāya kaustubhaprabhṛtīni ca |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtās tāś ca sarvair devāsuraiḥ kramāt ||
saṃskrtāḥ pūjitāḥ samyag dṛṣṭāḥ spṛṣṭāś ca vanditāḥ |
kasyacit tv atha kālasya divyās tāḥ kāmadhenavaḥ ||
brahmasthānārcitās tuṣṭā vavrire varam uttamam |
dakṣāt prajāpater janma marīcaṃ kaśyapaṃ prati ||
śivaloke ca gamanaṃ na bhūyo janma kutracit |
vane vāsas tṛṇaṃ toyaṃ bhojanaṃ niṣparigraham ||
dṛḍhena mṛdunā bandhaḥ pāśenāstṛtaveśmani |
ghāsagrāsādikaṃ bhūri niśi dīpaṃ tu bhāsvaram ||
itihāsapurāṇānāṃ śravaṇaṃ sopavīṇanam |
annamuṣṭir yathāśaktyā paricaryā yathākramam ||
tāḍanākrośakhedāś ca svapne ‘pi na kadācana |
tāsāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṣu nodvegaḥ kriyate kvacit ||
śodhanīyaś ca govāṭaḥ śuṣkaiḥ kṣārādibhiḥ sadā |
grīṣme vṛkṣākule veśma śītatoyavikardame ||
varṣāsu vātha śiśire sukhoṣṇe vātavarjite |
gokule rudramukhyāś ca pratiṣṭhāpyā gaṇaiḥ saha ||
jāpakaiḥ sarvamantraiś ca japtavyās tv ātmaśuddhaye |
ucchiṣṭamūtraviṭśleṣmamalaṃ jahyān na tatra ca ||
rajasvalā na praveśyā nānyajātir na puṃścalī |
na tāḥ paribhavet toye gokule tu viśeṣataḥ ||
[460]        na laṅghayed vatsatarīn krīḍayed goṣṭhasaṃnidhau |
na gantavyaṃ gavāṃ madhye sopānatkaiḥ sapādukaiḥ ||
hastyaśvarathayānaiś ca saṃvitānaiḥ kadācana |
dakṣiṇottarataḥ prahvair gantavyaaṃ ca padātibhiḥ ||
gāvaḥ kṛśāturāḥ pālyāḥ śraddhayā pitṛmātṛvat |
giriprapātasiṃharkṣaśītātapabhayāt tathā ||
mahākolāhale ghore durdine deśaviplave |
gavāṃ tṛṇāni deyāni śītalaṃ ca tato jalam ||
tathā |
gāvaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ rakṣyāḥ sarvapāpapraśāntaye |
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyair vaiśyaiḥ śūdraiś ca susamāhitaiḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
dvau māsau pāyayed vatsaṃ tṛtīye dvistanaṃ duhet |
caturthe tristanaṃ caiva yathānyāyaṃ yathābalam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
āṣāḍhyām āśvayujyāṃ ca pauṣyāṃ caitryāṃ ca sarvadā |
na gṛhṇīyād gavāṃ kṣīraṃ sarvaṃ vatsāya nikṣipet ||
na ṣaṇḍaṃ vāhayed dhenuṃ na gāṃ bhāreṇa pīḍayet |
yugādyeṣu yugānteṣu ṣaḍaśītimukheṣu ca ||
dakṣiṇottarage sūrye tathā viṣuvator dvayoḥ |
saṃkrānteṣu ca sarvāsu grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ||
pañcadaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ dvādaśyām aṣṭamīṣu ca |
[461]        upacāro gavāṃ kāryo māsi māsi yathākramam ||
lavaṇasya ca catvāri palāny aṣṭau ghṛtasya vā |
parakīyasya dugdhasya tathā deyāni ṣoḍaśa ||
dvātriṃśac chītalasyāpi jalasyāpi palāni ca |
ādau vicārya vai sarvaṃ pariṇāmaṃ balaṃ rucim ||
ākasmikaṃ tu dātavyaṃ puṇyārthaṃ tu gavāhnikam |
prabhāte lavaṇaṃ tatra dīyate ca tato jalam ||
tatas tṛṇāni bhojyaṃ ca poṣaṇaṃ māṃsavarjitam |
niśi dīpaḥ satantrīko divyā paurāṇikī kathā ||
evaṃ kṛte ratnapūrṇāṃ mahīṃ datvā bhavet phalam |
gopradānāt tu yat puṇyaṃ gavāṃ saṃrakṣaṇād bhavet ||
manuṣyais tṛṇatoyādyair gāvaḥ pālyā viśeṣataḥ |
deyāḥ poṣyāś ca rakṣyāś ca pūjyā grāhyāś ca sarvadā ||
padmapurāṇe |
sadā gāvaḥ praṇamyās tu mantreṇānena pārthiva |
namo gobhyaḥ śrīmatībhyaḥ saurabheyībhya eva ca ||
namo brahmasutābhyaś ca pavitrābhyo namo namaḥ |
mantrasya smaraṇād eva godānaphalam āpnuyāt ||
ādityapurāṇe |
lavaṇaṃ ca yathāśaktyā gavāṃ yo vai dadāti ca |
teṣāṃ puṇyakṛtāṃ lokā brahmaloke vrajanti te ||
yo ‘graṃ bhaktyā kiṃcid aprāśya dadyād
gobhyo nityaṃ govratī satyavādī |
[462]        śānto buddho gosahasrasya puṇyaṃ
saṃvatsareṇāpnuyād dharmaśīlaḥ ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
gām ālabhya namaskṛtya kṛtvā caiva pradakṣiṇam |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasuṃdharā ||
gavām asthi na laṅgheta mṛtagandhaṃ na varjayet |
yāvad āghrāti taṃ gandhaṃ tāvat puṇyena yujyate ||
devīpurāṇe |
gorūpāṇāṃ gavāṃ goṣṭhe yas tu dhūmaṃ na kārayet |
makṣikāṇāṃ ca narake makṣikābhiḥ sa bhakṣyate ||
mṛtavatsāṃ tu gāṃ yas tu damitvā pibate naraḥ |
tāḍitābhiś ciraṃ tiṣṭhet kṣudhārto vai narādhamaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
gokulasya tṛṣārtasya jalānte vasudhādhipa |
utpādayati yo vighnaṃ tam āhur brahmaghātakam ||
kṛtvā gavārthe śaraṇaṃ śītavātakṣamaṃ mahat |
āsaptamaṃ tārayati kulaṃ bharatasattama ||
śapatheṣu surabhīvākyam |
balvajena nivāpena kāṃsye bhavatu dohanam |
[463]        duhyeta paravatsena yo me harati duṣkṛtam ||
skandapurāṇe |
kapilā yasya goṣṭhe syād anagner brāhmaṇasya ca |
sa yāti dustaraṃ ghoraṃ narakaṃ tat tamomayam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
sadoṣā gaur gṛhe jātā paripālyā sadā svayam |
duḥśīlo ‘pi dvijaḥ pūjyo na tu śūdraḥ susaṃyataḥ ||
anāthānāṃ gavāṃ yatnāt kāryas tu śiśire maṭhaḥ |
puṇyārthaṃ tatra dīyante tṛṇatoyendhanāni ca ||
[464]
13
atha vṛṣotsargaḥ
tatra viṣṇuḥ [86.1–20] |
kārtikyām āśvayujyāṃ vā tatrādau vṛṣabhaṃ parīkṣet | jīvadvatsāyāḥ payasvinyā vatsam | sarvalakṣanopetam | nīlam | lohitaṃ vā mukhapucchapādeṣu sarvaśuklam | yūthasyācchādakam | tato gavāṃ madhye susamiddhāgniṃ paristīrya pauṣṇaṃ caruṃ śrapayitvā pūṣā gā anvetu na iha ratir iti ca hutvā vṛṣam ayaskāras tv āhvayet | ekasmin pārśve cakreṇāparasmin śūlenāṅkitaṃ ca hiraṇyavarṇā iti catasṛbhiḥ śan no devīr iti ca snāpayet | snātam alaṃkṛtaṃ snātālaṃkṛtābhiś catasṛbhir vatsatarībhiḥ sārdham ānīya rudrān puruṣasūktaṃ kūṣmāṇḍīś ca japet | pitā vatsānām iti vṛṣabhasya dakṣiṇe karṇe paṭhet | imaṃ ca |
vṛṣo hi bhagavān darmaś catuṣpādaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
vṛṇomi tam ahaṃ bhaktyā sa māṃ rakṣatu sarvataḥ ||
[465]                etaṃ yuvānaṃ patiṃ vo dadāmi
tena krīḍantīś caratha priyeṇa |
mā hāsmahi prajayā mā tanūbhir
mā radhāma dviṣate soma rājan ||
vṛṣaṃ vatsatarīyuktam aiśānyāṃ kārayed diśi |
hotur vastrayugaṃ dadyāt suvarṇaṃ kāṃsyam eva ca ||
ayaskārasya dātavyaṃ vetanaṃ manasepsitam |
bhojanaṃ bahusarpiṣkaṃ brāhmaṇāṃś cātra bhojayet ||
utsṛṣṭo vṛṣabho yasmin pibaty atha jalāśaye |
śṛṅgāgreṇollikhed bhūmiṃ yatra kvacana darpitaḥ ||
pitṝṇām annapānaṃ tat prabhūtam upatiṣṭhati |
brahmapurāṇe |
atha caitryāṃ vṛṣotsargaḥ kārtikyāṃ vā viśeṣataḥ |
kartavyas tv atha revatyāṃ tribhir varṇair dvijātibhiḥ ||
vṛṣabhaḥ kṛṣṇasāras tu pratyagras tu trihāyanaḥ |
manojño darśanīyaś ca sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ ||
aṣṭābhir dhenubhir yuktaś caturbhir atha vā kramāt |
trihāyanībhir dhanyābhiḥ surūpābhiś ca śobhitaḥ ||
sarvopakaraṇopetaḥ sarvasasyacaro mahān |
utsṛṣṭavyo vidhānena śrutismṛtinidarśanāt ||
prāgudakpravaṇe deśe manojñe nirjane vane |
[466]        na vāhyo na ca tatkṣīraṃ pātavyaṃ kenacit kvacit ||
svadhā pitṛbhyo mātṛbhyo bandhubhyaś cāpi tarpayet |
pitṛpakṣās tu ye kecid ye cānye mātṛpakṣajāḥ ||
guruśvaśurabandhūnāṃ ye kuleṣu samudbhavāḥ |
ye pretabhāvam āpannā ye cānye śrāddhavarjitāḥ ||
vṛṣotsargeṇa te sarve labhante tṛptim uttamām |
dadād anena mantreṇa tilākṣatayutaṃ jalam ||
pitṛbhyaś ca samāsena brāhmaṇebhyaś ca dakṣiṇām |
tataḥ pramuditās tena vṛṣabheṇa samanvitāḥ ||
vaneṣu gāvaḥ krīḍanti vṛṣotsargaprasiddhiṣu |
apravṛtte vṛṣotsarge dātā vakroktibhiḥ padaiḥ ||
brāhmaṇān āha yat kiṃcid yenotsṛṣṭaṃ tu nirjane |
tat kaścid anyo na nayed vibhājyaṃ ca yathākramam ||
vṛṣotsargād ṛte nānyat puṇyam asti mahītale |
matsyapurāṇe [207.2–41] |
dhenum ādau parīkṣeta suśīlāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām |
avyaṅgām aparikliṣṭāṃ jīvadvatsām arogiṇīm ||
snigdhavarṇāṃ snigdhakhurāṃ snigdhaśṛṅgāṃ tathaiva ca |
manoharākṛtiṃ saumyāṃ supramāṇām anuddhṛtām ||
[467]        āvartair dakṣiṇāvartair yuktā dakṣiṇataś ca yā |
vāmāvartair vāmataś ca vistīrṇajaghanā tathā ||
mudusaṃhatatāmroṣṭhī raktajihvā supūjitā |
āsyāvadīrghā sphuṭitaraktajihvā tathā ca yā ||
tāmrābhāvilanetrā ca śaphair aviralair dṛḍhaiḥ |
vaiḍūryamadhuvarṇaiś ca jalabudbhudasaṃnibhaiḥ ||
raktasnigdhaiś ca nayanai raktaiś caiva ca nīlakaiḥ |
saptacaturdaśadantā bhaved aśyāmatālukā ||
uraḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ śiraḥ kukṣiḥ śroṇī ca vasudhādhipa |
ṣaḍunnatāni dhenūnāṃ pūjayanti vicakṣaṇāḥ ||
karṇau netre lalāṭaṃ ca pañca bhāskaranandana |
samāyatāni śasyante pucchaṃ sāsnā ca sakthinī ||
catvāraś ca stanā rājann evam aṣṭau manīṣibhiḥ |
śirogrīvāyutās tv ete bhūmipāla smṛtā daśa ||
tasmāt tu taṃ parīkṣeta vṛṣabhaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam |
unnataskandhakakudaṃ mṛdulāṅgūlabhūṣitam ||
mahākaṭitaṭaskandhaṃ vaiḍūryamaṇilocanam |
pravālavarṇaśṛṅgāgraṃ sudīrghaṛtuvāladhim ||
navāṣṭadaśasaṃkhyais tu tīkṣṇāgrair daśanaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
mallikākṣaś ca bhoktavyo gṛhe ‘pi dhanadhānyadaḥ ||
[468]        varṇas tu tāmraḥ kapilo brāhmaṇasya praśasyate |
śveto raktaś ca kṛṣṇaś ca gauraḥ pāṭala eva ca ||
indranīlābhapṛṣṭhaś ca śabalaḥ pañcakālakaḥ |
pṛthukarṇo mahāskandhaḥ ślakṣṇaromā ca yo bhavet ||
raktākṣaḥ kapilo yaś ca raktaśṛṅgatalo bhavet |
śvetodaraḥ kṛṣṇapṛṣṭho brāhmaṇasya ca śasyate ||
snigdharaktena varṇena kṣatriyasya praśasyate |
kāñcanābhena vaiśyasya kṛṣṇenāpy antyajanmanaḥ ||
yasya prāgāyate śṛṅge svamukhābhimukhe sadā |
sarveṣām eva varṇānāṃ sa ca sarvārthasādhakaḥ ||
mārjārapādaḥ kapilo dhanyaḥ kapilalocanaḥ |
śveto mārjārapādas tu dhanyo maṇinibhekṣaṇaḥ ||
karaṭaḥ piṅgalaś caiva śvetapādas tathaiva ca |
svacchapādaśiro yaś ca dvipādaśveta eva ca ||
kapiñjalanibho dhanyas tathā tittirisaṃnibhaḥ |
ākarṇamūlācchvetaṃ tu mukhaṃ yasya prakāśate ||
nandīmukhaḥ sa vijñeyo raktavarṇo viśeṣataḥ |
śvetaṃ ca jaṭharaṃ yasya bhavet pṛṣṭhaṃ ca gopateḥ ||
vṛṣabhaḥ sa samudrāsyaḥ satataṃ kulavardhanaḥ |
mallikāpuṣpacitras tu dhanyo bhavati puṃgavaḥ ||
[469]        kamalair maṇḍalaiś cāpi citro bhavati bhāgyataḥ |
atasīpuṣpavarṇas tu tathā dhanyataraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
ete dhanyās tathādhanyān kīrtayiṣyāmi te nṛpa |
kṛṣṇatālvoṣṭhadaśanā rūkṣaśṛṅganakhāś ca ye ||
avyaktavarṇā hrasvāś ca vyāghrabhasmanibhāś ca ye |
dhvāṅkṣagṛdhrasavarṇāś ca tathā mūṣikasaṃnibhāḥ ||
kuṇṭhāḥ kāṇās tathā khañjāḥ kekarākṣās tathaiva ca |
viṣamaśvetapādāś ca udbhrāntanayanās tathā ||
naite vṛṣā vimoktavyā na ca dhāryās tathā gṛhe |
moktavyānāṃ ca dhāryāṇāṃ bhūyo vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
svastikākāraśṛṅgāś ca meghaughasadṛśasvanāḥ |
mahāpramāṇāś ca tathā mattamātaṅgagāminaḥ ||
mahoraskā mahocchvāsā mahābalaparākramāḥ |
śiraḥ karṇau lalāṭaṃ ca vāladhiś caraṇāni ca ||
netrapārśve ca kṛṣṇāni tasyānte candramāstviṣaḥ |
śvetāny anyāni śasyante kṛṣṇasya tu viśeṣataḥ ||
bhūmau karṣati lāṅgūlaṃ pralambasthūlavāladhiḥ |
purastād unnato nīlo vṛṣabhas tu praśasyate ||
śaktidhvajapatākābhā yeṣāṃ rājī virājate |
anaḍvāhaś ca te dhanyā vittasiddhijayāvahāḥ ||
[470]        pradakṣiṇaṃ nivartante svayaṃ te tu nivartitāḥ |
samunnataśirogrīvā dhanyās te yūthavardhanāḥ ||
raktaśṛṅgāgranayanaḥ śvetavarṇo bhaved yadi |
śaphaiḥ pravālasadṛśair nāsti dhanyataras tataḥ ||
ete dhāryāḥ prayatnena moktavyā yadi vā vṛṣāḥ |
dhāritā yadi vā muktā dhanadhānyavivardhanāḥ ||
caraṇāni mukhaṃ pucchaṃ yasya śvetāni gopateḥ |
lākṣārasasavarṇaś ca taṃ nīlam iti nirdiśet ||
vṛṣa eva sa moktavyo na saṃdhāryo gṛhe vaset |
tadartham eṣā carati loke gāthā purātanī ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
gaurīṃ vāpy udvahed bhāryāṃ nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet ||
evaṃ vṛṣaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃprayuktaṃ
gṛhodbhavaṃ krītam athāpi rājan |
muktvā na śocen maraṇaṃ mahātmā
mokṣe matiṃ cāham ato ‘bhidhāsye ||
ādityapurāṇe |
bhānur uvāca |
muñcanti vṛṣabhaṃ yet tu nīlaṃ caiva suśobhanam |
lāṅgūlākarṣasarvāṅgaṃ śṛṅgayuktaṃ manoharam ||
kārtikyāṃ muñcate yas tu datvā pūjāṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
trivarṣās tv atha gurviṇyo dadyād gāvo vṛṣasya ca ||
[471]        sāvitrīṃ ca japet tatra tathā caivāghamarṣaṇam |
karṇajāpyaṃ pradadyāt tu vṛṣabhasya na saṃśayaḥ ||
ghaṇṭāṃ lohakṛtāṃ dadyāc chṛṅgaiś ca paṭalaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
rudrasyāgre bhojayec ca brāhmaṇān vai yathāvidhi ||
yāvanti romakūpāni vṛṣabhasya bhavanti vai |
gavāṃ caiva tu romāṇi yāvat tiṣṭhanti vai mune |
tāvat koṭisahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate ||
yat kiṃcit kurute pāpaṃ puruṣo vṛttikarṣitaḥ |
te sarve vilayaṃ yānti gopateḥ parimocanāt ||
vṛṣabhasya tu śabdena pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ |
avalambamānā dṛśyante svargaloke na saṃśayaḥ ||
jale prakṣipya lāṅgūlaṃ toyaṃ codvahate vṛṣaḥ |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi pitaras tena tarpitāḥ ||
kūle samuddhṛtā yāvac chṛṅge tiṣṭhati mṛttikā |
bhakṣyabhojyamaayaiḥ śailaiḥ pitaras tena tarpitāḥ ||
gavāṃ madhye yadā caivaaṃ vṛṣabhaḥ krīḍate tu yaḥ |
apsaraughasahasreṇa krīḍante nityam eva tu ||
lāṅgūlam udyamya yāvaat toyeṣu krīḍate tu saḥ |
apsarogaṇasaṅghaiś ca tṛpyanti pitaraḥ sadā ||
[472]        sahasradattamātreṇa kanakena yathāvidhi |
tṛptis tu yā pitṛṇāṃ vai sā vṛṣeṇa samocyate ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
lohito yas tu varṇena mukhe pucche tu pāṇḍuraḥ |
śvetaḥ khuraviṣāṇābhyāṃ sa nīlo vṛṣa ucyate ||
[473]
14
atha purāṇaśravaṇavidhiḥ
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
śatānīka uvāca |
bhagavan kena vidhinā śrotavyaṃ bhārataṃ naraiḥ |
caritaṃ rāmabhadrasya purāṇāni viśeṣataḥ ||
kathaṃ ca vaiṣṇavā dharmāḥ śivadharmā viśeṣataḥ |
saurāṇāṃ vāpi viprendra śravaṇe ucyatāṃ vidhiḥ ||
sumantur uvāca |
hanta te kathayiṣye ‘haṃ purāṇaśravaṇe vidhim |
itihāsapurāṇāni śrutvā bhaktyā viśāṃpate ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo brahmahatyādibhir vibho |
sāyaṃ prātas tathā rātrau śuddho bhūtvā śṛṇoti yaḥ ||
tasya viṣṇus tathā brahmā tuṣyate śaṅkaras tathā |
[474]        vidhānaṃ vācakasyeha śṛṇu tāvad viśāṃpate ||
śuddhavāsā gṛhād etya sthānaṃ yat samayānvitam |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā yā tasmin devataiva hi ||
nātyuccam āha sarveṣām aśeṣaṃ guruvan nṛpa |
namaskārād atha śrāvyaṃ śivam astv iti cāntataḥ ||
nānyato nṛpśārdūla sarvair varṇair mahīmate |
śūdrāṇāṃ purato vaiśyā vaiśyānāṃ kṣatriyās tataḥ ||
kṣatriyāṇāṃ tathā viprā śṛṇvantv ete ‘grataḥ sadā |
madhye sthito ‘tha sarveṣāṃ vācako vācayn nṛpa ||
ye vāpi saṃkarā rājan narās te śūdrapṛṣṭhataḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ vācakaṃ vidyān nānyavarṇajam ādarāt ||
śrutvānyavarṇajād rājan vācakān narakaṃ vrajet |
itthaṃ hi śṛṇvatāṃ teṣāṃ varṇānām anupūrvaśaḥ ||
māsi māsi bhaved rājan pāraṇaṃ kurunandana |
śreyo ‘rtham ātmano rājan pūjayed vācakaṃ nṛpa ||
māsi pūrṇe nṛpaśreṣṭha dātavyaḥ svarṇamāṣakaḥ |
brāhmaṇena mahābāho dvau deyau kṣatriyeṇa tu ||
vācakasya nṛpaśreṣṭha vaiśyenāpi trayas tathā |
[475]        śūdreṇāpy atha catvāro dātavyāḥ svarṇamāṣakāḥ ||
prathame pāraṇe rājan vācakaṃ pūjya śaktitaḥ |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalam āpnoti mānavaḥ ||
kārtikādi mahābāho kārtike yāvad eva tu |
agniṣṭomaṃ gosavaṃ ca jyotiṣṭomaṃ tathā nṛpa ||
sautrāmaṇiṃ vājapeyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tathā vibho |
māheśvaraṃ tathā brāhmaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ ca bhūpate ||
ādityayajñasya tathā rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ |
phalam āpnoti rājendra māsair dvādaśabhiḥ kramāt ||
itthaṃ yajñaphalaṃ prāpye yāti lokāṃs tathottamān |
samāpte parvaṇi tathā svaśaktyā tarpayen nṛpa ||
vācakaṃ brāhmaṇāṃś caiva sarvakāmaiḥ prapūjayet |
gandhamālyāni divyāni vastrāṇy ābharaṇāni ca ||
vācakāya pradadyāt tu tato viprān prapūjayet |
hiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ gāvaḥ kāṃsyopadohanāḥ ||
datvā tu vācakāyeha śrutasya prāpnuyāt phalam |
[476]        vācakaḥ pūjito yena prasannās tasya devatāḥ ||
tasmād dānaṃ sadā pūrvaṃ tasya deyaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
śrāddhe yasya dvijo bhuṅkte vācakaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ ||
bhavanti pitaras tasya tṛptā varśaśataṃ nṛpa |
vispaṣṭam adbhutaṃ śāntaṃ spaṣṭākṣarapadaṃ tathā ||
kalasvarasamāyuktaṃ rasabhāvasamanvitam |
budhyamānaḥ sadātyarthaṃ granthārthaṃ kṛtsnaśo nṛpa ||
brāhmaṇādiṣu varṇeṣu granthārthaṃ vācayen nṛpa |
ya evaṃ vācayed rājan sa vipro vyāsa ucyate ||
ato ‘nyathā vācayāno jñeyo ‘sau pitṛnāmakaḥ |
itthaṃbhūto vased yasmin vācako vyāsasaṃmitaḥ ||
deśeṣu pattane rājan sa deśaḥ pravaraḥ smṛtaḥ |
praṇamya vācakaṃ bhaktyā yat phalaṃ prāpyate naraiḥ ||
na tat kratusahasreṇa prāpyate kurunandana |
yathaikato grahāḥ sarve ekatas tu divākaraḥ ||
tathaikato dvijāḥ sarve ekatas tu sa vācakaḥ |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca pāvanaṃ paramaṃ nṛpa ||
vācakaś ca yatiś caiva tathā vipraḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
ete sarve nṛpaśreṣṭha vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
[477]        trividhaṃ vācakaṃ vidyāt sadā ca guṇabhedataḥ |
śrāvakaś ca mahābāho trividho guṇabhedataḥ ||
dvāv etau kathyamānau tvaṃ nibodha gadato mama |
atidrutaṃ tathāspaṣṭaṃ visvaraṃ rasavarjitam ||
padacchedavihīnaṃ ca tathā bhāvavivarjitam |
abudhymāno granthārthaṃ lolaś cotsāhavarjitaḥ ||
īdṛśaṃ vācayed yas tu vācakas tu nareśvara |
krodhano ‘priyavādī cājñānād granthavidūṣakaḥ ||
na ca budhyati kaṣṭāni sa jñeyo vācakādhamaḥ |
vispaṣṭam adbhutaṃ śāntaṃ spaṣṭākṣarapadaṃ tathā ||
kalasvarasamāyuktaṃ rasabhāvavimaśritam |
abudhyamāno granthārthaṃ vācayed yas tu vācakaḥ ||
sa jñeyo rājaso rājan nidānīṃ sātvikaṃ śṛṇu |
granthāthaṃ budhyamānas tu samagraṃ kṛtsnaśo nṛpa ||
brāhmaṇādiṣu varṇeṣu arpayan vidhivan nṛpa |
ya evaṃ vācayed rājan sa jñeyo sātviko budhaiḥ ||
[478]        śraddhābhaktivihīno ‘sau lobhiṣṭho dūṣakas tathā |
hetuvādaparo rājan tathāsūyāsamanvitaḥ ||
nityāṃ naimittikīṃ kāmyām adadad dakṣiṇāṃ nṛpa |
vācakāya mahābāho śṛṇuyād yad tu mānavaḥ ||
sa jñeyas tāmaso rājan śrāvako mānavaḥ sadā |
na tasya purato vīra vācayet prājña eva hi ||
prasaṅgāc chṛṇuyād yas tu śraddhābhaktivivarjitaḥ |
śrotā kautukamātras tu sa jñeyo rājaso budhaiḥ ||
saṃtyajya sarvakarmāṇi bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
satataṃ pūjayānas tu vācakaṃ śraddhayā nṛpa ||
nitye naimittike kāmye gurave ca dadat tathā |
ya evaṃ śṛṇuyād vīra sa jñeyaḥ sātviko budhaiḥ ||
vyāsaḥ pūjyaḥ śrāvakāṇāṃ yathā vyāsavaco nṛpa |
tasmāt pūjyo nṛpaśreṣṭha prathamaṃ vācako budhaiḥ ||
āpatkāle ca vṛddhau ca yataś cāsau guruḥ sadā |
vaiśākhyāṃ samaye vīra tṛtīyāyāṃ ca suprabha ||
kārtikyām atha māghyāṃ ca saṃpūjyaḥ prathamo bhavet |
parvasv eteṣu ca vibho saṃpūjyo dharmataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
hiraṇyaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ ca dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ tathaiva ca |
annaṃ vāpi tathā pakvaṃ māṃsaṃ ca kurunandana ||
[479]        dātavyaṃ prathamaṃ tasmai śrāvakaiḥ sarvabhaktitaḥ |
datvā puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ patram indhanam eva ca ||
sārabhūtaṃ ca yac cānyat tasmai deyaṃ samantataḥ |
atha sarvais tathā kāryaṃ śrāvakaiḥ pūjanaṃ nṛpa ||
vācakas tu yathā nityaṃ sukham āste narādhipa |
na pīḍyate yathā dvandvais (?) tathā kāryaṃ varānana ||
haimante lomaśaṃ deyaṃ chatraṃ prāvṛṣi sattama |
upānahau kālayoge kāle vai kuśalomaśau ||
yadā dātuṃ na śaknoti māṣakaṃ kāñcanasya tu |
tatas tasya tadā dadyād rājataṃ śreyase ‘nagha ||
tadabhāve hiraṇyaṃ tu vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet |
mṛttikāpi hi dātavyā kurvatā saphalaṃ śrutam ||
ity eṣā kathitā nityā māsi māsi bhavet tadā |
naimittikī bhaved rājan grahaṇādiṣu parvasu ||
amale vāsasī rājan gandhamālyavibhūṣaṇam |
samāpte parvaṇi vibho dātavyaṃ bhūtim icchatā ||
jñātvā parvasamāptiṃ tu pūjayed vācakaṃ budhaḥ |
ātmānam api vikrīya ca icchet saphalaṃ śrutam ||
naimittikīṃ ca nityāṃ ca dakṣiṇāṃ na dadāti yaḥ |
śṛṇoti ca sadā tāta tasya tan niṣphalaṃ śrutam ||
caturguṇā bhaved rājan yā nityaṃ dakṣiṇā vibho |
[480]        samāpte parvaṇi vibho ity āha bhagavān śivaḥ ||
ity eṣa kathito rājan purāṇaśravaṇe vidhiḥ |
iti mahārājādhirājaśrīmadgovindacandradevamahāsaṃdhivigrahikeṇa
bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajena śrīlakṣmīdhareṇa viracite kṛtyakalpatarau
niyatakālakāṇḍaṃ samāptam. ||
